return (function()
local notify, hexToColor, UIKit

-- ============================================================
-- PALETTE

local P = {
    Bg      = Color3.fromRGB(13, 14, 19),
    Bg2     = Color3.fromRGB(20, 22, 30),

-- ============================================================
-- LUCIDE ICONS  (robust: supports many library data shapes)
-- ============================================================
local LUCIDE_URL = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eeeiqjjj/icon.github.io/refs/heads/main/lucide.lua"
local Lucide = nil
local LucideReady = false
local IconCache = {}
local lucideWarned = false

local function lucideFetch(url)
    local body
    pcall(function() body = game:HttpGet(url) end)
    if not body or body == "" then
        local req = (syn and syn.request) or (http and http.request) or http_request or request
        if req then
            pcall(function()
                local r = req({ Url = url, Method = "GET" })
                body = r and r.Body
            end)
        end
    end
    return body
end

task.spawn(function()
    for _ = 1, 3 do
        local ok, res = pcall(function()
            local body = lucideFetch(LUCIDE_URL)
            assert(body and body ~= "", "empty response")
            return assert(loadstring(body))()
        end)
        if ok and res ~= nil then
            Lucide = res
            LucideReady = true
            return
        end
        task.wait(1.5)
    end
end)

local SIZE_KEYS  = { "48px", "64px", "256px", "24px", 48, 64, 24 }
local SHEET_KEYS = { "Spritesheets", "SpriteSheets", "Spritesheet", "Sheets", "Assets", "assets" }

local function toVec2(v)
    if typeof(v) == "Vector2" then return v end
    if type(v) == "table" then
        local x = v[1] or v.X or v.x
        local y = v[2] or v.Y or v.y
        if type(x) == "number" and type(y) == "number" then return Vector2.new(x, y) end
    end
    return nil
end

local function toImageString(v)
    if type(v) == "number" then return "rbxassetid://" .. v end
    if type(v) == "string" and v ~= "" then
        if v:match("^%d+$") then return "rbxassetid://" .. v end
        return v
    end
    return nil
end

local function lookupSheet(container, idx)
    if type(container) ~= "table" then return nil end
    for _, k in ipairs(SHEET_KEYS) do
        local s = container[k]
        if type(s) == "table" then
            local v = s[idx] or s[tostring(idx)] or (tonumber(idx) and s[tonumber(idx)])
            if v then return v end
        end
    end
    return nil
end

-- entry -> image, rectSize, rectOffset
local function parseEntry(entry, containers)
    if entry == nil then return nil end
    if type(entry) == "string" or type(entry) == "number" then
        return toImageString(entry), nil, nil
    end
    if type(entry) ~= "table" then return nil end

    local raw = entry.Image or entry.image or entry.Url or entry.URL or entry.url
        or entry.id or entry.Id or entry.ImageId
    local size = toVec2(entry.ImageRectSize or entry.imageRectSize or entry.RectSize or entry.size or entry.Size)
    local offset = toVec2(entry.ImageRectOffset or entry.ImageRectPosition or entry.imageRectOffset
        or entry.imageRectPosition or entry.RectOffset or entry.offset or entry.Position)

    if raw == nil and entry[1] ~= nil then
        raw = entry[1]
        local second, third = entry[2], entry[3]
        if type(second) == "table" and (second.ImageRectSize or second.ImageRectPosition or second.ImageRectOffset
            or second.imageRectSize or second.imageRectOffset or second.imageRectPosition) then
            size = toVec2(second.ImageRectSize or second.imageRectSize)
            offset = toVec2(second.ImageRectPosition or second.ImageRectOffset
                or second.imageRectPosition or second.imageRectOffset)
        else
            size = toVec2(second)
            offset = toVec2(third)
        end
    end

    local image
    if type(raw) == "number" or (type(raw) == "string" and raw:match("^%d+$")) then
        for _, c in ipairs(containers) do
            local sheet = lookupSheet(c, raw)
            if sheet then image = toImageString(sheet); break end
        end
    end
    image = image or toImageString(raw)
    if not image then return nil end
    return image, size, offset
end

local function nameVariants(name)
    local v = { name }
    local u = name:gsub("%-", "_")
    if u ~= name then table.insert(v, u) end
    table.insert(v, "lucide-" .. name)
    table.insert(v, "lucide_" .. name)
    return v
end

local function findIn(tbl, name)
    if type(tbl) ~= "table" then return nil end
    for _, n in ipairs(nameVariants(name)) do
        if tbl[n] ~= nil then return tbl[n] end
    end
    for _, sk in ipairs(SIZE_KEYS) do
        local sub = tbl[sk]
        if type(sub) == "table" then
            for _, n in ipairs(nameVariants(name)) do
                if sub[n] ~= nil then return sub[n] end
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end

local function resolveIcon(name)
    if not Lucide then return nil end
    if IconCache[name] ~= nil then return IconCache[name] or nil end

    local mod = Lucide
    local result

    -- 1) function-style APIs
    local callers = {}
    if type(mod) == "function" then
        table.insert(callers, function() return mod(name) end)
    elseif type(mod) == "table" then
        for _, fname in ipairs({ "Icon", "GetIcon", "getIcon", "Get", "get", "GetAsset", "getAsset", "Lookup" }) do
            local f = mod[fname]
            if type(f) == "function" then
                table.insert(callers, function() return f(name) end)
                table.insert(callers, function() return f(mod, name) end)
                table.insert(callers, function() return f(name, "lucide") end)
            end
        end
    end
    for _, call in ipairs(callers) do
        local ok, data = pcall(call)
        if ok and data then
            local image, size, offset = parseEntry(data, { mod })
            if image then result = { image, size, offset }; break end
        end
    end

    -- 2) table-style APIs
    if not result and type(mod) == "table" then
        local holders = {}
        for _, key in ipairs({ "Icons", "icons", "lucide", "Lucide", "Data", "data" }) do
            if type(mod[key]) == "table" then table.insert(holders, mod[key]) end
        end
        table.insert(holders, mod)
        for _, h in ipairs(holders) do
            local entry = findIn(h, name)
            if entry ~= nil then
                local image, size, offset = parseEntry(entry, { mod, h })
                if image then result = { image, size, offset }; break end
            end
        end
    end

    IconCache[name] = result or false
    return result
end

local function setIcon(img, iconName, color)
    if not img or not iconName then return false end
    local r = resolveIcon(iconName)
    if not r then
        if Lucide and not lucideWarned then
            lucideWarned = true
            local keys = {}
            if type(Lucide) == "table" then
                for k in pairs(Lucide) do
                    table.insert(keys, tostring(k))
                    if #keys >= 12 then break end
                end
            end
        end
        img.Visible = false
        return false
    end
    img.Image = r[1]
    img.ImageRectSize = r[2] or Vector2.zero
    img.ImageRectOffset = r[3] or Vector2.zero
    img.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
    if color then img.ImageColor3 = color end
    img.ImageTransparency = 0
    img.Visible = true
    return true
end

-- ============================================================
-- SAFE GUI PARENT
-- ============================================================
local function getSafeParent()
    if typeof(gethui) == "function" then
        local ok, res = pcall(gethui)
        if ok and res then return res end
    end
    if typeof(syn) == "table" and syn.protect_gui then return "PROTECTED" end
    return PlayerGui
end

local function parentGui(gui)
    local target = getSafeParent()
    if target == "PROTECTED" then
        pcall(function() syn.protect_gui(gui) end)
        pcall(function() gui.Parent = CoreGui end)
    elseif target then
        gui.Parent = target
    else
        gui.Parent = PlayerGui
    end
    if typeof(protect_gui) == "function" then pcall(protect_gui, gui) end
end

-- ============================================================
-- HELPERS
-- ============================================================
local function corner(o, r)
    local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
    c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, r or 10)
    c.Parent = o
    return c
end

local function stroke(o, col, thick, trans)
    local s = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    s.Color = col or P.Line
    s.Thickness = thick or 1
    s.Transparency = trans or 0
    s.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    s.Parent = o
    return s
end

local function gradient(o, c1, c2, rot)
    local g = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    g.Color = ColorSequence.new(c1, c2)
    g.Rotation = rot or 0
    g.Parent = o
    return g
end

local function playSound(id, vol, spd)
    pcall(function()
        local s = Instance.new("Sound", SoundService)
        s.SoundId = id
        s.Volume = vol or 0.3
        s.PlaybackSpeed = spd or 1
        s:Play()
        game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(s, 1)
    end)
end

-- theme-tinted rim colour used by every glass border (accent blended a bit toward the text colour)
local function rimColor()
    return P.Accent:Lerp(P.Text, 0.25)
end

-- iOS-style glass border: bright specular edge that fades around the frame
local function glassBorder(o, thick, bright)
    local s = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    s.Name = "GlassBorder"
    s.Color = rimColor()
    s.Thickness = thick or 1.5
    s.Transparency = 0
    s.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    s.Parent = o
    local g = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    g.Rotation = 45
    local b = bright or 0.15
    g.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,    b),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.28, 0.80),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.62, 0.90),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1,    math.min(b + 0.15, 0.9)),
    })
    g.Parent = s
    return s
end

-- subtle hover / press look for solid buttons (slight lighten on hover, slight darken while held)
local function softHover(b, base, st)
    local hov = base:Lerp(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.08)
    local dn  = base:Lerp(Color3.new(0, 0, 0), 0.1)
    local ti  = TweenInfo.new(0.15)
    b.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(b, ti, { BackgroundColor3 = hov }):Play()
        if st then TweenService:Create(st, ti, { Transparency = 0.3 }):Play() end
    end)
    b.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(b, ti, { BackgroundColor3 = base }):Play()
        if st then TweenService:Create(st, ti, { Transparency = 0.5 }):Play() end
    end)
    b.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function() TweenService:Create(b, TweenInfo.new(0.08), { BackgroundColor3 = dn }):Play() end)
    b.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function() TweenService:Create(b, TweenInfo.new(0.12), { BackgroundColor3 = hov }):Play() end)
end

-- gradient themes: Grad = { colour1, colour2 } + GradRot paint the window background as a gradient.
-- Panels / lines are derived from the gradient's midpoint so everything stays readable.
do
local function addGradTheme(name, baseName, g1, g2, rot, over)
    local base
    for _, t in ipairs(Themes) do if t.Name == baseName then base = t break end end
    local t = {}
    for k, v in pairs(base) do t[k] = v end
    local mid = g1:Lerp(g2, 0.5)
    local tgt = (base.Text.R < 0.3) and Color3.new(0, 0, 0) or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    t.Name, t.Grad, t.GradRot = name, { g1, g2 }, rot
    t.Bg    = mid
    t.Bg2   = mid:Lerp(tgt, 0.05)
    t.Bg3   = mid:Lerp(tgt, 0.10)
    t.Bg4   = mid:Lerp(tgt, 0.16)
    t.Card  = mid:Lerp(tgt, 0.07)
    t.Line  = mid:Lerp(tgt, 0.18)
    t.Line2 = mid:Lerp(tgt, 0.27)
    for k, v in pairs(over or {}) do t[k] = v end
    table.insert(Themes, t)
end
addGradTheme("Aurora", "Cyber",   Color3.fromRGB(10, 26, 40),  Color3.fromRGB(48, 18, 72),  135, { Accent = Color3.fromRGB(80, 220, 200),  Accent2 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 120, 255) })
addGradTheme("Dusk",   "Sunset",  Color3.fromRGB(46, 18, 38),  Color3.fromRGB(14, 22, 54),  135, { Accent = Color3.fromRGB(255, 140, 110), Accent2 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 120, 240) })

addGradTheme("Fungi",  "Emerald", Color3.fromRGB(12, 42, 30), Color3.fromRGB(38, 22, 42), 135, { Accent = Color3.fromRGB(240, 108, 100), Accent2 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 208, 112), Icon = "https://cdn.phototourl.com/anon/2026-11-19-be1fa21d-c855-44fc-b4ed-b3934d91750a.png" })
end
local THEME_KEYS = { "Bg","Bg2","Bg3","Bg4","Card","Line","Line2","Text","Muted","Accent","Accent2" }
local CurrentTheme = "Midnight"
local Cfg = { Toggles = {}, Keybinds = {}, SubPages = {}, SubSelect = {}, ActiveSub = {} }   -- Sub*: sub-tab pages / selectors / active page per tab   -- config system + theme-gradient helpers live here (saves local slots)

-- window background gradient (solid themes just use the same colour twice)
Cfg.gradCur = { Color3.fromRGB(13, 14, 19), Color3.fromRGB(13, 14, 19), 0 }
Cfg.gradToken = 0
function Cfg.setMainGradient(c1, c2, rot)
    Cfg.gradToken = Cfg.gradToken + 1
    local my = Cfg.gradToken
    local f1, f2, fr = Cfg.gradCur[1], Cfg.gradCur[2], Cfg.gradCur[3]
    if Cfg.MainGrad then   -- start from what is on screen right now, so an interrupted switch can't jump / blend
        local kp = Cfg.MainGrad.Color.Keypoints
        f1, f2, fr = kp[1].Value, kp[#kp].Value, Cfg.MainGrad.Rotation
    end
    Cfg.gradCur = { c1, c2, rot }
    if not Cfg.MainGrad then return end
    task.spawn(function()
        local t0 = os.clock()
        while true do
            if my ~= Cfg.gradToken or not Cfg.MainGrad.Parent then return end
            local a = math.clamp((os.clock() - t0) / 0.3, 0, 1)
            local e = a * a * (3 - 2 * a)
            Cfg.MainGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new(f1:Lerp(c1, e), f2:Lerp(c2, e))
            Cfg.MainGrad.Rotation = fr + (rot - fr) * e
            if a >= 1 then return end
            RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
        end
    end)
end

local function sameColor(a, b)
    return math.abs(a.R-b.R) < 0.004 and math.abs(a.G-b.G) < 0.004 and math.abs(a.B-b.B) < 0.004
end

-- colours currently being tweened to: if the theme is switched again mid-transition, objects are matched
-- by the colour they were heading to (not the half-way colour), so two themes can never end up mixed
Cfg.themeGoals = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

local function applyTheme(name)
    local theme
    for _, t in ipairs(Themes) do if t.Name == name then theme = t break end end
    if not theme then return end

    -- old -> new color map (built from the palette currently in use)
    local old = {}
    for _, k in ipairs(THEME_KEYS) do old[k] = P[k] end
    local function remap(c)
        for _, k in ipairs(THEME_KEYS) do
            if sameColor(c, old[k]) then return theme[k] end
        end
        return nil
    end

    for _, k in ipairs(THEME_KEYS) do P[k] = theme[k] end
    CurrentTheme = name
    if Cfg.setBrandImage then pcall(Cfg.setBrandImage, theme.Icon) end   -- decoration themes swap the window icon (nil = the normal icon)
    if Cfg.repaintTabIcons then
        pcall(Cfg.repaintTabIcons)   -- tab icon gradient follows the theme accents
        task.delay(0.4, function() pcall(Cfg.repaintTabIcons) end)   -- again once the 0.3s theme tweens have finished
    end
    Cfg.setMainGradient(theme.Grad and theme.Grad[1] or theme.Bg, theme.Grad and theme.Grad[2] or theme.Bg, theme.GradRot or 135)

    local ti = TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
    local goals = Cfg.themeGoals
    local function cur(o, prop)
        local g = goals[o]
        return (g and g[prop]) or o[prop]
    end
    local function tweenTo(o, prop, n)
        local g = goals[o]
        if not g then g = {}; goals[o] = g end
        g[prop] = n
        local tw = TweenService:Create(o, ti, { [prop] = n })
        tw.Completed:Connect(function() if g[prop] == n then g[prop] = nil end end)
        tw:Play()
    end
    for _, o in ipairs(screenGui:GetDescendants()) do
        -- theme preview chips keep their own fixed colors
        if o:GetAttribute("NoTheme") then continue end
        if o:IsA("GuiObject") then
            local n = remap(cur(o, "BackgroundColor3"))
            if n then tweenTo(o, "BackgroundColor3", n) end
        end
        local bc = o:GetAttribute("BaseColor")   -- gradient-text labels remember their plain colour
        if bc then
            local nb = remap(bc)
            if nb then o:SetAttribute("BaseColor", nb) end
        end
        if o:IsA("TextBox") then
            local pn = remap(o.PlaceholderColor3)
            if pn then o.PlaceholderColor3 = pn end
        end
        if o:IsA("TextLabel") or o:IsA("TextButton") or o:IsA("TextBox") then
            local n = remap(cur(o, "TextColor3"))
            if n then tweenTo(o, "TextColor3", n) end
        elseif o:IsA("ImageLabel") or o:IsA("ImageButton") then
            local n = remap(cur(o, "ImageColor3"))
            if n then tweenTo(o, "ImageColor3", n) end
        end
        if o:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
            local n = remap(o.ScrollBarImageColor3)
            if n then o.ScrollBarImageColor3 = n end
        elseif o:IsA("UIStroke") then
            if o.Name == "GlassBorder" then
                tweenTo(o, "Color", rimColor())
            else
                local n = remap(cur(o, "Color"))
                if n then tweenTo(o, "Color", n) end
            end
        elseif o:IsA("UIGradient") and o.Name ~= "Sheen" then
            local kps, changed = o.Color.Keypoints, false
            local out = {}
            for i, kp in ipairs(kps) do
                local n = remap(kp.Value)
                if n then changed = true end
                out[i] = ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(kp.Time, n or kp.Value)
            end
            if changed then o.Color = ColorSequence.new(out) end
        end
    end
end

-- ============================================================
-- RICH TEXT + GRADIENT TEXT
--   Roblox rich text tags work in labels: <b> <i> <u> <s> <font color="#ff6b6b"> <stroke> <mark>
--   Gradient prefix: "[g:#788CE6,#A082E6]Some text"  (optional 3rd colour, optional ,anim)
--   e.g. "[g:#ff6b6b,#ffd166,anim]Hot item"
-- ============================================================
local function stripRich(t)
    t = tostring(t or "")
    t = t:gsub("^%[g:[^%]]*%]", "")
    t = t:gsub("<[^>]->", "")
    t = t:gsub("&lt;", "<"):gsub("&gt;", ">"):gsub("&amp;", "&")
    return t
end

local function parseHex(h)
    h = tostring(h or ""):gsub("[#%s]", "")
    if #h == 3 then h = h:gsub(".", function(c) return c .. c end) end
    if not h:match("^%x%x%x%x%x%x$") then return nil end
    return Color3.fromRGB(tonumber(h:sub(1, 2), 16), tonumber(h:sub(3, 4), 16), tonumber(h:sub(5, 6), 16))
end

-- applies (or removes) a colour gradient on a text label. cols = { Color3, Color3, ... }
local function textGradient(lbl, cols, animate, rotation)
    local old = lbl:FindFirstChild("TextGrad")
    if old then old:Destroy() end
    if lbl:GetAttribute("BaseColor") then
        lbl.TextColor3 = lbl:GetAttribute("BaseColor")
        lbl:SetAttribute("BaseColor", nil)
    end
    if not cols or #cols < 2 then return nil end
    lbl:SetAttribute("BaseColor", lbl.TextColor3)
    lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    local g = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    g.Name = "TextGrad"
    g.Rotation = rotation or 0
    local kps, n = {}, #cols
    if animate then   -- loop colours so the sweep is seamless
        local total = n * 2 - 1
        for i = 1, total do
            local idx = i <= n and i or (2 * n - i)
            kps[i] = ColorSequenceKeypoint.new((i - 1) / (total - 1), cols[idx])
        end
    else
        for i, c in ipairs(cols) do kps[i] = ColorSequenceKeypoint.new((i - 1) / (n - 1), c) end
    end
    g.Color = ColorSequence.new(kps)
    g.Parent = lbl
    if animate then
        task.spawn(function()
            local t0 = os.clock()
            while g.Parent do
                g.Offset = Vector2.new(math.sin((os.clock() - t0) * 1.4) * 0.5, 0)
                task.wait()
            end
        end)
    end
    return g
end

-- sets text on a label with rich text + optional [g:...] gradient prefix
local function richSet(lbl, text, defaultCols, defaultAnim)
    text = tostring(text or "")
    lbl.RichText = true
    local spec, body = text:match("^%[g:([^%]]*)%](.*)$")
    local cols, anim = {}, defaultAnim
    if spec then
        for part in spec:gmatch("[^,]+") do
            part = part:gsub("%s", "")
            if part:lower() == "anim" then anim = true
            else
                local c = parseHex(part)
                if c then cols[#cols + 1] = c end
            end
        end
        text = body
    elseif defaultCols then
        cols = defaultCols
    end
    lbl.Text = text
    textGradient(lbl, #cols >= 2 and cols or nil, anim)
end

-- ============================================================
-- TITLE BAR
-- ============================================================
local TitleBar = Instance.new("Frame")
TitleBar.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 56)
TitleBar.BackgroundTransparency = 1
TitleBar.BorderSizePixel = 0
TitleBar.ZIndex = 2
TitleBar.Parent = Main

-- window icon: sits in the sidebar column above the tabs, centred like a tab button but larger.
-- No background or border around it. Its width follows the sidebar (see applySidebar).
Cfg.SearchW, Cfg.SearchH = 190, 30
do
    local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    btn.Name = "WindowIcon"
    btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 130, 0, 100)
    btn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 2)
    btn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    btn.Text = ""
    btn.AutoButtonColor = false
    btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    btn.ZIndex = 4
    btn.Parent = Main

    local logo = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    logo.Name = "Logo"
    logo.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    logo.Size = UDim2.new(0, 104, 0, 104)
    logo.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
    logo.BackgroundTransparency = 1   -- no circle / outline around the icon
    logo.Image = "rbxassetid://118415481943025"   -- window icon (fixed)
    logo.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
    logo.ZIndex = 5
    logo.Active = false
    logo.Parent = btn

    Cfg.Brand, Cfg.BrandIcon = btn, logo

    -- Decoration themes carry their own window icon (theme.Icon = image link). Roblox can't show web links, so every icon is
    -- downloaded ONCE at startup (into the executor's workspace, shown through getcustomasset), its background is removed,
    -- and the result is cached - switching decorations afterwards is instant. Anything else shows the normal icon.
    local ORIG_ICON = "rbxassetid://118415481943025"
    local AssetService = game:GetService("AssetService")
    local iconCache, iconLoading, iconToken = {}, {}, 0
    local ICON_PX = 256   -- icons are shrunk to this before the background removal (shown at ~104px anyway)

    -- Removes the background: flood-fills inwards from the image border over every colour the border is made of
    -- (so plain, white and even fake-transparency checkerboard backgrounds all go). Anything not connected to the
    -- border (eyes, highlights inside the character) is kept. Returns true if it changed the image.
    local function removeBackground(img)
        local size = img.Size
        local w, h = size.X, size.Y
        local buf = img:ReadPixelsBuffer(Vector2.zero, size)
        local function px(x, y)
            local o = (y * w + x) * 4
            return buffer.readu8(buf, o), buffer.readu8(buf, o + 1), buffer.readu8(buf, o + 2), buffer.readu8(buf, o + 3)
        end
        local function key(r, g, b) return (r // 16) * 256 + (g // 16) * 16 + (b // 16) end

        -- which (quantised) colours make up the border?
        local counts, total = {}, 0
        local function sample(x, y)
            local r, g, b, a = px(x, y)
            if a < 10 then return end   -- already transparent
            local k = key(r, g, b)
            counts[k] = (counts[k] or 0) + 1
            total = total + 1
        end
        for x = 0, w - 1 do sample(x, 0) sample(x, h - 1) end
        for y = 1, h - 2 do sample(0, y) sample(w - 1, y) end
        if total == 0 then return false end   -- the border is already transparent
        local bg = {}
        for k, c in pairs(counts) do
            if c >= total * 0.06 then bg[k] = true end
        end
        if next(bg) == nil then return false end

        -- a pixel is background if its colour bucket (or a neighbouring bucket) is a border colour
        local function isBg(r, g, b, a)
            if a < 10 then return false end
            local rr, gg, bb = r // 16, g // 16, b // 16
            for dr = -1, 1 do
                for dg = -1, 1 do
                    for db = -1, 1 do
                        local R, G, B = rr + dr, gg + dg, bb + db
                        if R >= 0 and G >= 0 and B >= 0 and R < 16 and G < 16 and B < 16 and bg[R * 256 + G * 16 + B] then
                            return true
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            return false
        end

        local seen, stack, n = {}, {}, 0
        local function push(x, y)
            if x < 0 or y < 0 or x >= w or y >= h then return end
            local i = y * w + x
            if seen[i] then return end
            local r, g, b, a = px(x, y)
            if isBg(r, g, b, a) then
                seen[i] = true
                n = n + 1
                stack[n] = i
            end
        end
        for x = 0, w - 1 do push(x, 0) push(x, h - 1) end
        for y = 1, h - 2 do push(0, y) push(w - 1, y) end
        while n > 0 do
            local i = stack[n]; stack[n] = nil; n = n - 1
            local x, y = i % w, i // w
            push(x + 1, y) push(x - 1, y) push(x, y + 1) push(x, y - 1)
        end

        local changed = false
        for i in pairs(seen) do
            buffer.writeu32(buf, i * 4, 0)   -- fully transparent
            changed = true
        end
        if changed then img:WritePixelsBuffer(Vector2.zero, size, buf) end
        return changed
    end

    -- download -> getcustomasset -> background removed. Returns { Asset = "rbxasset://..", Editable = EditableImage? }
    local function buildIcon(url)
        local res = {}
        local ok, err = pcall(function()
            assert(writefile and getcustomasset, "no file access")
            local body = lucideFetch(url)
            assert(body and #body > 100, "download failed")
            if makefolder and isfolder and not isfolder("Nauru Hub") then makefolder("Nauru Hub") end
            local path = "Nauru Hub/icon_" .. (url:match("([%w%-]+)%.%w+$") or "x"):sub(-16) .. ".png"
            writefile(path, body)
            res.Asset = getcustomasset(path)
        end)
        if not ok then return nil end

        -- background removal needs EditableImage support; without it the image is shown as downloaded
        pcall(function()
            local img
            local okC = pcall(function() img = AssetService:CreateEditableImageAsync(Content.fromUri(res.Asset)) end)
            if not okC or not img then img = AssetService:CreateEditableImageAsync(res.Asset) end
            pcall(function() img:Resize(Vector2.new(ICON_PX, ICON_PX)) end)
            if removeBackground(img) then res.Editable = img end
        end)
        return res
    end

    local function applyIcon(res)
        if res.Editable then
            local ok = pcall(function() logo.ImageContent = Content.fromObject(res.Editable) end)
            if ok then return end
        end
        logo.Image = res.Asset
    end

    -- startup: download + process every decoration icon once, in the background
    local function preload(url)
        if iconCache[url] ~= nil or iconLoading[url] then return end
        iconLoading[url] = true
        task.spawn(function()
            local res = buildIcon(url)
            iconCache[url] = res or false
            iconLoading[url] = nil
        end)
    end
    for _, t in ipairs(Themes) do
        if t.Icon then preload(t.Icon) end
    end

    function Cfg.setBrandImage(url)
        iconToken = iconToken + 1
        local my = iconToken
        logo.ImageColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        if not url then logo.Image = ORIG_ICON; return end
        local res = iconCache[url]
        if res then applyIcon(res) return end   -- already prepared: instant
        if res == false then return end         -- download failed earlier: keep the current icon
        preload(url)
        task.spawn(function()   -- still downloading (switched very early): apply as soon as it is ready
            while iconLoading[url] do task.wait(0.05) end
            local r = iconCache[url]
            if r and my == iconToken then applyIcon(r) end
        end)
    end
end

-- the button lives in a clipped slot whose right edge is the minimize button's left edge,

local isSolid = false
local MAIN_TRANSPARENT = 0.14

do  -- Lucide icons for the title-bar buttons (text glyphs stay as fallback until Lucide is ready)
    local cur = {
        [MinBtn]   = { "circle-minus", "—" },
        [CloseBtn] = { "square-x",     "×" },
        [SolidBtn] = { "aperture",     "◐", { "glasses", "circle-dot" } },   -- "lens" doesn't exist in Lucide; aperture = camera lens
    }
    for btn in pairs(cur) do
        local ic = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
        ic.Name = "Ico"
        ic.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        ic.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
        ic.Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16)
        ic.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        ic.ImageColor3 = P.Muted
        ic.Visible = false
        ic.ZIndex = btn.ZIndex + 1
        ic.Parent = btn
    end
    local function apply(btn, animate)
        local ic, d = btn:FindFirstChild("Ico"), cur[btn]
        if not ic then return end
        if animate and ic.Visible then
            local t = TweenService:Create(ic, TweenInfo.new(0.1), { ImageTransparency = 1 })
            t:Play(); t.Completed:Wait()
        end
        local okIcon = LucideReady and setIcon(ic, d[1], ic.ImageColor3)
        if LucideReady and not okIcon and d[3] then
            for _, alt in ipairs(d[3]) do
                if setIcon(ic, alt, ic.ImageColor3) then okIcon = true; break end
            end
        end
        if okIcon then
            btn.Text = ""
            if animate then
                ic.ImageTransparency = 1
                TweenService:Create(ic, TweenInfo.new(0.16), { ImageTransparency = 0 }):Play()
            end
        else
            ic.Visible = false
            btn.Text = d[2]
        end
    end
    function TopIcons.min(minimized)
        cur[MinBtn] = { minimized and "circle-plus" or "circle-minus", minimized and "+" or "—" }
        task.spawn(apply, MinBtn, true)
    end
    function TopIcons.solid(on)
        cur[SolidBtn] = { on and "eye" or "aperture", on and "●" or "◐", { on and "eye" or "glasses", "circle-dot" } }
        task.spawn(apply, SolidBtn, true)
    end
    function TopIcons.hover(btn, on, col)
        local ic = btn:FindFirstChild("Ico")
        if ic then TweenService:Create(ic, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { ImageColor3 = col }):Play() end
        local g = btn:FindFirstChild("GlassBorder")
        if g then TweenService:Create(g, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { Transparency = on and 0.05 or 0.4 }):Play() end
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        local t0 = tick()
        while not LucideReady and tick() - t0 < 30 do task.wait(0.2) end
        for btn in pairs(cur) do apply(btn, false) end
    end)
end
local function applySolid(on, animate)
    isSolid = on
    local ti = TweenInfo.new(animate and 0.25 or 0)
    TweenService:Create(Main, ti, { BackgroundTransparency = on and 0 or MAIN_TRANSPARENT }):Play()
    TopIcons.solid(on)
    local sh = Main:FindFirstChild("Sheen")
    if sh then sh.Visible = not on end
end
SolidBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
    if SolidBtn:GetAttribute("Busy") then return end
    TweenService:Create(SolidBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { BackgroundTransparency = 0.05, TextColor3 = P.Text }):Play()
    TopIcons.hover(SolidBtn, true, P.Text)
end)
SolidBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
    if SolidBtn:GetAttribute("Busy") then return end
    TweenService:Create(SolidBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { BackgroundTransparency = 0.3, TextColor3 = isSolid and P.Accent or P.Muted }):Play()
    TopIcons.hover(SolidBtn, false, isSolid and P.Accent or P.Muted)
end)
SolidBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() applySolid(not isSolid, true) end)

-- "Frame" drag style: while dragging, only a thin outline (theme accent) follows the pointer and the
-- window stays put; on release the window glides to where the outline is (kept on screen).
-- ONE controller owns the drag for every handle (title bar + logo), only one press can own it at a
-- time, and it is ended by three independent checks, so the outline can never get stuck or duplicate.
local function makeDraggable(frame, handles)
    if typeof(handles) == "Instance" then handles = { handles } end
    local active = nil                      -- the press (InputObject) that owns the drag
    local moved = false
    local pressPoint, lastPoint = Vector2.zero, Vector2.zero
    local startX, startY, goalX, goalY = 0, 0, 0, 0
    local skeleton, skStroke, skScale, glideTween
    local conns = {}

    local function clampOffset(ox, oy)
        local vp = screenGui.AbsoluteSize
        local size, ap, pos = frame.AbsoluteSize, frame.AnchorPoint, frame.Position
        local tlx = vp.X * pos.X.Scale + ox - size.X * ap.X
        local tly = vp.Y * pos.Y.Scale + oy - size.Y * ap.Y
        local cx = math.clamp(tlx, -(size.X - 80), math.max(-(size.X - 80), vp.X - 80))
        local cy = math.clamp(tly, 0, math.max(0, vp.Y - 40))
        return ox + (cx - tlx), oy + (cy - tly)
    end

    local function makeSkeleton()
        for _, o in ipairs(screenGui:GetChildren()) do   -- never leave a stray outline behind
            if o.Name == "DragFrame" then o:Destroy() end
        end
        skeleton = Instance.new("Frame")
        skeleton.Name = "DragFrame"
        skeleton.AnchorPoint = frame.AnchorPoint
        skeleton.Position = frame.Position
        skeleton.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(frame.AbsoluteSize.X, frame.AbsoluteSize.Y)
        skeleton.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        skeleton.BorderSizePixel = 0
        skeleton.Active = false
        skeleton.ZIndex = 40
        skeleton:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true)
        skeleton.Parent = screenGui
        corner(skeleton, 18)
        skStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        skStroke.Color = P.Accent   -- follows the active theme
        skStroke.Thickness = 1.5
        skStroke.Transparency = 1
        skStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
        skStroke.Parent = skeleton
        skScale = Instance.new("UIScale")
        skScale.Scale = 1.02
        skScale.Parent = skeleton
        TweenService:Create(skStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), { Transparency = 0.25 }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(skScale, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint), { Scale = 1 }):Play()
    end

    -- ends the drag no matter how it was triggered; land = glide the window to the outline
    local function finish(land)
        active = nil
        local sk, st = skeleton, skStroke
        skeleton, skStroke, skScale = nil, nil, nil
        if not sk then return end
        if land and frame.Parent then
            local x, y = clampOffset(sk.Position.X.Offset, sk.Position.Y.Offset)
            local pos = frame.Position
            if glideTween then glideTween:Cancel() end
            glideTween = TweenService:Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
                { Position = UDim2.new(pos.X.Scale, x, pos.Y.Scale, y) })
            glideTween:Play()
        end
        if st then TweenService:Create(st, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), { Transparency = 1 }):Play() end
        task.delay(0.25, function() if sk.Parent then sk:Destroy() end end)
    end

    local function begin(input)
        if active or not frame.Visible then return end   -- a second touch / the logo while dragging is ignored
        if glideTween then glideTween:Cancel(); glideTween = nil end
        active, moved = input, false
        pressPoint = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
        lastPoint = pressPoint
        startX, startY = frame.Position.X.Offset, frame.Position.Y.Offset
        goalX, goalY = startX, startY
    end

    for _, h in ipairs(handles) do
        h.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                begin(input)
            end
        end)
    end

    conns[#conns + 1] = UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if not active then return end
        if (active.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement)
        or input == active then
            lastPoint = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
        end
    end)

    conns[#conns + 1] = UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if not active then return end
        if input == active
        or (active.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1) then
            finish(skeleton ~= nil)
        end
    end)

    conns[#conns + 1] = UserInputService.WindowFocusReleased:Connect(function()
        if active then finish(skeleton ~= nil) end
    end)

    conns[#conns + 1] = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
        if not active then return end
        -- safety nets: button already released / window hidden
        if active.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
        and not UserInputService:IsMouseButtonPressed(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1) then
            finish(skeleton ~= nil)
            return
        end
        if not frame.Visible then finish(false) return end
        local d = lastPoint - pressPoint
        if not moved then
            if d.Magnitude < 3 then return end
            moved = true
            makeSkeleton()
        end
        goalX, goalY = startX + d.X, startY + d.Y
        if skeleton then
            local a = 1 - math.exp(-45 * math.min(dt, 0.1))
            local pos = skeleton.Position
            local cx, cy = pos.X.Offset, pos.Y.Offset
            skeleton.Position = UDim2.new(pos.X.Scale, cx + (goalX - cx) * a, pos.Y.Scale, cy + (goalY - cy) * a)
        end
    end)

    screenGui.Destroying:Connect(function()
        for _, c in ipairs(conns) do c:Disconnect() end
    end)
end
makeDraggable(Main, { TitleBar, Cfg.Brand })

        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,    1),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.18, 0.62),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5,  0.30),

    })

-- ============================================================
-- TAB BAR — SMALLER + TRULY CENTERED
-- ============================================================
-- scrollbars stay hidden until you scroll, then fade out again
local function autoFadeScrollbar(sf, shown)
    shown = shown or 0.4
    sf.ScrollBarImageTransparency = 1
    sf.ScrollBarThickness = 0
    if true then return end
    local token = 0
    sf:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CanvasPosition"):Connect(function()
        token = token + 1
        local my = token
        TweenService:Create(sf, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), { ScrollBarImageTransparency = shown }):Play()
        task.delay(0.9, function()
            if token == my and sf.Parent then
                TweenService:Create(sf, TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), { ScrollBarImageTransparency = 1 }):Play()
            end
        end)
    end)
end

local TabsBar = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
TabsBar.Name = "TabsBar"
TabsBar.Size = UDim2.new(0, 138, 1, -216)
TabsBar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 114)
TabsBar.BackgroundTransparency = 1
TabsBar.BorderSizePixel = 0
TabsBar.ScrollBarThickness = 0   -- no scrollbar on the sidebar (it still scrolls with the wheel/touch)
TabsBar.ScrollBarImageColor3 = P.Line2
TabsBar.ScrollBarImageTransparency = 1
TabsBar.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y
TabsBar.ElasticBehavior = Enum.ElasticBehavior.Never
TabsBar.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
TabsBar.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None  -- we manage it manually
TabsBar.VerticalScrollBarInset = Enum.ScrollBarInset.None
TabsBar.ZIndex = 3
TabsBar.Parent = Main

local tabPad = Instance.new("UIPadding", TabsBar)
tabPad.PaddingLeft   = UDim.new(0, 4)   -- equal left/right padding so the tabs sit dead centre
tabPad.PaddingRight  = UDim.new(0, 4)
tabPad.PaddingTop    = UDim.new(0, 4)
tabPad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 4)

local TabsLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
TabsLayout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Vertical
TabsLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 10)
TabsLayout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top
TabsLayout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
TabsLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
TabsLayout.Parent = TabsBar

-- Center content when it fits, scroll when it doesn't
local pages = {}
local tabButtons = {}
local tabParts = {}
local tabCount = 0
local sidebarW = 138
local tabCompact = false
-- sidebar tabs always fit: they share the free height evenly (shrinking when there are many tabs, growing when few)
function Cfg.autoTabH(compact)
    local n = tabCount
    local lo, hi = compact and 76 or 28, compact and 88 or 40
    if n <= 0 then return compact and 54 or 38 end
    local sc = math.max(MainScale.Scale, 0.01)
    local barH = TabsBar.AbsoluteSize.Y / sc
    if barH <= 0 then barH = 580 - 216 end                -- not laid out yet (window still hidden)
    local availH = barH - 8                               -- minus the bar's top / bottom padding
    local h = math.floor((availH - 10 * (n - 1)) / n)     -- 10 = gap between tabs
    return math.clamp(h, lo, hi)
end
Cfg.compactTabH = function() return Cfg.autoTabH(true) end

local function refreshTabsCanvas()
    if tabCount > 0 then
        local h = Cfg.autoTabH(tabCompact)
        for _, t in pairs(tabParts) do
            if t.btn.Size.Y.Offset ~= h then t.btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, h) end
        end
    end
    -- AbsoluteContentSize is in real screen pixels (it includes the menu's UIScale) but CanvasSize is in the
    -- bar's own units, so divide by the scale. Without this, a scaled-down menu (phones) got a canvas that was
    -- too short: the last tabs were cut off, the list could not scroll and the arrow pills never appeared.
    local sc = math.max(MainScale.Scale, 0.01)
    local contentH = math.ceil(TabsLayout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y / sc)
        + tabPad.PaddingTop.Offset + tabPad.PaddingBottom.Offset
    TabsBar.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, contentH + 2)
end

-- ============================================================
-- SIDEBAR SCROLL INDICATORS
-- Small chevron pills at the top / bottom edge of the tab list. They only show while there is more to
-- scroll in that direction (so the list no longer looks cut off) and clicking one scrolls the tabs.
-- ============================================================
local function makeTabHint(up)
    local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
    b.Name = up and "TabsHintUp" or "TabsHintDown"
    b.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, up and 0 or 1)
    b.Size = UDim2.new(0, 34, 0, 18)
    b.BackgroundColor3 = P.Bg3   -- palette colours: the theme recolour pass (applyTheme) repaints these automatically
    b.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    b.AutoButtonColor = false
    b.Text = ""
    b.Active = false
    b.Visible = false
    b.BorderSizePixel = 0
    b.ZIndex = 6
    local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
    c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    c.Parent = b
    local st = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    st.Name = "HintStroke"
    st.Color = P.Line2
    st.Thickness = 1
    st.Transparency = 1
    st.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    st.Parent = b
    local bars = {}
    for i = 1, 2 do
        local bar = Instance.new("Frame")
        bar.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        bar.Size = UDim2.new(0, 9, 0, 2)
        bar.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, i == 1 and -3 or 3, 0.5, 0)
        -- two bars forming a "v" (down) or a "^" (up)
        local rot = (i == 1) and 40 or -40
        bar.Rotation = up and -rot or rot
        bar.BackgroundColor3 = P.Accent
        bar.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        bar.BorderSizePixel = 0
        bar.ZIndex = 7
        local bc = Instance.new("UICorner")
        bc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        bc.Parent = bar
        bar.Parent = b
        bars[i] = bar
    end
    b.Parent = Main
    return b, bars
end

local tabHintUp, tabHintUpBars     = makeTabHint(true)
local tabHintDown, tabHintDownBars = makeTabHint(false)
local tabHintShown = { [tabHintUp] = false, [tabHintDown] = false }

local function setTabHint(b, bars, show)
    if tabHintShown[b] == show then return end
    tabHintShown[b] = show
    b.Active = show
    local ti = TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
    if show then b.Visible = true end
    TweenService:Create(b, ti, { BackgroundTransparency = show and 0.1 or 1 }):Play()
    local hs = b:FindFirstChild("HintStroke")
    if hs then TweenService:Create(hs, ti, { Transparency = show and 0.45 or 1 }):Play() end
    for _, bar in ipairs(bars) do
        TweenService:Create(bar, ti, { BackgroundTransparency = show and 0 or 1 }):Play()
    end
    if not show then
        task.delay(0.2, function()
            if not tabHintShown[b] then b.Visible = false end
        end)
    end
end

local function updateTabHints()
    local maxY = math.max(0, TabsBar.AbsoluteCanvasSize.Y - TabsBar.AbsoluteWindowSize.Y)
    local y = TabsBar.CanvasPosition.Y
    local overflow = maxY > 2 and not Cfg.UIMinimized   -- minimized: the pills fade away with the rest of the sidebar
    setTabHint(tabHintUp,   tabHintUpBars,   overflow and y > 2)
    setTabHint(tabHintDown, tabHintDownBars, overflow and y < maxY - 2)
end
Cfg.updateTabHints = updateTabHints

-- keep the pills glued to the (animated) sidebar
local function placeTabHints()
    local w = TabsBar.Size.X.Offset
    local cx = TabsBar.Position.X.Offset + w / 2
    tabHintUp.Position = UDim2.new(0, cx, 0, TabsBar.Position.Y.Offset + 2)
    tabHintDown.Position = UDim2.new(0, cx, TabsBar.Size.Y.Scale, TabsBar.Position.Y.Offset + TabsBar.Size.Y.Offset - 2)
end

local function scrollTabs(dir)
    -- the arrows jump all the way to the top / bottom of the tab list (CanvasPosition is in the bar's own units, so divide by the UI scale)
    local sc = math.max(MainScale.Scale, 0.01)
    local maxY = math.max(0, TabsBar.CanvasSize.Y.Offset - TabsBar.AbsoluteWindowSize.Y / sc)
    local target = dir < 0 and 0 or maxY
    TweenService:Create(TabsBar, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
        { CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, target) }):Play()
end
tabHintUp.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() scrollTabs(-1) end)
tabHintDown.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() scrollTabs(1) end)

TabsBar:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CanvasPosition"):Connect(updateTabHints)
TabsBar:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteCanvasSize"):Connect(updateTabHints)
TabsBar:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteWindowSize"):Connect(updateTabHints)
TabsBar:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Size"):Connect(placeTabHints)
TabsBar:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Position"):Connect(placeTabHints)
placeTabHints()
task.defer(updateTabHints)

local tabTitleLine = Instance.new("Frame")
tabTitleLine.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 1)
tabTitleLine.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 1, -2)
tabTitleLine.BackgroundColor3 = P.Line2
tabTitleLine.BackgroundTransparency = 0
tabTitleLine.BorderSizePixel = 0
tabTitleLine.ZIndex = 3
tabTitleLine.Parent = tabTitleFrame
do
    local g = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    g.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,   0.35),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.7, 0.7),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1,   1),
    })
    g.Parent = tabTitleLine
end

local function layoutContent(animate)
    local ti = TweenInfo.new(animate and 0.38 or 0, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
    TweenService:Create(Content, ti, {
        Position = UDim2.new(0, sidebarW + 32, 0, 58 + headerH),
        Size     = UDim2.new(1, -(sidebarW + 44), 1, -(68 + headerH)),
    }):Play()
    TweenService:Create(tabTitleFrame, ti, {
        Position = UDim2.new(0, sidebarW + 32, 0, 58),
        Size     = UDim2.new(1, -(sidebarW + 44), 0, 24),
    }):Play()
end

local function applyTabTitle(on, animate)
    headerH = on and 24 or 0
    Cfg.TitleShowToken = (Cfg.TitleShowToken or 0) + 1
    local my = Cfg.TitleShowToken
    local function setAlpha(a)   -- 0 = fully shown, 1 = fully hidden
        tabTitleLabel.TextTransparency = a
        tabTitleIcon.ImageTransparency = a
        tabTitleLine.BackgroundTransparency = a
    end
    if not animate then
        setAlpha(0)
        tabTitleFrame.Visible = on
        layoutContent(false)
        return
    end
    Cfg.TitleFadeTweens = Cfg.TitleFadeTweens or {}
    for _, t in ipairs(Cfg.TitleFadeTweens) do t:Cancel() end
    table.clear(Cfg.TitleFadeTweens)
    local function fadeTo(a, info)
        for _, o in ipairs({ { tabTitleLabel, "TextTransparency" }, { tabTitleIcon, "ImageTransparency" }, { tabTitleLine, "BackgroundTransparency" } }) do
            local t = TweenService:Create(o[1], info, { [o[2]] = a })
            Cfg.TitleFadeTweens[#Cfg.TitleFadeTweens + 1] = t
            t:Play()
        end
    end
    if on then
        if not tabTitleFrame.Visible then setAlpha(1) end
        tabTitleFrame.Visible = true
        layoutContent(true)
        fadeTo(0, TweenInfo.new(0.45, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out, 0, false, 0.08))
    else
        fadeTo(1, TweenInfo.new(0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.In))
        layoutContent(true)
        task.delay(0.26, function()
            if my ~= Cfg.TitleShowToken then return end
            tabTitleFrame.Visible = false
            setAlpha(0)
        end)
    end
end

local function applyTabStyle(compact)
    for name, t in pairs(tabParts) do
        local ch = Cfg.autoTabH(compact)
        t.btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, ch)
        if compact then
            if t.ic.Visible then
                -- icon on top, the tab's title right below it
                local isz = math.clamp(math.floor(ch * 0.4), 22, 34)
                local labelH = 14
                local top = math.max(2, math.floor((ch - (isz + 4 + labelH)) / 2))
                t.holder.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
                t.holder.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
                t.holder.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                t.ic.Size = UDim2.new(0, isz, 0, isz)
                t.ic.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -math.floor(isz / 2), 0, top)
                t.lbl.Visible = true
                t.lbl.Text = name
                t.lbl.TextSize = 11
                t.lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
                t.lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, top + isz + 4)
                t.lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, labelH)
            else
                -- icons failed to load: fall back to the first letter
                t.holder.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
                t.holder.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0)
                t.holder.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                t.lbl.Visible = true
                t.lbl.Text = name:sub(1, 1)
                t.lbl.TextSize = 16
                t.lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
                t.lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                t.lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
            end
        else
            -- icon + label are measured as one group and centred in the tab
            local hasIc = t.ic.Visible or t.pending   -- reserve the icon slot while Lucide is still loading
            local off = hasIc and 22 or 0                -- ICON (18) + GAP (4)
            local tw  = t.textW or (#name * 7)
            t.holder.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            t.holder.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
            t.holder.Size = UDim2.new(0, off + tw, 1, 0)
            t.ic.Size = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 18)
            t.ic.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -9)
            t.lbl.Visible = true
            t.lbl.TextSize = 12
            t.lbl.Text = name
            t.lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, off, 0, 0)
            t.lbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, tw, 1, 0)
            t.lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        end
    end
end

local function applySidebar(compact, animate)
    tabCompact = compact
    sidebarW = compact and 96 or 138
    applyTabStyle(compact)
    local ti = TweenInfo.new(animate and 0.22 or 0, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
    TweenService:Create(TabsBar,    ti, { Size = UDim2.new(0, sidebarW, 1, -216) }):Play()
    TweenService:Create(TabDivider, ti, { Position = UDim2.new(0, sidebarW + 22, 0, 0) }):Play()
    if Cfg.Profile then
        TweenService:Create(Cfg.Profile, ti, { Size = UDim2.new(0, sidebarW, 0, Cfg.ProfileH or 84) }):Play()
        Cfg.layoutProfile(compact)
    end
    TweenService:Create(Cfg.Brand, ti, { Size = UDim2.new(0, sidebarW - 8, 0, 100) }):Play()
    TweenService:Create(Cfg.IconDivider, ti, { Size = UDim2.new(0, sidebarW + 22, 0, 1) }):Play()
    TweenService:Create(SearchWrap, ti, { Position = UDim2.new(0, sidebarW + 32, 0.5, -Cfg.SearchH / 2) }):Play()
    TweenService:Create(TitleLine, ti, { Position = UDim2.new(0, sidebarW + 32, 0, 55), Size = UDim2.new(1, -(sidebarW + 48), 0, 1) }):Play()
    layoutContent(animate)
end

local currentTabName = nil
local titleAnimToken = 0
local function refreshTabTitle(animate)
    local name = currentTabName
    if not name then return end
    titleAnimToken = titleAnimToken + 1
    local my = titleAnimToken
    Cfg.TitleTweens = Cfg.TitleTweens or {}
    for _, t in ipairs(Cfg.TitleTweens) do t:Cancel() end
    table.clear(Cfg.TitleTweens)
    local function play(o, info, goal)
        local t = TweenService:Create(o, info, goal)
        Cfg.TitleTweens[#Cfg.TitleTweens + 1] = t
        t:Play()
    end

    local parts = tabParts[name]
    local function applyNew()   -- writes the new tab's text + icon, returns whether the icon is shown and the text's x
        tabTitleLabel.Text = name
        local shown = LucideReady and parts and parts.iconName and setIcon(tabTitleIcon, parts.iconName, P.Accent)
        tabTitleIcon.Visible = shown and true or false
        local x = shown and 32 or 10
        tabTitleLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -(x), 1, -4)
        return shown and true or false, x
    end

    if not (animate and tabTitleFrame.Visible and Main.Visible) then
        local shown, x = applyNew()
        tabTitleLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, x, 0, 0)
        tabTitleLabel.TextTransparency = 0
        tabTitleIcon.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0.5, -10)
        if shown then tabTitleIcon.ImageTransparency = 0 end
        return
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        -- 1) current title drifts right a little and fades out
        local outInfo = TweenInfo.new(0.14, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
        local curX = tabTitleLabel.Position.X.Offset
        play(tabTitleLabel, outInfo, { Position = UDim2.new(0, curX + 10, 0, 0), TextTransparency = 1 })
        if tabTitleIcon.Visible then
            play(tabTitleIcon, outInfo, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0.5, -10), ImageTransparency = 1 })
        end
        task.wait(0.14)
        if my ~= titleAnimToken then return end

        -- 2) new title starts slightly to the left, invisible, and glides into place
        local shown, x = applyNew()
        tabTitleLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, x - 16, 0, 0)
        tabTitleLabel.TextTransparency = 1
        local inInfo = TweenInfo.new(0.42, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
        play(tabTitleLabel, inInfo, { Position = UDim2.new(0, x, 0, 0), TextTransparency = 0 })
        if shown then
            tabTitleIcon.Position = UDim2.new(0, -6, 0.5, -10)
            tabTitleIcon.ImageTransparency = 1
            play(tabTitleIcon, TweenInfo.new(0.42, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.Out, 0, false, 0.03),
                { Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0.5, -10), ImageTransparency = 0 })
        end
    end)
end
task.spawn(function()
    local t0 = tick()
    while not LucideReady and tick() - t0 < 30 do task.wait(0.2) end
    refreshTabTitle()
end)

-- the selected tab's icon gets a soft gradient built from the active theme's accent colours; the others stay muted
function Cfg.tabIconSeq()
    local a, b = P.Accent, P.Accent2
    return ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,   a:Lerp(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.35)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, a),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,   b),
    })
end

function Cfg.paintTabIcon(name)
    local parts = tabParts[name]
    if not parts then return end
    local ic = parts.ic
    local g = ic:FindFirstChild("SelGrad")
    if currentTabName == name then
        if not g then
            g = Instance.new("UIGradient")
            g.Name = "SelGrad"
            g.Rotation = 50
            g:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true)
            g.Parent = ic
        end
        g.Color = Cfg.tabIconSeq()   -- re-read every time so a theme change recolours it
        -- a muted-colour tween that is still running (started before this tab was selected, e.g. on first load)
        -- would drag the icon back to grey and make the gradient look faint, so stop it first
        if parts.tw then parts.tw:Cancel(); parts.tw = nil end
        ic:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true)   -- the theme recolour pass must not tween the selected icon either
        ic.ImageColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    else
        if g then g:Destroy() end
        ic:SetAttribute("NoTheme", nil)
        if parts.tw then parts.tw:Cancel() end
        parts.tw = TweenService:Create(ic, TweenInfo.new(0.18), { ImageColor3 = P.Muted })
        parts.tw:Play()
    end
end

function Cfg.repaintTabIcons()
    for n in pairs(tabParts) do Cfg.paintTabIcon(n) end
end

local tabAnimToken = 0
local function switchTab(name)
    local prevTab = currentTabName
    currentTabName = name
    -- tapping the tab that is already open changes nothing: leave the title transition alone, otherwise the
    -- re-tap cancels the animation that is still playing and the title just snaps into place
    if prevTab ~= name then
        refreshTabTitle(prevTab ~= nil)
    end

    -- tab switch animation: new page slides up + fades in
    tabAnimToken = tabAnimToken + 1
    local animToken = tabAnimToken
    local newPage = pages[name]
    local doAnim = newPage and prevTab ~= nil and prevTab ~= name
        and Main.Visible and Content.GroupTransparency < 0.05
    if doAnim then
        newPage.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 22)
        Content.GroupTransparency = 0.65
    end

    for n, p in pairs(pages) do
        p.Visible = (n == name)
        local btn = tabButtons[n]
        if btn then
            local s = btn:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("UIStroke")
            local ic = btn:FindFirstChild("TabIcon", true)
            local tl = btn:FindFirstChild("TabLabel", true)
            if n == name then
                TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.18), {
                    BackgroundColor3 = P.Bg4, BackgroundTransparency = 0.25, TextColor3 = P.Text
                }):Play()
                if s then TweenService:Create(s, TweenInfo.new(0.18), { Transparency = 0 }):Play() end
                if tl then TweenService:Create(tl, TweenInfo.new(0.18), { TextColor3 = P.Text }):Play() end
            else
                TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.18), {
                    BackgroundColor3 = P.Bg2, BackgroundTransparency = 0.5, TextColor3 = P.Muted
                }):Play()
                if s then TweenService:Create(s, TweenInfo.new(0.18), { Transparency = 0.5 }):Play() end
                if tl then TweenService:Create(tl, TweenInfo.new(0.18), { TextColor3 = P.Muted }):Play() end
            end
        end
    end

    for n in pairs(tabParts) do Cfg.paintTabIcon(n) end

    if doAnim then
        local ti = TweenInfo.new(0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
        TweenService:Create(newPage, ti, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0) }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(Content, ti, { GroupTransparency = 0 }):Play()
    end
end

local tabLocks = {}
local notify   -- assigned in the notifications section

-- tooltip shown next to a tab while it's hovered
local tabDescs = {}
local showTabTip, hideTabTip
do
    local TS = game:GetService("TextService")
    local tip = Instance.new("CanvasGroup")
    tip.Name = "TabTip"
    tip.BackgroundColor3 = P.Bg3
    tip.BorderSizePixel = 0
    tip.GroupTransparency = 1
    tip.Visible = false
    tip.ZIndex = 90
    tip.Parent = screenGui
    corner(tip, 9)
    local tipStroke = glassBorder(tip, 1.2, 0.3)
    tipStroke.Transparency = 1
    -- the tooltip sizes itself around its text (AutomaticSize) and the line breaks are inserted by hand, so the
    -- engine can never re-wrap the text onto an extra line that the box then cuts off
    tip.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.XY
    tip.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
    local tipPad = Instance.new("UIPadding")
    tipPad.PaddingLeft, tipPad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 10), UDim.new(0, 10)
    tipPad.PaddingTop, tipPad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 8), UDim.new(0, 8)
    tipPad.Parent = tip
    local tipLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    tipLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    tipLbl.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.XY
    tipLbl.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
    tipLbl.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    tipLbl.TextSize = 12
    tipLbl.TextColor3 = P.Text
    tipLbl.TextWrapped = false
    tipLbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None
    tipLbl.RichText = true
    tipLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    tipLbl.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
    tipLbl.ZIndex = 91
    tipLbl.Parent = tip

    local function wrapLines(text, ts, maxW)
        local out = {}
        for para in (tostring(text) .. "\n"):gmatch("(.-)\n") do
            local cur = ""
            for word in para:gmatch("%S+") do
                local try = (cur == "") and word or (cur .. " " .. word)
                local okW, sz = pcall(function()
                    return TS:GetTextSize(stripRich(try), ts, Enum.Font.Gotham, Vector2.new(10000, 1000))
                end)
                if okW and sz.X > maxW and cur ~= "" then
                    out[#out + 1] = cur
                    cur = word
                else
                    cur = try
                end
            end
            out[#out + 1] = cur
        end
        return table.concat(out, "\n")
    end
    local token = 0

    -- CanvasGroup only fades its CONTENTS; the UIStroke sits outside the canvas, so it has to be
    -- tweened with the exact same info or the outline lingers after everything else is gone
    local FADE_IN  = TweenInfo.new(0.16, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
    local FADE_OUT = TweenInfo.new(0.12, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
    local function fadeTip(on, pos)
        local info = on and FADE_IN or FADE_OUT
        local goal = { GroupTransparency = on and 0 or 1 }
        if pos then goal.Position = pos end
        TweenService:Create(tip, info, goal):Play()
        TweenService:Create(tipStroke, info, { Transparency = on and 0 or 1 }):Play()
    end

    function showTabTip(btn, text)
        token = token + 1
        local my = token
        task.delay(0.25, function()
            if my ~= token or not btn.Parent or not Main.Visible then return end
            local sc = math.max(MainScale.Scale, 0.01)
            local ts = math.max(8, math.floor(12 * sc + 0.5))
            local padX, padY = math.floor(10 * sc + 0.5), math.floor(8 * sc + 0.5)
            tipPad.PaddingLeft, tipPad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, padX), UDim.new(0, padX)
            tipPad.PaddingTop, tipPad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, padY), UDim.new(0, padY + math.ceil(2 * sc))
            tipLbl.TextSize = ts
            tipLbl.Text = wrapLines(text, ts, 210 * sc)
            -- let the engine lay the box out around the text (one frame), then read its real size
            tip.GroupTransparency = 1
            tipStroke.Transparency = 1
            tip.Visible = true
            RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
            if my ~= token or not btn.Parent then return end
            local tsz = tip.AbsoluteSize
            -- tab's right edge, vertically centred on the tab (screenGui origin subtracted for safety)
            local origin = screenGui.AbsolutePosition
            local ap, as = btn.AbsolutePosition - origin, btn.AbsoluteSize
            local x = ap.X + as.X + 6
            local y = ap.Y + as.Y / 2 - tsz.Y / 2
            local vp = screenGui.AbsoluteSize
            y = math.clamp(y, 4, math.max(4, vp.Y - tsz.Y - 4))
            tip.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x - 5, y)
            fadeTip(true, UDim2.fromOffset(x, y))
        end)
    end
    function hideTabTip()
        token = token + 1
        local my = token
        fadeTip(false)
        task.delay(FADE_OUT.Time + 0.02, function() if my == token then tip.Visible = false end end)
    end
end

-- createTab(name, icon, description)  -- the description (optional) shows up when the tab is hovered
local function createTab(name, iconName, desc)
    tabDescs[name] = desc
    local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    btn.Name = "Tab_" .. name
    btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 38)
    btn.BackgroundColor3 = P.Bg2
    btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
    btn.Text = ""                            -- text moved to a label so icon fits
    btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    btn.TextSize = 12
    btn.TextColor3 = P.Muted
    btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    btn.AutoButtonColor = false
    btn.ZIndex = 4
    btn.Parent = TabsBar
    corner(btn, 10)
    local bs = glassBorder(btn, 1.2, 0.35)   -- iOS glass outline, same family as the section cards
    bs.Transparency = 0.5

    local innerPad = Instance.new("UIPadding", btn)
    innerPad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 6)
    innerPad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 6)

    local TextService = game:GetService("TextService")
    local okSz, sz = pcall(function()
        return TextService:GetTextSize(name, 12, Enum.Font.GothamBold, Vector2.new(1000, 100))
    end)
    local textW = (okSz and sz.X or (#name * 7)) + 2
    local GAP, ICON = 4, 18

    -- holder is always centered in the tab, so every tab looks identical
    local holder = Instance.new("Frame")
    holder.Name = "Holder"
    holder.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
    holder.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0.5, 0)     -- left aligned (sidebar style)
    holder.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 1, 0)
    holder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    holder.ZIndex = 5
    holder.Parent = btn

    -- icon (hidden until Lucide loads)
    local ic = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    ic.Name = "TabIcon"
    ic.Size = UDim2.new(0, ICON, 0, ICON)
    ic.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -ICON / 2)
    ic.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    ic.ImageColor3 = P.Muted
    ic.Visible = false
    ic.ZIndex = 5
    ic.Parent = holder

    local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    lbl.Name = "TabLabel"
    lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    lbl.Text = name
    lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    lbl.TextSize = 12
    lbl.TextColor3 = P.Muted
    lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    lbl.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
    lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -(ICON + GAP), 1, 0)
    lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, ICON + GAP, 0, 0)
    lbl.ZIndex = 5
    lbl.Parent = holder

    tabButtons[name] = btn
    tabParts[name] = { holder = holder, ic = ic, lbl = lbl, btn = btn, textW = textW, pending = true, iconName = iconName }
    tabCount = tabCount + 1
    applyTabStyle(tabCompact)

    task.spawn(function()
        if not LucideReady then
            local t0 = tick()
            while not LucideReady and tick() - t0 < 30 do task.wait(0.2) end
        end
        if LucideReady and setIcon(ic, iconName, P.Muted) then
            ic.Visible = true
            Cfg.paintTabIcon(name)
        end
        if tabParts[name] then tabParts[name].pending = false end
        applyTabStyle(tabCompact)
    end)

    btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local lk = tabLocks[name]
        if lk then
            if notify then notify("Locked", lk.reason or (name .. " is locked."), "warning", 3) end
            return
        end
        switchTab(name)
    end)
    btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() if tabDescs[name] then showTabTip(btn, tabDescs[name]) end end)
    btn.MouseLeave:Connect(hideTabTip)
    btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(hideTabTip)
    btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        if pages[name] and pages[name].Visible then return end
        TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { BackgroundTransparency = 0.38 }):Play()
        local gs = btn:FindFirstChild("GlassBorder")
        if gs then TweenService:Create(gs, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { Transparency = 0.38 }):Play() end
    end)
    btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        if pages[name] and pages[name].Visible then return end
        TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { BackgroundTransparency = 0.5 }):Play()
        local gs = btn:FindFirstChild("GlassBorder")
        if gs then TweenService:Create(gs, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { Transparency = 0.5 }):Play() end
    end)

    local page = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    page.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    page.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    page.BorderSizePixel = 0
    page.ScrollBarThickness = 0
    page.ScrollBarImageColor3 = P.Line2
    page.ScrollBarImageTransparency = 0.4
    page.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    page.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y
    page.ElasticBehavior = Enum.ElasticBehavior.Never
    page.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    page.Visible = false
    page.ZIndex = 4
    page.Parent = Content
    autoFadeScrollbar(page, 0.4)

    local pad = Instance.new("UIPadding")
    pad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 0)   -- sections start right under the title divider (no extra gap)
    pad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 16)
    pad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 10)
    pad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 10)
    pad.Parent = page

    local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 8)   -- gap between sections
    layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    layout.Parent = page

    pages[name] = page


    return page
end

-- profile card at the bottom of the sidebar: avatar, display name and the current game; click opens Home
-- (the Name / Avatar chips on the Home banner hide the name and the avatar here too)
do
    local PROF_H = 84
    Cfg.ProfileH = PROF_H
    Cfg.hideName, Cfg.hideAvatar = false, false
    local card = Instance.new("TextButton")
    card.Name = "Profile"
    card.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1)
    card.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 1, -10)
    card.Size = UDim2.new(0, sidebarW, 0, PROF_H)
    card.BackgroundTransparency = 1   -- no panel behind the profile
    card.BorderSizePixel = 0
    card.Text = ""
    card.AutoButtonColor = false
    card.ZIndex = 4
    card.Parent = Main

    local head = Instance.new("Frame")
    head.Name = "Avatar"
    head.BackgroundColor3 = P.Bg4
    head.BorderSizePixel = 0
    head.ZIndex = 5
    head.Parent = card
    Instance.new("UICorner", head).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    local headStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", head)
    headStroke.Color = P.Line2
    headStroke.Transparency = 0.2
    headStroke.Thickness = 1

    local img = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    img.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    img.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    img.Image = "rbxthumb://type=AvatarHeadShot&id=" .. tostring(LocalPlayer.UserId) .. "&w=150&h=150"
    img.ZIndex = 6
    img.Parent = head
    Instance.new("UICorner", img).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

    -- shown instead of the headshot while the Avatar chip is on
    local ph = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    ph.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    ph.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
    ph.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
    ph.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    ph.ImageColor3 = P.Muted
    ph.ImageTransparency = 1
    ph.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
    ph.ZIndex = 6
    ph.Parent = head

    local dot = Instance.new("Frame")   -- "online" dot
    dot.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1)
    dot.Position = UDim2.new(1, 1, 1, 1)
    dot.Size = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 12)
    dot.BackgroundColor3 = P.Good
    dot.BorderSizePixel = 0
    dot.ZIndex = 7
    dot.Parent = head
    Instance.new("UICorner", dot).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    local dotStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", dot)
    dotStroke.Color = P.Bg3
    dotStroke.Thickness = 2

    local nameLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    nameLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    nameLbl.Text = LocalPlayer.DisplayName
    nameLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    nameLbl.TextColor3 = P.Text
    nameLbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
    nameLbl.ZIndex = 5
    nameLbl.Parent = card

    local subLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")   -- game name, right under the nickname
    subLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    subLbl.Text = tostring(game.Name)
    subLbl.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    subLbl.TextSize = 13
    subLbl.TextColor3 = P.Muted
    subLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    subLbl.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
    subLbl.TextWrapped = true   -- long game names use two lines instead of shrinking
    subLbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
    subLbl.ZIndex = 5
    subLbl.Parent = card

    -- name / avatar privacy (driven by the Name / Avatar chips on the Home banner)
    function Cfg.renderProfile(instant)
        nameLbl.Text = Cfg.hideName and "Hidden" or LocalPlayer.DisplayName
        local ti = TweenInfo.new(instant and 0 or 0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
        TweenService:Create(img, ti, { ImageTransparency = Cfg.hideAvatar and 1 or 0 }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(ph, ti, { ImageTransparency = Cfg.hideAvatar and 0.2 or 1 }):Play()

        -- hidden name -> muted text, hidden avatar -> dim ring + grey dot
        TweenService:Create(nameLbl, ti, { TextColor3 = Cfg.hideName and P.Muted or P.Text }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(subLbl, ti, { TextTransparency = 0 }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(head, ti, { BackgroundColor3 = Cfg.hideAvatar and P.Bg3 or P.Bg4 }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(headStroke, ti, {
            Color = Cfg.hideAvatar and P.Muted or P.Line2,
            Transparency = Cfg.hideAvatar and 0.1 or 0.2,
        }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(dot, ti, { BackgroundColor3 = Cfg.hideAvatar and P.Muted or P.Good }):Play()
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        local t0 = tick()
        while not LucideReady and tick() - t0 < 30 do task.wait(0.2) end
        if LucideReady and ph.Parent and setIcon(ph, "user", P.Muted) then
            Cfg.renderProfile(true)   -- setIcon makes the icon opaque, so apply the current privacy state again
        end
    end)

    -- wide sidebar: avatar on the left, name + game next to it; icon-only sidebar: avatar with the name under it
    function Cfg.layoutProfile(compact)
        if compact then
            head.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
            head.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 5)
            head.Size = UDim2.new(0, 36, 0, 36)
            nameLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 43)
            nameLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 15)
            nameLbl.TextSize = 13
            nameLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
            subLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 58)   -- game name stays visible under the nickname in icon-only mode
            subLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 25)
            subLbl.TextSize = 12
            subLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
            subLbl.Visible = true
        else
            head.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
            head.Position = UDim2.new(0, 9, 0.5, 0)
            head.Size = UDim2.new(0, 48, 0, 48)
            nameLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 65, 0.5, -19)
            nameLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0, 18)
            nameLbl.TextSize = 14
            nameLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            subLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 65, 0.5, 1)
            subLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0, 28)
            subLbl.TextSize = 13
            subLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            subLbl.Visible = true
        end
    end
    Cfg.layoutProfile(tabCompact)

    -- like the reference profile card, the small line shows the game we are in
    task.spawn(function()
        local ok, info = pcall(function() return game:GetService("MarketplaceService"):GetProductInfo(game.PlaceId) end)
        if ok and type(info) == "table" and type(info.Name) == "string" and info.Name ~= "" and subLbl.Parent then
            subLbl.Text = info.Name
        end
    end)

    card.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if tabButtons["Home"] and not tabLocks["Home"] then
            switchTab("Home")
        end
    end)

    Cfg.Profile = card
end

TabsBar:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(refreshTabsCanvas)
TabsLayout:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(refreshTabsCanvas)
-- the canvas depends on the menu scale (shrinking to fit a phone, the scale slider, the resize grip)
MainScale:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Scale"):Connect(function()
    refreshTabsCanvas()
    task.defer(refreshTabsCanvas)   -- once more after the layout has settled at the new scale
end)

-- ============================================================
-- WIDGETS
-- ============================================================
local VisualSetters = {}
-- Cfg.Toggles: key -> function(bool): updates the switch AND runs its callback (used by config loading)
local SliderSetters = {}
local applyMenuScale   -- defined after the window is built

-- Section card with icon
local secCounter = 0
local SectionCtl = {}   -- every section's collapse controller
-- section(page, title, icon, searchable)  -- searchable = true adds a search bar that filters just this section
-- trims leading/trailing blank space + newlines so a description never ends in an empty line (that was the gap at the bottom)
function Cfg.trimDesc(t)
    t = tostring(t or "")
    t = t:gsub("^[%s\r\n]+", "")
    t = t:gsub("[%s\r\n]+$", "")
    return t
end

-- creates / updates the small grey description under a section title
local function setSectionDesc(wrap, text)
    text = Cfg.trimDesc(text)
    local df = wrap:FindFirstChild("SectionDesc")
    if not text or text == "" then
        if df then df:Destroy() end
        return
    end
    if not df then
        df = Instance.new("Frame")
        df.Name = "SectionDesc"
        df.LayoutOrder = -3
        df.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
        df.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        df.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        df.BorderSizePixel = 0
        df.Parent = wrap
        local dl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        dl.Name = "Text"
        dl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
        dl.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        dl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        dl.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        dl.TextSize = 11
        dl.TextColor3 = P.Muted
