        dl.TextWrapped = true
        dl.RichText = true
        dl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        dl.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
        dl.Parent = df
    end
    local dl = df:FindFirstChild("Text")
    if dl then richSet(dl, text) end
end

-- ============================================================
-- SECTION POP-OUT
--   A small button next to each section's collapse arrow lifts the section into a floating
--   panel that can be dragged anywhere over the screen. A slim "popped out, click to dock" bar
--   stays behind in the page; clicking the button again (or the bar) flies the panel back and docks it.
--   Animation: the bar eases down from the section's height while the panel lifts off with a soft
--   spring and a deepening shadow; docking flies it home as the bar grows back to full height.
-- ============================================================
do
    local layer = Instance.new("Frame")
    layer.Name = "PopLayer"
    layer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    layer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    layer.BorderSizePixel = 0
    layer.ZIndex = 35
    layer.Visible = Main.Visible
    layer.Parent = screenGui
    Main:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function() layer.Visible = Main.Visible end)

    local topZ = 1
    local pops = {}      -- [wrap] = record of every section that is currently popped out
    local drag = nil     -- { Input, Start, Origin, Rec }

    local function sc() return math.max(MainScale.Scale, 0.01) end

    local function clampXY(rec, x, y)
        local vp = layer.AbsoluteSize
        local sz = rec.Wrap.AbsoluteSize
        x = math.clamp(x, 60 - sz.X, vp.X - 60)   -- always keep part of the header reachable
        y = math.clamp(y, 0, math.max(vp.Y - 30, 0))
        return x, y
    end

    local function raise(rec)
        topZ = topZ + 1
        rec.Wrap.ZIndex = topZ * 2
        if rec.Shadow then rec.Shadow.ZIndex = topZ * 2 - 1 end
    end

    -- one set of pointer handlers for every panel
    UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if not drag then return end
        local t = input.UserInputType
        if not (t == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input == drag.Input) then return end
        if drag.Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            and not UserInputService:IsMouseButtonPressed(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1) then
            drag = nil
            return
        end
        local rec = drag.Rec
        if not rec or not pops[rec.Wrap] or rec.Docking then drag = nil return end
        local d = input.Position - drag.Start
        local x, y = clampXY(rec, drag.Origin.X + d.X, drag.Origin.Y + d.Y)
        rec.Wrap.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y)
    end)
    UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if not drag then return end
        if input == drag.Input or input.UserInputType == drag.Input.UserInputType then drag = nil end
    end)
    UserInputService.WindowFocusReleased:Connect(function() drag = nil end)

    MainScale:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Scale"):Connect(function()
        if Cfg.popScaleLocked then return end   -- minimizing rescales the window; floating panels must not shrink with it
        for _, rec in pairs(pops) do rec.Scale.Scale = sc() end
    end)
    function Cfg.syncPopScale()
        for _, rec in pairs(pops) do rec.Scale.Scale = sc() end
    end

    function Cfg.dockAll(instant)
        local list = {}
        for _, rec in pairs(pops) do list[#list + 1] = rec end
        for _, rec in ipairs(list) do rec.Dock(instant) end
    end

    -- builds the button and wires everything for one section
    function Cfg.attachPop(wrap, header, ctl, chev)
        local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        btn.Name = "PopOut"
        btn.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
        btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 18)
        btn.Position = UDim2.new(1, chev.Visible and -24 or 0, 0.5, 0)
        btn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        btn.Text = ""
        btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        btn.TextSize = 11
        btn.TextColor3 = P.Muted
        btn.AutoButtonColor = false
        btn.ZIndex = 5
        btn.Parent = header
        chev:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function()
            btn.Position = UDim2.new(1, chev.Visible and -24 or 0, 0.5, 0)
        end)

        local ic = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
        ic.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        ic.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
        ic.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14)
        ic.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        ic.ImageColor3 = P.Muted
        ic.Visible = false
        ic.ZIndex = 6
        ic.Parent = btn

        local hover = false
        local function tint()
            local col = (pops[wrap] and P.Accent) or (hover and P.Text) or P.Muted
            TweenService:Create(ic, TweenInfo.new(0.12), { ImageColor3 = col }):Play()
            btn.TextColor3 = col
        end
        task.spawn(function()
            local t0 = tick()
            while not LucideReady and tick() - t0 < 30 do task.wait(0.2) end
            local ok = false
            if LucideReady then
                for _, n in ipairs({ "picture-in-picture-2", "monitor", "square-arrow-out-up-right" }) do
                    if setIcon(ic, n, P.Muted) then ok = true break end
                end
            end
            if ok then ic.Visible = true else btn.Text = "[]" end
        end)

        local conns = {}

        -- fade helpers: all visible transparencies inside the panel are faded together by one tween
        local function snapshotFade(root)
            local list = {}
            local function add(inst, prop)
                local ok, v = pcall(function() return inst[prop] end)
                if ok and type(v) == "number" and v < 1 then
                    list[#list + 1] = { inst = inst, prop = prop, orig = v }
                end
            end
            local function scan(inst)
                if inst:IsA("GuiObject") then
                    add(inst, "BackgroundTransparency")
                    if inst:IsA("TextLabel") or inst:IsA("TextButton") or inst:IsA("TextBox") then
                        add(inst, "TextTransparency")
                        add(inst, "TextStrokeTransparency")
                    end
                    if inst:IsA("ImageLabel") or inst:IsA("ImageButton") then
                        add(inst, "ImageTransparency")
                    end
                    if inst:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
                        add(inst, "ScrollBarImageTransparency")
                    end
                elseif inst:IsA("UIStroke") then
                    add(inst, "Transparency")
                end
            end
            scan(root)
            for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do scan(d) end
            return list
        end

        local function runFade(list, info, onDone)
            local nv = Instance.new("NumberValue")
            local function apply(a)
                for _, e in ipairs(list) do
                    pcall(function() e.inst[e.prop] = e.orig + (1 - e.orig) * a end)
                end
            end
            local conn = nv.Changed:Connect(apply)
            local tw = TweenService:Create(nv, info, { Value = 1 })
            tw.Completed:Once(function()
                conn:Disconnect()
                nv:Destroy()
                apply(1)
                if onDone then onDone() end
            end)
            tw:Play()
        end

        -- is the dock slot really visible right now? (false while the window is minimized / hidden,
        -- or when its tab is not the open one)
        local function slotShown(slot)
            if not slot.Parent or slot.AbsoluteSize.X <= 0 then return false end
            if not Main.Visible then return false end
            local o = slot
            while o and not o:IsA("LayerCollector") do
                if o:IsA("GuiObject") and not o.Visible then return false end
                if o:IsA("CanvasGroup") and o.GroupTransparency > 0.5 then return false end
                o = o.Parent
            end
            return true
        end

        local lastSpot = nil   -- where this section was last floated (remembered for its next pop-out)
        local QUINT, BACK = Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingStyle.Back

        -- section name for the bar left behind in the page
        local function titleText()
            local lbl = header:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel")
            local s = lbl and stripRich(lbl.Text) or ""
            if s == "" then s = "Section" end
            return (s:gsub("&", "&amp;"):gsub("<", "&lt;"):gsub(">", "&gt;"))
        end

        -- the drop shadow lives on the pop layer (a section frame is laid out by its own UIListLayout),
        -- and follows the panel's position / size / scale
        local function placeShadow(rec)
            local sh = rec.Shadow
            if not sh or not sh.Parent or not wrap.Parent then return end
            local k = rec.Scale.Scale
            local p0 = wrap.AbsolutePosition - layer.AbsolutePosition
            sh.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(p0.X - 24 * k, p0.Y - 18 * k)
            sh.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(wrap.AbsoluteSize.X + 48 * k, wrap.AbsoluteSize.Y + 44 * k)
        end

        local function dock(instant)
            local rec = pops[wrap]
            if not rec or rec.Docking then return end
            rec.Docking = true
            if drag and drag.Rec == rec then drag = nil end
            if rec.PosTween then rec.PosTween:Cancel() end
            lastSpot = Vector2.new(wrap.Position.X.Offset, wrap.Position.Y.Offset)

            local function land()
                if pops[wrap] ~= rec then return end
                pops[wrap] = nil
                for _, c in ipairs(conns) do c:Disconnect() end
                table.clear(conns)
                local slot = rec.Slot
                if slot.Parent then
                    local sx = slot.Size.X
                    wrap.Parent = slot.Parent
                    wrap.LayoutOrder = slot.LayoutOrder
                    wrap.Size = UDim2.new(sx.Scale, sx.Offset, 0, 0)
                    ctl.OrigSize = wrap.Size
                end
                wrap.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
                wrap.ZIndex = 1
                wrap.BackgroundTransparency = rec.PrevT
                rec.Scale:Destroy()
                rec.Stroke:Destroy()
                if rec.Shadow then rec.Shadow:Destroy() end
                slot:Destroy()
                tint()
            end

            -- the dock slot is not on screen (UI minimized/hidden): do not fly anywhere, just fade out
            -- in place and drop back into the section while invisible
            local function fadeAway()
                local list = snapshotFade(wrap)
                if rec.Shadow then
                    TweenService:Create(rec.Shadow, TweenInfo.new(0.25), { ImageTransparency = 1 }):Play()
                end
                runFade(list, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), function()
                    if pops[wrap] ~= rec then return end
                    land()
                    for _, e in ipairs(list) do
                        if e.inst ~= rec.Stroke and (e.inst == wrap or e.inst:IsDescendantOf(wrap)) then
                            pcall(function() e.inst[e.prop] = (e.inst == wrap) and rec.PrevT or e.orig end)
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end

            local function fly()
                if pops[wrap] ~= rec then return end
                local slot = rec.Slot
                if not slotShown(slot) then
                    fadeAway()
                    return
                end
                local s = sc()
                local h = wrap.AbsoluteSize.Y / s
                local sx = slot.Size.X
                -- the bar grows back to the section's height while the panel flies home into it
                TweenService:Create(slot, TweenInfo.new(0.36, QUINT, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
                    { Size = UDim2.new(sx.Scale, sx.Offset, 0, h) }):Play()
                if rec.SlotLabel and rec.SlotLabel.Parent then
                    TweenService:Create(rec.SlotLabel, TweenInfo.new(0.12), { TextTransparency = 1 }):Play()
                end
                -- the bar's top stays put as it grows, so its current spot is the landing
                local goal = slot.AbsolutePosition - layer.AbsolutePosition
                local tw = TweenService:Create(wrap, TweenInfo.new(0.36, QUINT, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
                    { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(goal.X, goal.Y) })
                tw.Completed:Once(function() land() end)
                tw:Play()
                TweenService:Create(rec.Scale, TweenInfo.new(0.2), { Scale = s }):Play()
                if rec.Shadow then
                    TweenService:Create(rec.Shadow, TweenInfo.new(0.3), { ImageTransparency = 1 }):Play()
                end
                task.delay(0.6, land)   -- safety net
            end

            if instant or not rec.Slot.Parent then
                land()
                return
            end
            if ctl.Busy then
                -- let a running collapse / expand finish first
                task.spawn(function()
                    local t0 = tick()
                    while ctl.Busy and tick() - t0 < 1.5 do task.wait(0.05) end
                    fly()
                end)
                return
            end
            fly()
        end

        local function popOut()
            if pops[wrap] or ctl.Busy or not wrap.Parent then return end
            local s = sc()
            local absPos, absSize = wrap.AbsolutePosition, wrap.AbsoluteSize
            local startH = absSize.Y / s
            local slimH = math.min(40, startH)

            -- the bar left behind in the page: starts at the section's height so nothing below jumps,
            -- then eases down to a slim bar while its label fades in
            local slot = Instance.new("TextButton")
            slot.Name = "PopSlot"
            slot.AutoButtonColor = false
            slot.Text = ""
            slot.LayoutOrder = wrap.LayoutOrder
            slot.Size = UDim2.new(wrap.Size.X.Scale, wrap.Size.X.Offset, 0, startH)
            slot.BackgroundColor3 = P.Bg3
            slot.BackgroundTransparency = 0.6
            slot.BorderSizePixel = 0
            slot.ClipsDescendants = true
            slot.Parent = wrap.Parent
            corner(slot, 8)
            stroke(slot, P.Line2, 1, 0.3)
            local sl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            sl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, slimH)
            sl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0)
            sl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            sl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
            sl.TextSize = 12
            sl.TextColor3 = P.Muted
            sl.RichText = true
            sl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
            sl.Text = titleText() .. '<font transparency="0.4">  \u{00B7}  popped out, click to dock</font>'
            sl.TextTransparency = 1
            sl.Parent = slot
            slot.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                TweenService:Create(slot, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { BackgroundTransparency = 0.35 }):Play()
            end)
            slot.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                TweenService:Create(slot, TweenInfo.new(0.25), { BackgroundTransparency = 0.6 }):Play()
            end)
            TweenService:Create(slot, TweenInfo.new(0.42, QUINT, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
                { Size = UDim2.new(wrap.Size.X.Scale, wrap.Size.X.Offset, 0, slimH) }):Play()
            TweenService:Create(sl, TweenInfo.new(0.3), { TextTransparency = 0 }):Play()

            local shadow = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
            shadow.Name = "PopShadow"
            shadow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            shadow.Image = "rbxassetid://6014261993"
            shadow.ImageColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
            shadow.ImageTransparency = 1
            shadow.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice
            shadow.SliceCenter = Rect.new(49, 49, 450, 450)
            shadow.Active = false
            shadow.Parent = layer

            local rec = {
                Wrap = wrap, Slot = slot, SlotLabel = sl, Shadow = shadow, PrevT = wrap.BackgroundTransparency,
                Scale = Instance.new("UIScale"), Stroke = nil, Docking = false, Dock = dock,
            }
            pops[wrap] = rec
            rec.Scale.Scale = s * 0.97
            rec.Scale.Parent = wrap
            rec.Stroke = stroke(wrap, P.Accent, 1.2, 0.35)

            local from = absPos - layer.AbsolutePosition
            wrap.Size = UDim2.new(0, absSize.X / s, 0, 0)
            wrap.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(from.X, from.Y)
            wrap.BackgroundTransparency = 0.04
            wrap.Parent = layer
            raise(rec)
            placeShadow(rec)

            -- the panel follows the slot's width; the shadow follows the panel
            conns[#conns + 1] = wrap:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(function() placeShadow(rec) end)
            conns[#conns + 1] = wrap:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsolutePosition"):Connect(function() placeShadow(rec) end)
            conns[#conns + 1] = slot:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(function()
                if rec.Docking then return end
                local w = slot.AbsoluteSize.X / sc()
                if w > 0 then wrap.Size = UDim2.new(0, w, 0, 0) end
            end)
            conns[#conns + 1] = slot.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() dock(false) end)
            -- pressing anywhere on the panel brings it to the front
            conns[#conns + 1] = wrap.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
                if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    raise(rec)
                end
            end)
            tint()

            -- lift-off: glides up and out to its float spot (clamped on screen, remembered for next time)
            -- with a soft spring in scale and the shadow deepening under it
            local spot = lastSpot or Vector2.new(from.X + 22 * s, from.Y - 14 * s)
            local vp = layer.AbsoluteSize
            spot = Vector2.new(
                math.clamp(spot.X, 60 - absSize.X, vp.X - 60),
                math.clamp(spot.Y, 0, math.max(vp.Y - 30, 0))
            )
            rec.PosTween = TweenService:Create(wrap, TweenInfo.new(0.5, QUINT, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
                { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(spot.X, spot.Y) })
            rec.PosTween:Play()
            TweenService:Create(rec.Scale, TweenInfo.new(0.55, BACK, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Scale = s }):Play()
            TweenService:Create(shadow, TweenInfo.new(0.4), { ImageTransparency = 0.35 }):Play()
        end

        -- drag the floating panel by its header
        header.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            local rec = pops[wrap]
            if not rec or rec.Docking then return end
            local t = input.UserInputType
            if t ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and t ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
            raise(rec)
            if rec.PosTween then rec.PosTween:Cancel() end   -- grabbing the panel mid-lift stops the glide
            if drag then return end
            local p = wrap.Position
            drag = { Input = input, Start = input.Position, Origin = Vector2.new(p.X.Offset, p.Y.Offset), Rec = rec }
        end)

        btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if pops[wrap] then dock(false) else popOut() end
        end)
        btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() hover = true tint() end)
        btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() hover = false tint() end)
        return btn
    end
end

local function section(page, title, iconName, searchable, desc)
    secCounter = secCounter + 1
    local wrap = Instance.new("Frame")
    wrap:SetAttribute("SecIdx", secCounter)
    wrap.LayoutOrder = secCounter
    wrap.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 0)
    wrap.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    wrap.BackgroundColor3 = P.Bg2
    wrap.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
    wrap.BorderSizePixel = 0
    wrap.Parent = page
    corner(wrap, 6)
    do  -- very subtle glassy edge
        local gb = glassBorder(wrap, 1, 0.8)
        local gg = gb:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient")
        if gg then
            gg.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,    0.80),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.3,  0.93),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.65, 0.96),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1,    0.88),
            })
        end
    end

    local pad = Instance.new("UIPadding")
    pad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 10)
    pad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 10)
    pad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 14)
    pad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 14)
    pad.Parent = wrap

    local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 8)
    layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    layout.Parent = wrap

    local header = Instance.new("Frame")
    header.LayoutOrder = -4
    header.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18)
    header.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    header.Parent = wrap

    local icon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    icon.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14)
    icon.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -7)
    icon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    icon.ImageColor3 = P.Accent
    icon.Visible = false
    icon.Parent = header

    local dot = Instance.new("Frame")
    dot.Size = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 6)
    dot.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0.5, -3)
    dot.BackgroundColor3 = P.Accent
    dot.BorderSizePixel = 0
    dot.Parent = header
    corner(dot, 3)

    local headerLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    headerLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -46, 1, 0)
    headerLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 0)
    headerLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    headerLbl.Text = title
    headerLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    headerLbl.TextSize = 11
    headerLbl.TextColor3 = P.Muted
    headerLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    headerLbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
    headerLbl.Parent = header
    richSet(headerLbl, title)

    if desc and desc ~= "" then setSectionDesc(wrap, desc) end

    -- divider under the section header
    local divider = Instance.new("Frame")
    divider.LayoutOrder = -2
    divider.Name = "SectionDivider"
    divider.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1)
    divider.BackgroundColor3 = P.Line2
    divider.BackgroundTransparency = 0.45
    divider.BorderSizePixel = 0
    divider.Parent = wrap

    if searchable then
        local sw = Instance.new("Frame")
        sw.Name = "SectionSearch"
        sw.LayoutOrder = -1
        sw.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30)
        sw.BackgroundColor3 = P.Bg3
        sw.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
        sw.BorderSizePixel = 0
        sw.Parent = wrap
        corner(sw, 9)
        local sws = glassBorder(sw, 1.2, 0.35)
        sws.Transparency = 0.5
        local swIcon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
        swIcon.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14)
        swIcon.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0.5, -7)
        swIcon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        swIcon.ImageColor3 = P.Muted
        swIcon.Visible = false
        swIcon.ZIndex = 3
        swIcon.Parent = sw
        local swBox = Instance.new("TextBox")
        swBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 1, 0)
        swBox.Position = UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 0)
        swBox.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        swBox.Text = ""
        swBox.PlaceholderText = "Search this section..."
        swBox.PlaceholderColor3 = P.Muted
        swBox.TextColor3 = P.Text
        swBox.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        swBox.TextSize = 12
        swBox.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        swBox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
        swBox.ZIndex = 3
        swBox.Parent = sw
        task.spawn(function()
            local t0 = tick()
            while not LucideReady and tick() - t0 < 30 do task.wait(0.2) end
            if LucideReady then setIcon(swIcon, "search", P.Muted) end
        end)
        swBox.Focused:Connect(function()
            TweenService:Create(sws, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { Color = P.Accent, Transparency = 0 }):Play()
        end)
        swBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
            TweenService:Create(sws, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { Color = rimColor(), Transparency = 0.5 }):Play()
        end)
        -- Filtering flips many rows' Visible at once. With an auto-sized card that can leave the card's
        -- height stale for a moment (the padded background vanishes, then pops back). So: debounce while
        -- typing, only touch rows that really change, then force the card to re-measure itself.
        local filterToken = 0
        local function applySectionFilter()
            if wrap:GetAttribute("Collapsed") then return end
            local q = (swBox.Text:lower():gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", ""))
            for _, c in ipairs(wrap:GetChildren()) do
                if (c:IsA("Frame") or c:IsA("TextButton")) and c ~= header
                and c.Name ~= "SectionDivider" and c.Name ~= "SectionSearch" and c.Name ~= "SectionDesc" then
                    local txt = ""
                    for _, d in ipairs(c:GetDescendants()) do
                        if d:IsA("TextLabel") then txt = txt .. " " .. stripRich(d.Text) end
                    end
                    local show = q == "" or txt:lower():find(q, 1, true) ~= nil
                    if c.Visible ~= show then c.Visible = show end
                end
            end
            -- force a fresh measure (skipped while the collapse animation owns the card's size)
            local function nudge()
                if wrap.Parent and wrap.AutomaticSize == Enum.AutomaticSize.Y and not wrap:GetAttribute("Collapsed") then
                    wrap.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
                    wrap.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
                end
            end
            nudge()
            RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
            nudge()
            task.delay(0.1, nudge)
        end
        swBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
            filterToken = filterToken + 1
            local my = filterToken
            task.delay(0.12, function()
                if my == filterToken then applySectionFilter() end
            end)
        end)
    end

    -- collapse arrow (same chevron-down icon the dropdowns use), only shown when "Collapsible Sections" is on
    local chev = Instance.new("TextButton")
    chev.Name = "Chevron"
    chev.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
    chev.Size = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 18)
    chev.Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.5, 0)
    chev.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    chev.Text = ""
    chev.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    chev.TextSize = 12
    chev.TextColor3 = P.Muted
    chev.AutoButtonColor = false
    chev.Visible = false
    chev.ZIndex = 5
    chev.Parent = header
    local chevIcon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    chevIcon.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    chevIcon.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
    chevIcon.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14)
    chevIcon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    chevIcon.ImageColor3 = P.Muted
    chevIcon.Visible = false
    chevIcon.ZIndex = 6
    chevIcon.Parent = chev
    task.spawn(function()
        local t0 = tick()
        while not LucideReady and tick() - t0 < 30 do task.wait(0.2) end
        if not (LucideReady and setIcon(chevIcon, "chevron-down", P.Muted)) then chev.Text = "v" end
    end)

    local ctl = { Wrap = wrap, Layout = layout, Pad = pad, Header = headerLbl, Collapsed = false, State = false, Busy = false, Rows = {}, OrigSize = wrap.Size }
    local SLIDE_CLOSE = TweenInfo.new(0.8,  Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut)
    local SLIDE_OPEN  = TweenInfo.new(0.7,  Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut)
    local DIV_T = divider.BackgroundTransparency   -- resting transparency of the divider line
    -- height of the card when collapsed: padding + header (+ the description, which always stays visible)
    local function collapsedH()
        local h = pad.PaddingTop.Offset + 18 + pad.PaddingBottom.Offset
        local df = wrap:FindFirstChild("SectionDesc")
        if df then h = h + layout.Padding.Offset + df.AbsoluteSize.Y / math.max(MainScale.Scale, 0.01) end
        return h
    end
    local NONE = Enum.AutomaticSize.None

    -- the card is clipped and ONLY its height is tweened; rows are never touched while moving,
    -- so nothing can end up half-transparent or missing afterwards
    local function finish()
        wrap.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        wrap.Size = ctl.OrigSize
        wrap.ClipsDescendants = false
        ctl.Busy = false
    end

    local function setCollapsed(collapsed, animate)
        if ctl.State == collapsed then return end
        ctl.State = collapsed
        wrap:SetAttribute("Collapsed", collapsed or nil)
        TweenService:Create(chevIcon,
            TweenInfo.new(animate and 0.55 or 0, Enum.EasingStyle.Cubic, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut),
            { Rotation = collapsed and -90 or 0 }):Play()
        local sc = math.max(MainScale.Scale, 0.01)

        if collapsed then
            local rows = {}
            for _, c in ipairs(wrap:GetChildren()) do
                if (c:IsA("Frame") or c:IsA("TextButton")) and c ~= header and c.Name ~= "SectionDesc" and c.Visible then
                    rows[#rows + 1] = c
                end
            end
            ctl.Rows = rows
            if not animate then
                for _, r in ipairs(rows) do r.Visible = false end
                return
            end
            ctl.Busy = true
            ctl.OrigSize = wrap.Size
            local os = ctl.OrigSize
            -- measure the current height BEFORE turning auto-size off; once it is off the card
            -- resolves to its raw Size (height 0), which made it collapse instantly and then
            -- grow back to the header height
            local startH = wrap.AbsoluteSize.Y / sc
            wrap.AutomaticSize = NONE
            wrap.Size = UDim2.new(os.X.Scale, os.X.Offset, 0, startH)
            wrap.ClipsDescendants = true
            TweenService:Create(wrap, SLIDE_CLOSE, { Size = UDim2.new(os.X.Scale, os.X.Offset, 0, collapsedH()) }):Play()
            -- the divider is the last thing left under the header: fade it out while the card
            -- slides so it never visibly vanishes after the animation has finished
            TweenService:Create(divider, TweenInfo.new(SLIDE_CLOSE.Time * 0.55, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
                { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()
            task.delay(SLIDE_CLOSE.Time + 0.03, function()
                for _, r in ipairs(rows) do r.Visible = false end
                divider.BackgroundTransparency = DIV_T   -- hidden anyway; ready for the next open
                finish()
            end)
        else
            local rows = ctl.Rows
            if not animate then
                for _, r in ipairs(rows) do r.Visible = true end
                return
            end
            ctl.Busy = true
            ctl.OrigSize = wrap.Size
            local os = ctl.OrigSize
            wrap.AutomaticSize = NONE
            wrap.Size = UDim2.new(os.X.Scale, os.X.Offset, 0, collapsedH())
            wrap.ClipsDescendants = true
            divider.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            for _, r in ipairs(rows) do r.Visible = true end
            TweenService:Create(divider, TweenInfo.new(SLIDE_OPEN.Time * 0.6, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.In),
                { BackgroundTransparency = DIV_T }):Play()
            task.spawn(function()
                -- wait until the layout has actually re-measured the rows that were just made visible
                -- (one Heartbeat is too early: it still reports the collapsed height, so the tween
                -- ended up going header -> header and the card then snapped open)
                local t0 = tick()
                local full = layout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y / sc + pad.PaddingTop.Offset + pad.PaddingBottom.Offset
                while full <= collapsedH() + 2 and tick() - t0 < 0.3 do
                    RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
                    full = layout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y / sc + pad.PaddingTop.Offset + pad.PaddingBottom.Offset
                end
                TweenService:Create(wrap, SLIDE_OPEN, { Size = UDim2.new(os.X.Scale, os.X.Offset, 0, full) }):Play()
                task.delay(SLIDE_OPEN.Time + 0.03, finish)
            end)
        end
    end

    function ctl.Apply(animate)
        local on = State.CollapsibleSections
        chev.Visible = on
        headerLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, on and -70 or -46, 1, 0)
        if ctl.Busy then
            task.delay(0.85, function() ctl.Apply(animate) end)
            return
        end
        setCollapsed(on and ctl.Collapsed or false, animate)
    end
    function ctl.Toggle()
        if ctl.Busy then return end
        ctl.Collapsed = not ctl.Collapsed
        ctl.Apply(true)
    end
    chev.MouseButton1Click:Connect(ctl.Toggle)
    chev.MouseEnter:Connect(function() TweenService:Create(chevIcon, TweenInfo.new(0.12), { ImageColor3 = P.Text }):Play() end)
    chev.MouseLeave:Connect(function() TweenService:Create(chevIcon, TweenInfo.new(0.12), { ImageColor3 = P.Muted }):Play() end)
    table.insert(SectionCtl, ctl)
    Cfg.attachPop(wrap, header, ctl, chev)
    if State.BoldSectionTitles then   -- defaults apply to sections created after the Settings toggles were built too
        headerLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        headerLbl.TextSize = 12
        headerLbl.TextColor3 = P.Text
    end
    if State.CollapsibleSections then ctl.Apply(false) end

    -- icon swap
    task.spawn(function()
        if not LucideReady then
            local t0 = tick()
            while not LucideReady and tick() - t0 < 30 do task.wait(0.2) end
        end
        if LucideReady and setIcon(icon, iconName, P.Accent) then
            icon.Visible = true
            dot.Visible = false
            headerLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 0)
        end
    end)

    return wrap
end

-- ============================================================
-- ROW CARD WRAPPER  (padding background on every widget row)
-- ============================================================
local RowCards = {}        -- every row card (used by the "Compact Elements" option)
local CompactItems = {}    -- extra per-widget compact hooks: function(on, animate)
local applyCompactRow      -- defined next to applyCompact (below)

-- compactHeight (optional): height the row shrinks to when "Compact Elements" is on
local function makeRowCard(parent, height, compactHeight)
    local row = Instance.new("Frame")
    row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height)
    row.BackgroundColor3 = P.Card
    row.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
    row.BorderSizePixel = 0
    row.Parent = parent
    corner(row, 9)
    local rowStroke = stroke(row, P.Line, 1, 0.75)

    -- padding wrapper so children sit inside cleanly
    local pad = Instance.new("UIPadding", row)
    pad.PaddingLeft   = UDim.new(0, 12)
    pad.PaddingRight  = UDim.new(0, 12)
    pad.PaddingTop    = UDim.new(0, 0)
    pad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 0)

    RowCards[#RowCards + 1] = { Row = row, Stroke = rowStroke, Pad = pad, H = height, CH = compactHeight }
    return row
end

local function toggle(parent, label, default, cb, key, iconName, desc)
    if type(desc) == "string" then desc = Cfg.trimDesc(desc) end
    local hasDesc = type(desc) == "string" and desc ~= ""
    local HEADH = 34   -- height of the title line when a description sits underneath it
    local row = makeRowCard(parent, 40, 32)
    if hasDesc then   -- the description lives inside the card (in its padding), right below the title
        row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, HEADH)   -- minimum only: the card grows to exactly fit the text, no empty band at the end
        row.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        local rp = row:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIPadding")
        if rp then rp.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 8) end
    end

    local icon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    icon.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14)
    icon.Position = hasDesc and UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, (HEADH - 14) / 2) or UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -7)
    icon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    icon.ImageColor3 = P.Muted
    icon.Visible = false
    icon.ZIndex = 3
    icon.Parent = row

    local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    lbl.Size = hasDesc and UDim2.new(1, -60, 0, HEADH) or UDim2.new(1, -60, 1, 0)
    lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    lbl.Text = label
    lbl.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    lbl.TextSize = 13
    lbl.TextColor3 = P.Text
    lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    lbl.TextWrapped = true
    lbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
    lbl.ZIndex = 3
    lbl.Parent = row
    richSet(lbl, label)

    if hasDesc then
        local d = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        d.Name = "Desc"
        d.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, HEADH - 6)
        d.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
        d.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        d.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        d.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        d.TextSize = 11
        d.TextColor3 = P.Muted
        d.TextWrapped = true
        d.RichText = true
        d.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        d.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
        d.Text = desc
        d.ZIndex = 3
        d.Parent = row
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        if not LucideReady then
            local t0 = tick()
            while not LucideReady and tick() - t0 < 30 do task.wait(0.2) end
        end
        if LucideReady and setIcon(icon, iconName, P.Muted) then
            icon.Visible = true
            TweenService:Create(lbl, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
                Position = UDim2.new(0, 24, 0, 0),
                Size = hasDesc and UDim2.new(1, -84, 0, HEADH) or UDim2.new(1, -84, 1, 0)
            }):Play()
        end
    end)

    local sw = Instance.new("TextButton")
    sw.Size = UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 22)
    sw.Position = hasDesc and UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, (HEADH - 22) / 2) or UDim2.new(1, -40, 0.5, -11)
    sw.BackgroundColor3 = default and P.Accent or P.Bg3
    sw.Text = ""
    sw.BorderSizePixel = 0
    sw.AutoButtonColor = false
    sw.ZIndex = 3
    sw.Parent = row
    corner(sw, 11)
    local swS = stroke(sw, default and P.Accent or P.Line, 1, 0.4)

    local knob = Instance.new("Frame")
    knob.Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16)
    knob.Position = default and UDim2.new(1, -18, 0.5, -8) or UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.5, -8)
    knob.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    knob.BorderSizePixel = 0
    knob.ZIndex = 4
    knob.Parent = sw
    corner(knob, 8)

    local on = default
    local function set(v, animate)
        on = v
        local c = v and P.Accent or P.Bg3
        local pos = v and UDim2.new(1, -18, 0.5, -8) or UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.5, -8)
        local strokeCol = v and P.Accent or P.Line
        if animate then
            TweenService:Create(sw, TweenInfo.new(0.18), { BackgroundColor3 = c }):Play()
            TweenService:Create(knob, TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), { Position = pos }):Play()
            TweenService:Create(swS, TweenInfo.new(0.18), { Color = strokeCol }):Play()
        else
            sw.BackgroundColor3 = c
            knob.Position = pos
            swS.Color = strokeCol
        end
    end

    sw.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        set(not on, true)
        if cb then task.spawn(cb, on) end
    end)

    if key then
        if not VisualSetters[key] then VisualSetters[key] = {} end
        table.insert(VisualSetters[key], function(v) set(v, true) end)
        Cfg.Toggles[key] = function(v)
            v = (v == true)
            if on ~= v then
                set(v, true)
                if cb then task.spawn(cb, v) end
            end
        end
    end

    return { Set = set, Instance = row }
end

local function button(parent, text, cb, iconName)
    local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
    b.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 38)
    b.BackgroundColor3 = P.Card
    b.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
    b.Text = ""
    b.BorderSizePixel = 0
    b.AutoButtonColor = false
    b.Parent = parent
    corner(b, 9)
    local bs = stroke(b, P.Line, 1, 0.75)
    CompactItems[#CompactItems + 1] = function(on, animate)
        TweenService:Create(b, TweenInfo.new(animate and 0.2 or 0), { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, on and 32 or 38) }):Play()
    end

    local innerPad = Instance.new("UIPadding", b)
    innerPad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 12)
    innerPad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 12)

    local icon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    icon.Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16)
    icon.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -8)
    icon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    icon.ImageColor3 = P.Muted
    icon.Visible = false
    icon.ZIndex = 3
    icon.Parent = b

    local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    lbl.Text = text
    lbl.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    lbl.TextSize = 13
    lbl.TextColor3 = P.Text
    lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    lbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
    lbl.ZIndex = 3
    lbl.Parent = b
    richSet(lbl, text)

    -- iconName can also be a Roblox image asset (a number or "rbxassetid://...") instead of a Lucide icon name
    local customAsset
    if type(iconName) == "number" then
        customAsset = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(iconName)
    elseif type(iconName) == "string" and iconName:match("^%d+$") then
        customAsset = "rbxassetid://" .. iconName
    elseif type(iconName) == "string" and iconName:match("^rbxassetid://") then
        customAsset = iconName
    end

    if customAsset then
        icon:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true)
        icon.Size = UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 28)
        icon.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -14)
        icon.Image = customAsset
        icon.ImageColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        icon.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
        icon.Visible = true
        lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 38, 0, 0)
        lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -38, 1, 0)
    else
        task.spawn(function()
            if not LucideReady then
                local t0 = tick()
                while not LucideReady and tick() - t0 < 30 do task.wait(0.2) end
            end
            if LucideReady and setIcon(icon, iconName, P.Muted) then
                icon.Visible = true
                TweenService:Create(lbl, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
                    Position = UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 0),
                    Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 1, 0)
                }):Play()
            end
        end)
    end

    -- very soft hover; holding the button only dims it a touch more (no bright accent flash)
    b.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(b, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { BackgroundColor3 = P.Bg3, BackgroundTransparency = 0.25 }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(bs, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { Color = P.Line2, Transparency = 0.6 }):Play()
    end)
    b.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(b, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { BackgroundColor3 = P.Card, BackgroundTransparency = 0.35 }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(bs, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { Color = P.Line, Transparency = 0.75 }):Play()
    end)
    b.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(b, TweenInfo.new(0.08), { BackgroundTransparency = 0.3 }):Play()
    end)
    b.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(b, TweenInfo.new(0.12), { BackgroundTransparency = 0.25 }):Play()
    end)
    b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if cb then task.spawn(cb) end
    end)
    return b
end

-- number -> text without floating point noise (1.2000000000000002 -> "1.2", 2.0 -> "2")
local function fmtNum(n)
    n = tonumber(n)
    if not n then return "0" end
    local t = string.format("%.3f", n)
    t = t:gsub("0+$", ""):gsub("%.$", "")
    return t
end

local function slider(parent, label, min, max, valKey, cb, iconName, commitOnRelease, step)
    local row = makeRowCard(parent, 60, 52)

    local icon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    icon.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14)
    icon.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 11)
    icon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    icon.ImageColor3 = P.Muted
    icon.Visible = false
    icon.ZIndex = 3
    icon.Parent = row

    local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -60, 0, 20)
    lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 8)
    lbl.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
    lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    lbl.Text = label
    lbl.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    lbl.TextSize = 12
    lbl.TextColor3 = P.Text
    lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    lbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
    lbl.ZIndex = 3
    lbl.Parent = row

    task.spawn(function()
        if not LucideReady then
            local t0 = tick()
            while not LucideReady and tick() - t0 < 30 do task.wait(0.2) end
        end
        if LucideReady and setIcon(icon, iconName, P.Muted) then
            icon.Visible = true
            TweenService:Create(lbl, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
                Position = UDim2.new(0, 24, 0, 8),
                Size = UDim2.new(1, -84, 0, 20)
            }):Play()
        end
    end)

    local valBox = Instance.new("TextBox")
    valBox.Size = UDim2.new(0, 52, 0, 20)
    valBox.Position = UDim2.new(1, -52, 0, 8)
    valBox.BackgroundColor3 = P.Bg3
    valBox.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
    valBox.Text = fmtNum(Values[valKey])
    valBox.TextColor3 = P.Text
    valBox.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    valBox.TextSize = 11
    valBox.ClipsDescendants = true
    valBox.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    valBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
    valBox.ZIndex = 3
    valBox.Parent = row
    corner(valBox, 8)
    local vbS = stroke(valBox, P.Line, 1, 0.5)

    valBox.Focused:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(vbS, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { Color = P.Accent, Transparency = 0.3 }):Play()
    end)
    valBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(vbS, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { Color = P.Line, Transparency = 0.5 }):Play()
    end)

    local track = Instance.new("Frame")
    track.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 5)
    track.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 46)
    track.BackgroundColor3 = P.Bg3
    track.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
    track.BorderSizePixel = 0
    track.ZIndex = 3
    track.Parent = row
    corner(track, 3)
    CompactItems[#CompactItems + 1] = function(on, animate)
        TweenService:Create(track, TweenInfo.new(animate and 0.2 or 0), { Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, on and 41 or 46) }):Play()
    end

    local fill = Instance.new("Frame")
    fill.Size = UDim2.new((Values[valKey] - min) / (max - min), 0, 1, 0)
    fill.BackgroundColor3 = P.Accent
    fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
    fill.ZIndex = 3
    fill.Parent = track
    corner(fill, 3)
    gradient(fill, P.Accent, P.Accent2)

    local thumb = Instance.new("Frame")
    thumb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 18)
    thumb.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    thumb.Position = UDim2.new((Values[valKey] - min) / (max - min), 0, 0.5, 0)
    thumb.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    thumb.BorderSizePixel = 0
    thumb.ZIndex = 4
    thumb.Parent = track
    corner(thumb, 2)

    -- floating value indicator (bubble above the thumb)
    local bubble = Instance.new("Frame")
    bubble.Name = "ValueBubble"
    bubble.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)
    bubble.Size = UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 18)
    bubble.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, -8)
    bubble.BackgroundColor3 = P.Accent
    bubble.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    bubble.BorderSizePixel = 0
    bubble.ZIndex = 7
    bubble.Parent = track
    corner(bubble, 3)
    local bubbleLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    bubbleLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    bubbleLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    bubbleLbl.Text = fmtNum(Values[valKey])
    bubbleLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    bubbleLbl.TextSize = 10
    bubbleLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    bubbleLbl.TextTransparency = 1
    bubbleLbl.ZIndex = 8
    bubbleLbl.Parent = bubble

    -- min / max end labels
    local function endLabel(txt, xScale, align)
        local l = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        l.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(xScale, 1)
        l.Size = UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 10)
        l.Position = UDim2.new(xScale, 0, 0, -3)
        l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        l.Text = txt
        l.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        l.TextSize = 9
        l.TextColor3 = P.Muted
        l.TextXAlignment = align
        l.ZIndex = 3
        l.Parent = track
        return l
    end
    endLabel(fmtNum(min), 0, Enum.TextXAlignment.Left)
    endLabel(fmtNum(max), 1, Enum.TextXAlignment.Right)

    local function placeBubble(pos)
        pos = math.clamp(pos, 0, 1)
        bubble.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)   -- always centred exactly on the thumb
        bubble.Position = UDim2.new(pos, 0, 0, -11)
    end
    placeBubble((Values[valKey] - min) / (max - min))
    -- always follow the thumb, whatever moves it (drag, typing in the box, setters)
    thumb:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Position"):Connect(function() placeBubble(thumb.Position.X.Scale) end)
    track:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(function() placeBubble(thumb.Position.X.Scale) end)

    local function showBubble(on)
        local ti = TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
        TweenService:Create(bubble, ti, { BackgroundTransparency = on and 0 or 1 }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(bubbleLbl, ti, { TextTransparency = on and 0 or 1 }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(thumb, ti, {
            Size = on and UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 22) or UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 18)
        }):Play()
    end

    local hit = Instance.new("TextButton")
    hit.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 3, 0)
    hit.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, -1, 0)
    hit.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    hit.Text = ""
    hit.ZIndex = 5
    hit.Parent = track

    local dragging = false
    local function update(input)
        local pos = math.clamp((input.Position.X - track.AbsolutePosition.X) / track.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, 1)
        local newVal = math.floor((min + (max - min) * pos) * 10) / 10
        if step then
            newVal = math.clamp(math.floor((min + (max - min) * pos) / step + 0.5) * step, min, max)
            pos = (newVal - min) / (max - min)
        end
        Values[valKey] = newVal
        valBox.Text = fmtNum(newVal)
        bubbleLbl.Text = fmtNum(newVal)
        fill.Size = UDim2.new(pos, 0, 1, 0)
        thumb.Position = UDim2.new(pos, 0, 0.5, 0); placeBubble(pos)
        if cb and not commitOnRelease then cb(newVal) end
    end

    hit.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function(x)
        dragging = true
        showBubble(true)
        update({ Position = Vector3.new(x, 0, 0) })
    end)
    hit.MouseEnter:Connect(function() if not dragging then showBubble(true) end end)
    hit.MouseLeave:Connect(function() if not dragging then showBubble(false) end end)
    UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
        if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
        or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            if dragging then
                dragging = false; showBubble(false)
                if cb and commitOnRelease then cb(Values[valKey]) end
            end
        end
    end)
    UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
        if dragging and (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
        or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then update(i) end
    end)

    valBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
        local n = tonumber(valBox.Text)
        if n then
            n = math.clamp(n, min, max)
            if step then n = math.clamp(math.floor(n / step + 0.5) * step, min, max) end
            Values[valKey] = n
            valBox.Text = fmtNum(n)
            bubbleLbl.Text = fmtNum(n)
            fill.Size = UDim2.new((n - min) / (max - min), 0, 1, 0)
            thumb.Position = UDim2.new((n - min) / (max - min), 0, 0.5, 0); placeBubble((n - min) / (max - min))
            if cb then cb(n) end
        end
    end)

    SliderSetters[valKey] = function(v)
        local n = math.clamp(v, min, max)
        Values[valKey] = n
        valBox.Text = fmtNum(n)
        bubbleLbl.Text = fmtNum(n)
        fill.Size = UDim2.new((n - min) / (max - min), 0, 1, 0)
        thumb.Position = UDim2.new((n - min) / (max - min), 0, 0.5, 0); placeBubble((n - min) / (max - min))
    end

    return row
end

local function keybindRow(parent, label, bindKey, iconName)
    local row = makeRowCard(parent, 38, 30)

    local icon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    icon.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14)
    icon.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -7)
    icon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    icon.ImageColor3 = P.Muted
    icon.Visible = false
    icon.ZIndex = 3
    icon.Parent = row

    local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -80, 1, 0)
    lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    lbl.Text = label
    lbl.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    lbl.TextSize = 12
    lbl.TextColor3 = P.Text
    lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    lbl.TextWrapped = true
    lbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
    lbl.ZIndex = 3
    lbl.Parent = row

    task.spawn(function()
        if not LucideReady then
            local t0 = tick()
            while not LucideReady and tick() - t0 < 30 do task.wait(0.2) end
        end
        if LucideReady and setIcon(icon, iconName, P.Muted) then
            icon.Visible = true
            TweenService:Create(lbl, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
                Position = UDim2.new(0, 24, 0, 0),
                Size = UDim2.new(1, -104, 1, 0)
            }):Play()
        end
    end)

    local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
    b.Size = UDim2.new(0, 72, 0, 22)
    b.Position = UDim2.new(1, -72, 0.5, -11)
    b.BackgroundColor3 = P.Bg3
    b.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
    b.Text = KEYBINDS[bindKey] and KEYBINDS[bindKey].Name or "?"
    b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    b.TextSize = 11
    b.TextColor3 = P.Accent
    b.BorderSizePixel = 0
    b.AutoButtonColor = false
    b.ZIndex = 3
    b.Parent = row
    corner(b, 8)
    local bs = stroke(b, P.Line, 1, 0.5)

    Cfg.Keybinds[bindKey] = function() b.Text = KEYBINDS[bindKey] and KEYBINDS[bindKey].Name or "?" end
    local listening = false
    b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        listening = true
        IsBinding = true
        b.Text = "..."
        b.TextColor3 = P.Accent2
        TweenService:Create(bs, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { Color = P.Accent2, Transparency = 0.3 }):Play()
    end)

    UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if listening and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
            if input.KeyCode ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then
                KEYBINDS[bindKey] = input.KeyCode
                b.Text = input.KeyCode.Name
                b.TextColor3 = P.Accent
                listening = false
                IsBinding = false
                TweenService:Create(bs, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { Color = P.Line, Transparency = 0.5 }):Play()
            end
        end
    end)

    return row
end

-- ============================================================
-- NOTIFICATIONS
-- ============================================================
local TextService = game:GetService("TextService")

local openDialog
local addTag, sectionTag

do
local notifyHolder = Instance.new("Frame")
notifyHolder.Name = "Notifications"
notifyHolder.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1)
notifyHolder.Position = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, -16)
notifyHolder.Size = UDim2.new(0, 300, 1, -32)
notifyHolder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
notifyHolder.Active = false
notifyHolder.ZIndex = 80
notifyHolder.Parent = screenGui
do
    local nl = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    nl.Padding = UDim.new(0, 8)
    nl.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    nl.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Bottom
    nl.Parent = notifyHolder
end

local NOTIFY_TYPES = {
    info    = { icon = "info",           color = function() return P.Accent end },
    success = { icon = "circle-check",   color = function() return P.Good end },
    warning = { icon = "alert-triangle", color = function() return Color3.fromRGB(240, 185, 80) end },
    error   = { icon = "x-circle",       color = function() return P.Bad end },
}
local notifyCounter = 0
local activeToasts = {}

function notify(title, text, kind, duration, opts)
    opts = opts or {}
    kind = tostring(kind or "info"):lower()
    if not NOTIFY_TYPES[kind] then kind = "info" end
    duration = duration or 4
    text = text or ""
    if not LucideReady then
        local t0 = tick()
        while not LucideReady and tick() - t0 < 1.5 do task.wait(0.1) end
    end

    local def = NOTIFY_TYPES[kind]
    local col = def.color()
    notifyCounter = notifyCounter + 1

    local wrapper = Instance.new("Frame")
    wrapper.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    wrapper.LayoutOrder = notifyCounter
    wrapper.Parent = notifyHolder

    local toast = Instance.new("Frame")
    toast.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    toast.Position = UDim2.new(1, 330, 0, 0)
    toast.BackgroundColor3 = P.Bg2
    toast.BackgroundTransparency = 0
    toast.BorderSizePixel = 0
    toast.ZIndex = 81
    toast.Parent = wrapper
    corner(toast, 12)
    glassBorder(toast, 1.2, 0.3)

    local bar = Instance.new("Frame")
    bar.Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 1, -20)
    bar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 10)
    bar.BackgroundColor3 = col
    bar.BorderSizePixel = 0
    bar.ZIndex = 82
    bar.Parent = toast
    corner(bar, 2)

    local ic = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    ic.Size = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 18)
    ic.Position = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 10)
    ic.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    ic.ZIndex = 82
    ic.Parent = toast
    local hasIcon = LucideReady and setIcon(ic, def.icon, col)
    if not hasIcon then ic.Visible = false end
    local tx = hasIcon and 44 or 20

    local textH = 0
    if text ~= "" then
        local ok, sz = pcall(function()
            return TextService:GetTextSize(stripRich(text), 12, Enum.Font.Gotham, Vector2.new(300 - tx - 16, 1000))
        end)
        textH = ok and sz.Y or 14
    end
    local h = 10 + 18 + (textH > 0 and (textH + 4) or 0) + 16
    wrapper.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, h)

    local tl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    tl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -(tx + 64), 0, 18)
    tl.Position = UDim2.new(0, tx, 0, 10)
    tl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    tl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    tl.TextSize = 13
    tl.TextColor3 = P.Text
    tl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    tl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
    tl.ZIndex = 82
    tl.Parent = toast
    -- title gets a gradient by default (white -> the toast colour); opts.Gradient = { c1, c2, ... } overrides it
    richSet(tl, title or "Notification", opts.Gradient, opts.Animate)

    if textH > 0 then
        local bl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        bl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -(tx + 16), 0, textH)
        bl.Name = "Body"
        bl.Position = UDim2.new(0, tx, 0, 32)
        bl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        bl.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        bl.TextSize = 12
        bl.TextColor3 = P.Muted
        bl.TextWrapped = true
        bl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        bl.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
        bl.ZIndex = 82
        bl.Parent = toast
        richSet(bl, text)
    end

    local x = Instance.new("TextButton")
    x.Size = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 20)
    x.Position = UDim2.new(1, -26, 0, 7)
    x.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    x.Text = "×"
    x.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    x.TextSize = 16
    x.TextColor3 = P.Muted
    x.AutoButtonColor = false
    x.ZIndex = 83
    x.Parent = toast

    -- clean timer: a thin bar that drains along the bottom of the toast
    local track = Instance.new("Frame")
    track.Name = "Track"
    track.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 3)
    track.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 1, -9)
    track.BackgroundColor3 = P.Line
    track.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
    track.BorderSizePixel = 0
    track.ZIndex = 82
    track.Parent = toast
    corner(track, 2)
    local fill = Instance.new("Frame")
    fill.Name = "Fill"
    fill.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    fill.BackgroundColor3 = col
    fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
    fill.ZIndex = 83
    fill.Parent = track
    corner(fill, 2)
    -- seconds left, next to the close button
    local timeLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    timeLbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, 36, 0, 14)
    timeLbl.Position = UDim2.new(1, -62, 0, 12)
    timeLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    timeLbl.Text = string.format("%.1fs", duration)
    timeLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    timeLbl.TextSize = 10
    timeLbl.TextColor3 = col
    timeLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
    timeLbl.ZIndex = 83
    timeLbl.Parent = toast

    local gone = false
    local function dismiss()
        if gone then return end
        gone = true
        local i = table.find(activeToasts, dismiss)
        if i then table.remove(activeToasts, i) end
        local t = TweenService:Create(toast, TweenInfo.new(0.32, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In),
            { Position = UDim2.new(1, 330, 0, 0) })
        t:Play()
        t.Completed:Wait()
        local t2 = TweenService:Create(wrapper, TweenInfo.new(0.18), { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0) })
        t2:Play()
        t2.Completed:Wait()
        wrapper:Destroy()
    end
    x.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() task.spawn(dismiss) end)

    table.insert(activeToasts, dismiss)
    while #activeToasts > 5 do
        local oldest = table.remove(activeToasts, 1)
        task.spawn(oldest)
    end

    -- smooth slide in, then the accent bar, icon and text settle in right behind it
    local QO = Enum.EasingStyle.Quint
    local bodyLbl = toast:FindFirstChild("Body")
    local fullBar = bar.Size
    bar.Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 0)
    TweenService:Create(toast, TweenInfo.new(0.5, QO, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
        { Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0) }):Play()
    local settle = TweenInfo.new(0.45, QO, Enum.EasingDirection.Out, 0, false, 0.12)
    TweenService:Create(bar, settle, { Size = fullBar }):Play()
    TweenService:Create(fill, TweenInfo.new(duration, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear),
        { Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0) }):Play()
    task.spawn(function()
        local t0 = tick()
        while not gone do
            local left = duration - (tick() - t0)
            if left <= 0 then break end
            timeLbl.Text = string.format("%.1fs", left)
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
        task.spawn(dismiss)
    end)
    return dismiss
end
end -- notifications scope

-- ============================================================
-- DIALOGS (generic: title, text, optional input, up to 3 buttons)
-- ============================================================
do
local dlgOverlay = Instance.new("TextButton")
dlgOverlay.Name = "DialogOverlay"
dlgOverlay.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
dlgOverlay.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
dlgOverlay.BackgroundTransparency = 1
dlgOverlay.Text = ""
dlgOverlay.AutoButtonColor = false
dlgOverlay.Active = true
dlgOverlay.Visible = false
dlgOverlay.ZIndex = 60
dlgOverlay.Parent = screenGui

local dlg = Instance.new("CanvasGroup")
dlg.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
dlg.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
dlg.Size = UDim2.new(0, 330, 0, 200)
dlg.BackgroundColor3 = P.Bg2
dlg.BackgroundTransparency = 0
dlg.BorderSizePixel = 0
dlg.Active = true
dlg.ZIndex = 61
dlg.Parent = dlgOverlay
corner(dlg, 16)
glassBorder(dlg, 1.5, 0.1)
local DLG_SLIDE = 14   -- px the dialog slides up from (no scaling -> text never re-rasterizes)

local dlgIcon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
dlgIcon.Size = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 22)
dlgIcon.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -11, 0, 16)
dlgIcon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
dlgIcon.Visible = false
dlgIcon.ZIndex = 62
dlgIcon.Parent = dlg

local dlgTitle = Instance.new("TextLabel")
dlgTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 22)
dlgTitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
dlgTitle.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
dlgTitle.TextSize = 16
dlgTitle.TextColor3 = P.Text
dlgTitle.ZIndex = 62
dlgTitle.Parent = dlg

local dlgText = Instance.new("TextLabel")
dlgText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
dlgText.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
dlgText.TextSize = 12
dlgText.TextWrapped = true
dlgText.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
dlgText.TextColor3 = P.Muted
dlgText.ZIndex = 62
dlgText.Parent = dlg

local dlgInput = Instance.new("TextBox")
dlgInput.BackgroundColor3 = P.Bg3
dlgInput.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
dlgInput.BorderSizePixel = 0
dlgInput.ClearTextOnFocus = false
dlgInput.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
dlgInput.TextSize = 13
dlgInput.TextColor3 = P.Text
dlgInput.PlaceholderColor3 = P.Muted
dlgInput.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
dlgInput.Text = ""
dlgInput.Visible = false
dlgInput.ZIndex = 62
dlgInput.Parent = dlg
corner(dlgInput, 8)
local dlgInputStroke = stroke(dlgInput, P.Line, 1, 0.5)
do
    local ip = Instance.new("UIPadding", dlgInput)
    ip.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 10)
    ip.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 10)
end
dlgInput.Focused:Connect(function()
    TweenService:Create(dlgInputStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { Color = P.Accent, Transparency = 0.2 }):Play()
end)

local dlgBtnRow = Instance.new("Frame")
dlgBtnRow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
dlgBtnRow.ZIndex = 62
dlgBtnRow.Parent = dlg
do
    local bl = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    bl.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
    bl.Padding = UDim.new(0, 8)
    bl.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    bl.Parent = dlgBtnRow
end

local dlgToken = 0
local dlgButtons = {}
local dlgCurrent, dlgSubmit = nil, nil

local function closeDialog()
    if not dlgOverlay.Visible then return end
    dlgToken = dlgToken + 1
    local my = dlgToken
    dlgCurrent, dlgSubmit = nil, nil
    local fadeOut = TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
    TweenService:Create(dlgOverlay, fadeOut, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()
    TweenService:Create(dlg, fadeOut, { GroupTransparency = 1 }):Play()
    local dStroke = dlg:FindFirstChild("GlassBorder")
    if dStroke then TweenService:Create(dStroke, fadeOut, { Transparency = 1 }):Play() end
    TweenService:Create(dlg, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In),
        { Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, DLG_SLIDE) }):Play()
    task.delay(0.3, function()
        if dlgToken == my then dlgOverlay.Visible = false end
    end)
end

-- opts: Title, Text, Icon, IconColor, Input = { Placeholder, Default },
--       Buttons = { { Text, Style = "primary"|"danger"|"default", Callback = function(inputText) } },
--       Dismissable (default true)
function openDialog(opts)
    opts = opts or {}
    dlgToken = dlgToken + 1
    dlgCurrent = opts
    for _, b in ipairs(dlgButtons) do b:Destroy() end
    dlgButtons = {}

    if not LucideReady then
        local t0 = tick()
        while not LucideReady and tick() - t0 < 1.5 do task.wait(0.1) end
    end

    local hasIcon = opts.Icon and LucideReady and setIcon(dlgIcon, opts.Icon, opts.IconColor or P.Accent)
    dlgIcon.Visible = hasIcon and true or false

    local top = hasIcon and 44 or 20
    richSet(dlgTitle, opts.Title or "Notice")
    dlgTitle.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, top)

    local text = opts.Text or ""
    local textH = 0
    if text ~= "" then
        local ok, sz = pcall(function()
            return TextService:GetTextSize(stripRich(text), 12, Enum.Font.Gotham, Vector2.new(298, 1000))
        end)
        textH = ok and sz.Y or 28
    end
    richSet(dlgText, text)
    dlgText.Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, top + 26)
    dlgText.Size = UDim2.new(1, -32, 0, textH)
    dlgTitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    dlgText.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center

    local y = top + 26 + textH
    if opts.Input then
        y = y + 10
        dlgInput.Visible = true
        dlgInput.PlaceholderText = opts.Input.Placeholder or ""
        dlgInput.Text = opts.Input.Default or ""
        dlgInput.Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, y)
        dlgInput.Size = UDim2.new(1, -32, 0, 32)
        y = y + 32
    else
        dlgInput.Visible = false
    end
    y = y + 16
    dlgBtnRow.Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, y)
    dlgBtnRow.Size = UDim2.new(1, -32, 0, 36)
    dlg.Size = UDim2.new(0, 330, 0, y + 36 + 16)

    local btns = opts.Buttons
    if not btns or #btns == 0 then btns = { { Text = "OK", Style = "primary" } } end
    local n = #btns
    for i, def in ipairs(btns) do
        local style = def.Style or "default"
        local bg = (style == "primary" and P.Accent) or (style == "danger" and Color3.fromRGB(196, 48, 58)) or P.Bg3   -- same red as the Destroy UI "Yes" button
        local fg = (style == "default") and P.Text or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
        b.LayoutOrder = i
        b.Size = UDim2.new(1 / n, -math.ceil(8 * (n - 1) / n), 1, 0)
        b.BackgroundColor3 = bg
        b.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
        b.Text = def.Text or "OK"
        b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        b.TextSize = 13
        b.TextColor3 = fg
        b.BorderSizePixel = 0
        b.AutoButtonColor = false
        b.ZIndex = 63
        if style == "danger" then b:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true) end   -- keep the fixed red across themes
        b.Parent = dlgBtnRow
        corner(b, 10)
        softHover(b, bg, stroke(b, P.Line2, 1, 0.5))
        local function fire()
            local txt = opts.Input and dlgInput.Text or nil
            closeDialog()
            if def.Callback then task.spawn(def.Callback, txt) end
        end
        b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(fire)
        if i == n then dlgSubmit = fire end
        table.insert(dlgButtons, b)
    end

    local dStroke = dlg:FindFirstChild("GlassBorder")
    if not dlgOverlay.Visible then
        dlg.GroupTransparency = 1
        if dStroke then dStroke.Transparency = 1 end
        dlg.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, DLG_SLIDE)
    end
    dlgOverlay.Visible = true
    local fadeIn = TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
    TweenService:Create(dlgOverlay, fadeIn, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.45 }):Play()
    TweenService:Create(dlg, fadeIn, { GroupTransparency = 0 }):Play()
    if dStroke then TweenService:Create(dStroke, fadeIn, { Transparency = 0 }):Play() end
    TweenService:Create(dlg, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
        { Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0) }):Play()
end

dlgOverlay.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    if dlgCurrent and dlgCurrent.Dismissable ~= false then closeDialog() end
end)
dlgInput.FocusLost:Connect(function(enter)
    TweenService:Create(dlgInputStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { Color = P.Line, Transparency = 0.5 }):Play()
    -- Enter only confirms the text box; the dialog is submitted with its buttons
end)
end -- dialogs scope

-- ============================================================
-- DROPDOWN (the option list opens as a scrollable overlay on top of the UI)
-- ============================================================
local DropdownSetters = {}
Cfg.OpenDropdown = nil   -- close function of the dropdown that is open right now (only one at a time; kept on Cfg to save local slots)

-- true when the instance and all of its ancestors (up to the ScreenGui) are visible
function Cfg.effVisible(inst)
    local o = inst
    while o and o ~= screenGui do
        if o:IsA("GuiObject") and not o.Visible then return false end
        o = o.Parent
    end
    return o == screenGui
end

-- searchable (optional): adds a search box on top of the option list
-- extra (optional, last argument):
--   { AllowNone = false,                                  -- multi: the last picked option can't be removed
--     Icons  = { [option] = "lucide-name" },              -- small icon in front of an option
--     Inputs = { [option] = { Default, Min, Max, Callback } } }   -- number box shown inside an option
local function dropdown(parent, label, options, valKey, cb, iconName, multi, searchable, extra)
    extra = extra or {}
    searchable = true   -- every dropdown gets a search bar at the top of its list
    local HEAD_WIDE, HEAD_NARROW, ITEM, BOXW, SEARCH_H = 40, 66, 28, 124, 28
    local MAX_ROWS, PANEL_MINW, PAD, EMPTY_H = 6, 190, 6, 64
    local ACTION_H = 24   -- height of the "Select All / Deselect All" row (multi-select only)
    local BOXH, cmp = 26, false   -- cmp = "Compact Elements" is on
    local row = makeRowCard(parent, HEAD_WIDE)
    local card = RowCards[#RowCards]

    local icon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    icon.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14)
    icon.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 13)
    icon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    icon.ImageColor3 = P.Muted
    icon.Visible = false
    icon.ZIndex = 3
    icon.Parent = row

    local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    lbl.Text = label
    lbl.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    lbl.TextSize = 13
    lbl.TextColor3 = P.Text
    lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    lbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
    lbl.ZIndex = 3
    lbl.Parent = row

    local box = Instance.new("TextButton")
    box.BackgroundColor3 = P.Bg3
    box.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
    box.Text = ""
    box.BorderSizePixel = 0
    box.AutoButtonColor = false
    box.ZIndex = 3
    box.Parent = row
    corner(box, 8)
    local boxStroke = stroke(box, P.Line, 1, 0.5)

    local valLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    valLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 1, 0)
    valLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0)
    valLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    valLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    valLbl.TextSize = 11
    valLbl.TextColor3 = P.Accent
    valLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    valLbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
    valLbl.ZIndex = 4
    valLbl.Parent = box

    local arrow = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    arrow.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    arrow.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14)
    arrow.Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0.5, 0)
    arrow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    arrow.ZIndex = 4
    arrow.Parent = box
    task.spawn(function()
        if not LucideReady then
            local t0 = tick()
            while not LucideReady and tick() - t0 < 30 do task.wait(0.2) end
        end
        if not (LucideReady and setIcon(arrow, "chevron-down", P.Muted)) then
            -- fallback: a small "v" so it never shows as an empty box
            arrow.Visible = true
            arrow.ImageTransparency = 1
            local t = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            t.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
            t.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            t.Text = "v"
            t.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            t.TextSize = 12
            t.TextColor3 = P.Muted
            t.ZIndex = 5
            t.Parent = arrow
        end
    end)

    -- state ------------------------------------------------------------------
    local itemBtns = {}                 -- [option] = { btn = TextButton, lbl = TextLabel }
    local inputVals = {}                -- [option] = current number of an option that has a number box
    for opt, def in pairs(extra.Inputs or {}) do inputVals[opt] = def.Default end
    local visibleCount = #options
    local narrow, iconShown, head = nil, false, HEAD_WIDE
    local isOpen, animT, animDir, stepConn = false, 0, 0, nil
    local catcher, panel, panelScale, panelStroke, scroll, emptyLbl, searchWrap, searchBox
    local close   -- forward declaration

    -- header layout -------------------------------------------------------------
    local function applyLayout()
        local lx = iconShown and 24 or 0
        if narrow then
            head = HEAD_NARROW
            icon.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, cmp and 9 or 11)
            lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, lx, 0, cmp and 2 or 4)
            lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -lx, 0, cmp and 24 or 26)
            box.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, cmp and 28 or 34)
            box.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, BOXH)
        else
            head = HEAD_WIDE
            icon.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, (HEAD_WIDE - 14) / 2)
            lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, lx, 0, 0)
            lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -(lx + BOXW + 10), 0, HEAD_WIDE)
            box.Position = UDim2.new(1, -BOXW, 0, (HEAD_WIDE - BOXH) / 2)
            box.Size = UDim2.new(0, BOXW, 0, BOXH)
        end
        row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, head)
    end

    local function checkNarrow()
        local w = row.AbsoluteSize.X / math.max(MainScale.Scale, 0.01)
        if w <= 0 then return end
        local n = w < 270
        if n ~= narrow then narrow = n; applyLayout() end
    end
    row:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(checkNarrow)
    narrow = false
    applyLayout()
    task.defer(checkNarrow)

    task.spawn(function()
        if not LucideReady then
            local t0 = tick()
            while not LucideReady and tick() - t0 < 30 do task.wait(0.2) end
        end
        if LucideReady and setIcon(icon, iconName, P.Muted) then
            icon.Visible = true
            iconShown = true
            applyLayout()
        end
    end)

    -- selection helpers -----------------------------------------------------------
    local function isSel(opt)
        if multi then return table.find(Values[valKey], opt) ~= nil end
        return opt == Values[valKey]
    end
    local function showText()
        if not multi then
            local cur = tostring(Values[valKey] or "")
            if cur == "" then return "None" end
            if extra.Inputs and extra.Inputs[cur] and inputVals[cur] ~= nil then
                return cur .. " (" .. tostring(inputVals[cur]) .. ")"
            end
            return cur
        end
        local picked = {}
        for _, o in ipairs(options) do if isSel(o) then picked[#picked + 1] = o end end
        if #picked == 0 then return "None" end
        if #picked <= 2 then return table.concat(picked, ", ") end
        return #picked .. " selected"
    end
    local function refreshSel()
        valLbl.Text = showText()
        for opt, it in pairs(itemBtns) do
            local sel = isSel(opt)
            TweenService:Create(it.lbl, TweenInfo.new(0.12), { TextColor3 = sel and P.Accent or P.Text }):Play()
            TweenService:Create(it.btn, TweenInfo.new(0.12), { BackgroundTransparency = sel and 0.55 or 0.85 }):Play()
            if it.box then   -- checkbox: filled + tick when chosen, empty outline when not
                TweenService:Create(it.box, TweenInfo.new(0.14), { BackgroundTransparency = sel and 0 or 1 }):Play()
                TweenService:Create(it.boxStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.14), { Color = sel and P.Accent or P.Line2 }):Play()
                TweenService:Create(it.tick, TweenInfo.new(0.14), { ImageTransparency = sel and 0 or 1 }):Play()
            end
        end
    end

    local function set(v, silent)
        if multi then   -- v is a list of options
            if type(v) ~= "table" then return end
            local clean = {}
            for _, o in ipairs(options) do if table.find(v, o) then clean[#clean + 1] = o end end
            Values[valKey] = clean
            refreshSel()
            if cb and not silent then task.spawn(cb, clean) end
            return
        end
        if not table.find(options, v) then return end
        Values[valKey] = v
        refreshSel()
        if cb and not silent then task.spawn(cb, v) end
    end

    -- overlay panel sizing ----------------------------------------------------------
    local function searchOff()
        local off = searchable and (SEARCH_H + 6) or 0
