        if multi then off = off + ACTION_H + 6 end
        return off
    end
    local function listHeight()
        if #options == 0 or visibleCount == 0 then return (#options == 0) and EMPTY_H or ITEM + 8 end
        return math.min(visibleCount, MAX_ROWS) * ITEM
    end
    local function panelHeight() return PAD * 2 + searchOff() + listHeight() end

    local function layoutPanel()
        if not panel then return end
        local lh = listHeight()
        local hasItems = #options > 0 and visibleCount > 0
        panel.Size = UDim2.new(0, panel.Size.X.Offset, 0, panelHeight())
        scroll.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, searchOff())
        scroll.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, lh)
        scroll.Visible = hasItems
        emptyLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, searchOff())
        emptyLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, lh)
        emptyLbl.Text = (#options == 0) and "Nothing to see here!" or "No results"
        emptyLbl.Visible = not hasItems
    end

    local function applyFilter(q)
        q = tostring(q or ""):lower():gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
        local n = 0
        for _, opt in ipairs(options) do
            local it = itemBtns[opt]
            if it then
                local show = q == "" or tostring(opt):lower():find(q, 1, true) ~= nil
                it.btn.Visible = show
                if show then n = n + 1 end
            end
        end
        visibleCount = n
        layoutPanel()
    end

    -- option buttons ----------------------------------------------------------------------
    local function destroyItems()
        for _, it in pairs(itemBtns) do it.btn:Destroy() end
        itemBtns = {}
    end

    local function buildItems()
        for i, opt in ipairs(options) do
            local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
            b.LayoutOrder = i
            b.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, ITEM - 2)
            b.BackgroundColor3 = P.Bg3
            b.BackgroundTransparency = 0.85
            b.Text = ""
            b.BorderSizePixel = 0
            b.AutoButtonColor = false
            b.ZIndex = 2
            b.Parent = scroll
            corner(b, 7)

            -- checkbox (shows whether the option is chosen)
            local box = Instance.new("Frame")
            box.Name = "Checkbox"
            box.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14)
            box.Position = UDim2.new(0, 9, 0.5, -7)
            box.BackgroundColor3 = P.Accent
            box.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            box.BorderSizePixel = 0
            box.ZIndex = 3
            box.Parent = b
            corner(box, 4)
            local boxStroke = stroke(box, P.Line2, 1.2, 0)
            local checkImg = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
            checkImg.Name = "Tick"
            checkImg.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            checkImg.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
            local boxIconName = extra.Icons and extra.Icons[opt] or "check"
            checkImg.Size = extra.Icons and extra.Icons[opt] and UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 12) or UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 10)
            checkImg.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            checkImg.ImageColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
            checkImg.ImageTransparency = 1
            checkImg.ZIndex = 4
            checkImg.Parent = box
            task.spawn(function()
                local t0 = os.clock()
                while not LucideReady and os.clock() - t0 < 30 do task.wait(0.2) end
                if LucideReady and checkImg.Parent then
                    local was = isSel(opt)
                    local okIcon = setIcon(checkImg, boxIconName, Color3.new(1, 1, 1))
                    if not okIcon and boxIconName ~= "check" then   -- unknown icon name: fall back to the normal tick
                        checkImg.Size = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 10)
                        okIcon = setIcon(checkImg, "check", Color3.new(1, 1, 1))
                    end
                    if okIcon then checkImg.ImageTransparency = was and 0 or 1 end
                end
            end)

            local leftX, rightX = 32, 8
            local inp = extra.Inputs and extra.Inputs[opt]
            if inp then rightX = 8 + 46 + 4 end

            local l = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            l.Position = UDim2.new(0, leftX, 0, 0)
            l.Size = UDim2.new(1, -(leftX + rightX), 1, 0)
            l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            l.Text = tostring(opt)
            l.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
            l.TextSize = 12
            l.TextColor3 = P.Text
            l.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            l.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
            l.ZIndex = 3
            l.Parent = b

            if inp then
                local nb = Instance.new("TextBox")
                nb.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
                nb.Position = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0.5, 0)
                nb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 46, 0, 20)
                nb.BackgroundColor3 = P.Bg4
                nb.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
                nb.BorderSizePixel = 0
                nb.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                nb.TextSize = 11
                nb.TextColor3 = P.Accent
                nb.PlaceholderColor3 = P.Muted
                nb.Text = tostring(inputVals[opt] ~= nil and inputVals[opt] or "")
                nb.ClearTextOnFocus = false
                nb.ZIndex = 4
                nb.Parent = b
                corner(nb, 6)
                nb.FocusLost:Connect(function()
                    local n = tonumber(nb.Text)
                    if n then
                        if inp.Min then n = math.max(n, inp.Min) end
                        if inp.Max then n = math.min(n, inp.Max) end
                        n = math.floor(n + 0.5)
                        nb.Text = tostring(n)
                        inputVals[opt] = n
                        valLbl.Text = showText()
                        if inp.Callback then task.spawn(inp.Callback, n) end
                    else
                        nb.Text = tostring(inputVals[opt] ~= nil and inputVals[opt] or "")
                    end
                end)
            end

            itemBtns[opt] = { btn = b, lbl = l, box = box, boxStroke = boxStroke, tick = checkImg }
            local sel0 = isSel(opt)
            l.TextColor3 = sel0 and P.Accent or P.Text
            b.BackgroundTransparency = sel0 and 0.55 or 0.85
            box.BackgroundTransparency = sel0 and 0 or 1
            boxStroke.Color = sel0 and P.Accent or P.Line2
            checkImg.ImageTransparency = sel0 and 0 or 1

            b.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                if not isSel(opt) then TweenService:Create(b, TweenInfo.new(0.1), { BackgroundTransparency = 0.6 }):Play() end
            end)
            b.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                if not isSel(opt) then TweenService:Create(b, TweenInfo.new(0.1), { BackgroundTransparency = 0.85 }):Play() end
            end)
            b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                if multi then   -- stays open so several options can be ticked
                    local cur = {}
                    for _, o in ipairs(Values[valKey]) do cur[#cur + 1] = o end
                    local at = table.find(cur, opt)
                    if at then
                        if #cur <= 1 and extra.AllowNone == false then return end
                        table.remove(cur, at)
                    else
                        cur[#cur + 1] = opt
                    end
                    set(cur)
                else
                    if opt ~= Values[valKey] then set(opt) end
                    close()
                end
            end)
        end
    end

    -- Select All / Deselect All: affects the options currently visible (so it respects the search filter)
    local function bulkSet(selectAll)
        if not multi then return end
        local cur = {}
        for _, o in ipairs(Values[valKey]) do cur[#cur + 1] = o end
        local firstVisible
        for _, opt in ipairs(options) do
            local it = itemBtns[opt]
            if it and it.btn.Visible then
                firstVisible = firstVisible or opt
                local at = table.find(cur, opt)
                if selectAll then
                    if not at then cur[#cur + 1] = opt end
                elseif at then
                    table.remove(cur, at)
                end
            end
        end
        if #cur == 0 and extra.AllowNone == false then   -- this dropdown must keep at least one option
            cur[1] = firstVisible or options[1]
            if cur[1] == nil then return end
        end
        set(cur)
    end

    -- the floating panel (built the first time the dropdown is opened) -------------------------
    local function ensurePanel()
        if panel then return end

        catcher = Instance.new("TextButton")   -- invisible full-screen layer: clicking outside closes the list
        catcher.Name = "DropdownCatcher"
        catcher.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        catcher.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        catcher.Text = ""
        catcher.AutoButtonColor = false
        catcher.Active = true
        catcher.Visible = false
        catcher.ZIndex = 40
        catcher.Parent = screenGui
        catcher.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() close() end)

        panel = Instance.new("CanvasGroup")
        panel.Name = "DropdownPanel"
        panel.BackgroundColor3 = P.Bg2
        panel.BackgroundTransparency = 0
        panel.BorderSizePixel = 0
        panel.Active = true
        panel.Visible = false
        panel.GroupTransparency = 1
        panel.ZIndex = 41
        panel.Size = UDim2.new(0, PANEL_MINW, 0, 40)
        panel.Parent = screenGui
        corner(panel, 10)
        panelStroke = stroke(panel, P.Line2, 1, 0.2)
        panelScale = Instance.new("UIScale")
        panelScale.Scale = MainScale.Scale
        panelScale.Parent = panel
        local pp = Instance.new("UIPadding")
        pp.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, PAD)
        pp.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, PAD)
        pp.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, PAD)
        pp.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, PAD)
        pp.Parent = panel

        if searchable then
            searchWrap = Instance.new("Frame")
            searchWrap.Name = "DropdownSearch"
            searchWrap.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, SEARCH_H)
            searchWrap.BackgroundColor3 = P.Bg3
            searchWrap.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
            searchWrap.BorderSizePixel = 0
            searchWrap.ZIndex = 2
            searchWrap.Parent = panel
            corner(searchWrap, 8)
            local sStroke = glassBorder(searchWrap, 1.2, 0.35)   -- same glass outline as the main search bar / tabs
            sStroke.Transparency = 0.5

            local sIcon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
            sIcon.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14)
            sIcon.Position = UDim2.new(0, 9, 0.5, -7)
            sIcon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            sIcon.ImageColor3 = P.Muted
            sIcon.Visible = false
            sIcon.ZIndex = 3
            sIcon.Parent = searchWrap
            task.spawn(function()
                local t0 = tick()
                while not LucideReady and tick() - t0 < 30 do task.wait(0.2) end
                if LucideReady and setIcon(sIcon, "search", P.Muted) then sIcon.Visible = true end
            end)

            searchBox = Instance.new("TextBox")
            searchBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, -36, 1, 0)
            searchBox.Position = UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 0)
            searchBox.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            searchBox.Text = ""
            searchBox.PlaceholderText = "Search..."
            searchBox.PlaceholderColor3 = P.Muted
            searchBox.TextColor3 = P.Text
            searchBox.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
            searchBox.TextSize = 12
            searchBox.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            searchBox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
            searchBox.ZIndex = 3
            searchBox.Parent = searchWrap
            searchBox.Focused:Connect(function()
                TweenService:Create(sStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { Color = P.Accent, Transparency = 0 }):Play()
            end)
            searchBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
                TweenService:Create(sStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { Color = rimColor(), Transparency = 0.5 }):Play()
            end)
            searchBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function() applyFilter(searchBox.Text) end)

            if multi then   -- horizontal stack right under the search bar
                local actions = Instance.new("Frame")
                actions.Name = "DropdownActions"
                actions.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, SEARCH_H + 6)
                actions.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, ACTION_H)
                actions.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                actions.ZIndex = 2
                actions.Parent = panel

                local function actionBtn(text, xScale, xOff, selectAll)
                    local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
                    b.Name = selectAll and "SelectAll" or "DeselectAll"
                    b.Position = UDim2.new(xScale, xOff, 0, 0)
                    b.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -3, 1, 0)
                    b.BackgroundColor3 = P.Bg3
                    b.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
                    b.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    b.AutoButtonColor = false
                    b.Text = text
                    b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                    b.TextSize = 11
                    b.TextColor3 = P.Text
                    b.ZIndex = 3
                    b.Parent = actions
                    corner(b, 7)
                    stroke(b, P.Line2, 1, 0.55)
                    b.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                        TweenService:Create(b, TweenInfo.new(0.1), { BackgroundTransparency = 0.1, TextColor3 = P.Accent }):Play()
                    end)
                    b.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                        TweenService:Create(b, TweenInfo.new(0.1), { BackgroundTransparency = 0.3, TextColor3 = P.Text }):Play()
                    end)
                    b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() bulkSet(selectAll) end)
                end
                actionBtn("Select All", 0, 0, true)
                actionBtn("Deselect All", 0.5, 3, false)
            end
        end

        scroll = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
        scroll.Name = "Options"
        scroll.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        scroll.BorderSizePixel = 0
        scroll.ScrollBarThickness = 0
        scroll.ScrollBarImageColor3 = P.Line2
        scroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        scroll.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        scroll.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y
        scroll.ElasticBehavior = Enum.ElasticBehavior.Never   -- no overscroll: it stops at the first / last option
        scroll.ZIndex = 2
        scroll.Parent = panel
        local ll = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        ll.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        ll.Padding = UDim.new(0, 2)
        ll.Parent = scroll
        local sp = Instance.new("UIPadding")
        sp.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 5)
        sp.Parent = scroll

        emptyLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")   -- "Nothing to see here!" / "No results"
        emptyLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        emptyLbl.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        emptyLbl.TextSize = 12
        emptyLbl.TextColor3 = P.Muted
        emptyLbl.TextTransparency = 0.25
        emptyLbl.Visible = false
        emptyLbl.ZIndex = 2
        emptyLbl.Parent = panel

        buildItems()
        layoutPanel()
    end

    -- positions the panel under (or above) the box and runs the fade animation ------------------------
    -- Deterministic position (no per-frame feedback, so it cannot jitter): the box / menu coordinates are screen pixels,
    -- and a zero-size probe frame at (0,0) tells us the (constant) offset between screen pixels and this gui's space.
    local probe
    local function place()
        local sc = math.max(MainScale.Scale, 0.01)
        local bp, bs = box.AbsolutePosition, box.AbsoluteSize
        local vp = screenGui.AbsoluteSize
        panelScale.Scale = sc
        local wU = math.max(bs.X / sc, PANEL_MINW)
        local hU = panel.Size.Y.Offset
        local wPx, hPx = wU * sc, hU * sc
        local x = math.clamp(bp.X + bs.X - wPx, 4, math.max(4, vp.X - wPx - 4))   -- right edge lines up with the box
        -- vertical room is measured against the MAIN UI (not the whole screen): if the list would hang over
        -- the bottom edge of the menu it opens ABOVE its box instead
        local mp, ms = Main.AbsolutePosition, Main.AbsoluteSize
        local top = math.max(4, mp.Y + 4)
        local bottom = math.min(vp.Y - 4, mp.Y + ms.Y - 4)
        if bottom - top < 40 then top, bottom = 4, vp.Y - 4 end   -- menu not measurable: fall back to the screen
        local y, dir = bp.Y + bs.Y + 4 * sc, 1
        if y + hPx > bottom then   -- no room below the box inside the menu
            local above = bp.Y - 4 * sc - hPx
            local spaceBelow = bottom - y
            local spaceAbove = bp.Y - 4 * sc - top
            if above >= top or spaceAbove > spaceBelow then   -- fits above, or at least there is more room up there
                y, dir = math.max(top, above), -1
            else
                y = math.max(top, bottom - hPx)
            end
        end
        local opening = animDir >= 0
        local ease = opening and (animT * (2 - animT)) or animT   -- opening eases out; closing is one even fade
        panel.Size = UDim2.new(0, wU, 0, hU)
        local want = Vector2.new(x, y - (opening and (1 - ease) * 6 * sc * dir or 0))   -- no slide while closing
        if not probe or not probe.Parent then
            probe = Instance.new("Frame")
            probe.Name = "DropdownProbe"
            probe.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
            probe.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
            probe.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            probe.BorderSizePixel = 0
            probe.Parent = screenGui
        end
        local inset = probe.AbsolutePosition
        -- whole pixels only: sub-pixel positions shimmer while the box moves
        panel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(math.floor(want.X - inset.X + 0.5), math.floor(want.Y - inset.Y + 0.5))
        panel.GroupTransparency = 1 - ease
        if panelStroke then panelStroke.Transparency = 1 - (1 - 0.2) * ease end   -- outline fades with everything else
    end

    local function step(dt)
        if isOpen then
            local ok = Main.Visible and Cfg.effVisible(box)
            local sf = ok and box:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("ScrollingFrame") or nil
            if sf then   -- the box was scrolled out of its page
                local c = box.AbsolutePosition + box.AbsoluteSize / 2
                local sp, ss = sf.AbsolutePosition, sf.AbsoluteSize
                ok = c.X >= sp.X and c.X <= sp.X + ss.X and c.Y >= sp.Y and c.Y <= sp.Y + ss.Y
            end
            if not ok then close() end
        end
        animT = math.clamp(animT + animDir * dt / 0.16, 0, 1)
        place()
        if animDir < 0 and animT <= 0 then
            panel.Visible = false
            if stepConn then stepConn:Disconnect(); stepConn = nil end
        end
    end

    local function open()
        if isOpen then return end
        if Cfg.OpenDropdown then Cfg.OpenDropdown() end
        ensurePanel()
        if searchBox and searchBox.Text ~= "" then searchBox.Text = "" else applyFilter("") end
        isOpen = true
        Cfg.OpenDropdown = close
        catcher.Visible = true
        panel.Visible = true
        layoutPanel()
        animDir = 1
        place()
        if not stepConn then stepConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(step) end
        TweenService:Create(arrow, TweenInfo.new(0.2), { Rotation = 180 }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(boxStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { Color = P.Accent, Transparency = 0.2 }):Play()
        -- bring the picked option into view (single select)
        if not multi then
            local idx = table.find(options, Values[valKey])
            if idx and idx > MAX_ROWS then
                task.defer(function()
                    if scroll then scroll.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, (idx - 2) * ITEM) end
                end)
            elseif scroll then
                scroll.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0)
            end
        elseif scroll then
            scroll.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0)
        end
    end

    close = function()
        if not isOpen then return end
        isOpen = false
        if Cfg.OpenDropdown == close then Cfg.OpenDropdown = nil end
        if catcher then catcher.Visible = false end
        if searchBox then searchBox:ReleaseFocus() end
        animDir = -1
        if not stepConn and panel then stepConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(step) end
        TweenService:Create(arrow, TweenInfo.new(0.2), { Rotation = 0 }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(boxStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { Color = P.Line, Transparency = 0.5 }):Play()
    end

    box.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if isOpen then close() else open() end
    end)

    row.Destroying:Connect(function()
        if Cfg.OpenDropdown == close then Cfg.OpenDropdown = nil end
        if stepConn then stepConn:Disconnect(); stepConn = nil end
        if catcher then catcher:Destroy() end
        if panel then panel:Destroy() end
        if probe then probe:Destroy() end
    end)

    -- replaces the option list at runtime (e.g. the saved-config list)
    local function setOptions(newOpts)
        options = newOpts or {}
        destroyItems()
        visibleCount = #options
        if multi then
            local keep = {}
            for _, o in ipairs(options) do if table.find(Values[valKey], o) then keep[#keep + 1] = o end end
            Values[valKey] = keep
        elseif not table.find(options, Values[valKey]) then
            Values[valKey] = options[1] or ""
        end
        refreshSel()
        if panel then
            buildItems()
            if searchBox and searchBox.Text ~= "" then applyFilter(searchBox.Text) else layoutPanel() end
        end
    end

    -- compact mode: shorter header / box / items so it matches the other compact rows
    CompactItems[#CompactItems + 1] = function(on, animate)
        cmp = on
        HEAD_WIDE, HEAD_NARROW = on and 32 or 40, on and 54 or 66
        ITEM, BOXH = on and 24 or 28, on and 22 or 26
        for _, it in pairs(itemBtns) do it.btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, ITEM - 2) end
        applyLayout()
        layoutPanel()
        local ti = TweenInfo.new(animate and 0.2 or 0)
        TweenService:Create(row, ti, { BackgroundTransparency = on and 1 or 0.35 }):Play()
        if card then TweenService:Create(card.Stroke, ti, { Transparency = on and 1 or 0.75 }):Play() end
    end

    if multi then
        if type(Values[valKey]) ~= "table" then Values[valKey] = {} end
        set(Values[valKey], true)
    elseif not table.find(options, Values[valKey]) then
        Values[valKey] = options[1] or ""
    end
    refreshSel()
    DropdownSetters[valKey] = function(v) set(v, true) end

    return { Set = set, Instance = row, SetOptions = setOptions }
end

-- ============================================================
-- PROGRESS BAR
-- ============================================================
local function progressBar(parent, label, iconName)
    local row = makeRowCard(parent, 54)

    local icon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    icon.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14)
    icon.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 11)
    icon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    icon.ImageColor3 = P.Muted
    icon.Visible = false
    icon.ZIndex = 3
    icon.Parent = row

    local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -60, 0, 20)
    lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 8)
    lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    lbl.Text = label
    lbl.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    lbl.TextSize = 12
    lbl.TextColor3 = P.Text
    lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    lbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
    lbl.ZIndex = 3
    lbl.Parent = row

    local pct = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    pct.Size = UDim2.new(0, 52, 0, 20)
    pct.Position = UDim2.new(1, -52, 0, 8)
    pct.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    pct.Text = "0%"
    pct.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    pct.TextSize = 11
    pct.TextColor3 = P.Accent
    pct.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
    pct.ZIndex = 3
    pct.Parent = row

    task.spawn(function()
        if not LucideReady then
            local t0 = tick()
            while not LucideReady and tick() - t0 < 30 do task.wait(0.2) end
        end
        if LucideReady and setIcon(icon, iconName, P.Muted) then
            icon.Visible = true
            lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 24, 0, 8)
            lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -84, 0, 20)
        end
    end)

    local track = Instance.new("Frame")
    track.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 8)
    track.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 36)
    track.BackgroundColor3 = P.Bg3
    track.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
    track.BorderSizePixel = 0
    track.ZIndex = 3
    track.Parent = row
    corner(track, 4)

    local fill = Instance.new("Frame")
    fill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
    fill.BackgroundColor3 = P.Accent
    fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
    fill.ZIndex = 4
    fill.Parent = track
    corner(fill, 4)
    gradient(fill, P.Accent, P.Accent2)

    local api = { Instance = row }
    function api.Set(frac, text, animate)
        frac = math.clamp(frac or 0, 0, 1)
        pct.Text = text or (math.floor(frac * 100 + 0.5) .. "%")
        if animate == false then
            fill.Size = UDim2.new(frac, 0, 1, 0)
        else
            TweenService:Create(fill, TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), { Size = UDim2.new(frac, 0, 1, 0) }):Play()
        end
    end
    return api
end

-- simple "label ........ value" row
local function infoRow(parent, label, value)
    local row = makeRowCard(parent, 34, 28)
    local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    lbl.Size = UDim2.new(0.45, 0, 1, 0)
    lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    lbl.Text = label
    lbl.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    lbl.TextSize = 12
    lbl.TextColor3 = P.Text
    lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    lbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
    lbl.ZIndex = 3
    lbl.Parent = row
    local v = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    v.Size = UDim2.new(0.55, 0, 1, 0)
    v.Position = UDim2.new(0.45, 0, 0, 0)
    v.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    v.Name = "Value"
    v.Text = tostring(value)
    v.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    v.TextSize = 11
    v.TextColor3 = P.Muted
    v.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
    v.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
    v.ZIndex = 3
    v.Parent = row
    return row
end


-- ============================================================
-- INPUT + PARAGRAPH WIDGETS
-- (kept on one global table so they don't use up any more local slots)
--   UIKit.input(parent, label, default, placeholder, cb, valKey, iconName, opts)
--       opts = { Numeric = bool, MaxLength = n, ClearOnFocus = bool }
--       cb(text, enterPressed) fires when the box loses focus
--   UIKit.paragraph(parent, title, text, opts)
--       opts = { Image = <lucide name | asset id | rbxassetid://>,   -- small image next to the title
--                Thumbnail = <asset id | rbxassetid://>,             -- banner image above the text
--                ThumbnailHeight = 110,
--                Viewport = <Model | BasePart>,                      -- 3D preview (cloned, centred, slowly spinning)
--                ViewportSpin = false,                               -- optional: stop the rotation
--                Placeholder = true | "text" }                       -- shown while the thumbnail/viewport loads / if there is none
--       api.SetViewport(instance | nil)
--       VIEWPORT GROUP: two previews side by side, separated by a gap
--           opts.Viewports = { <Model | BasePart | false>, <Model | BasePart | false> }   (false = placeholder)
--           opts.ViewportLabels = { "Left", "Right" }   -- optional small captions
--           opts.ViewportGap = 8                         -- optional space between the two (pixels)
--       api.SetViewports({ a, b } | nil)
-- ============================================================
UIKit = { InputSetters = {} }
UIKit.setSectionDesc = setSectionDesc   -- UIKit.setSectionDesc(section, "new text")

function UIKit.assetId(v)
    if type(v) == "number" then return "rbxassetid://" .. v end
    if type(v) ~= "string" or v == "" then return nil end
    if v:match("^rbxassetid://") or v:match("^rbxasset://") or v:match("^rbxthumb://") or v:match("^https?://") then return v end
    if v:match("^%d+$") then return "rbxassetid://" .. v end
    return nil
end

-- sets an ImageLabel from an asset id/url, or from a Lucide icon name
function UIKit.setImage(img, src, color)
    local id = UIKit.assetId(src)
    if id then
        img.Image = id
        img.ImageRectSize = Vector2.zero
        img.ImageRectOffset = Vector2.zero
        img.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
        img.ImageColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        img.ImageTransparency = 0
        img.Visible = true
        return
    end
    if type(src) == "string" and src ~= "" then
        task.spawn(function()
            local t0 = tick()
            while not LucideReady and tick() - t0 < 30 do task.wait(0.2) end
            if LucideReady then setIcon(img, src, color or P.Muted) end
        end)
    end
end

function UIKit.input(parent, label, default, placeholder, cb, valKey, iconName, opts)
    opts = opts or {}
    local HEAD_WIDE, HEAD_NARROW, BOXW = 40, 66, 160
    local row = makeRowCard(parent, HEAD_WIDE)

    local icon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    icon.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14)
    icon.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 13)
    icon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    icon.ImageColor3 = P.Muted
    icon.Visible = false
    icon.ZIndex = 3
    icon.Parent = row

    local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    lbl.Text = label
    lbl.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    lbl.TextSize = 13
    lbl.TextColor3 = P.Text
    lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    lbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
    lbl.ZIndex = 3
    lbl.Parent = row

    local box = Instance.new("Frame")
    box.BackgroundColor3 = P.Bg3
    box.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
    box.BorderSizePixel = 0
    box.ZIndex = 3
    box.Parent = row
    corner(box, 8)
    local boxStroke = stroke(box, P.Line, 1, 0.5)

    local tb = Instance.new("TextBox")
    tb.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, 0)
    tb.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0)
    tb.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    tb.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    tb.TextSize = 12
    tb.TextColor3 = P.Text
    tb.PlaceholderText = placeholder or ""
    tb.PlaceholderColor3 = P.Muted
    tb.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    tb.ClearTextOnFocus = opts.ClearOnFocus == true
    tb.ClipsDescendants = true
    tb.ZIndex = 4
    tb.Parent = box

    local narrow, iconShown = nil, false
    local function applyLayout()
        local lx = iconShown and 24 or 0
        if narrow then
            icon.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 11)
            lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, lx, 0, 4)
            lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -lx, 0, 26)
            box.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 34)
            box.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26)
            row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, HEAD_NARROW)
        else
            icon.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 13)
            lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, lx, 0, 0)
            lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -(lx + BOXW + 10), 0, HEAD_WIDE)
            box.Position = UDim2.new(1, -BOXW, 0, 7)
            box.Size = UDim2.new(0, BOXW, 0, 26)
            row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, HEAD_WIDE)
        end
    end
    local function checkNarrow()
        local w = row.AbsoluteSize.X / math.max(MainScale.Scale, 0.01)
        if w <= 0 then return end
        local n = w < 270
        if n ~= narrow then narrow = n; applyLayout() end
    end
    row:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(checkNarrow)
    narrow = false
    applyLayout()
    task.defer(checkNarrow)

    task.spawn(function()
        if not iconName then return end
        local t0 = tick()
        while not LucideReady and tick() - t0 < 30 do task.wait(0.2) end
        if LucideReady and setIcon(icon, iconName, P.Muted) then
            icon.Visible = true
            iconShown = true
            applyLayout()
        end
    end)

    local function clean(t)
        t = tostring(t or "")
        if opts.Numeric then t = t:gsub("[^%d%.%-]", "") end
        if opts.MaxLength then t = t:sub(1, opts.MaxLength) end
        return t
    end

    if valKey then
        if type(Values[valKey]) ~= "string" then Values[valKey] = clean(default) end
        tb.Text = Values[valKey]
    else
        tb.Text = clean(default)
    end

    tb:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
        local c = clean(tb.Text)
        if c ~= tb.Text then tb.Text = c end
    end)
    tb.Focused:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(boxStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { Color = P.Accent, Transparency = 0.2 }):Play()
    end)
    tb.FocusLost:Connect(function(enter)
        TweenService:Create(boxStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { Color = P.Line, Transparency = 0.5 }):Play()
        local v = clean(tb.Text)
        if valKey then Values[valKey] = v end
        if cb then task.spawn(cb, v, enter) end
    end)

    local api = { Instance = row, TextBox = tb }
    function api.Get() return tb.Text end
    function api.Set(v, silent)
        local c = clean(v)
        tb.Text = c
        if valKey then Values[valKey] = c end
        if cb and not silent then task.spawn(cb, c, false) end
    end
    function api.SetPlaceholder(t) tb.PlaceholderText = tostring(t or "") end
    if valKey then UIKit.InputSetters[valKey] = function(v) api.Set(v, true) end end
    return api
end

-- one spinning 3D preview cell (used by paragraph viewport groups)
--   opts = { Placeholder = "text", Label = "caption", Spin = false }     api: Instance, Set(src), SetLabel(t), Destroy()
function UIKit.viewportCell(parent, src, opts)
    opts = opts or {}
    local cell = Instance.new("Frame")
    cell.Name = "ViewportCell"
    cell.BackgroundColor3 = P.Bg3
    cell.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
    cell.BorderSizePixel = 0
    cell.ClipsDescendants = true
    cell.ZIndex = 3
    cell.Parent = parent
    corner(cell, 9)
    stroke(cell, P.Line, 1, 0.6)

    local ph = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    ph.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    ph.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
    ph.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 14)
    ph.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    ph.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    ph.TextSize = 10
    ph.TextColor3 = P.Muted
    ph.TextWrapped = true
    ph.ZIndex = 4
    ph.Parent = cell

    local vp = Instance.new("ViewportFrame")
    vp.Name = "Viewport"
    vp.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    vp.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    vp.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 160)
    vp.LightColor = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    vp.LightDirection = Vector3.new(-1, -1.5, -1)
    vp.Visible = false
    vp.ZIndex = 5
    vp.Parent = cell
    local cam = Instance.new("Camera")
    cam.FieldOfView = 50
    cam.Parent = vp
    vp.CurrentCamera = cam

    local caption = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    caption.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)
    caption.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -5)
    caption.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 14)
    caption.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    caption.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    caption.TextSize = 10
    caption.TextColor3 = P.Text
    caption.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
    caption.RichText = true
    caption.Visible = false
    caption.ZIndex = 7
    caption.Parent = cell

    local token, conn, model = 0, nil, nil
    local function clear()
        token = token + 1
        if conn then conn:Disconnect(); conn = nil end
        if model then model:Destroy(); model = nil end
        vp.Visible = false
    end

    local api = { Instance = cell }
    function api.SetLabel(t)
        caption.Text = tostring(t or "")
        caption.Visible = caption.Text ~= ""
    end
    function api.Set(newSrc)
        clear()
        if typeof(newSrc) ~= "Instance" then
            ph.Text = type(opts.Placeholder) == "string" and opts.Placeholder or "No model"
            ph.Visible = true
            return
        end
        local clone
        if not pcall(function() clone = newSrc:Clone() end) or not clone then
            ph.Text = "Model unavailable"; ph.Visible = true; return
        end
        local m = clone
        if not clone:IsA("Model") then
            m = Instance.new("Model")
            clone.Parent = m
        end
        for _, d in ipairs(m:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("BasePart") then d.Anchored = true end
            if d:IsA("Script") or d:IsA("LocalScript") then d:Destroy() end
        end
        m.Parent = vp
        model = m
        local okBox, cf, size = pcall(function() return m:GetBoundingBox() end)
        if not okBox then ph.Text = "Model unavailable"; ph.Visible = true; return end
        local center = cf.Position
        local radius = math.max(size.Magnitude / 2, 0.5)
        local dist = radius / math.tan(math.rad(cam.FieldOfView / 2)) * 1.05
        local angle = 0
        local function place()
            local dir = Vector3.new(math.sin(angle), 0.25, math.cos(angle)).Unit
            cam.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(center + dir * dist, center)
        end
        place()
        ph.Visible = false
        vp.Visible = true
        if opts.Spin ~= false then
            local my = token
            conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
                if my ~= token or not vp:IsDescendantOf(game) then
                    if conn then conn:Disconnect(); conn = nil end
                    return
                end
                angle = angle + dt * 0.8
                place()
            end)
        end
    end
    function api.Destroy()
        clear()
        cell:Destroy()
    end
    api.SetLabel(opts.Label)
    api.Set(src)
    return api
end

function UIKit.paragraph(parent, title, text, opts)
    opts = opts or {}
    local row = makeRowCard(parent, 40)
    row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
    row.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    local pad = row:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIPadding")
    if pad then pad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 10); pad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 12) end
    local list = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    list.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    list.Padding = UDim.new(0, 8)
    list.Parent = row

    -- header: small image + title
    local header = Instance.new("Frame")
    header.LayoutOrder = 2
    header.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20)
    header.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    header.Parent = row
    local img = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    img.Size = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 18)
    img.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -9)
    img.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    img.ImageColor3 = P.Accent
    img.Visible = false
    img.ZIndex = 3
    img.Parent = header
    local imgCorner = corner(img, 4)
    local imgSize = tonumber(opts.ImageSize) or 18   -- opts.ImageSize makes the small image bigger (e.g. 56)
    img.Size = UDim2.new(0, imgSize, 0, imgSize)
    img.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -imgSize / 2)
    if imgSize > 24 then imgCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, math.floor(imgSize / 4)) end
    local titleLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    titleLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    titleLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    titleLbl.TextSize = 13
    titleLbl.TextColor3 = P.Text
    titleLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    titleLbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None
    titleLbl.TextWrapped = true   -- long titles wrap onto more lines instead of ending in "..."
    titleLbl.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y   -- grows downward, so Auto Text Size can enlarge it instead of the fit pass shrinking it
    titleLbl.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
    titleLbl.RichText = true
    titleLbl.ZIndex = 3
    titleLbl.Parent = header

    -- body text (grows with its content)
    local body = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    body.LayoutOrder = 3
    body.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
    body.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    body.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    body.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    body.TextSize = 12
    body.TextColor3 = P.Muted
    body.TextWrapped = true
    body.RichText = true
    body.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    body.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
    body.ZIndex = 3
    body.Parent = row

    -- thumbnail (with placeholder underneath it)
    local thumbWrap, thumb, phIcon, phText
    local vp, vpCam, vpConn, vpModel
    local vpToken = 0
    local thumbToken = 0
    local groupFrame, groupCells = nil, {}
    local clearGroup   -- defined below (hides the two-viewport group)
    local function ensureThumb()
        if thumbWrap then return end
        thumbWrap = Instance.new("Frame")
        thumbWrap.Name = "Thumbnail"
        thumbWrap.LayoutOrder = 1
        thumbWrap.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, opts.ThumbnailHeight or 110)
        thumbWrap.BackgroundColor3 = P.Bg3
        thumbWrap.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
        thumbWrap.BorderSizePixel = 0
        thumbWrap.ClipsDescendants = true
        thumbWrap.ZIndex = 3
        thumbWrap.Parent = row
        if opts.FullWidthThumbnail then   -- banner reaches the card's left/right/top edges (ignores the card padding)
            local H = opts.ThumbnailHeight or 110
            local holder = Instance.new("Frame")
            holder.Name = "ThumbnailHolder"
            holder.LayoutOrder = 1
            holder.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, H - 10)
            holder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            holder.BorderSizePixel = 0
            holder.Parent = row
            thumbWrap.LayoutOrder = 0
            thumbWrap.Position = UDim2.new(0, -12, 0, -10)
            thumbWrap.Size = UDim2.new(1, 24, 0, H)
            thumbWrap.Parent = holder
            thumbWrap:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function() holder.Visible = thumbWrap.Visible end)
        end
        corner(thumbWrap, 9)
        if opts.FadeBottom then
            -- no box behind or around the banner: the picture itself fades out towards the bottom
            -- and blends into the card
            thumbWrap.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        else
            stroke(thumbWrap, P.Line, 1, 0.6)
        end

        phIcon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
        phIcon.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        phIcon.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, -9)
        phIcon.Size = UDim2.new(0, 24, 0, 24)
        phIcon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        phIcon.ImageColor3 = P.Muted
        phIcon.ImageTransparency = 0.3
        phIcon.Visible = false
        phIcon.ZIndex = 4
        phIcon.Parent = thumbWrap
        task.spawn(function()
            local t0 = tick()
            while not LucideReady and tick() - t0 < 30 do task.wait(0.2) end
            if LucideReady then setIcon(phIcon, "image", P.Muted); phIcon.ImageTransparency = 0.3 end
        end)

        phText = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        phText.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        phText.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 16)
        phText.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 14)
        phText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        phText.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        phText.TextSize = 10
        phText.TextColor3 = P.Muted
        phText.ZIndex = 4
        phText.Parent = thumbWrap

        thumb = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
        thumb.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        thumb.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        thumb.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
        thumb.ImageTransparency = 1
        thumb.ZIndex = 5
        thumb.Parent = thumbWrap
        corner(thumb, 9)
        if opts.FadeBottom then
            local fade = Instance.new("UIGradient")
            fade.Rotation = 90   -- top to bottom
            fade.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.35, 0.05),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.7, 0.55),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
            })
            fade.Parent = thumb
        end

        vp = Instance.new("ViewportFrame")
        vp.Name = "Viewport"
        vp.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        vp.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        vp.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 160)
        vp.LightColor = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        vp.LightDirection = Vector3.new(-1, -1.5, -1)
        vp.Visible = false
        vp.ZIndex = 6
        vp.Parent = thumbWrap
        vpCam = Instance.new("Camera")
        vpCam.FieldOfView = 50
        vpCam.Parent = vp
        vp.CurrentCamera = vpCam
    end

    local function clearViewport()
        vpToken = vpToken + 1
        if vpConn then vpConn:Disconnect(); vpConn = nil end
        if vpModel then vpModel:Destroy(); vpModel = nil end
        if vp then vp.Visible = false end
    end

    local function placeholderText()
        return type(opts.Placeholder) == "string" and opts.Placeholder or "Image placeholder"
    end

    local function setThumbnail(src)
        opts.Thumbnail = src
        if clearGroup then clearGroup() end
        -- src can be one image or a list of fallbacks (tried in order until one loads)
        local candidates = {}
        if type(src) == "table" then
            for _, c in ipairs(src) do
                local cid = UIKit.assetId(c)
                if cid then candidates[#candidates + 1] = cid end
            end
        else
            local cid = UIKit.assetId(src)
            if cid then candidates[1] = cid end
        end
        if #candidates == 0 and not opts.Placeholder then
            if thumbWrap then thumbWrap.Visible = false end
            return
        end
        ensureThumb()
        clearViewport()
        thumbWrap.Visible = true
        thumbToken = thumbToken + 1
        local my = thumbToken
        thumb.ImageTransparency = 1
        phIcon.Visible = true
        phText.Visible = true
        if #candidates == 0 then
            thumb.Image = ""
            phText.Text = placeholderText()
            return
        end
        phText.Text = "Loading image..."

        local function reveal()
            if my ~= thumbToken then return end
            TweenService:Create(thumb, TweenInfo.new(0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { ImageTransparency = 0 }):Play()
            phIcon.Visible = false
            phText.Visible = false
        end

        task.spawn(function()
            for _, cand in ipairs(candidates) do
                if my ~= thumbToken then return end
                thumb.Image = cand
                local ok = false
                if cand:match("^rbxasset://") then
                    ok = true   -- local file written by the script: always trusted (IsLoaded is unreliable for these)
                else
                    local done = false
                    task.spawn(function()
                        pcall(function()
                            game:GetService("ContentProvider"):PreloadAsync({ thumb }, function(_, status)
                                if status == Enum.AssetFetchStatus.Success then ok = true end
                            end)
                        end)
                        done = true
                    end)
                    local t0 = tick()
                    while not done and tick() - t0 < 8 do task.wait(0.1) end
                    ok = ok or thumb.IsLoaded
                end
                if ok then reveal() return end
            end
            if my == thumbToken then phText.Text = "Image unavailable" end
        end)
    end

    -- viewport: src = Model / BasePart (cloned, centred and slowly spun). nil clears it and falls back
    -- to the thumbnail / placeholder. opts.ViewportSpin = false stops the rotation.
    local function setViewport(src)
        opts.Viewport = src
        if clearGroup then clearGroup() end
        if src == nil then
            clearViewport()
            setThumbnail(opts.Thumbnail)
            return
        end
        ensureThumb()
        clearViewport()
        thumbToken = thumbToken + 1
        thumb.ImageTransparency = 1
        thumbWrap.Visible = true
        phIcon.Visible = true
        phText.Visible = true
        phText.Text = type(opts.Placeholder) == "string" and opts.Placeholder or "Loading model..."
        if typeof(src) ~= "Instance" then return end

        local my = vpToken
        local clone
        local okClone = pcall(function() clone = src:Clone() end)
        if not okClone or not clone then phText.Text = "Model unavailable"; return end
        local model = clone
        if not clone:IsA("Model") then
            model = Instance.new("Model")
            clone.Parent = model
        end
        for _, d in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("BasePart") then d.Anchored = true end
            if d:IsA("Script") or d:IsA("LocalScript") then d:Destroy() end
        end
        model.Parent = vp
        vpModel = model
        local okBox, cf, size = pcall(function() return model:GetBoundingBox() end)
        if not okBox then phText.Text = "Model unavailable"; return end
        local center = cf.Position
        local radius = math.max(size.Magnitude / 2, 0.5)
        local dist = radius / math.tan(math.rad(vpCam.FieldOfView / 2)) * 1.05
        local angle = 0
        local function place()
            local dir = Vector3.new(math.sin(angle), 0.25, math.cos(angle)).Unit
            vpCam.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(center + dir * dist, center)
        end
        place()
        vp.Visible = true
        phIcon.Visible = false
        phText.Visible = false
        if opts.ViewportSpin ~= false then
            vpConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
                if my ~= vpToken or not vp:IsDescendantOf(game) then
                    if vpConn then vpConn:Disconnect(); vpConn = nil end
                    return
                end
                angle = angle + dt * 0.8
                place()
            end)
        end
    end

    -- viewport group: two previews side by side with a gap between them
    clearGroup = function()
        for _, c in ipairs(groupCells) do c.Destroy() end
        groupCells = {}
        if groupFrame then groupFrame.Visible = false end
    end
    local function setViewports(list)
        opts.Viewports = list
        if list == nil then clearGroup(); return end
        clearViewport()
        thumbToken = thumbToken + 1
        if thumbWrap then thumbWrap.Visible = false end
        local gap = opts.ViewportGap or 8
        if not groupFrame then
            groupFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
            groupFrame.Name = "ViewportGroup"
            groupFrame.LayoutOrder = 1
            groupFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            groupFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
            groupFrame.Parent = row
            local gl = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
            gl.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
            gl.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
            gl.Parent = groupFrame
            groupFrame:SetAttribute("GroupLayout", true)
        end
        groupFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, opts.ThumbnailHeight or 110)
        groupFrame:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIListLayout").Padding = UDim.new(0, gap)
        clearGroup()
        groupFrame.Visible = true
        for i = 1, 2 do
            local cell = UIKit.viewportCell(groupFrame, list[i] or false, {
                Placeholder = type(opts.Placeholder) == "string" and opts.Placeholder or nil,
                Label = opts.ViewportLabels and opts.ViewportLabels[i] or nil,
                Spin = opts.ViewportSpin,
            })
            cell.Instance.LayoutOrder = i
            cell.Instance.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -math.floor(gap / 2), 1, 0)
            groupCells[i] = cell
        end
    end

    -- the header grows with the (wrapped) title text
    local function fitTitle()
        local sc = math.max(MainScale.Scale, 0.01)
        local h = math.max(20, imgSize + 4, math.ceil(titleLbl.TextBounds.Y / sc) + 2)
        if header.Size.Y.Offset ~= h then header.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, h) end
    end
    titleLbl:GetPropertyChangedSignal("TextBounds"):Connect(fitTitle)
    titleLbl:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(fitTitle)

    local function setTitle(t, image)
        if t ~= nil then titleLbl.Text = tostring(t) end
        if image ~= nil then opts.Image = image end
        local hasImg = opts.Image ~= nil and opts.Image ~= false
        img.Visible = false
        if hasImg then UIKit.setImage(img, opts.Image, P.Accent) end
        local x = hasImg and (imgSize + 8) or 0
        titleLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, x, 0.5, 0)
        titleLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -x, 0, 0)
        header.Visible = hasImg or (titleLbl.Text ~= "")
        task.defer(fitTitle)
    end

    local function setText(t)
        body.Text = Cfg.trimDesc(t)
        body.Visible = body.Text ~= ""
    end

    setTitle(title or "", opts.Image)
    setText(text)
    if opts.Viewports ~= nil then
        setViewports(opts.Viewports)
    elseif opts.Viewport ~= nil then
        setViewport(opts.Viewport)
    elseif opts.Thumbnail ~= nil or opts.Placeholder then
        setThumbnail(opts.Thumbnail)
    end

    local api = { Instance = row }
    function api.SetViewport(m) setViewport(m) end
    function api.SetViewports(list) setViewports(list) end
    function api.SetTitle(t) setTitle(t) end
    function api.SetText(t) setText(t) end
    function api.SetImage(i) setTitle(nil, i or false) end
    function api.SetThumbnail(i) setThumbnail(i) end
    function api.Set(o)
        o = o or {}
        if o.Title ~= nil or o.Image ~= nil then setTitle(o.Title, o.Image) end
        if o.Text ~= nil then setText(o.Text) end
        if o.Thumbnail ~= nil then setThumbnail(o.Thumbnail) end
        if o.Viewport ~= nil then setViewport(o.Viewport) end
        if o.Viewports ~= nil then setViewports(o.Viewports) end
    end
    return api
end

-- UIKit.divider(parent, text, opts)
--   no text  -> a thin fading line.   text -> line - text - line
--   opts = { Align = "center" | "left" | "right" }       (default "center")
--   text supports rich text and the [g:#hex,#hex] gradient prefix
--   api: Instance, SetText(t), SetAlign(a)
function UIKit.divider(parent, text, opts)
    opts = opts or {}
    local row = Instance.new("Frame")
    row.Name = "Divider"
    row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    row.BorderSizePixel = 0
    row.Parent = parent

    local function mkLine(fadeIn)   -- fadeIn: true = fades in towards the text (left line), false = fades out away from it
        local l = Instance.new("Frame")
        l.BackgroundColor3 = P.Line2
        l.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
        l.BorderSizePixel = 0
        l.ZIndex = 3
        l.Parent = row
        local g = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        g.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(fadeIn and 1 or 0, fadeIn and 0 or 1)
        g.Parent = l
        return l, g
    end
    local lineL, gradL = mkLine(true)
    local lineR = mkLine(false)

    local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    lbl.TextSize = 11
    lbl.TextColor3 = P.Muted
    lbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
    lbl.ZIndex = 3
    lbl.Parent = row

    local content, align = tostring(text or ""), tostring(opts.Align or "center"):lower()
    local function layout()
        local hasText = content ~= ""
        row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, hasText and 18 or 8)
        lbl.Visible = hasText
        lineR.Visible = hasText
        if not hasText then
            -- single line across the row, faded at both ends
            lineL.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
            lineL.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0)
            lineL.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1)
            gradL.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.15, 0),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.85, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
            })
            return
        end
        gradL.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(1, 0)
        local ok, sz = pcall(function()
            return TextService:GetTextSize(stripRich(content), 11, Enum.Font.GothamBold, Vector2.new(1000, 20))
        end)
        local w = (ok and sz.X or #content * 7) + 2
        local GAP, SHORT = 8, 18
        lbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, w, 1, 0)
        lineL.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
        lineL.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0)
        lineR.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
        lineR.Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.5, 0)
        if align == "left" then
            lbl.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
            lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, SHORT + GAP, 0.5, 0)
            lineL.Size = UDim2.new(0, SHORT, 0, 1)
            lineR.Size = UDim2.new(1, -(SHORT + GAP + w + GAP), 0, 1)
        elseif align == "right" then
            lbl.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
            lbl.Position = UDim2.new(1, -(SHORT + GAP), 0.5, 0)
            lineR.Size = UDim2.new(0, SHORT, 0, 1)
            lineL.Size = UDim2.new(1, -(SHORT + GAP + w + GAP), 0, 1)
        else
            lbl.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
            lineL.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -(w / 2 + GAP), 0, 1)
            lineR.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -(w / 2 + GAP), 0, 1)
        end
        richSet(lbl, content)
    end
    layout()

    local api = { Instance = row }
    function api.SetText(t) content = tostring(t or ""); layout() end
    function api.SetAlign(a) align = tostring(a or "center"):lower(); layout() end
    return api
end

local function copyText(t)
    local fn = setclipboard or toclipboard or (syn and syn.write_clipboard)
    if not fn then return false end
    return pcall(fn, t)
end

-- ============================================================
-- COLOR HELPERS + COLOR PICKER
-- ============================================================
local function colorToHex(c)
    return string.format("#%02X%02X%02X",
        math.floor(c.R * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(c.G * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(c.B * 255 + 0.5))
end

function hexToColor(h)
    h = tostring(h or ""):gsub("[#%s]", "")
    if #h == 3 then h = h:gsub(".", function(ch) return ch .. ch end) end
    if not h:match("^%x%x%x%x%x%x$") then return nil end
    return Color3.fromRGB(tonumber(h:sub(1, 2), 16), tonumber(h:sub(3, 4), 16), tonumber(h:sub(5, 6), 16))
end

local ColorSetters = {}

-- cb(color) fires while dragging, onCommit(color) fires on release / hex enter
local function colorPicker(parent, label, valKey, cb, iconName, onCommit)
    local HEAD, PANEL = 40, 152
    local row = makeRowCard(parent, HEAD)
    row.ClipsDescendants = true

    local icon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    icon.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14)
    icon.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 13)
    icon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    icon.ImageColor3 = P.Muted
    icon.Visible = false
    icon.ZIndex = 3
    icon.Parent = row

    local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -62, 0, HEAD)
    lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    lbl.Text = label
    lbl.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    lbl.TextSize = 13
    lbl.TextColor3 = P.Text
    lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    lbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
    lbl.ZIndex = 3
    lbl.Parent = row

    task.spawn(function()
        if not LucideReady then
            local t0 = tick()
            while not LucideReady and tick() - t0 < 30 do task.wait(0.2) end
        end
        if LucideReady and setIcon(icon, iconName, P.Muted) then
            icon.Visible = true
            lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 24, 0, 0)
            lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -86, 0, HEAD)
        end
    end)

    local swatch = Instance.new("TextButton")
    swatch.Size = UDim2.new(0, 48, 0, 22)
    swatch.Position = UDim2.new(1, -48, 0, 9)
    swatch.Text = ""
    swatch.BorderSizePixel = 0
    swatch.AutoButtonColor = false
    swatch.ZIndex = 3
    swatch:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true)
    swatch.Parent = row
    corner(swatch, 8)
    local swStroke = stroke(swatch, P.Line2, 1, 0.3)

    local panel = Instance.new("Frame")
    panel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, PANEL)
    panel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, HEAD + 4)
    panel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    panel.ZIndex = 3
    panel.Parent = row

    -- saturation / value square
    local sv = Instance.new("Frame")
    sv.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 96)
    sv.BorderSizePixel = 0
    sv.ZIndex = 3
    sv:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true)
    sv.Parent = panel
    corner(sv, 8)

    local white = Instance.new("Frame")
    white.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    white.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    white.BorderSizePixel = 0
    white.ZIndex = 4
    white:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true)
    white.Parent = sv
    corner(white, 8)
    do
        local g = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        g.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0, 1)
        g:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true)
        g.Parent = white
    end

    local black = Instance.new("Frame")
    black.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    black.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
    black.BorderSizePixel = 0
    black.ZIndex = 5
    black:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true)
    black.Parent = sv
    corner(black, 8)
    do
        local g = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        g.Rotation = 90
        g.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(1, 0)
        g:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true)
        g.Parent = black
    end

    local knob = Instance.new("Frame")
    knob.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    knob.Size = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 12)
    knob.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    knob.BorderSizePixel = 0
    knob.ZIndex = 7
    knob:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true)
    knob.Parent = sv
    corner(knob, 6)
    stroke(knob, Color3.new(0, 0, 0), 1.5, 0.2)

    local svHit = Instance.new("TextButton")
    svHit.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    svHit.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    svHit.Text = ""
    svHit.ZIndex = 8
    svHit.Parent = sv

    -- hue bar
    local hueBar = Instance.new("Frame")
    hueBar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 104)
    hueBar.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 14)
    hueBar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    hueBar.BorderSizePixel = 0
    hueBar.ZIndex = 3
    hueBar:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true)
    hueBar.Parent = panel
    corner(hueBar, 7)
    do
        local kps = {}
        for i = 0, 6 do kps[#kps + 1] = ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(i / 6, Color3.fromHSV(i / 6, 1, 1)) end
        local g = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        g.Color = ColorSequence.new(kps)
        g:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true)
        g.Parent = hueBar
    end
    local hueKnob = Instance.new("Frame")
    hueKnob.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    hueKnob.Size = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 18)
    hueKnob.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    hueKnob.BorderSizePixel = 0
    hueKnob.ZIndex = 5
    hueKnob:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true)
    hueKnob.Parent = hueBar
    corner(hueKnob, 4)
    stroke(hueKnob, Color3.new(0, 0, 0), 1.5, 0.2)
    local hueHit = Instance.new("TextButton")
    hueHit.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 3, 0)
    hueHit.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, -1, 0)
    hueHit.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    hueHit.Text = ""
    hueHit.ZIndex = 8
    hueHit.Parent = hueBar

    -- hex input
    local hexBox = Instance.new("TextBox")
    hexBox.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 126)
    hexBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, -76, 0, 24)
    hexBox.BackgroundColor3 = P.Bg3
    hexBox.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
    hexBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
    hexBox.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    hexBox.TextSize = 11
    hexBox.TextColor3 = P.Text
    hexBox.PlaceholderText = "#RRGGBB"
    hexBox.PlaceholderColor3 = P.Muted
    hexBox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
    hexBox.ZIndex = 3
    hexBox.Parent = panel
    corner(hexBox, 8)
    local hexStroke = stroke(hexBox, P.Line, 1, 0.5)
    hexBox.Focused:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(hexStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { Color = P.Accent, Transparency = 0.3 }):Play()
    end)

    local applyBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    applyBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -68, 0, 126)
    applyBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 68, 0, 24)
    applyBtn.BackgroundColor3 = P.Accent
    applyBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
    applyBtn.Text = "Apply"
    applyBtn.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    applyBtn.TextSize = 12
    applyBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    applyBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    applyBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
    applyBtn.ZIndex = 3
    applyBtn.Parent = panel
    corner(applyBtn, 8)
    applyBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() TweenService:Create(applyBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.12), { BackgroundTransparency = 0.12 }):Play() end)
    applyBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() TweenService:Create(applyBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.12), { BackgroundTransparency = 0.25 }):Play() end)

    local h, sat, val = 0, 0, 1
    local applied = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    local function current() return Color3.fromHSV(h, sat, val) end
    local function refresh(skipHex)
        sv.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromHSV(h, 1, 1)
        -- keep the indicators fully inside their bars (offset shrinks to 0 at the middle)
        knob.Position = UDim2.new(sat, math.floor((0.5 - sat) * 12), 1 - val, math.floor((0.5 - (1 - val)) * 12))
        hueKnob.Position = UDim2.new(h, math.floor((0.5 - h) * 8), 0.5, 0)
        swatch.BackgroundColor3 = current()
        if not skipHex then hexBox.Text = colorToHex(current()) end
    end
    local function preview(c)
        h, sat, val = c:ToHSV()
        refresh()
    end

    local drag = nil
    local function dragTo(pos)
        if drag == "sv" then
            sat = math.clamp((pos.X - sv.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(sv.AbsoluteSize.X, 1), 0, 1)
            val = 1 - math.clamp((pos.Y - sv.AbsolutePosition.Y) / math.max(sv.AbsoluteSize.Y, 1), 0, 1)
        elseif drag == "hue" then
            h = math.clamp((pos.X - hueBar.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(hueBar.AbsoluteSize.X, 1), 0, 0.999)
        else
            return
        end
        refresh()
    end
    local function begin(mode, input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
        or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            drag = mode
            dragTo(input.Position)
        end
    end
    svHit.InputBegan:Connect(function(i) begin("sv", i) end)
    hueHit.InputBegan:Connect(function(i) begin("hue", i) end)
    UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
        if drag and (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
        or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
            dragTo(i.Position)
        end
    end)
    UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
        if drag and (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
        or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
            drag = nil
        end
    end)

    hexBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(hexStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { Color = P.Line, Transparency = 0.5 }):Play()
        local c = hexToColor(hexBox.Text)
        if c then preview(c) else hexBox.Text = colorToHex(current()) end
    end)

    -- nothing takes effect until Apply is pressed
    local isOpen = false
    local function setOpen(open)
        isOpen = open
        if not open then preview(applied) end   -- closing without applying discards the preview
        TweenService:Create(row, open and TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
                                      or TweenInfo.new(0.6, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, open and (HEAD + 4 + PANEL + 6) or HEAD)
        }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(swStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
            Color = open and P.Accent or P.Line2, Transparency = open and 0 or 0.3
        }):Play()
    end
    swatch.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setOpen(not isOpen) end)

    -- Apply commits the colour and folds the picker back up
    applyBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local c = current()
        applied = c
        Values[valKey] = colorToHex(c)
        if cb then task.spawn(cb, c) end
        if onCommit then task.spawn(onCommit, c) end
        setOpen(false)
    end)

    local function setApplied(c)
        applied = c
        Values[valKey] = colorToHex(c)
        preview(c)
    end
    setApplied(hexToColor(Values[valKey]) or Color3.new(1, 1, 1))
    ColorSetters[valKey] = function(hex)
        local c = hexToColor(hex)
        if c then setApplied(c) end
    end
    return { Set = setApplied, Instance = row }
end

-- ============================================================
-- TAGS (small pills next to a label)
-- ============================================================
do
local TAG_KINDS = {
    new    = function() return P.Accent end,
    pro    = function() return P.Accent2 end,
    hot    = function() return P.Bad end,
    good   = function() return P.Good end,
    beta   = function() return Color3.fromRGB(240, 185, 80) end,
    locked = function() return P.Muted end,
    premium = function() return Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 70) end,
}

local function makePill(parent, text, kind)
    local col
    if typeof(kind) == "Color3" then
        col = kind
    else
        local fn = TAG_KINDS[tostring(kind or "new"):lower()]
        col = fn and fn() or P.Accent
    end
    text = tostring(text):upper()
    local ok, sz = pcall(function()
        return TextService:GetTextSize(text, 9, Enum.Font.GothamBold, Vector2.new(300, 20))
    end)
    local pill = Instance.new("Frame")
    pill.Name = "Tag"
    pill.Size = UDim2.new(0, (ok and sz.X or #text * 6) + 12, 0, 16)
    pill.BackgroundColor3 = col
    pill.BackgroundTransparency = 0.82
    pill.BorderSizePixel = 0
    pill.ZIndex = 6
    pill.Parent = parent
    corner(pill, 8)
    stroke(pill, col, 1, 0.55)
    local l = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    l.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    l.Text = text
    l.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    l.TextSize = 9
    l.TextColor3 = col
    l.ZIndex = 7
    l.Parent = pill
    return pill
end

-- w = a widget (toggle/button/slider/dropdown/...) or a row Instance
function addTag(w, text, kind, reserve)
    local inst = typeof(w) == "Instance" and w or w.Instance
    local lbl = inst:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel", true)
    if not lbl then return nil end
    local prior = 0   -- tags already on this row / header: the new one sits right after them
    for _, ch in ipairs(inst:GetChildren()) do
        if ch.Name == "Tag" and ch:IsA("Frame") then prior = prior + ch.Size.X.Offset + 6 end
    end
    local pill = makePill(inst, text, kind)
    pill.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
    local pad = inst:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIPadding")
    local padW = pad and (pad.PaddingLeft.Offset + pad.PaddingRight.Offset) or 0
    reserve = reserve or 56
    local function place()
        if not pill.Parent then return end
        -- TextBounds is measured on screen (UIScale included) while positions are in unscaled units
        local x = lbl.Position.X.Offset + lbl.TextBounds.X / math.max(MainScale.Scale, 0.01) + 16 + prior
        pill.Position = UDim2.new(0, x,
            lbl.Position.Y.Scale + lbl.Size.Y.Scale / 2,
            lbl.Position.Y.Offset + lbl.Size.Y.Offset / 2)
        local inner = inst.AbsoluteSize.X / math.max(MainScale.Scale, 0.01) - padW
        pill.Visible = lbl.TextBounds.X > 0 and (x + pill.Size.X.Offset) <= (inner - reserve)
    end
    for _, prop in ipairs({ "TextBounds", "Position", "Size" }) do
        lbl:GetPropertyChangedSignal(prop):Connect(place)
    end
    inst:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(place)
    MainScale:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Scale"):Connect(function() place(); task.defer(place) end)
    task.defer(place)
    return pill
end

function sectionTag(wrap, text, kind)
    local header = wrap:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Frame")
    if header then return addTag(header, text, kind, 56) end
end
end -- tags scope

-- ============================================================
-- LOCKS (rows, sections, tabs)
-- ============================================================
local function lockWidget(w, reason, startLocked, label)
    local inst = typeof(w) == "Instance" and w or w.Instance
    local up = inst:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIPadding")
    local pl = up and up.PaddingLeft.Offset or 0
    local pr = up and up.PaddingRight.Offset or 0
    local pt = up and up.PaddingTop.Offset or 0
    local pb = up and up.PaddingBottom.Offset or 0
    local uc = inst:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner")
    local radius = uc and uc.CornerRadius.Offset or 9

    local cover
    local ctl = { Reason = reason, Label = label or "PATCHED" }
    local isPremium = (label == "PREMIUM")
    local coverIcon = isPremium and "crown" or "lock"
    local coverCol = isPremium and Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 70) or P.Text

    function ctl.IsLocked() return cover ~= nil end

    function ctl.Lock(r)
        if r then ctl.Reason = r end
        if cover then return end
        local c = Instance.new("TextButton")
        c.Name = "LockCover"
        c.Position = UDim2.new(0, -pl, 0, -pt)
        c.Size = UDim2.new(1, pl + pr, 1, pt + pb)
        c.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        c.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        c.Text = ""
        c.AutoButtonColor = false
        c.Active = true
        c.BorderSizePixel = 0
        c.ZIndex = 30
        c.Parent = ctl.Host or inst   -- (a section-wide lock lives in the header: the section's own list layout would push it below the rows)
        corner(c, radius)
        c.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()   -- optional click action (the PREMIUM popup); other locks just swallow the click
            if ctl.OnClick then pcall(ctl.OnClick) end
        end)

        local grp = Instance.new("Frame")
        grp.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        grp.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
        grp.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 28)
        grp.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X
        grp.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        grp.ZIndex = 31
        grp.Parent = c
        local gl = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        gl.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
        gl.Padding = UDim.new(0, 8)
        gl.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
        gl.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
        gl.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        gl.Parent = grp

        local ic = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
        ic.LayoutOrder = 1
        ic.Size = UDim2.new(0, 26, 0, 26)
        ic.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        ic.ZIndex = 31
        ic.Parent = grp
        if not (LucideReady and setIcon(ic, coverIcon, coverCol)) then
            ic.Visible = false
            task.spawn(function()
                local t0 = tick()
                while not LucideReady and tick() - t0 < 30 do task.wait(0.2) end
                if ic.Parent and not (LucideReady and setIcon(ic, coverIcon, coverCol)) then ic:Destroy() end
            end)
        end

        local t = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        t.LayoutOrder = 2
        t.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
        t.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X
        t.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        t.Text = ctl.Label
        t.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        t.TextSize = 20
        t.TextColor3 = coverCol
        t.ZIndex = 31
        t.Parent = grp

        -- the cover is an Active TextButton with no handlers: it swallows every click/tap, so nothing under it can be pressed, and it fires no notification
        -- fade in together as well: background, icon and text
        local tiIn = TweenInfo.new(0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
        t.TextTransparency = 1
        TweenService:Create(t, tiIn, { TextTransparency = 0 }):Play()
        if ic.Parent and ic.Visible then
            ic.ImageTransparency = 1
            TweenService:Create(ic, tiIn, { ImageTransparency = 0 }):Play()
        end
        TweenService:Create(c, tiIn, { BackgroundTransparency = ctl.ScrimT or 0.3 }):Play()
        cover = c
    end

    function ctl.Unlock()
        local c = cover
        if not c then return end
        cover = nil
        -- background, lock icon and LOCKED text all fade out together
        local ti = TweenInfo.new(0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
        TweenService:Create(c, ti, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()
        for _, d in ipairs(c:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("ImageLabel") then TweenService:Create(d, ti, { ImageTransparency = 1 }):Play()
            elseif d:IsA("TextLabel") then TweenService:Create(d, ti, { TextTransparency = 1 }):Play() end
        end
        task.delay(0.25, function() c:Destroy() end)
    end

    if startLocked ~= false then ctl.Lock() end
    return ctl
end

-- locks every row in a section and adds a LOCKED tag to its header
local function lockSection(wrap, reason, label, onClick)
    label = label or "PATCHED"
    local tagText = string.upper(label)
    local tagReserve = math.max(56, #tagText * 8 + 14)
    local header = wrap:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Frame")
    local ctls = {}
    for _, c in ipairs(wrap:GetChildren()) do
        if (c:IsA("Frame") or c:IsA("TextButton")) and c ~= header and c.Name ~= "SectionDivider"
            and c.Name ~= "Divider" and c.Name ~= "Space" and c.Name ~= "SectionDesc" then   -- the description stays readable
            local lw = lockWidget(c, reason, nil, label)
            lw.OnClick = onClick
            table.insert(ctls, lw)
        end
    end
    local tagKind = (label == "PREMIUM") and "premium" or "locked"
    local tag = header and addTag(header, tagText, tagKind, tagReserve)
    local api = {}
    function api.IsLocked() return tag ~= nil end
    function api.Unlock()
        for _, c in ipairs(ctls) do c.Unlock() end
        if tag then tag:Destroy(); tag = nil end
    end
    function api.Lock()
        for _, c in ipairs(ctls) do c.Lock() end
        if not tag and header then tag = addTag(header, tagText, tagKind, tagReserve) end
    end
    return api
end

-- ONE lock for the whole section: a single cover from the first row down to the last one (instead of a cover on every
-- row), plus the tag on the header. The title and the description stay visible above it.
local function lockSectionOnce(wrap, reason, label, onClick)
    label = label or "PATCHED"
    local tagText = string.upper(label)
    local tagReserve = math.max(56, #tagText * 8 + 14)
    local header = wrap:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Frame")
    local pad = wrap:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIPadding")
    local pl = pad and pad.PaddingLeft.Offset or 0
    local pr = pad and pad.PaddingRight.Offset or 0
    local SKIP = { Divider = true, SectionDivider = true, Space = true, SectionDesc = true, LockCover = true }

    local lw = lockWidget(wrap, reason, false, label)
    lw.OnClick = onClick
    lw.Host = header        -- the header has no list layout, so the cover can be placed freely
    lw.ScrimT = 0.5         -- a lighter scrim than a per-row lock: the rows stay readable under it
    if header then
        header.ClipsDescendants = false
        header.ZIndex = 29   -- the header (and the cover in it) draws above the rows
    end

    local function update()
        local c = header and header:FindFirstChild("LockCover")
        if not c then return end
        local minY, maxY
        for _, ch in ipairs(wrap:GetChildren()) do
