            if (ch:IsA("Frame") or ch:IsA("TextButton")) and ch ~= header and not SKIP[ch.Name] and ch.Visible then
                local y0 = ch.AbsolutePosition.Y
                local y1 = y0 + ch.AbsoluteSize.Y
                minY = minY and math.min(minY, y0) or y0
                maxY = maxY and math.max(maxY, y1) or y1
            end
        end
        if not minY then return end
        local s = math.max(MainScale.Scale, 0.01)
        local hp = header.AbsolutePosition
        local top = (minY - hp.Y) / s - 4
        local bottom = (maxY - hp.Y) / s + 4
        c.Position = UDim2.new(0, (wrap.AbsolutePosition.X - hp.X) / s, 0, top)
        c.Size = UDim2.new(0, wrap.AbsoluteSize.X / s, 0, math.max(bottom - top, 10))
    end

    lw.Lock()
    update()
    local lay = wrap:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIListLayout")
    if lay then lay:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(update) end
    wrap:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(update)
    MainScale:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Scale"):Connect(function() task.defer(update) end)
    task.delay(0.2, update)
    task.delay(1, update)

    local tagKind = (label == "PREMIUM") and "premium" or "locked"
    local tag = header and addTag(header, tagText, tagKind, tagReserve)
    local api = {}
    function api.IsLocked() return tag ~= nil end
    function api.Unlock()
        lw.Unlock()
        if tag then tag:Destroy(); tag = nil end
    end
    function api.Lock()
        lw.Lock()
        update()
        if not tag and header then tag = addTag(header, tagText, tagKind, tagReserve) end
    end
    return api
end

local function lockTab(name, reason)
    local btn = tabButtons[name]
    if not btn then return end
    tabLocks[name] = { reason = reason }
    if not btn:FindFirstChild("TabLock") then
        local ic = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
        ic.Name = "TabLock"
        ic.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
        ic.Size = UDim2.new(0, 11, 0, 11)
        ic.Position = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 4)
        ic.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        ic.ZIndex = 8
        ic.Parent = btn
        if not (LucideReady and setIcon(ic, "lock", P.Muted)) then
            ic.Visible = false
            task.spawn(function()
                local t0 = tick()
                while not LucideReady and tick() - t0 < 30 do task.wait(0.2) end
                if ic.Parent and not (LucideReady and setIcon(ic, "lock", P.Muted)) then ic:Destroy() end
            end)
        end
    end
    local pg = pages[name]
    if pg and pg.Visible then
        for _, other in ipairs(TAB_ORDER) do
            if other ~= name and not tabLocks[other] then switchTab(other) break end
        end
    end
end

local function unlockTab(name)
    tabLocks[name] = nil
    local btn = tabButtons[name]
    local ic = btn and btn:FindFirstChild("TabLock")
    if ic then ic:Destroy() end
end

-- ============================================================
-- SECTION LAYOUT (single column / two columns)
-- ============================================================
local colHolders = {}

local function pageSections(page)
    local list = {}
    for _, d in ipairs(page:GetDescendants()) do
        if d:IsA("Frame") and d:GetAttribute("SecIdx") then table.insert(list, d) end
    end
    table.sort(list, function(a, b) return a:GetAttribute("SecIdx") < b:GetAttribute("SecIdx") end)
    return list
end

local function estimateSectionHeight(wrap)
    local h, n = 28 + 18 + 1, 2          -- padding + header + divider
    for _, c in ipairs(wrap:GetChildren()) do
        if (c:IsA("Frame") or c:IsA("TextButton")) and c.Name ~= "SectionDivider" and c ~= wrap:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Frame") then
            local ch = c.Size.Y.Offset
            if c.Name == "SectionDesc" then ch = 30 end
            if ch <= 0 then ch = c:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("UIGridLayout") and 200 or 40 end
            h = h + ch
            n = n + 1
        end
    end
    return h + 10 * (n - 1)
end

local function applyColumns(on)
    if Cfg.dockAll then Cfg.dockAll(true) end   -- popped-out sections go home before the layout changes
    -- pages that own sub-tabs are only containers; their sub pages are laid out instead
    local layoutPages = {}
    for name, page in pairs(pages) do
        if not page:GetAttribute("HasSubTabs") then layoutPages[name] = page end
    end
    for name, page in pairs(Cfg.SubPages) do layoutPages[name] = page end
    for name, page in pairs(layoutPages) do
        local secs = pageSections(page)
        local holder = colHolders[name]
        if on and not page:GetAttribute("SingleColumn") then   -- (Home pins itself to one full-width column)
            if not holder then
                local hf = Instance.new("Frame")
                hf.Name = "Columns"
                hf.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)   -- fills the page; every column scrolls on its own
                hf.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
                hf.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                hf.LayoutOrder = 0
                hf.Parent = page
                local hl = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
                hl.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
                hl.Padding = UDim.new(0, 4)   -- middle gap (two columns); with the 2px column padding on each side it looks 8px, same as between stacked sections
                hl.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top
                hl.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
                hl.Parent = hf
                local function mkCol(order)
                    local c = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
                    c.Name = order == 1 and "ColLeft" or "ColRight"
                    c.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -2, 1, 0)
                    c.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    c.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    c.ScrollBarThickness = 0   -- no visible bar; wheel / drag still scrolls
                    c.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
                    c.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
                    c.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y
                    c.ElasticBehavior = Enum.ElasticBehavior.Never
                    c.LayoutOrder = order
                    c.ZIndex = 4
                    c.Parent = hf
                    local cp = Instance.new("UIPadding")
                    cp.PaddingTop, cp.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 2), UDim.new(0, 16)
                    cp.PaddingLeft, cp.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 2), UDim.new(0, 2)
                    cp.Parent = c
                    local cl = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
                    cl.Padding = UDim.new(0, 8)
                    cl.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
                    cl.Parent = c
                    return c
                end
                holder = { Frame = hf, L = mkCol(1), R = mkCol(2) }
                colHolders[name] = holder
            end
            -- the page itself stops scrolling (the columns do it) unless something else sits directly on it
            local isSec = {}
            for _, w in ipairs(secs) do isSec[w] = true end
            local extras = 0
            for _, ch in ipairs(page:GetChildren()) do
                if ch:IsA("GuiObject") and ch ~= holder.Frame and not isSec[ch] then extras = extras + 1 end
            end
            if extras == 0 then
                holder.Frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                holder.Frame.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
                page.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
                page.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
                page.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0)
                page.ScrollingEnabled = false
                for _, c in ipairs({ holder.L, holder.R }) do
                    c:SetAttribute("FitMode", false)
                    c.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
                    c.ScrollingEnabled = true
                    c.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -2, 1, 0)
                end
            else
                holder.Frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
                holder.Frame.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
                page.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
                page.ScrollingEnabled = true
                for _, c in ipairs({ holder.L, holder.R }) do
                    c.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
                    c.ScrollingEnabled = false
                    c.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -2, 0, 0)
                    local cl = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIListLayout")
                    c:SetAttribute("FitMode", true)
                    local function fit()
                        if not c:GetAttribute("FitMode") then return end
                        c.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -2, 0, (cl and cl.AbsoluteContentSize.Y or 0) + 18)
                    end
                    if cl and not c:GetAttribute("FitHooked") then
                        c:SetAttribute("FitHooked", true)
                        cl:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(fit)
                    end
                    fit()
                end
            end
            holder.Frame.Visible = true
            local hL, hR = 0, 0
            local placedLeft = {}   -- [SecIdx] = true when that section went to the left column
            for _, w in ipairs(secs) do
                local h = estimateSectionHeight(w)
                local force = w:GetAttribute("ForceCol")   -- optional: a section can pin itself to one column
                local toLeft = (force == "L") or (force ~= "R" and hL <= hR)
                local under = w:GetAttribute("UnderIdx")   -- optional: stack right under another section (same column)
                if under and placedLeft[under] ~= nil then toLeft = placedLeft[under] end
                placedLeft[w:GetAttribute("SecIdx")] = toLeft
                w.Parent = toLeft and holder.L or holder.R
                w.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
                if toLeft then hL = hL + h + 16 else hR = hR + h + 16 end
            end
        else
            for _, w in ipairs(secs) do
                w.Parent = page
                w.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 0)
                w.LayoutOrder = w:GetAttribute("SecIdx")
            end
            if holder then holder.Frame.Visible = false end
            page.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
            page.ScrollingEnabled = true
        end
    end
end


local colToken = 0
local function animateColumns(on)
    colToken = colToken + 1
    local my = colToken
    -- fade the page area out
    local t1 = TweenService:Create(Content, TweenInfo.new(0.14, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In),
        { GroupTransparency = 1 })
    t1:Play()
    t1.Completed:Wait()
    if my ~= colToken then return end

    applyColumns(on)
    for _, pg in pairs(pages) do pg.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 16) end
    task.wait()
    if my ~= colToken then return end

    -- fade back in while the page slides up into place
    local ti = TweenInfo.new(0.24, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
    TweenService:Create(Content, ti, { GroupTransparency = 0 }):Play()
    for _, pg in pairs(pages) do
        TweenService:Create(pg, ti, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0) }):Play()
    end
end

-- ============================================================
-- CONFIG SYSTEM  (named configs in a folder + optional auto load)
--   Nothing is saved or loaded automatically. A config only loads on startup
--   if it was saved with "Save as Auto Load".
-- ============================================================
do
    local CFG_DIR  = "OziohubConfigs"
    local CFG_AUTO = CFG_DIR .. "/_autoload.txt"

    function Cfg.ensureDir()
        if isfolder and makefolder and not isfolder(CFG_DIR) then pcall(makefolder, CFG_DIR) end
    end
    function Cfg.clean(name)
        name = tostring(name or ""):gsub("[^%w%s%-_]", ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
        return name:sub(1, 32)
    end
    function Cfg.path(name) return CFG_DIR .. "/" .. name .. ".json" end
    function Cfg.exists(name)
        return (isfile and name ~= "" and isfile(Cfg.path(name))) and true or false
    end
    function Cfg.list()
        local out = {}
        if not (listfiles and isfolder) then return out end
        Cfg.ensureDir()
        local ok, files = pcall(listfiles, CFG_DIR)
        if not ok or type(files) ~= "table" then return out end
        for _, f in ipairs(files) do
            local n = tostring(f):match("([^/\\]+)%.json$")
            if n then out[#out + 1] = n end
        end
        table.sort(out, function(a, b) return a:lower() < b:lower() end)
        return out
    end

    function Cfg.collect()
        local data = {}
        for k, v in pairs(State) do data[k] = v end
        for k, v in pairs(Values) do
            if k ~= "ConfigName" and k ~= "ConfigSelected" then data[k] = v end
        end
        data.KEYS = {}
        for k, v in pairs(KEYBINDS) do data.KEYS[k] = v.Name end
        data.Theme = CurrentTheme
        data.Solid = isSolid
        return data
    end

    -- returns ok, cleanedNameOrError
    function Cfg.save(name)
        name = Cfg.clean(name)
        if name == "" then return false, "Enter a config name first." end
        if not (writefile and makefolder) then return false, "Your executor has no file access." end
        Cfg.ensureDir()
        local ok, err = pcall(function() writefile(Cfg.path(name), HttpService:JSONEncode(Cfg.collect())) end)
        if not ok then return false, tostring(err) end
        return true, name
    end
    function Cfg.read(name)
        if not (readfile and isfile) or name == "" then return nil end
        local p = Cfg.path(name)
        if not isfile(p) then return nil end
        local ok, data = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(p)) end)
        if ok and type(data) == "table" then return data end
        return nil
    end
    function Cfg.delete(name)
        if not (delfile and isfile) or name == "" then return false end
        local p = Cfg.path(name)
        if isfile(p) then pcall(delfile, p) end
        return not isfile(p)
    end

    function Cfg.getAuto()
        if not (readfile and isfile and isfile(CFG_AUTO)) then return nil end
        local ok, n = pcall(readfile, CFG_AUTO)
        if not ok then return nil end
        n = Cfg.clean(n)
        if n == "" or not Cfg.exists(n) then return nil end
        return n
    end
    function Cfg.setAuto(name)   -- nil clears it
        if not writefile then return false end
        Cfg.ensureDir()
        return (pcall(writefile, CFG_AUTO, name or ""))
    end

    -- applies a decoded config to the running hub (switches, sliders, keybinds, theme ...)
    function Cfg.apply(data)
        for k, v in pairs(data) do
            if Values[k] ~= nil and k ~= "ConfigName" and k ~= "ConfigSelected" and type(v) == type(Values[k]) then
                Values[k] = v
            end
        end
        if type(data.KEYS) == "table" then
            for k, n in pairs(data.KEYS) do
                if KEYBINDS[k] then
                    local ok, kc = pcall(function() return Enum.KeyCode[n] end)
                    if ok and kc then
                        KEYBINDS[k] = kc
                        if Cfg.Keybinds[k] then Cfg.Keybinds[k]() end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        -- switches: anything missing from the config is turned off
        for key, fn in pairs(Cfg.Toggles) do
            pcall(fn, data[key] == true)
        end

        for k, fn in pairs(SliderSetters)   do if Values[k] ~= nil then pcall(fn, Values[k]) end end
        for k, fn in pairs(DropdownSetters) do if k ~= "ConfigSelected" and Values[k] ~= nil then pcall(fn, Values[k]) end end
        for k, fn in pairs(ColorSetters)    do if Values[k] ~= nil then pcall(fn, Values[k]) end end
        for k, fn in pairs(UIKit.InputSetters) do if k ~= "ConfigName" and Values[k] ~= nil then pcall(fn, Values[k]) end end
        if applyMenuScale then pcall(applyMenuScale, Values.Scale_Menu or 1, true, true) end

        if type(data.Theme) == "string" and data.Theme ~= CurrentTheme then pcall(applyTheme, data.Theme) end
        if Cfg.refreshChips then pcall(Cfg.refreshChips) end
        if type(data.Solid) == "boolean" and data.Solid ~= isSolid then pcall(applySolid, data.Solid, true) end
    end
end

-- ============================================================
-- SIDEBAR OPTIONS: compact elements + bold section titles
-- ============================================================
-- "Compact Elements": rows lose their card background / border / side padding and get smaller
applyCompactRow = function(e, on, animate)
    local row = e.Row
    if not row.Parent then return end
    local ti = TweenInfo.new(animate and 0.2 or 0)
    TweenService:Create(row, ti, { BackgroundTransparency = on and 1 or 0.35 }):Play()
    TweenService:Create(e.Stroke, ti, { Transparency = on and 1 or 0.75 }):Play()
    e.Pad.PaddingLeft  = UDim.new(0, on and 0 or 12)
    e.Pad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, on and 0 or 12)
    if e.CH then   -- only fixed-height rows (dropdowns / inputs animate their own height)
        TweenService:Create(row, ti, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, on and e.CH or e.H) }):Play()
    end
    for _, d in ipairs(row:GetChildren()) do   -- text one point smaller
        if d:IsA("TextLabel") or d:IsA("TextButton") or d:IsA("TextBox") then
            local o = d:GetAttribute("OrigTS")
            if not o then o = d.TextSize; d:SetAttribute("OrigTS", o) end
            d.TextSize = on and math.max(8, o - 1) or o
        end
    end
end

local function applyCompact(on, animate)
    for _, e in ipairs(RowCards) do applyCompactRow(e, on, animate) end
    for _, fn in ipairs(CompactItems) do pcall(fn, on, animate) end
    for _, c in ipairs(SectionCtl) do          -- rows sit closer together too, and no big empty band above / below them
        if c.Layout then c.Layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, on and 4 or 8) end
        if c.Pad and not c.Busy then
            TweenService:Create(c.Pad, TweenInfo.new(animate and 0.2 or 0, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), {
                PaddingTop    = UDim.new(0, on and 7 or 10),
                PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, on and 5 or 10),
            }):Play()
        end
    end
end

-- "Bold Section Titles": section titles use the heaviest font, a bit larger and brighter
local function applyBoldSections(on, animate)
    for _, c in ipairs(SectionCtl) do
        local h = c.Header
        if h then
            h.Font = on and Enum.Font.GothamBlack or Enum.Font.GothamBold
            h.TextSize = on and 12 or 11
            TweenService:Create(h, TweenInfo.new(animate and 0.2 or 0), { TextColor3 = on and P.Text or P.Muted }):Play()
        end
    end
end


do
    local Hub = {}
    UIKit.Hub = Hub
    local keyCounter = 0
    local usedKeys = {}
    -- stable config keys (tab + title): saved configs keep working when widgets are added or reordered
    local function nextKey(p, scope, title)
        if title == nil then keyCounter = keyCounter + 1 return "hub_" .. p .. keyCounter end
        local base = "hub_" .. p .. "_" .. tostring(scope or "") .. "_" .. tostring(title)
        local k, n = base, 1
        while usedKeys[k] do n = n + 1; k = base .. "_" .. n end
        usedKeys[k] = true
        return k
    end
    UIKit.Registry = {}   -- every toggle / slider / dropdown with its default ("Reset All Settings")
    -- gives returned elements a .Parent (status loops stop once the element is destroyed)
    local function withParent(t)
        return setmetatable(t, { __index = function(_, k)
            if k == "Parent" then
                local f = rawget(t, "ElementFrame")
                return f and f.Parent or nil
            end
        end })
    end
    local function plain(t) return stripRich(t) end

    -- sections are re-balanced into two columns shortly after the last one was added
    local colToken = 0
    local function scheduleColumns()
        if not State.TwoColumns then return end
        colToken = colToken + 1
        local my = colToken
        task.delay(0.35, function()
            if my == colToken and State.TwoColumns then pcall(applyColumns, true) end
        end)
    end

    -- optional description line under a row; returns container, addDesc(), wrapper
    local function withDesc(parent, desc)
        if type(desc) ~= "string" or desc == "" then return parent, nil, nil end
        local wf = Instance.new("Frame")
        wf.Name = "DescWrap"
        wf.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
        wf.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        wf.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        wf.BorderSizePixel = 0
        wf.Parent = parent
        local l = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        l.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        l.Padding = UDim.new(0, 4)
        l.Parent = wf
        return wf, function()
            local d = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            d.LayoutOrder = 5
            d.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
            d.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
            d.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            d.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
            d.TextSize = 11
            d.TextColor3 = P.Muted
            d.TextWrapped = true
            d.RichText = true
            d.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            d.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
            d.Text = Cfg.trimDesc(desc)
            d.Parent = wf
            local pd = Instance.new("UIPadding")
            pd.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 4)
            pd.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 4)
            pd.Parent = d
        end, wf
    end

    -- ---------- HStack + ViewportButton (3D preview tiles) ----------
    local groups = {}
    local function newHStack(parent, cols)
        cols = cols or 3
        local gap = 6
        local row = Instance.new("Frame")
        row.Name = "HStack"
        row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 100)
        row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        row.BorderSizePixel = 0
        row.Parent = parent
        local l = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        l.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
        l.Padding = UDim.new(0, gap)
        l.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        l.Parent = row
        local obj = { ElementFrame = row }

        function obj:ViewportButton(opts)
            opts = opts or {}
            local h = opts.Height or 100
            row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, h)
            local outline = opts.OutlineColor or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
            local toggleable = opts.Toggleable ~= false

            local Main = Instance.new("TextButton")
            Main.Name = "Main"
            Main.Size = UDim2.new(1 / cols, -(gap * (cols - 1)) / cols, 1, 0)
            Main.BackgroundColor3 = P.Bg3
            Main.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
            Main.BorderSizePixel = 0
            Main.AutoButtonColor = false
            Main.Text = ""
            Main.ClipsDescendants = true
            Main.ZIndex = 3
            Main.Parent = row
            corner(Main, 9)
            local st = stroke(Main, P.Line, 1, 0.6)

            -- optional gradient background: opts.Gradient = { Color3, Color3 }
            local gradBg
            if typeof(opts.Gradient) == "table" and opts.Gradient[1] and opts.Gradient[2] then
                gradBg = Instance.new("Frame")
                gradBg.Name = "GradientBg"
                gradBg.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                gradBg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
                gradBg.BackgroundTransparency = 0.6
                gradBg.BorderSizePixel = 0
                gradBg.Active = false
                gradBg.ZIndex = 3
                gradBg:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true)
                gradBg.Parent = Main
                corner(gradBg, 9)
                local gg = Instance.new("UIGradient")
                gg.Color = ColorSequence.new(opts.Gradient[1], opts.Gradient[2])
                gg.Rotation = opts.GradientRotation or 55
                gg.Parent = gradBg
            end

            local canvas = Instance.new("Frame")
            canvas.Name = "Canvas"
            canvas.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
            canvas.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            canvas.ZIndex = 3
            canvas.Active = false
            canvas.Parent = Main

            local vp = Instance.new("ViewportFrame")
            vp.Name = "Viewport"
            vp.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -20)
            vp.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            vp.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 160)
            vp.LightColor = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
            vp.LightDirection = Vector3.new(-1, -1.5, -1)
            vp.ZIndex = 4
            vp.Active = false
            vp.Parent = canvas
            local cam = Instance.new("Camera")
            cam.FieldOfView = 50
            cam.Parent = vp
            vp.CurrentCamera = cam

            local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            label.Name = "Label"
            label.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)
            label.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 8, 1, -5)
            label.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 14)
            label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            label.TextSize = 10
            label.TextColor3 = P.Text
            label.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
            label.Text = tostring(opts.Title or "")
            label.ZIndex = 6
            label.Parent = canvas
            if gradBg then
                label.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
                label.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
                label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.7
                label:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true)
            end

            local classImg
            if (type(opts.Image) == "string" and opts.Image ~= "") or opts.Icon then
                vp.Visible = false
                if type(opts.Image) == "string" and opts.Image ~= "" then
                    -- picture zoomed in so it fills the whole button (no gaps)
                    classImg = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
                    classImg.Name = "ClassImage"
                    classImg.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                    classImg.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
                    classImg.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    classImg.Image = opts.Image
                    classImg.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
                    classImg.ImageTransparency = 0.35
                    classImg.ZIndex = 4
                    classImg.Active = false
                    classImg.Parent = canvas
                    corner(classImg, 9)
                end
                -- gradient strip behind the name at the bottom of the button
                local g1 = (opts.Gradient and opts.Gradient[1]) or Color3.fromRGB(20, 22, 30)
                local g2 = (opts.Gradient and opts.Gradient[2]) or g1
                local bar = Instance.new("Frame")
                bar.Name = "DescBar"
                bar.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1)
                bar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
                bar.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36)
                bar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
                bar.BorderSizePixel = 0
                bar.Active = false
                bar.ZIndex = 5
                bar:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true)
                bar.Parent = canvas
                corner(bar, 9)
                local bg = Instance.new("UIGradient")
                bg.Rotation = 90
                bg.Color = ColorSequence.new(g2, g1)
                bg.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.45, 0.35),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0),
                })
                bg.Parent = bar
                -- icon next to the text
                if opts.Icon then
                    local iconImg = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
                    iconImg.Name = "ClassIcon"
                    iconImg.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1)
                    iconImg.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14)
                    iconImg.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    iconImg.ImageColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
                    iconImg.ImageTransparency = 1
                    iconImg.ZIndex = 6
                    iconImg.Active = false
                    iconImg:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true)
                    iconImg.Parent = canvas
                    local function placeIcon()
                        local tw = label.TextBounds.X / math.max(MainScale.Scale, 0.01)
                        iconImg.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 8 - tw / 2 - 5, 1, -5)
                    end
                    label:GetPropertyChangedSignal("TextBounds"):Connect(placeIcon)
                    placeIcon()
                    local names = type(opts.Icon) == "table" and opts.Icon or { opts.Icon }
                    task.spawn(function()
                        local t0 = os.clock()
                        while not LucideReady and os.clock() - t0 < 30 do task.wait(0.2) end
                        if not LucideReady then return end
                        for _, n in ipairs(names) do
                            if setIcon(iconImg, n, Color3.new(1, 1, 1)) then
                                iconImg.ImageTransparency = 1
                                TweenService:Create(iconImg, TweenInfo.new(0.3), { ImageTransparency = 0 }):Play()
                                placeIcon()
                                return
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end
            elseif typeof(opts.Object) == "Instance" then
                pcall(function()
                    local clone = opts.Clone and opts.Object:Clone() or opts.Object
                    local m = clone
                    if not clone:IsA("Model") then
                        m = Instance.new("Model")
                        clone.Parent = m
                    end
                    for _, d in ipairs(m:GetDescendants()) do
                        if d:IsA("BasePart") then d.Anchored = true end
                        if d:IsA("Script") or d:IsA("LocalScript") then d:Destroy() end
                    end
                    m.Parent = vp
                    local cf, size = m:GetBoundingBox()
                    local center = cf.Position
                    local extent = size.Magnitude * 1.2
                    local offset = CFrame.Angles(math.rad(opts.AngleX or 0), math.rad(opts.AngleY or 0), 0)
                        * Vector3.new(0, extent * 0.1, extent * (opts.Zoom or 0.9))
                    cam.CFrame = CFrame.new(center + offset, center)
                end)
            end

            local api = { Main = Main }
            local group = opts.SelectionGroup
            if group then
                groups[group] = groups[group] or {}
                table.insert(groups[group], api)
            end
            local sel = false
            local fx = TweenInfo.new(0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
            function api.SetVisual(on, animate)
                sel = on
                local goalStroke = {
                    Color = on and outline or P.Line,
                    Thickness = on and 2 or 1,
                    Transparency = on and 0 or 0.6,
                }
                local goalBg = { BackgroundTransparency = on and 0.05 or 0.2 }
                local goalGrad = { BackgroundTransparency = on and 0 or 0.5 }
                if animate then   -- outline fades in when picked, fades out when another one is picked
                    TweenService:Create(st, fx, goalStroke):Play()
                    TweenService:Create(Main, fx, goalBg):Play()
                    if gradBg then TweenService:Create(gradBg, fx, goalGrad):Play() end
                    if classImg then TweenService:Create(classImg, fx, { ImageTransparency = on and 0 or 0.35 }):Play() end
                else
                    st.Color, st.Thickness, st.Transparency = goalStroke.Color, goalStroke.Thickness, goalStroke.Transparency
                    Main.BackgroundTransparency = goalBg.BackgroundTransparency
                    if gradBg then gradBg.BackgroundTransparency = goalGrad.BackgroundTransparency end
                    if classImg then classImg.ImageTransparency = on and 0 or 0.35 end
                end
            end
            local function choose(on, fire)
                if on and group then
                    for _, other in ipairs(groups[group]) do
                        if other ~= api then other.SetVisual(false, true) end
                    end
                end
                api.SetVisual(on, true)
                if fire and opts.Callback then task.spawn(opts.Callback, on) end
            end
            function api.Select() choose(true, true) end
            Main.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                if sel then
                    if toggleable then choose(false, true) end
                else
                    choose(true, true)
                end
            end)
            if opts.Selected then api.SetVisual(true) end
            return api
        end
        return obj
    end

    -- link -> displayable asset (downloads it once into the workspace; Roblox can't show web links directly)
    local function resolveImage(link)
        if type(link) ~= "string" or link == "" then return nil end
        if link:match("^rbxasset") then return link end
        if link:match("^%d+$") then return "rbxassetid://" .. link end
        local ok, result = pcall(function()
            local ext  = link:match("%.(%w+)$") or "png"
            local name = "NauruHub_img_" .. link:gsub("[^%w]", ""):sub(-30) .. "." .. ext
            if not (isfile and isfile(name)) then writefile(name, game:HttpGet(link)) end
            return getcustomasset(name)
        end)
        return ok and result or nil
    end

    -- ---------- tab factory ----------
    local PCOL = {
        Grey   = Color3.fromHex("#484848"),
        Blue   = Color3.fromHex("#039be5"),
        Orange = Color3.fromRGB(240, 150, 70),
        Green  = Color3.fromHex("#43a047"),
    }

    local function makeTab(pageName, iconName)
        local tab = {}
        local cur
        local curSub          -- page of the sub-tab new sections are added to (nil = the tab itself)
        local subs, subBar, subActive, subOrder = {}, nil, nil, 0
        local SUB_TOP = 40    -- height of the sub-tab bar
        local subRecenter     -- lays the sub-tab pills out (names always shown: centred / tighter / smaller text / scrolling)
        local function target() return curSub or pages[pageName] end
        local function cont()
            if not cur then
                cur = section(target(), string.upper(pageName), iconName, false)
                scheduleColumns()
            end
            return cur
        end

        local function styleSub(sb, on, animate)
            local goalBtn = { BackgroundColor3 = on and P.Accent or P.Bg3, BackgroundTransparency = on and 0.9 or 0.55 }
            local goalSt  = { Color = on and P.Accent or P.Line, Transparency = on and 0.7 or 0.75 }
            local goalLbl = { TextColor3 = on and P.Text or P.Muted }
            local goalIc  = { ImageColor3 = on and P.Text or P.Muted }
            if animate then
                local ti = TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
                TweenService:Create(sb.btn, ti, goalBtn):Play()
                TweenService:Create(sb.st, ti, goalSt):Play()
                TweenService:Create(sb.lbl, ti, goalLbl):Play()
                TweenService:Create(sb.ic, ti, goalIc):Play()
            else
                for k, v in pairs(goalBtn) do sb.btn[k] = v end
                for k, v in pairs(goalSt) do sb.st[k] = v end
                for k, v in pairs(goalLbl) do sb.lbl[k] = v end
                for k, v in pairs(goalIc) do sb.ic[k] = v end
            end
        end

        local subGen = 0
        local function selectSub(title, animate)
            local picked = subs[title]
            if not picked then return end
            local prev = subActive and subs[subActive] or nil
            for _, sb in pairs(subs) do
                styleSub(sb, sb == picked, animate)
            end
            subActive = title
            Cfg.ActiveSub[pageName] = picked.page
            subGen = subGen + 1
            local gen = subGen
            if animate and prev and prev ~= picked and picked.wrap and prev.wrap then
                -- the old page slides out and fades while the new one slides in from the same side and fades in;
                -- the direction follows the order of the sub-tab buttons
                local dir = picked.order > prev.order and 1 or -1
                for _, sb in pairs(subs) do
                    if sb ~= picked and sb ~= prev then
                        sb.wrap.Visible = false
                        sb.page.Visible = false
                    end
                end
                prev.page.Visible = true
                if not prev.wrap.Visible then
                    prev.wrap.GroupTransparency = 0
                    prev.wrap.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
                    prev.wrap.Visible = true
                end
                TweenService:Create(prev.wrap, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
                    { GroupTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-dir * 28, 0) }):Play()
                picked.page.Visible = true
                picked.wrap.GroupTransparency = 1
                picked.wrap.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(dir * 28, 0)
                picked.wrap.Visible = true
                TweenService:Create(picked.wrap, TweenInfo.new(0.34, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
                    { GroupTransparency = 0, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0) }):Play()
                task.delay(0.22, function()
                    if gen == subGen then
                        prev.wrap.Visible = false
                        prev.page.Visible = false
                    end
                end)
            else
                for _, sb in pairs(subs) do
                    local on = (sb == picked)
                    sb.page.Visible = on
                    if sb.wrap then
                        sb.wrap.Visible = on
                        sb.wrap.GroupTransparency = 0
                        sb.wrap.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        -- tab:SubTab({ Title = "Items", Icon = "backpack" })
        -- Adds a button to the sub-tab bar at the top of the tab. Everything added to this tab afterwards
        -- (Section / Toggle / ...) lands inside that sub-tab until the next SubTab call.
        -- Call it before adding the tab's first section. Calling it again with the same title reuses it.
        function tab:SubTab(o)
            o = type(o) == "table" and o or { Title = o }
            local title = tostring(o.Title or "Tab")
            local sb = subs[title]
            if not sb then
                local base = pages[pageName]
                if not subBar then
                    -- the tab's own page stops being a scroller and becomes a container (bar + sub pages)
                    base:SetAttribute("HasSubTabs", true)
                    base.ScrollingEnabled = false
                    base.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
                    base.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
                    base.ScrollBarThickness = 0
                    for _, ch in ipairs(base:GetChildren()) do
                        if ch:IsA("UIPadding") or ch:IsA("UIListLayout") then ch:Destroy() end
                    end
                    subBar = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
                    subBar.Name = "SubTabBar"
                    subBar.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, SUB_TOP)
                    subBar.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    subBar.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    subBar.ScrollBarThickness = 0
                    subBar.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.X
                    subBar.ElasticBehavior = Enum.ElasticBehavior.Never
                    subBar.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
                    subBar.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
                    subBar.ZIndex = 5
                    subBar.Parent = base
                    -- every sub page lives inside this group so a sub-tab switch can fade the page as a whole
                    local host = Instance.new("CanvasGroup")
                    host.Name = "SubHost"
                    host.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, SUB_TOP)
                    host.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -SUB_TOP)
                    host.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    host.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    host.ZIndex = 4
                    host.Parent = base
                    Cfg.SubHosts = Cfg.SubHosts or {}
                    Cfg.SubHosts[pageName] = host
                    -- divider marking where the sub-tab bar ends (fades out at both ends)
                    local subDiv = Instance.new("Frame")
                    subDiv.Name = "SubTabDivider"
                    subDiv.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, SUB_TOP - 2)
                    subDiv.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 1)
                    subDiv.BackgroundColor3 = P.Line2
                    subDiv.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
                    subDiv.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    subDiv.ZIndex = 5
                    subDiv.Parent = base
                    local sdg = Instance.new("UIGradient")
                    sdg.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
                        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
                        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.12, 0),
                        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.88, 0),
                        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
                    })
                    sdg.Parent = subDiv
                    local bp = Instance.new("UIPadding")
                    bp.PaddingLeft, bp.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 10), UDim.new(0, 10)
                    bp.PaddingTop, bp.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 3), UDim.new(0, 5)   -- inset so the pills' outlines are never clipped by the bar
                    bp.Parent = subBar
                    local bl = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
                    bl.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
                    bl.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
                    bl.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
                    bl.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
                    bl.Parent = subBar
                    -- layout: the sub-tab names are ALWAYS shown. When they do not fit they are made to fit, in this order:
                    --   1) natural size, centred   2) tighter pills   3) smaller text (down to 9)   4) scroll sideways
                    local TextSvc = game:GetService("TextService")
                    local MIN_SUB_TEXT = 9
                    subRecenter = function()
                        local sc = math.max(MainScale.Scale, 0.01)
                        local barW = subBar.AbsoluteSize.X / sc
                        if barW <= 0 then return end
                        local n = 0
                        for _ in pairs(subs) do n = n + 1 end
                        if n == 0 then return end
                        local avail = barW - 20
                        local wanted = Cfg.textBoost and Cfg.textBoost(13) or 13   -- the size Auto Text Size wants for the names
                        local function widthAt(size, compact)
                            local total = 0
                            for _, x in pairs(subs) do
                                local ok, sz = pcall(TextSvc.GetTextSize, TextSvc, x.lbl.Text, size, Enum.Font.GothamBold, Vector2.new(1000, 100))
                                local tw = ok and sz.X or (#x.lbl.Text * size * 0.62)
                                -- padding + icon of the pill (fullW0 - textW0), a little less padding when compact
                                total = total + tw + (x.fullW0 - x.textW0) - (compact and 10 or 0)
                            end
                            return total + (compact and 4 or 6) * (n - 1)
                        end
                        local size, compact = wanted, false
                        if widthAt(size, false) > avail then
                            compact = true
                            while size > MIN_SUB_TEXT and widthAt(size, true) > avail do size = size - 1 end
                        end
                        local total = widthAt(size, compact)
                        bl.Padding = UDim.new(0, compact and 4 or 6)
                        local pxp = compact and 8 or 13
                        for _, x in pairs(subs) do
                            x.lbl:SetAttribute("NoFit", true)   -- sized here; Auto Text Fit would blow the names back up
                            if x.lbl.TextSize ~= size then x.lbl.TextSize = size end
                            x.lbl.Visible = true
                            x.btn.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X
                            x.btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
                            local xp = x.btn:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIPadding")
                            if xp then xp.PaddingLeft, xp.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, pxp), UDim.new(0, pxp) end
                        end
                        if total <= avail then
                            local side = math.floor((barW - total) / 2)
                            bp.PaddingLeft, bp.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, side), UDim.new(0, side)
                            subBar.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
                        else
                            bp.PaddingLeft, bp.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 10), UDim.new(0, 10)
                            subBar.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, total + 20, 0, 0)
                        end
                    end
                    -- Auto Text Size changes (screen size / menu scale / toggle) re-run the layout
                    Cfg.SubRecenters = Cfg.SubRecenters or {}
                    table.insert(Cfg.SubRecenters, function() if subRecenter then subRecenter() end end)
                    subBar:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(subRecenter)
                    task.defer(subRecenter)
                end

                subOrder = subOrder + 1
                local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
                btn.Name = "Sub_" .. title
                btn.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X
                btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
                btn.BackgroundColor3 = P.Bg3
                btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.55
                btn.Text = ""
                btn.AutoButtonColor = false
                btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
                btn.LayoutOrder = subOrder
                btn.ZIndex = 6
                btn.Parent = subBar
                corner(btn, 8)
                local st = stroke(btn, P.Line, 1, 0.75)
                local pd = Instance.new("UIPadding")
                pd.PaddingLeft, pd.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 13), UDim.new(0, 13)
                pd.Parent = btn
                local il = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
                il.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
                il.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
                il.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
                il.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
                il.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
                il.Parent = btn
                local ic = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
                ic.Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16)
                ic.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                ic.ImageColor3 = P.Muted
                ic.Visible = false
                ic.LayoutOrder = 1
                ic.ZIndex = 7
                ic.Parent = btn
                local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                lbl.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X
                lbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
                lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                lbl.TextSize = 13
                lbl.TextColor3 = P.Muted
                lbl.Text = title
                lbl.LayoutOrder = 2
                lbl.ZIndex = 7
                lbl:SetAttribute("AutoGrow", true)   -- sizes itself to its text -> Auto Text Size can enlarge it freely
                lbl.Parent = btn
                local okW, szW = pcall(function()
                    return game:GetService("TextService"):GetTextSize(title, 13, Enum.Font.GothamBold, Vector2.new(1000, 100))
                end)
                local fullW = 26 + (o.Icon and 22 or 0) + (okW and szW.X or #title * 8) + 2

                local page = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
                page.Name = "SubPage_" .. title
                page.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
                page.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                page.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                page.BorderSizePixel = 0
                page.ScrollBarThickness = 0
                page.ScrollBarImageColor3 = P.Line2
                page.ScrollBarImageTransparency = 0.4
                page.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
                page.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y
                page.ElasticBehavior = Enum.ElasticBehavior.Never
                page.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
                page.Visible = false
                page.ZIndex = 4
                page:SetAttribute("SubName", title)
                -- each sub page sits in its own group so it can fade / slide on its own during a sub-tab switch
                local wrap = Instance.new("CanvasGroup")
                wrap.Name = "SubWrap_" .. title
                wrap.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                wrap.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                wrap.BorderSizePixel = 0
                wrap.ZIndex = 4
                wrap.Visible = false
                wrap.Parent = Cfg.SubHosts[pageName]
                page.Parent = wrap
                autoFadeScrollbar(page, 0.4)
                local pp = Instance.new("UIPadding")
                pp.PaddingTop, pp.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 0), UDim.new(0, 16)
                pp.PaddingLeft, pp.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 10), UDim.new(0, 10)
                pp.Parent = page
                local pl = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
                pl.Padding = UDim.new(0, 8)
                pl.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
                pl.Parent = page

                sb = { btn = btn, st = st, lbl = lbl, ic = ic, page = page, wrap = wrap, order = subOrder, fullW = fullW, iconReady = false,
                       fullW0 = fullW, textW0 = (okW and szW.X or #title * 8) }
                lbl:GetPropertyChangedSignal("TextSize"):Connect(function()
                    task.defer(function() if subRecenter then subRecenter() end end)   -- bigger text -> pills wider -> re-balance
                end)
                subs[title] = sb
                Cfg.SubPages[pageName .. "/" .. title] = page
                Cfg.SubSelect[page] = function() selectSub(title, true) end
                btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    if subActive ~= title then selectSub(title, true) end
                end)
                -- hover: inactive pills brighten slightly, the open one is left alone
                local hti = TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
                btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                    if subActive == title then return end
                    TweenService:Create(btn, hti, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.35 }):Play()
                    TweenService:Create(st, hti, { Transparency = 0.45 }):Play()
                    TweenService:Create(lbl, hti, { TextColor3 = P.Text }):Play()
                    TweenService:Create(ic, hti, { ImageColor3 = P.Text }):Play()
                end)
                btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                    if subActive == title then return end
                    TweenService:Create(btn, hti, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.55 }):Play()
                    TweenService:Create(st, hti, { Transparency = 0.75 }):Play()
                    TweenService:Create(lbl, hti, { TextColor3 = P.Muted }):Play()
                    TweenService:Create(ic, hti, { ImageColor3 = P.Muted }):Play()
                end)
                if not subActive then selectSub(title, false) end
                if o.Icon then
                    task.spawn(function()
                        local t0 = os.clock()
                        while not LucideReady and os.clock() - t0 < 30 do task.wait(0.2) end
                        if LucideReady and setIcon(ic, o.Icon, ic.ImageColor3) then
                            ic.Visible = true
                            sb.iconReady = true
                            if subRecenter then subRecenter() end
                        end
                    end)
                end
                if subRecenter then subRecenter() end
            end
            curSub = sb.page
            cur = nil
            scheduleColumns()
            return { Select = function() selectSub(title, true) end }
        end

        function tab:Section(o)
            cur = section(target(), string.upper(tostring(o.Title or "")), o.Icon, false, o.Desc)
            if o.Column == "Left" then cur:SetAttribute("ForceCol", "L") elseif o.Column == "Right" then cur:SetAttribute("ForceCol", "R") end
            scheduleColumns()
            for _, t in ipairs(o.Tags or {}) do
                pcall(sectionTag, cur, plain(t.Title), t.Color or "new")
            end
            return withParent({ ElementFrame = cur })
        end

        function tab:Toggle(o)
            local key = nextKey("tg", pageName, o.Title)
            local def = o.Default
            if def == nil then def = o.Value end
            def = (def == true)
            State[key] = def
            local userCb = o.Callback
            local w = toggle(cont(), o.Title or "", def, function(v)
                State[key] = (v == true)
                if userCb then userCb(v) end
            end, key, o.Icon, o.Desc)
            for _, t in ipairs(o.Tags or {}) do addTag(w, plain(t.Title), t.Color or "new") end
            table.insert(UIKit.Registry, { kind = "toggle", key = key, default = def })
            return withParent({
                ElementFrame = w.Instance,
                Set = function(_, v)
                    if Cfg.Toggles[key] then Cfg.Toggles[key](v == true) end
                end,
            })
        end

        function tab:Slider(o)
            local key = nextKey("sl", pageName, o.Title)
            local v = o.Value or {}
            local mn, mx = v.Min or 0, v.Max or 100
            local def = v.Default or mn
            Values[key] = def
            local parent, addDesc, frame = withDesc(cont(), o.Desc)
            local row = slider(parent, plain(o.Title or ""), mn, mx, key, o.Callback, o.Icon, false, o.Step)
            if addDesc then addDesc() end
            -- loading a config / resetting also pushes the value through the callback
            local rawSet = SliderSetters[key]
            SliderSetters[key] = function(x)
                rawSet(x)
                if o.Callback then o.Callback(Values[key]) end
            end
            table.insert(UIKit.Registry, { kind = "slider", key = key, default = def })
            return withParent({
                ElementFrame = frame or row,
                Set = function(_, x) SliderSetters[key](tonumber(x) or def) end,
            })
        end

        function tab:Dropdown(o)
            local key = nextKey("dd", pageName, o.Title)
            local multi = o.Multi == true
            local vals = {}
            for i, x in ipairs(o.Values or {}) do vals[i] = x end
            local def
            if multi then
                local init = {}
                if type(o.Value) == "table" then for _, x in ipairs(o.Value) do init[#init + 1] = x end end
                Values[key] = init
                def = table.clone(init)
            else
                Values[key] = o.Value ~= nil and o.Value or vals[1]
                def = Values[key]
            end
            local parent, addDesc, frame = withDesc(cont(), o.Desc)
            local w = dropdown(parent, plain(o.Title or ""), vals, key, o.Callback, o.Icon, multi, o.SearchBarEnabled == true, { AllowNone = o.AllowNone, Icons = o.Icons })
            if addDesc then addDesc() end
            DropdownSetters[key] = function(x) w.Set(x, false) end   -- config load / reset also runs the callback
            table.insert(UIKit.Registry, { kind = "dropdown", key = key, default = def })
            return withParent({
                ElementFrame = frame or w.Instance,
                Refresh  = function(_, names)
                    local c = {}
                    for i, x in ipairs(names or {}) do c[i] = x end
                    w.SetOptions(c)
                end,
                SetValue = function(_, x) w.Set(x, true) end,
                Set      = function(_, x) w.Set(x, false) end,
                Select   = function(_, x) w.Set(x, false) end,
            })
        end

        function tab:Input(o)
            local key = nextKey("in", pageName, o.Title)
            Values[key] = tostring(o.Default or o.Value or "")
            local w = UIKit.input(cont(), plain(o.Title or ""), Values[key], o.Placeholder, o.Callback, key, o.Icon,
                { Numeric = o.Numeric == true, MaxLength = o.MaxLength })
            return withParent({
                ElementFrame = w.Instance,
                Get = function() return w.Get() end,
                Set = function(_, v) w.Set(v, false) end,
            })
        end

        function tab:Button(o)
            local parent, addDesc, frame = withDesc(cont(), o.Desc)
            local b = button(parent, plain(o.Title or ""), o.Callback, o.Icon)
            if addDesc then addDesc() end
            return withParent({ ElementFrame = frame or b })
        end

        function tab:Colorpicker(o)
            local key = nextKey("cp", pageName, o.Title)
            Values[key] = typeof(o.Default) == "Color3" and colorToHex(o.Default) or "#FFFFFF"
            local w = colorPicker(cont(), plain(o.Title or ""), key, o.Callback, o.Icon)
            -- loading a config / resetting also pushes the colour through the callback
            local rawSet = ColorSetters[key]
            if rawSet and o.Callback then
                ColorSetters[key] = function(hex)
                    rawSet(hex)
                    local c = hexToColor(hex)
                    if c then o.Callback(c) end
                end
            end
            return { ElementFrame = w.Instance }
        end

        -- CheckboxGroup is now drawn as a dropdown (same options / callbacks as before)
        function tab:CheckboxGroup(o)
            local key = nextKey("dd", pageName, o.Title)
            local multi = o.Multi == true
            local titles, byTitle, icons, inputs = {}, {}, {}, {}
            for i, op in ipairs(o.Options or {}) do
                local t = tostring(op.Title or op.Value)
                titles[i] = t
                byTitle[t] = op.Value
                if op.Icon then icons[t] = op.Icon end
                if op.Input then inputs[t] = op.Input end
            end
            if multi then
                local init = {}
                for i, op in ipairs(o.Options or {}) do if op.Default then init[#init + 1] = titles[i] end end
                if #init == 0 and o.AllowNone == false and titles[1] then init[1] = titles[1] end
                Values[key] = init
            else
                local d
                for i, op in ipairs(o.Options or {}) do if op.Default then d = titles[i] end end
                Values[key] = d or titles[1] or ""
            end
            local function report(v)
                if not o.Callback then return end
                if multi then
                    local out = {}
                    for _, t in ipairs(v) do out[#out + 1] = byTitle[t] end
                    o.Callback(out)
                else
                    o.Callback(byTitle[v])
                end
            end
            local w = dropdown(cont(), plain(o.Title or ""), titles, key, report, o.Icon, multi, o.SearchBarEnabled == true, {
                AllowNone = o.AllowNone, Icons = icons, Inputs = inputs,
            })
            DropdownSetters[key] = function(v) w.Set(v, false) end   -- loading a config also re-applies the callback
            -- report the defaults once (the old checkbox group did the same)
            local cur = Values[key]
            if multi then cur = table.clone(cur) end
            pcall(report, cur)
            return {
                ElementFrame = w.Instance,
                SetValue = function(_, v) w.Set(v, true) end,
            }
        end

        function tab:Paragraph(o)
            local api = UIKit.paragraph(cont(), o.Title or "", o.Desc or "", {
                Image = o.Image, Thumbnail = o.Thumbnail,
                ImageSize = o.ImageSize, ThumbnailHeight = o.ThumbnailHeight,
                FullWidthThumbnail = o.FullWidthThumbnail, FadeBottom = o.FadeBottom,
            })
            local row = api.Instance
            local col = o.Color and PCOL[o.Color]
            if col then   -- solid tinted card (no see-through background)
                row:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true)
                row.BackgroundColor3 = col
                row.BackgroundTransparency = 0
                local st = row:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
                if st then st.Transparency = 1 end   -- no outline
                for _, d in ipairs(row:GetDescendants()) do   -- readable text on the solid colour
                    if d:IsA("TextLabel") then d.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1) end
                end
            end
            for _, b in ipairs(o.Buttons or {}) do
                button(cont(), plain(b.Title or ""), b.Callback, b.Icon)
            end
            return withParent({
                ElementFrame = row,
                SetDesc  = function(_, t) api.SetText(t) end,
                SetTitle = function(_, t) api.SetTitle(t) end,
            })
        end

        function tab:Divider()
            local api = UIKit.divider(cont(), nil)
            return { ElementFrame = api.Instance }
        end

        function tab:Space()
            local f = Instance.new("Frame")
            f.Name = "Space"
            f.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 2)
            f.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            f.BorderSizePixel = 0
            f.Parent = cont()
            return { ElementFrame = f }
        end

        -- stat card: big number on the left, an arrow, and subtle text on the right
        function tab:Stat(o)
            o = o or {}
            local card = Instance.new("Frame")
            card.Name = "Stat"
            card.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 54)
            card.BackgroundColor3 = P.Bg3
            card.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
            card.BorderSizePixel = 0
            card.ZIndex = 3
            card.Parent = cont()
            corner(card, 10)
            stroke(card, P.Line, 1, 0.6)

            local num = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            num.Name = "Number"
            num.Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 0)
            num.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
            num.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X
            num.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            num.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            num.TextSize = 36
            num.TextColor3 = P.Good
            num.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            num.Text = tostring(o.Value or 0)
            num.ZIndex = 4
            num.Parent = card

            local arrow = Instance.new("Frame")   -- sits just after the number
            arrow.Name = "Arrow"
            arrow.Size = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 18)
            arrow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            arrow.ZIndex = 4
            arrow.Parent = card
            local fb = Instance.new("TextLabel")   -- shown until the Lucide icon is ready
            fb.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
            fb.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            fb.Text = "\226\134\146"
            fb.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            fb.TextSize = 16
            fb.TextColor3 = P.Muted
            fb.ZIndex = 5
            fb.Parent = arrow
            local img = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
            img.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
            img.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            img.ImageColor3 = P.Muted
            img.Visible = false
            img.ZIndex = 5
            img.Parent = arrow
            task.spawn(function()
                local t0 = os.clock()
                while not LucideReady and os.clock() - t0 < 30 do task.wait(0.2) end
                if LucideReady and setIcon(img, "arrow-right", P.Muted) then
                    img.Visible = true
                    fb.Visible = false
                end
            end)
            local txt = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            txt.Name = "Caption"
            txt.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
            txt.Position = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 0)
            txt.Size = UDim2.new(0.42, 0, 1, 0)
            txt.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            txt.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
            txt.TextSize = 12
            txt.TextColor3 = P.Muted
            txt.TextTransparency = 0.15
            txt.TextWrapped = true
            txt.RichText = true
            txt.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
            txt.Text = tostring(o.Text or "")
            txt.ZIndex = 4
            txt.Parent = card

            -- The arrow is anchored just left of the caption (it never moves when the number grows).
            -- A long number shrinks to fit the room on the left instead of pushing the arrow into the text.
            local function layoutStat()
                local sc = math.max(MainScale.Scale, 0.01)
                local cardW = card.AbsoluteSize.X / sc
                if cardW <= 0 then return end
                local capRight = cardW + txt.Position.X.Offset
                local capW = math.min(txt.TextBounds.X / sc, cardW * 0.42)
                local arrowX = math.max(56, capRight - capW - 32 - 18)   -- 32px of air between the arrow and the caption text
                arrow.Position = UDim2.new(0, arrowX, 0.5, -9)
                local avail = arrowX - 10 - 16
                local okT, sz = pcall(function()
                    return game:GetService("TextService"):GetTextSize(num.Text, 36, Enum.Font.GothamBold, Vector2.new(10000, 100))
                end)
                local w36 = okT and sz.X or (#num.Text * 24)
                num.TextSize = (w36 > avail and avail > 0) and math.max(16, math.floor(36 * avail / w36)) or 36
            end
            Cfg.statLayout = Cfg.statLayout or setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
            Cfg.statLayout[card] = layoutStat
            card:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(layoutStat)
            txt:GetPropertyChangedSignal("TextBounds"):Connect(layoutStat)
            num:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(layoutStat)
            layoutStat()

            -- optional picture right next to the caption (o.Image = link or asset id)
            if o.Image then
                local pic = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
                pic.Name = "Picture"
                pic.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
                pic.Position = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0.5, 0)
                pic.Size = UDim2.new(0, 44, 0, 44)
                pic.BackgroundColor3 = P.Bg4
                pic.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                pic.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit   -- Fit shows the whole picture (Crop cut it in half)
                pic.Visible = false
                pic.ZIndex = 4
                pic.Parent = card
                corner(pic, 10)
                task.spawn(function()
                    local asset = resolveImage(o.Image)
                    if not asset or not pic.Parent then return end
                    pic.Image = asset
                    pic.Visible = true
                    txt.Position = UDim2.new(1, -(12 + 44 + 12), 0, 0)   -- caption sits right next to the picture
                    if Cfg.statLayout and Cfg.statLayout[card] then Cfg.statLayout[card]() end
                end)
            end

            return {
                ElementFrame = card,
                SetValue = function(_, v) num.Text = tostring(v) end,
                SetText  = function(_, t) txt.Text = tostring(t) end,
            }
        end

        -- small arrow pointing down at whatever comes next (e.g. the item tiles)
        function tab:Arrow()
            local f = Instance.new("Frame")
            f.Name = "Arrow"
            f.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20)
            f.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            f.BorderSizePixel = 0
            f.ZIndex = 3
            f.Parent = cont()
            local fb = Instance.new("TextLabel")   -- shown until the Lucide icon is ready
            fb.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
            fb.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            fb.Text = "\226\134\147"
            fb.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            fb.TextSize = 18
            fb.TextColor3 = P.Accent
            fb.ZIndex = 4
            fb.Parent = f
            local img = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
            img.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            img.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
            img.Size = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 20)
            img.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            img.ImageColor3 = P.Accent
            img.Visible = false
            img.ZIndex = 4
            img.Parent = f
            task.spawn(function()
                local t0 = os.clock()
                while not LucideReady and os.clock() - t0 < 30 do task.wait(0.2) end
                if LucideReady and setIcon(img, "arrow-down", P.Accent) then
                    img.Visible = true
                    fb.Visible = false
                end
            end)
            return { ElementFrame = f }
        end

        function tab:HStack(cols)
            return newHStack(cont(), cols)
        end

        return tab
    end

    Hub.makeTab = makeTab
    Hub.Tabs = {}

    -- creates the sidebar button + page and returns a WindUI-style tab object
    function Hub.newTab(title, icon, desc)
        createTab(title, icon, desc)
        TAB_ORDER[#TAB_ORDER + 1] = title
        refreshTabsCanvas()
        local t = makeTab(title, icon)
        t.Title = title
        function t:Select() switchTab(title) end
        Hub.Tabs[title] = t
        return t
    end

    Hub.WindUI = {
        Notify = function(_, o)
            o = o or {}
            local icon = tostring(o.Icon or "")
            local kind = "info"
            if icon == "check" or icon == "check-check" then
                kind = "success"
            elseif icon == "triangle-alert" or icon == "alert-triangle" or icon == "circle-alert" then
                kind = "warning"
            end
            notify(tostring(o.Title or ""), tostring(o.Content or ""), kind, o.Duration or 4)
        end,
    }

    Hub.Window = {}
    function Hub.Window:Tab(cfg)
        cfg = cfg or {}
        return Hub.newTab(tostring(cfg.Title or "Tab"), cfg.Icon, cfg.Desc)
    end
    function Hub.Window:SelectTab(i)
        local n = TAB_ORDER[i]
        if n then switchTab(n) end
    end
    function Hub.Window:Tag() end
    function Hub.Window:OnDestroy(fn)
        screenGui.Destroying:Connect(function() pcall(fn) end)
    end
    function Hub.Window:Destroy()
        pcall(function() screenGui:Destroy() end)
    end

    -- restores every registered toggle / slider / dropdown to its default (runs their callbacks)
    function Hub.resetAll()
        local n = 0
        for _, e in ipairs(UIKit.Registry) do
            local ok = false
            if e.kind == "toggle" and Cfg.Toggles[e.key] then
                ok = pcall(Cfg.Toggles[e.key], e.default)
            elseif e.kind == "slider" and SliderSetters[e.key] then
                ok = pcall(SliderSetters[e.key], e.default)
            elseif e.kind == "dropdown" and DropdownSetters[e.key] then
                ok = pcall(DropdownSetters[e.key], type(e.default) == "table" and table.clone(e.default) or e.default)
            end
            if ok then n = n + 1 end
        end
        return n
    end
end


    -- ============================================================
    -- UI DECORATIONS  (Settings > UI Decorations)
    -- Detailed cat ears, bunny ears, devil horns, a halo, barnacles, a crab or a rotatable fungi garden that grow out from behind the window.
    -- They live on their own layer BEHIND the window (the window clips its own children), and are positioned
    -- every frame from the window's real on-screen rect, so they follow dragging, scaling and resizing.
    -- The settings (Values.DecorStyles / DecorSize) are polled, so loading a config
    -- or changing the theme re-applies them automatically.
    -- ============================================================
    do
        local okDecor, errDecor = pcall(function()
        local D = {
            STYLES   = { "Tentacles", "Cat", "Bunny", "Devil Horns", "Halo", "Barnacles", "Crab", "Ghost", "Fungi" },   -- also the draw order (back to front)
            -- "Own Colors" (default): every decoration uses its own colour below. Picking a palette colours them all the same.
            OWN      = { ["Tentacles"] = "Void Purple", ["Cat"] = "Sakura Pink", ["Bunny"] = "Ghost White", ["Devil Horns"] = "Blood Red", ["Halo"] = "Golden", ["Barnacles"] = "Deep Sea", ["Crab"] = "Crab Red", ["Ghost"] = "Ghost Spirit", ["Fungi"] = "Fly Agaric" },
            animate  = true,
            active   = {},
            born     = 0,
            shown    = false,
            lastPoll = 0,
            sig      = nil,
            rect     = { x = 0, y = 0, w = 0, h = 0 },
        }
        -- each decoration has a matching UI theme (added at the top of the file). Picking one switches to it; picking None
        -- goes back to the theme that was active before (unless the person already chose another theme by hand).
        D.THEME = { ["Tentacles"] = "Kraken", ["Cat"] = "Kitty", ["Bunny"] = "Bunny", ["Devil Horns"] = "Devil", ["Halo"] = "Heaven", ["Barnacles"] = "Reef", ["Crab"] = "Crab", ["Ghost"] = "Ghost", ["Fungi"] = "Fungi" }
        function D.themeFor(v)
            local name = D.THEME[v]
            if name then
                if not D.prevTheme then D.prevTheme = CurrentTheme end
                D.themeSet = name
                pcall(applyTheme, name)
            else
                if D.prevTheme and CurrentTheme == D.themeSet then pcall(applyTheme, D.prevTheme) end
                D.prevTheme, D.themeSet = nil, nil
            end
            if Cfg.refreshChips then pcall(Cfg.refreshChips) end
        end
        D.OPTIONS = { "None" }   -- the dropdown is single-select, so it needs an explicit "None"
        for _, n in ipairs(D.STYLES) do D.OPTIONS[#D.OPTIONS + 1] = n end
        Cfg.Decor = D

        local C3, BLACK, WHITE = Color3.fromRGB, Color3.new(0, 0, 0), Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        D.PALS = {
            ["Crab Red"]     = { base = C3(212, 36, 40),   light = C3(255, 104, 86),  dark = C3(84, 10, 16),   inner = C3(255, 206, 190) },
            ["Sakura Pink"]  = { base = C3(255, 140, 185), light = C3(255, 205, 225), dark = C3(150, 50, 95),  inner = C3(255, 228, 238) },
            ["Void Purple"]  = { base = C3(128, 70, 200),  light = C3(190, 140, 255), dark = C3(50, 20, 95),   inner = C3(222, 190, 255) },
            ["Blood Red"]    = { base = C3(200, 40, 55),   light = C3(255, 120, 120), dark = C3(80, 10, 20),   inner = C3(255, 175, 175) },
            ["Toxic Green"]  = { base = C3(70, 200, 90),   light = C3(170, 255, 150), dark = C3(20, 85, 40),   inner = C3(212, 255, 192) },
            ["Deep Sea"]     = { base = C3(30, 120, 190),  light = C3(110, 220, 230), dark = C3(10, 45, 90),   inner = C3(172, 242, 246) },
            ["Golden"]       = { base = C3(235, 175, 50),  light = C3(255, 232, 145), dark = C3(120, 75, 10),  inner = C3(255, 242, 195) },
            ["Ghost White"]  = { base = C3(225, 228, 240), light = C3(255, 255, 255), dark = C3(120, 125, 150), inner = C3(255, 205, 220) },
            ["Ghost Spirit"] = { base = C3(214, 220, 255), light = C3(255, 255, 255), dark = C3(62, 54, 112),  inner = C3(255, 170, 205) },
            ["Shadow Black"] = { base = C3(38, 38, 48),    light = C3(100, 100, 125), dark = C3(8, 8, 12),     inner = C3(125, 72, 95) },
            ["Fly Agaric"]  = { base = C3(224, 62, 70),   light = C3(255, 128, 118), dark = C3(86, 24, 40),   inner = C3(255, 244, 222) },
        }

        if type(Values.DecorStyles) ~= "string" then
            -- default (or an old multi-select config): keep only the first style that was picked
            local old = type(Values.DecorStyles) == "table" and Values.DecorStyles[1] or nil
            if old == "Cat Ears" then old = "Cat" elseif old == "Bunny Ears" then old = "Bunny" end
            Values.DecorStyles = (old and table.find(D.STYLES, old)) and old or "None"
        end

        local function palette(style)
            return D.PALS[D.OWN[style]]   -- each style uses its own colours
        end

        -- ---- small helpers ------------------------------------------------------------------
        local HALF = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        local function mk(class, props, parent)
            local o = Instance.new(class)
            o:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true)   -- the theme recolor pass must leave decorations alone
            for k, v in pairs(props) do o[k] = v end
            o.Parent = parent
            return o
        end
        local function round(o, r) mk("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(r, 0) }, o) end
        local function pill(parent, color, z)
            local f = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = HALF, BackgroundColor3 = color, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = z }, parent)
            round(f, 0.5)
            return f
        end

        -- A chain of overlapping round segments = a smooth tapered "limb" (tentacles and horns).
        -- Every segment has a dark outline segment under it, so the silhouette is clean and no seams show.
        local function newChain(holder, N, colA, colB, outlineCol, kind)
            local c = { N = N, segs = {}, out = {}, px = {}, py = {}, mx = {}, my = {}, ang = {}, wd = {} }
            for i = 1, N do
                c.out[i] = pill(holder, outlineCol, i)
                local seg = pill(holder, colA:Lerp(colB, (i - 0.5) / N), N + i)
                -- soft specular rib along the top of the limb
                local hl = mk("Frame", {
                    Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.08, 0.14), Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.84, 0.22),
                    BackgroundColor3 = WHITE, BackgroundTransparency = (kind == "tentacle") and 0.5 or ((kind == "horn") and 0.38 or 0.78), BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = N + i,
                }, seg)
                round(hl, 0.5)
                -- shaded belly
                if kind ~= "tentacle" then
                    local sh = mk("Frame", {
                        Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.06, 0.66), Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.88, 0.26),
