                        BackgroundColor3 = BLACK, BackgroundTransparency = 0.82, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = N + i,
                    }, seg)
                    round(sh, 0.5)
                end
                if kind == "horn" and i % 3 == 0 and i < N - 1 then   -- horn ridges
                    mk("Frame", {
                        AnchorPoint = HALF, Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.1, 0.96),
                        BackgroundColor3 = outlineCol, BackgroundTransparency = 0.3, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = N + i,
                    }, seg)
                end
                c.segs[i] = seg
            end
            return c
        end

        -- c.px / c.py (0..N) are the joint positions; this lays the segments along them
        local function placeChain(c, step, w0, w1, pw, ow, prof)
            local N, px, py = c.N, c.px, c.py
            local resize = (c.kStep ~= step) or (c.kW ~= w0)
            c.kStep, c.kW, c.resized = step, w0, resize
            local len = step * 1.6 + 1
            for i = 1, N do
                local x0, y0, x1, y1 = px[i - 1], py[i - 1], px[i], py[i]
                local mx, my = (x0 + x1) * 0.5, (y0 + y1) * 0.5
                local a = math.atan2(y1 - y0, x1 - x0)
                local rot = math.deg(a)
                local wi = prof and (prof((i - 0.5) / N) + w1) or (w0 * (1 - (i - 0.5) / N) ^ pw + w1)
                local pos = UDim2.fromOffset(mx, my)
                local seg, out = c.segs[i], c.out[i]
                seg.Position, seg.Rotation = pos, rot
                out.Position, out.Rotation = pos, rot
                if resize then
                    seg.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(len, wi)
                    out.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(len + ow * 2, wi + ow * 2)
                end
                c.mx[i], c.my[i], c.ang[i], c.wd[i] = mx, my, a, wi
            end
        end

        -- ---- TENTACLES ----------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- { window-edge x, edge y (0..1), heading in degrees (0 = right, 90 = down), curl side, length factor }
        local ANCH = {
            { 0.00, 0.78,  160,  1, 1.00 },
            { 1.00, 0.72,   20, -1, 1.00 },
            { 0.16, 1.00,  105,  1, 1.00 },
            { 0.86, 1.00,   75, -1, 1.10 },
            { 0.42, 1.00,   90, -1, 0.85 },
            { 0.00, 0.40,  195, -1, 0.80 },
            { 1.00, 0.40,  -15,  1, 0.80 },
            { 0.64, 1.00,   95,  1, 0.90 },
            { 0.04, 0.00, -135, -1, 0.70 },
            { 0.96, 0.00,  -45,  1, 0.70 },
            { 0.27, 0.00, -100, -1, 0.75 },   -- along the top edge
            { 0.50, 0.00,  -90,  1, 0.70 },
            { 0.73, 0.00,  -80, -1, 0.75 },
        }

        local function buildTentacles(root, pal)
            local N = 18   -- segments per tentacle (fewer = much less to move every frame)
            local amount = 13   -- fixed number of tentacles (no slider): 8 round the sides/bottom + 5 along the top
            local list = {}
            for k = 1, amount do
                local holder = mk("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = k }, root)
                local T = {
                    a    = ANCH[k],
                    c    = newChain(holder, N, pal.base:Lerp(pal.dark, 0.12), pal.light:Lerp(pal.base, 0.35), pal.dark, "tentacle"),
                    suck = {},
                    spot = {},
                    ph   = k * 1.9,
                    spd  = 1.1 + (k % 3) * 0.28,
                    curl = math.rad(70 + (k * 37) % 55),
                    wave = 0.5 + (k % 2) * 0.18,
                    -- grab interaction: click yanks the tentacle, then it springs back
                    grabAng  = 0,   -- extra angular offset injected at the mid-point
                    grabVel  = 0,
                    grabAt   = -99,
                    squishT  = 0,   -- sucker "blink" timer (0..1) driven by click
                }
                for i = 2, N - 2, 2 do   -- suckers along the inner side (every 2nd segment; the dark pill is the outline - no UIStroke, they are costly)
                    local s = pill(holder, pal.dark, 2 * N + i)
                    local inner = pill(s, pal.inner, 1)
                    inner.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
                    inner.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.76, 0.76)
                    local dot = pill(s, pal.base:Lerp(pal.dark, 0.35), 2)   -- the round dimple in the middle of each sucker
                    dot.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
                    dot.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.34, 0.34)
                    T.suck[i] = s
                end
                for i = 3, N - 3, 3 do   -- darker skin spots along the back
                    local s = pill(holder, pal.dark, 2 * N + i)
                    s.BackgroundTransparency = 0.4
                    T.spot[i] = s
                end
                -- invisible click button that covers the tentacle's mid-to-tip region
                local btn = mk("TextButton", {
                    AnchorPoint = HALF, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    AutoButtonColor = false, ZIndex = 400,
                }, root)
                btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    local now = os.clock()
                    if now - T.grabAt < 0.3 then return end   -- debounce
                    T.grabAt = now
                    -- fling the tentacle: a large angular kick at the mid-point, opposite to its current curl side
                    T.grabVel = T.grabVel + math.rad(220) * -ANCH[k][4]
                    T.squishT = now   -- trigger sucker blink
                end)
                T.btn = btn
                list[#list + 1] = T
            end
            local lastNow
            return function(rect, sc, t, g)
                local now = os.clock()
                local dt = math.min(now - (lastNow or now), 0.05)
                lastNow = now
                for _, T in ipairs(list) do
                    local a, c = T.a, T.c
                    -- grab spring: kick decays back to 0 with a damped oscillator
                    T.grabVel = T.grabVel + (-95 * T.grabAng - 5.5 * T.grabVel) * dt
                    T.grabAng = T.grabAng + T.grabVel * dt

                    local th0 = math.rad(a[3])
                    local bx = rect.x + a[1] * rect.w - math.cos(th0) * 12 * sc   -- base sits a little inside the window
                    local by = rect.y + a[2] * rect.h - math.sin(th0) * 12 * sc
                    local step = (250 * sc * a[5] * g) / N
                    local px, py = c.px, c.py
                    local x, y = bx, by
                    px[0], py[0] = x, y
                    for i = 1, N do
                        local tt = i / N
                        -- grab kick is strongest at the mid-point (bell curve peak at tt=0.5)
                        local grabBell = math.exp(-((tt - 0.5) ^ 2) / 0.06)
                        local h = th0 + a[4] * T.curl * tt ^ 1.5 + math.sin(t * T.spd + T.ph + tt * 3.4) * T.wave * tt
                                  + T.grabAng * grabBell
                        x = x + math.cos(h) * step
                        y = y + math.sin(h) * step
                        px[i], py[i] = x, y
                    end
                    placeChain(c, step, 34 * sc, 7 * sc, 0.8, math.max(2, 4.4 * sc))

                    -- sucker blink: briefly squish suckers smaller on click, then spring back
                    local squishAge = now - T.squishT
                    local squishScale = 1
                    if squishAge < 0.55 then
                        squishScale = 1 - 0.55 * math.exp(-squishAge * 9) * math.sin(squishAge * 28)
                    end

                    local resizeSuck = c.resized or squishScale ~= 1 or T.lastSquish ~= 1
                    T.lastSquish = squishScale
                    for i = 2, N - 2, 2 do
                        local s = T.suck[i]
                        local off = c.wd[i] * 0.24 * a[4]
                        s.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(c.mx[i] - math.sin(c.ang[i]) * off, c.my[i] + math.cos(c.ang[i]) * off)
                        if resizeSuck then
                            local d = math.max(3, c.wd[i] * 0.6) * squishScale
                            s.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(d, d)
                        end
                    end
                    for i, s in pairs(T.spot) do
                        local off = -c.wd[i] * 0.2 * a[4]
                        s.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(c.mx[i] - math.sin(c.ang[i]) * off, c.my[i] + math.cos(c.ang[i]) * off)
                        if c.resized then
                            local d = math.max(2, c.wd[i] * 0.3)
                            s.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(d, d)
                        end
                    end

                    -- keep the click button centred over the tentacle's middle third
                    local midSeg = math.floor(N * 0.55)
                    local mx = (px[math.floor(N * 0.3)] + px[midSeg]) * 0.5
                    local my = (py[math.floor(N * 0.3)] + py[midSeg]) * 0.5
                    T.btn.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(mx, my)
                    T.btn.Size     = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(14, 34 * sc * g * 1.6), math.max(14, 34 * sc * g * 1.6))
                end
            end
        end

        -- ---- WHISKERS (shared by Cat and Bunny) -------------------------------------
        -- three thin curved whiskers on the middle of each side of the window, dark outline under a light core. Only one set is ever
        -- built, even when both ear styles are selected.
        local function buildWhiskers(root, pal)
            local SEG = 5
            local ws = {}
            for _, side in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
                for k = -1, 1 do
                    local w = { side = side, k = k, segs = {} }
                    for i = 1, SEG do
                        local out  = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = HALF, BackgroundColor3 = pal.dark, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 1 }, root)
                        local fill = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = HALF, BackgroundColor3 = pal.light:Lerp(WHITE, 0.35), BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 2 }, root)
                        round(out, 0.5)
                        round(fill, 0.5)
                        w.segs[i] = { out = out, fill = fill }
                    end
                    ws[#ws + 1] = w
                end
            end
            return function(rect, sc, t, g)
                for _, w in ipairs(ws) do
                    local s = w.side
                    local x = rect.x + rect.w * (0.5 + 0.5 * s) - s * 10 * sc          -- starts a little inside the edge, hidden by the window
                    local y = rect.y + rect.h * 0.5 + w.k * 15 * sc   -- vertical centre of the side
                    local base = (w.k == -1) and -0.12 or ((w.k == 0) and 0.1 or 0.32)  -- the top one points up a touch, the bottom one droops
                    local len = (150 * sc * g) / SEG
                    for i = 1, SEG do
                        local droop = base + 0.11 * i + math.sin(t * 1.3 + w.k * 1.1 + s + i * 0.3) * 0.03 * i
                        local h = (s > 0) and droop or (math.pi - droop)
                        local nx, ny = x + math.cos(h) * len, y + math.sin(h) * len
                        local th = math.max(2, (5.2 - (i - 1) * 0.8) * sc)   -- chunky cartoon whiskers, thinner towards the tip
                        local pos, rot = UDim2.fromOffset((x + nx) * 0.5, (y + ny) * 0.5), math.deg(h)
                        local sg = w.segs[i]
                        sg.out.Position,  sg.out.Rotation,  sg.out.Size  = pos, rot, UDim2.fromOffset(len + 4, th + 4.4 * math.max(sc, 0.5))
                        sg.fill.Position, sg.fill.Rotation, sg.fill.Size = pos, rot, UDim2.fromOffset(len + 1, th)
                        x, y = nx, ny
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        local function maybeWhiskers(root, pal)
            if D.whiskerMade then return nil end
            D.whiskerMade = true
            return buildWhiskers(root, pal)
        end

        -- ---- CAT EARS (+ a fur ruff between them) -------------------------------------------
        -- an ear = a rounded square turned 45 degrees and centred on the window's top edge: the half behind the
        -- window is hidden, the half above it is a soft triangle. The fur between the ears is rows of the same
        -- trick, in small sizes, fanned out from the centre.
        -- Clicking an ear kicks a damped spring (it swings, overshoots and settles smoothly, it never snaps back),
        -- flares the fur strands inside it, and sends a ripple through the ruff: tufts puff up, brighten and flutter.
        local function liftChain(c, add)   -- raise every layer of a chain so one chain can sit above another
            for i = 1, c.N do
                c.out[i].ZIndex = c.out[i].ZIndex + add
                c.segs[i].ZIndex = c.segs[i].ZIndex + add
                for _, d in ipairs(c.segs[i]:GetChildren()) do
                    if d:IsA("GuiObject") then d.ZIndex = d.ZIndex + add end
                end
            end
        end

        local function buildCatEars(root, pal)
            -- Cartoon cat ears (crab style): fat rounded-triangle ears with a thick dark outline, a gloss stripe and a
            -- shade stripe, a big chunky pink inner ear with its own outline, and a row of fat round fluff balls between them.
            local ears = {}
            local bodyCol = pal.base:Lerp(pal.light, 0.1)
            for _, side in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
                local holder = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = HALF, BackgroundTransparency = 1, ZIndex = 3 }, root)
                local outer = mk("Frame", {
                    AnchorPoint = HALF, Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Rotation = 45,
                    BackgroundColor3 = bodyCol, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 1,
                }, holder)
                round(outer, 0.3)   -- rounder, softer corners
                local st = mk("UIStroke", { Color = pal.dark, Thickness = 5.6, Transparency = 0 }, outer)   -- thick crab-style outline
                mk("UIGradient", { Color = ColorSequence.new(bodyCol, pal.light:Lerp(bodyCol, 0.4)), Rotation = 45 }, outer)
                local inner = mk("Frame", {
                    AnchorPoint = HALF, Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.45), Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.62, 0.62), Rotation = 45,
                    BackgroundColor3 = pal.inner, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 2,
                }, holder)
                round(inner, 0.34)
                local innerSt = mk("UIStroke", { Color = pal.dark, Thickness = 3.4, Transparency = 0.1 }, inner)
                -- gloss stripe (upper outer edge) and soft shade stripe (lower)
                local gloss = mk("Frame", {
                    AnchorPoint = HALF, Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5 + side * 0.16, 0.2), Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.12, 0.3),
                    Rotation = side * 36, BackgroundColor3 = WHITE, BackgroundTransparency = 0.25, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 4,
                }, holder)
                round(gloss, 0.5)
                local shade = mk("Frame", {
                    AnchorPoint = HALF, Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.74), Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.1),
                    BackgroundColor3 = BLACK, BackgroundTransparency = 0.7, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 1,
                }, holder)
                round(shade, 0.5)
                local core = mk("Frame", {   -- big round "freckle" in the inner ear
                    AnchorPoint = HALF, Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.2, 0.2),
                    BackgroundColor3 = pal.dark, BackgroundTransparency = 0.55, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 3,
                }, holder)
                round(core, 0.5)
                local strands, tufts = {}, {}   -- kept (empty) so the click animation below still works

                local ear = {
                    side = side, xf = 0.5 + side * 0.36, holder = holder, stroke = st, inner = inner, core = core, innerSt = innerSt,
                    strands = strands, tufts = tufts, pal = pal,
                    ang = 0, vel = 0, puff = 0, clickAt = -99, flash = false,
                }
                local btn = mk("TextButton", {
                    AnchorPoint = HALF, Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
                    Size = UDim2.fromScale(1.4, 1.4), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    Text = "", BorderSizePixel = 0, AutoButtonColor = false, ZIndex = 10,
                }, holder)
                btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    local now = os.clock()
                    if now - ear.clickAt < 0.3 then return end   -- debounce
                    ear.clickAt = now
                    ear.vel = ear.vel + 340 * side   -- a sharp kick: the spring in the frame loop does the rest
                    ear.puff = 1
                end)
                ears[#ears + 1] = ear
            end

            -- fluff ruff between the ears: fat round balls with thick outlines (outline twins under fills)
            local M = 9
            local fur = {}
            for j = 1, M do
                local rng = 0.5 + 0.5 * math.sin(j * 12.9898)
                local cFill = (j % 2 == 0) and bodyCol:Lerp(pal.light, 0.25) or bodyCol
                local out  = pill(root, pal.dark, 1)
                local fill = pill(root, cFill, 2)
                local hl   = pill(root, WHITE, 3)
                hl.BackgroundTransparency = 0.45
                fur[j] = { out = out, fill = fill, hl = hl, n = rng, cFill = cFill, hot = false }
            end

            local wu = maybeWhiskers(root, pal)
            local lastNow
            return function(rect, sc, t, g)
                local now = os.clock()
                local dt = math.min(now - (lastNow or now), 0.05)
                lastNow = now
                if wu then wu(rect, sc, t, g) end

                for _, e in ipairs(ears) do
                    e.vel = e.vel + (-170 * e.ang - 8.5 * e.vel) * dt
                    e.ang = e.ang + e.vel * dt
                    e.puff = e.puff * math.exp(-4.5 * dt)

                    local S = 134 * sc * g   -- big, easy-to-spot ears
                    local twitch = math.max(0, math.sin(t * 0.75 + e.side * 1.9)) ^ 22 * 11 * e.side
                    e.holder.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(S, S)
                    e.holder.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(rect.x + rect.w * (0.5 + e.side * 0.36), rect.y - 0.05 * S)   -- sits higher so most of the ear sticks out
                    e.holder.Rotation = e.side * 18 + twitch + e.ang
                    e.stroke.Thickness = math.max(2.5, 7 * sc * math.min(1, g * 3))
                    e.innerSt.Thickness = math.max(2, 4.4 * sc * math.min(1, g * 3))

                    local p = e.puff
                    if p > 0.004 then   -- inner ear brightens and the freckle pops
                        e.flash = true
                        e.inner.BackgroundColor3 = e.pal.inner:Lerp(WHITE, 0.7 * p)
                        local cs = 0.2 + 0.16 * p
                        e.core.Size = UDim2.fromScale(cs, cs)
                    elseif e.flash then
                        e.flash = false
                        e.inner.BackgroundColor3 = e.pal.inner
                        e.core.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.2, 0.2)
                    end
                end

                local th = math.max(2, 4.4 * sc) * math.min(1, g * 3)
                for j, f in ipairs(fur) do
                    local u  = (j - 0.5) / M
                    local xf = 0.2 + 0.6 * u
                    local x  = rect.x + rect.w * xf
                    local rip, pf = 0, 0
                    for _, e in ipairs(ears) do
                        local el = now - e.clickAt - math.abs(xf - e.xf) * 0.55
                        if el > 0 and el < 1.8 then
                            local dec = math.exp(-3.2 * el) * math.min(1, el / 0.08)
                            rip = rip + math.sin(el * math.pi * 2 * 3.2) * math.exp(-3.2 * el)
                            pf = pf + dec
                        end
                    end
                    pf = math.min(pf, 1)
                    local d = (30 + 8 * f.n) * sc * g * (1 + 0.35 * pf)
                    local bob = math.sin(t * 1.6 + j * 0.9) * 2 * sc + rip * 5 * sc
                    local cy = rect.y + 0.08 * d - bob * 0.5
                    f.out.Position  = UDim2.fromOffset(x, cy)
                    f.out.Size      = UDim2.fromOffset(d + th * 2, d + th * 2)
                    f.fill.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, cy)
                    f.fill.Size     = UDim2.fromOffset(d, d)
                    f.hl.Position   = UDim2.fromOffset(x - d * 0.18, cy - d * 0.2)
                    f.hl.Size       = UDim2.fromOffset(d * 0.3, d * 0.2)
                    f.hl.Rotation   = -30
                    if pf > 0.01 then
                        f.hot = true
                        f.fill.BackgroundColor3 = f.cFill:Lerp(WHITE, 0.45 * pf)
                    elseif f.hot then
                        f.hot = false
                        f.fill.BackgroundColor3 = f.cFill
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        -- ---- BUNNY EARS ---------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- Flat storybook bunny ears: one tall upright ear and one with a folded-over, floppy tip. Every part is a capsule with
        -- a dark outline; all outlines sit under all fills, so overlapping parts merge with no seam lines.
        -- The folded tip hinges on a rounded end that wraps the top of the stem, so the fold always grows out of the stem
        -- (the back edge of the stem flows straight into the top edge of the fold) however far it droops.
        -- Fur: clumps of strands along every outer edge, fur marks on the body and the pink inner ear, and a fluffy tuft
        -- where each ear comes out of the window.
        local function capsule(parent, fillCol, lineCol, zOut, zFill, shaded)
            local c = {}
            c.out  = pill(parent, lineCol, zOut)
            c.fill = pill(parent, fillCol, zFill)
            mk("UIGradient", { Color = ColorSequence.new(fillCol:Lerp(WHITE, 0.2), fillCol), Rotation = 90 }, c.fill)
            if shaded then   -- crab-style soft shade stripe underneath and gloss stripe on top
                c.sh = pill(parent, BLACK, zFill + 1)
                c.sh.BackgroundTransparency = 0.72
                c.hl = pill(parent, WHITE, zFill + 2)
                c.hl.BackgroundTransparency = 0.45
            end
            return c
        end

        local function setCap(c, cx, cy, w, h, rot, th)
            local pos = UDim2.fromOffset(cx, cy)
            c.out.Position,  c.out.Rotation,  c.out.Size  = pos, rot, UDim2.fromOffset(w + th * 2, h + th * 2)
            c.fill.Position, c.fill.Rotation, c.fill.Size = pos, rot, UDim2.fromOffset(w, h)
            if c.hl then
                local r = math.rad(rot)
                local nx, ny = math.cos(r), math.sin(r)   -- the capsule's local right, on screen (ears are upright, so this is "across")
                local ux, uy = math.sin(r), -math.cos(r)  -- the capsule's local up, on screen
                local long = (h >= w)
                local ax, ay = long and ux or nx, long and uy or ny      -- along the long axis
                local px, py = long and nx or -ny, long and ny or nx     -- across
                local across = math.min(w, h)
                local length = math.max(w, h)
                c.hl.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(cx - px * across * 0.22 + ax * length * 0.12, cy - py * across * 0.22 + ay * length * 0.12)
                c.hl.Size     = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(across * 0.16, 0.5), math.max(length * 0.45, 0.5))
                c.hl.Rotation = rot + (long and 0 or 90)
                c.sh.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(cx + px * across * 0.3, cy + py * across * 0.3)
                c.sh.Size     = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(across * 0.14, 0.5), math.max(length * 0.6, 0.5))
                c.sh.Rotation = rot + (long and 0 or 90)
            end
        end

        -- local ear coordinates (lx to the right, ly up, from the ear's root) -> screen, turned by `a` radians about the root
        local function toWorld(bx, by, a, lx, ly)
            local c, s = math.cos(a), math.sin(a)
            return bx + lx * c + ly * s, by - ly * c + lx * s
        end
        local function dirWorld(a, lx, ly)   -- the same turn, for a direction
            local c, s = math.cos(a), math.sin(a)
            return lx * c + ly * s, -ly * c + lx * s
        end
        local function setGeo(gt, cx, cy, ax, ay, hl, hw)   -- a capsule's centre, long axis (towards the tip), half length, half width
            gt.cx, gt.cy, gt.ax, gt.ay, gt.hl, gt.hw = cx, cy, ax, ay, hl, hw
        end

        local function buildBunnyEars(root, pal)
            local body      = pal.base:Lerp(pal.light, 0.6)
            local line      = pal.dark
            local innerLine = pal.dark
            local furLight  = body:Lerp(WHITE, 0.35)
            local furMark   = body:Lerp(pal.dark, 0.28)
            local innerMark = pal.inner:Lerp(pal.dark, 0.3)
            local ears = {}
            local seed = 0
            local function rnd() seed = seed + 1; return 0.5 + 0.5 * math.sin(seed * 12.9898) end

            for _, side in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
                local holder = mk("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 1 }, root)
                local ear = {
                    side = side, flop = (side == 1), ang = 0, vel = 0, tang = 0, tvel = 0, clickAt = -99,
                    strands = {}, marks = {}, rootFur = {}, geo = { b = {}, f = {}, i = {} },
                }
                ear.b = capsule(holder, body, line, 0, 20, true)                          -- the ear (the stem, on the floppy one)
                if ear.flop then ear.f = capsule(holder, body, line, 10, 30, true) end    -- the folded-over tip (above the stem)
                ear.i = capsule(holder, pal.inner, innerLine, 21, 22, false)               -- pink inner ear (above the stem, below the fold)

                -- a clump = three strands growing from the same spot, fanned apart and different lengths
                local function addClump(part, kind, s, edge, theta)
                    local spreads  = { -0.26, 0, 0.26 }
                    local fills    = { body, furLight, body:Lerp(furLight, 0.5) }
                    local tapers   = { 0.55, 0.40, 0.70 }   -- base width multiplier (tip is narrower)
                    local freqs    = { 2.1, 1.75, 2.45 }     -- sway frequency for each strand
                    for q = 1, 3 do
                        ear.strands[#ear.strands + 1] = {
                            out  = pill(holder, line, 12),
                            fill = pill(holder, fills[q], 14),
                            part = part, kind = kind, s = s, edge = edge, theta = theta,
                            spread = spreads[q], n = rnd(), ph = seed * 1.7,
                            taper = tapers[q], freq = freqs[q],
                        }
                    end
                end
                local function addMark(part, s, across, len)
                    local f = pill(holder, (part == "i") and innerMark or furMark, (part == "i") and 23 or ((part == "f") and 35 or 25))
                    f.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
                    ear.marks[#ear.marks + 1] = { f = f, part = part, s = s, across = across, len = len }
                end

                if not ear.flop then
                else
                end

                if not ear.flop then
                    for _, m in ipairs({ { 0.3, 0.45, 14 }, { 0.62, -0.4, 12 } }) do addMark("b", m[1], m[2], m[3]) end
                else
                    for _, m in ipairs({ { 0.3, 0.4, 14 } }) do addMark("b", m[1], m[2], m[3]) end
                    for _, m in ipairs({ { 0.55, -0.35, 14 } }) do addMark("f", m[1], m[2], m[3]) end
                end
                for k = 1, 3 do   -- a few fat round puffs where the ear comes out of the window edge
                    ear.rootFur[k] = {
                        out = pill(holder, line, 44), fill = pill(holder, (k % 2 == 0) and furLight or body, 46),
                        u = (k - 2) / 1.2, n = rnd(),
                        taper = 1 - 0.25 * math.abs(k - 2),
                        freq  = 1.3 + 0.35 * math.sin(k * 1.9),              -- staggered sway speeds
                    }
                end

                ear.btn = mk("TextButton", {
                    AnchorPoint = HALF, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", BorderSizePixel = 0, AutoButtonColor = false, ZIndex = 400,
                }, root)
                ear.btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    local now = os.clock()
                    if now - ear.clickAt < 0.25 then return end
                    ear.clickAt = now
                    ear.vel = ear.vel + 380   -- outward flick (degrees / second)
                end)
                ears[#ears + 1] = ear
            end
            local wu = maybeWhiskers(root, pal)

            -- ---- NOSE -----------------------------------------------------------------------
            -- A small rounded triangle / heart-nub centred just above the window's top edge.
            -- Layers (back to front): dark outline pill → pink fill pill → highlight sliver → two nostrils.
            local noseHolder = mk("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
                                             Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 60 }, root)
            local noseCol  = pal.inner:Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(255, 150, 170), 0.55)   -- soft rosy pink
            local noseDk   = noseCol:Lerp(pal.dark, 0.55)                           -- outline / nostril colour
            local noseOut  = pill(noseHolder, noseDk,  60)                           -- dark outline
            local noseFill = pill(noseHolder, noseCol, 62)                           -- pink fill
            mk("UIGradient", { Color = ColorSequence.new(noseCol:Lerp(WHITE, 0.38), noseCol), Rotation = 90 }, noseFill)
            -- specular highlight at the top of the nose
            local noseGlint = pill(noseHolder, WHITE, 63)
            noseGlint.BackgroundTransparency = 0.55
            -- two nostrils
            local nostrilL = pill(noseHolder, noseDk, 64)
            local nostrilR = pill(noseHolder, noseDk, 64)
            nostrilL.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
            nostrilR.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
            -- sniff state: triggered when any ear is clicked
            local sniffAt = -99

            -- wire each ear's existing click button to also trigger a sniff
            for _, e in ipairs(ears) do
                e.btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    sniffAt = os.clock()
                end)
            end

            local lastNow
            return function(rect, sc, t, g)
                local now = os.clock()
                local dt = math.min(now - (lastNow or now), 0.05)
                lastNow = now
                if wu then wu(rect, sc, t, g) end

                -- nose: centred horizontally, sits ON the top edge of the window (lower half overlaps the UI)
                -- pill() uses AnchorPoint=HALF, so position is the pill's centre.
                -- We want the nose to straddle the top edge: centre at rect.y so equal halves above/below.
                local bob = math.sin(t * 1.1) * 2.5 * sc * g
                local sniffAge = now - sniffAt
                local sniffBulge = 1
                if sniffAge < 0.5 then
                    sniffBulge = 1 + 0.32 * math.exp(-sniffAge * 9) * math.abs(math.sin(sniffAge * 22))
                end
                local nw = math.max(10, 48 * sc * g * sniffBulge)   -- wider nose
                local nh = math.max(7,  34 * sc * g * sniffBulge)   -- taller so it's clearly visible
                local th = math.max(1.5, 2.2 * sc) * math.min(1, g * 3)
                local nx = rect.x + rect.w * 0.5
                -- sit ON the edge: centre the pill at the top edge so the bottom half is inside the UI
                local ny = rect.y + bob

                noseOut.Position  = UDim2.fromOffset(nx, ny)
                noseOut.Size      = UDim2.fromOffset(nw + th * 2, nh + th * 2)
                noseFill.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(nx, ny)
                noseFill.Size     = UDim2.fromOffset(nw, nh)

                -- glint: small oval upper-left quadrant
                local gw, gh = nw * 0.3, nh * 0.24
                noseGlint.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(nx - nw * 0.2, ny - nh * 0.2)
                noseGlint.Size     = UDim2.fromOffset(gw, gh)

                -- nostrils: two small dark ovals in the lower half
                local nd   = math.max(2, nw * 0.20)
                local noff = nw * 0.26
                nostrilL.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(nx - noff, ny + nh * 0.14)
                nostrilL.Size     = UDim2.fromOffset(nd, nd * 0.78)
                nostrilR.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(nx + noff, ny + nh * 0.14)
                nostrilR.Size     = UDim2.fromOffset(nd, nd * 0.78)

                for _, e in ipairs(ears) do
                    -- damped springs: the whole ear, and a softer one for the floppy tip that chases it
                    e.vel  = e.vel  + (-120 * e.ang - 6 * e.vel) * dt
                    e.ang  = e.ang  + e.vel * dt
                    e.tvel = e.tvel + (70 * (e.ang * 1.4 - e.tang) - 4.5 * e.tvel) * dt
                    e.tang = e.tang + e.tvel * dt

                    local grow  = math.min(1, g * 3)                      -- outlines grow in with the ear and vanish with it
                    local th    = math.max(2.5, 5.6 * sc) * grow   -- thick crab-style outline
                    local thIn  = math.max(1.8, 3.4 * sc) * grow
                    local thH   = math.max(1.8, 4 * sc) * grow
                    local W     = 74 * sc * g   -- fatter, chunkier ears
                    local twitch = math.max(0, math.sin(t * 0.65 + e.side * 2.0)) ^ 20 * math.rad(7)
                    local bx, by = rect.x + rect.w * (0.5 + e.side * 0.24), rect.y + 16 * sc
                    local a = e.side * (math.rad(8) + math.rad(e.ang) + twitch) + math.sin(t * 0.9 + e.side * 1.3) * math.rad(2.2)
                    local deg = math.deg(a)
                    local upx, upy = dirWorld(a, 0, 1)                    -- the ear's "up", on screen
                    local geo = e.geo

                    if not e.flop then
                        local H = 190 * sc * g
                        local cx, cy = toWorld(bx, by, a, 0, H * 0.5)
                        setCap(e.b, cx, cy, W, H, deg, th)
                        setGeo(geo.b, cx, cy, upx, upy, H * 0.5, W * 0.5)
                        local ix, iy = toWorld(bx, by, a, 0, H * 0.43)
                        setCap(e.i, ix, iy, W * 0.56, H * 0.68, deg, thIn)
                        setGeo(geo.i, ix, iy, upx, upy, H * 0.35, W * 0.25)
                        e.btn.Position, e.btn.Size, e.btn.Rotation = UDim2.fromOffset(cx, cy), UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(8, W * 1.5), math.max(8, H)), deg
                    else
                        local Hs = 128 * sc * g          -- stem
                        local Fl = 124 * sc * g          -- folded tip: length
                        local Fw = 82 * sc * g           -- folded tip: width
                        local d  = 24 + e.tang * 0.9 + math.sin(t * 0.8 + 1.1) * 3   -- how far the tip hangs down (degrees)
                        local rd = math.rad(d)
                        local s  = e.side
                        local cx, cy = toWorld(bx, by, a, 0, Hs * 0.5)
                        setCap(e.b, cx, cy, W, Hs, deg, th)
                        setGeo(geo.b, cx, cy, upx, upy, Hs * 0.5, W * 0.5)
                        -- the fold: its rounded hinge end is centred on the stem's rounded top (shifted so the back edges line
                        -- up), the capsule then points down and outward from there
                        local hx, hy = s * (Fw - W) * 0.5, Hs - W * 0.5
                        local alx, aly = s * math.cos(rd), -math.sin(rd)
                        local half = Fl * 0.5 - Fw * 0.5
                        local fx, fy = toWorld(bx, by, a, hx + alx * half, hy + aly * half)
                        setCap(e.f, fx, fy, Fl, Fw, deg + s * d, th)
                        local awx, awy = dirWorld(a, alx, aly)
                        setGeo(geo.f, fx, fy, awx, awy, Fl * 0.5, Fw * 0.5)
                        local ix, iy = toWorld(bx, by, a, 0, Hs * 0.4)
                        setCap(e.i, ix, iy, W * 0.56, Hs * 0.6, deg, thIn)
                        setGeo(geo.i, ix, iy, upx, upy, Hs * 0.31, W * 0.25)
                        local qx, qy = toWorld(bx, by, a, s * Fl * 0.18, Hs * 0.62)
                        e.btn.Position, e.btn.Size, e.btn.Rotation = UDim2.fromOffset(qx, qy), UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(8, W + Fl * 0.75), math.max(8, Hs + Fw * 0.5)), deg
                    end

                    -- no hair strands any more: just chunky round freckles on the ear (like the crab's claws)
                    for _, m in ipairs(e.marks) do
                        local gt = geo[m.part]
                        local px, py = -gt.ay, gt.ax
                        local sPos = (m.s * 2 - 1) * math.max(0, gt.hl - gt.hw) * 0.85
                        local x = gt.cx + gt.ax * sPos + px * m.across * gt.hw * 0.5
                        local y = gt.cy + gt.ay * sPos + py * m.across * gt.hw * 0.5
                        m.f.BackgroundTransparency = 0.55
                        local d = m.len * sc * g * 0.7
                        m.f.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y)
                        m.f.Rotation = 0
                        m.f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(d, d)
                    end

                    -- fat round puffs where the ear leaves the window
                    for _, rf in ipairs(e.rootFur) do
                        local d = (30 - 5 * math.abs(rf.u)) * sc * g
                        local x0, y0 = toWorld(bx, by, a, rf.u * W * 0.34, 2 * sc + math.sin(t * (rf.freq or 1.5) + rf.u * 3) * 1.5 * sc)
                        rf.fill.Position, rf.fill.Rotation, rf.fill.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(x0, y0), 0, UDim2.fromOffset(d, d)
                        rf.out.Position,  rf.out.Rotation,  rf.out.Size  = UDim2.fromOffset(x0, y0), 0, UDim2.fromOffset(d + thH * 2, d + thH * 2)
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        -- ---- DEVIL HORNS --------------------------------------------------------------------
        local function buildHorns(root, pal)
            local N = 16
            local horns = {}
            for _, side in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
                local hn = { side = side, c = newChain(root, N, pal.base:Lerp(pal.dark, 0.25), pal.light, pal.dark, "horn") }
                -- chunky cartoon glints on each horn: a big one low down and a small one further up
                local g1, g2 = pill(root, WHITE, 2 * N + 3), pill(root, WHITE, 2 * N + 3)
                g1.BackgroundTransparency, g2.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3, 0.45
                hn.g1, hn.g2 = g1, g2
                horns[#horns + 1] = hn
            end

            -- ---- DEVIL TAIL: thick swishing tail out of the bottom-right of the window, freckled, with a chunky heart-shaped tip ----
            local TN = 18
            local tail = newChain(root, TN, pal.base:Lerp(pal.dark, 0.15), pal.light:Lerp(pal.base, 0.35), pal.dark, "tail")
            local tailSpots = {}
            for i = 3, TN - 3, 3 do
                local sp = pill(root, pal.dark, 2 * TN + i)
                sp.BackgroundTransparency = 0.4
                tailSpots[i] = sp
            end
            -- tip = diamond + two round lobes (all outlines under all fills so the silhouette is one clean shape)
            local tOutD = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = HALF, BackgroundColor3 = pal.dark, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 2 * TN + 20 }, root)
            round(tOutD, 0.14)
            local tOutL, tOutR = pill(root, pal.dark, 2 * TN + 20), pill(root, pal.dark, 2 * TN + 20)
            local tipCol = pal.base:Lerp(pal.light, 0.25)
            local tFillD = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = HALF, BackgroundColor3 = tipCol, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 2 * TN + 21 }, root)
            round(tFillD, 0.14)
            local tFillL, tFillR = pill(root, tipCol, 2 * TN + 21), pill(root, tipCol, 2 * TN + 21)
            local tGloss = pill(root, WHITE, 2 * TN + 22)
            tGloss.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
            local tRib = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = HALF, BackgroundColor3 = pal.dark, BackgroundTransparency = 0.55, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 2 * TN + 22 }, root)
            round(tRib, 0.5)

            -- ---- PITCHFORK: big chunky cartoon pitchfork sticking out of the window's left side ----
            -- Laid out in design units (holder = 70 x 290, origin top-centre); every piece is an outline twin + a fill.
            local FW, FH = 70, 290
            local fork = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = HALF, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 3 }, root)
            local parts, zc = {}, 0
            local wood  = pal.dark:Lerp(pal.base, 0.3)
            local steel = pal.light:Lerp(pal.base, 0.2)
            local function fpart(xd, yd, wd, hd, rot, col, r, tr, noOut)
                zc = zc + 1
                local pos = UDim2.fromScale(0.5 + xd / FW, yd / FH)
                local o
                if not noOut then
                    o = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = HALF, Position = pos, Rotation = rot, BackgroundColor3 = pal.dark, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 1 }, fork)
                    round(o, r or 0.5)
                end
                local f = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = HALF, Position = pos, Rotation = rot, BackgroundColor3 = col, BackgroundTransparency = tr or 0, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 10 + zc }, fork)
                round(f, r or 0.5)
                parts[#parts + 1] = { o = o, f = f, w = wd, h = hd }
                return f
            end
            fpart(0, 188, 12, 206, 0, wood, 0.5)                                -- handle
            fpart(-2.5, 190, 3, 190, 0, WHITE, 0.5, 0.65, true)                 -- handle shine
            for _, y in ipairs({ 140, 158, 176, 194 }) do                       -- grip wraps
                fpart(0, y, 17, 5, 0, pal.dark, 0.5, 0.1, true)
            end
            fpart(0, 284, 24, 24, 0, pal.base, 0.5)                             -- round pommel
            fpart(-4, 280, 6, 6, 0, WHITE, 0.5, 0.35, true)
            fpart(0, 78, 68, 13, 0, steel, 0.45)                                -- crossbar
            for _, x in ipairs({ -22, 0, 22 }) do fpart(x, 78, 4.5, 4.5, 0, pal.dark, 0.5, 0.25, true) end   -- rivets
            fpart(0, 38, 13, 76, 0, steel, 0.45)                                -- centre prong
            fpart(-2.5, 40, 3.5, 58, 0, WHITE, 0.5, 0.45, true)                 -- prong shine
            fpart(0, 4, 20, 20, 45, steel, 0.15)                                -- centre spear tip
            for _, sd in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do                                   -- curved outer prongs: out, then back in
                fpart(sd * 31, 64, 12, 28, sd * 10,  steel, 0.45)
                fpart(sd * 34, 42, 11, 26, 0,        steel, 0.45)
                fpart(sd * 32, 22, 10, 24, -sd * 10, steel, 0.45)
                fpart(sd * 29, 7, 15, 15, 45, steel, 0.15)                      -- tip
                fpart(sd * 32, 40, 3, 40, 0, WHITE, 0.5, 0.5, true)             -- shine
            end
            fpart(0, 92, 28, 17, 0, pal.base, 0.35)                             -- collar
            for _, sd in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do fpart(sd * 16, 92, 11, 11, 45, pal.base, 0.15) end   -- collar spikes
            fpart(0, 92, 11, 11, 45, pal.light:Lerp(WHITE, 0.5), 0.2)           -- gem
            local lastK = -1

            -- click the pitchfork: it jabs outwards and wobbles back on springs
            local fk, lastFT = { a = 0, v = 0, j = 0, jv = 0, at = -99, dir = 1 }, nil
            local forkBtn = mk("TextButton", { AnchorPoint = HALF, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", BorderSizePixel = 0, AutoButtonColor = false, ZIndex = 400 }, root)
            forkBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                local now = os.clock()
                if now - fk.at < 0.25 then return end
                fk.at = now
                fk.dir = -fk.dir
                fk.v  = fk.v + 380 * fk.dir
                fk.jv = fk.jv + 520
            end)

            return function(rect, sc, t, g)
                for _, hn in ipairs(horns) do
                    local c = hn.c
                    local step = (140 * sc * g) / N
                    local x = rect.x + rect.w * (0.5 + hn.side * 0.27)
                    local y = rect.y + 10 * sc
                    c.px[0], c.py[0] = x, y
                    for i = 1, N do
                        local tt = i / N
                        local hl = math.rad(-124) + math.rad(80) * tt ^ 1.25 + math.sin(t * 0.9 + hn.side) * 0.03 * tt
                        local h = (hn.side < 0) and hl or (-math.pi - hl)   -- the right horn mirrors the left
                        x = x + math.cos(h) * step
                        y = y + math.sin(h) * step
                        c.px[i], c.py[i] = x, y
                    end
                    placeChain(c, step, 40 * sc, 3 * sc, 0.75, math.max(2, 4 * sc))
                    for gi, idx in ipairs({ 4, 9 }) do
                        local gl = (gi == 1) and hn.g1 or hn.g2
                        local off = -c.wd[idx] * 0.2 * hn.side   -- towards the outer / upper side of the horn
                        gl.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(c.mx[idx] - math.sin(c.ang[idx]) * off, c.my[idx] + math.cos(c.ang[idx]) * off)
                        local d = math.max(1, c.wd[idx] * ((gi == 1) and 0.3 or 0.26))
                        gl.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(d, d)
                    end
                end

                -- tail: down out of the window, then curls out to the right and up, swishing
                do
                    local step = (175 * sc * g) / TN
                    local x, y = rect.x + rect.w * 0.86, rect.y + rect.h - 14 * sc
                    local px, py = tail.px, tail.py
                    px[0], py[0] = x, y
                    local th0, h = math.rad(82), 0
                    for i = 1, TN do
                        local tt = i / TN
                        h = th0 - math.rad(155) * tt ^ 1.25 + math.sin(t * 2.2 + tt * 3.2) * 0.25 * tt
                        x = x + math.cos(h) * step
                        y = y + math.sin(h) * step
                        px[i], py[i] = x, y
                    end
                    placeChain(tail, step, 24 * sc * g, 7 * sc * g, 0.6, math.max(2, 2.8 * sc * g))
                    for i, sp in pairs(tailSpots) do   -- freckles along the back of the tail
                        local off = -tail.wd[i] * 0.18
                        sp.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(tail.mx[i] - math.sin(tail.ang[i]) * off, tail.my[i] + math.cos(tail.ang[i]) * off)
                        local d = math.max(1, tail.wd[i] * 0.34)
                        sp.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(d, d)
                    end
                    -- heart-shaped tip pointing along the tail
                    local S = math.max(1, 32 * sc * g)
                    local fx, fy = math.cos(h), math.sin(h)
                    local qx, qy = -fy, fx
                    local cx, cy = px[TN] + fx * S * 0.55, py[TN] + fy * S * 0.55
                    local ow = math.max(2, 3 * sc * g)
                    local rot = math.deg(h) + 45
                    tFillD.Position, tFillD.Rotation, tFillD.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(cx, cy), rot, UDim2.fromOffset(S, S)
                    tOutD.Position,  tOutD.Rotation,  tOutD.Size  = UDim2.fromOffset(cx, cy), rot, UDim2.fromOffset(S + ow * 2, S + ow * 2)
                    local ld = S * 0.64
                    for sgn = -1, 1, 2 do
                        local lx, ly = cx - fx * S * 0.13 + qx * S * 0.3 * sgn, cy - fy * S * 0.13 + qy * S * 0.3 * sgn
                        local fl, ol = (sgn < 0) and tFillL or tFillR, (sgn < 0) and tOutL or tOutR
                        fl.Position, fl.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(lx, ly), UDim2.fromOffset(ld, ld)
                        ol.Position, ol.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(lx, ly), UDim2.fromOffset(ld + ow * 2, ld + ow * 2)
                    end
                    local gd = S * 0.2
                    tGloss.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(cx - fx * S * 0.1 - qx * S * 0.22 - fy * 0, cy - fy * S * 0.1 - qy * S * 0.22)
                    tGloss.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(gd, gd)
                    tRib.Position, tRib.Rotation = UDim2.fromOffset(cx + fx * S * 0.02, cy + fy * S * 0.02), math.deg(h)
                    tRib.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(S * 0.7, math.max(1, S * 0.07))
                end

                -- pitchfork: grows out from behind the window and sways a little
                do
                    local k = sc * g
                    local L = FH * k
                    local dtF = math.min(t - (lastFT or t), 0.05)
                    lastFT = t
                    fk.v = fk.v + (-110 * fk.a - 5.5 * fk.v) * dtF    -- wobble spring (degrees)
                    fk.a = fk.a + fk.v * dtF
                    fk.jv = fk.jv + (-140 * fk.j - 8 * fk.jv) * dtF   -- jab spring (design units along the handle)
                    fk.j = fk.j + fk.jv * dtF
                    local rotD = -24 + math.sin(t * 1.1) * 3 + fk.a
                    local rr = math.rad(rotD)
                    local bx, by = rect.x + 30 * sc, rect.y + rect.h * 0.86   -- pommel end, hidden inside the window
                    local jx = fk.j * k
                    local fcx = bx + math.sin(rr) * (L * 0.5 + jx)
                    local fcy = by - math.cos(rr) * (L * 0.5 + jx)
                    fork.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(fcx, fcy)
                    fork.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1, FW * k), math.max(1, L))
                    fork.Rotation = rotD
                    forkBtn.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(fcx, fcy)
                    forkBtn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(8, FW * k * 1.1), math.max(8, L * 0.8))
                    forkBtn.Rotation = rotD
                    if k ~= lastK then   -- piece sizes only change with the window scale / grow-out
                        lastK = k
                        local ow = math.max(2, 4.5 * k)
                        for _, p in ipairs(parts) do
                            p.f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1, p.w * k), math.max(1, p.h * k))
                            if p.o then p.o.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1, p.w * k + ow), math.max(1, p.h * k + ow)) end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        -- ---- GHOST --------------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- A chubby cartoon ghost peeking over the top edge of the window: big glossy eyes that blink, a little
        -- "o" mouth, pink cheeks and two stubby arms that wave. It floats and sways. Click it: it jumps up and
        -- shouts "BOO!", its eyes and mouth go wide and the arms flail, then everything settles back.
        -- ---- FLYING GHOST (second ghost, drifts across the ui) ----------------------------
        -- A friendly translucent ghost that floats across the window from left to right, then does it again at
        -- another height. It is drawn twice: once BEHIND the window (so it is only seen outside the window) and once
        -- as a very faint copy on a layer ABOVE the window that is clipped to the window rect. While it floats
        -- "inside" the ui you can just make out its body through the glass. Nothing here takes input, so the front
        -- ui is never blocked or covered.
        local FLY = {
            SIZE   = 190,    -- design px of the whole sprite canvas (the ghost itself is ~45% of it)
            OUT_TR = 0.2,    -- transparency while outside the window
            IN_TR  = 0.84,   -- transparency while inside the window (higher = fainter)
            DUR    = 11,     -- seconds for one crossing
            LANES  = { 0.28, 0.74, 0.46, 1.08, 0.16, 0.62, 0.86, 0.36 },   -- crossing heights (fraction of window height; 1.08 skims the bottom edge)
        }

        local function makeFlyer(parent, z)
            local okCG, cg = pcall(Instance.new, "CanvasGroup")
            local useCG = okCG and cg ~= nil
            local root = useCG and cg or Instance.new("Frame")
            root:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true)
            root.AnchorPoint, root.BackgroundTransparency, root.BorderSizePixel = HALF, 1, 0
            root.ZIndex, root.Visible, root.Active = z, false, false
            root.Parent = parent

            local parts = {}   -- only used as a fallback when CanvasGroup is unavailable
            local function fp(par, color, cx, cy, w, h, zz, tr, sq)
                local f = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = HALF, BackgroundColor3 = color, BackgroundTransparency = tr or 0, BorderSizePixel = 0,
                                        ZIndex = zz, Position = UDim2.fromScale(cx, cy), Size = UDim2.fromScale(w, h) }, par)
                if not sq then round(f, 0.5) end
                parts[#parts + 1] = { o = f, b = tr or 0 }
                return f
            end

            local body, rim, ink = C3(236, 240, 255), C3(176, 186, 255), C3(44, 38, 92)
            local bumps = {}
            local function shape(color, grow, z)
                fp(root, color, 0.5, 0.34, 0.45 + grow, 0.45 + grow, z)               -- head
                fp(root, color, 0.5, 0.56, 0.45 + grow, 0.44, z, 0, true)             -- torso
                for i = 1, 3 do                                                        -- wavy tail bumps
                    local bx = 0.5 + (i - 2) * 0.15
                    bumps[#bumps + 1] = { o = fp(root, color, bx, 0.78, 0.153 + grow, 0.153 + grow, z), x = bx, i = i }
                end
            end
            shape(body, 0, 4)

            local armL = fp(root, body, 0.27, 0.5, 0.14, 0.07, 5)
            local armR = fp(root, body, 0.73, 0.5, 0.14, 0.07, 5)
            local function eye()
                local e = fp(root, ink, 0.5, 0.36, 0.06, 0.1, 7)
                fp(e, WHITE, 0.34, 0.3, 0.42, 0.3, 1)
                return e
            end
            local eyeL, eyeR = eye(), eye()
            local mouth  = fp(root, ink, 0.5, 0.47, 0.04, 0.055, 7)
            local blushL = fp(root, C3(255, 150, 188), 0.36, 0.44, 0.07, 0.035, 6, 0.3)
            local blushR = fp(root, C3(255, 150, 188), 0.64, 0.44, 0.07, 0.035, 6, 0.3)
            local shine  = fp(root, WHITE, 0.4, 0.2, 0.11, 0.035, 6, 0.3)
            shine.Rotation = -28

            local fly = {}
            function fly.hide()
                if root.Visible then root.Visible = false end
            end
            function fly.set(x, y, S, rot, gt, t, look, blink)
                root.Visible = true
                root.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y)
                root.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1, S), math.max(1, S))
                root.Rotation = rot
                if useCG then
                    root.GroupTransparency = gt
                else
                    for _, p in ipairs(parts) do p.o.BackgroundTransparency = 1 - (1 - p.b) * (1 - gt) end
                end
                for _, b in ipairs(bumps) do
                    b.o.Position = UDim2.fromScale(b.x, 0.78 + math.sin(t * 4.5 + b.i * 1.9) * 0.014)
                end
                armL.Rotation = 20 + math.sin(t * 3.2) * 16
                armR.Rotation = -20 - math.sin(t * 3.2 + 1.2) * 16
                local eh = 0.1 * (1 - 0.9 * blink)
                eyeL.Position, eyeL.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.42 + look, 0.36), UDim2.fromScale(0.06, eh)
                eyeR.Position, eyeR.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.58 + look, 0.36), UDim2.fromScale(0.06, eh)
                mouth.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5 + look * 1.2, 0.47 + math.sin(t * 2.4) * 0.003)
                blushL.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.36 + look, 0.44)
                blushR.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.64 + look, 0.44)
                shine.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.4 + look * 0.5, 0.2)
            end
            return fly
        end

        local function buildGhost(root, pal)
            local outCol  = pal.dark
            local armCol  = pal.base:Lerp(pal.light, 0.35)
            local eyeCol  = pal.dark:Lerp(BLACK, 0.55)
            local PEEK    = 84   -- design px of the head that sticks out above the window

            -- outline twin under every fill, so the joined shapes read as one silhouette
            local function cap(color, z)
                local o = pill(root, outCol, 1)
                local f = pill(root, color, z)
                return { o = o, f = f }
            end
            local function put(c, cx, cy, w, h, rot, ow)
                local pos = UDim2.fromOffset(cx, cy)
                local W, H = math.max(1, w), math.max(1, h)
                c.f.Position, c.f.Rotation, c.f.Size = pos, rot, UDim2.fromOffset(W, H)
                c.o.Position, c.o.Rotation, c.o.Size = pos, rot, UDim2.fromOffset(W + ow * 2, H + ow * 2)
            end
            local armL, armR = cap(armCol, 10), cap(armCol, 10)
            local body = cap(pal.light, 11)
            mk("UIGradient", { Color = ColorSequence.new(pal.light, pal.base:Lerp(pal.light, 0.3)), Rotation = 90 }, body.f)

            local function face(color, z, tr)
                local d = pill(root, color, z)
                d.BackgroundTransparency = tr or 0
                return d
            end
            local function eye()
                local e = face(eyeCol, 13)
                local g1 = pill(e, WHITE, 1)
                g1.Position, g1.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.34, 0.3), UDim2.fromScale(0.42, 0.3)
                local g2 = pill(e, WHITE, 2)
                g2.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
                g2.Position, g2.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.68, 0.7), UDim2.fromScale(0.18, 0.13)
                return e
            end
            local eyeL, eyeR = eye(), eye()
            local mouth = face(eyeCol, 13)
            local tongue = pill(mouth, C3(255, 128, 152), 1)
            tongue.Position, tongue.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.76), UDim2.fromScale(0.7, 0.5)
            local blushL, blushR = face(C3(255, 150, 188), 12, 0.15), face(C3(255, 150, 188), 12, 0.15)
            local shine = face(WHITE, 14, 0.3)    -- forehead gloss
            local shine2 = face(WHITE, 14, 0.4)

            local boo = mk("TextLabel", { AnchorPoint = HALF, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "BOO!", Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack,
                                          TextColor3 = WHITE, TextSize = 28, ZIndex = 30, Visible = false, Rotation = -8,
                                          Size = UDim2.fromOffset(120, 40) }, root)
            local booS = mk("UIStroke", { Color = outCol, Thickness = 3 }, boo)

            -- candles standing on the top edge of the window (bases hidden behind it). Cartoon faces, wax streaks and rings,
            -- flickering flames and dripping wax. They slowly melt down while lit. Click one to blow it out / light it
            -- again; a candle that has burnt down to a stub goes out, and clicking it swaps in a fresh one.
            local WAX = {
                { C3(250, 240, 214), C3(226, 206, 164) },
                { C3(224, 210, 255), C3(176, 156, 228) },
                { C3(255, 216, 232), C3(228, 154, 186) },
                { C3(208, 245, 232), C3(144, 202, 178) },
            }
            local CANDLES = {
                { f = 0.07, h = 40 }, { f = 0.16, h = 58 }, { f = 0.25, h = 44 }, { f = 0.33, h = 64 },
                { f = 0.67, h = 62 }, { f = 0.75, h = 46 }, { f = 0.84, h = 56 }, { f = 0.93, h = 42 },
            }
            local INK, MELT, MIN_H = C3(58, 40, 70), 150, 20   -- MELT = seconds for a lit candle to burn from full height down to a stub
            local TOPC = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
            local BOTC = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)

            local function flameLayer(par, color, z, grow, dy)   -- cartoon teardrop = circle + diamond
                local c1 = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = HALF, BackgroundColor3 = color, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = z,
                                         Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.667 + dy), Size = UDim2.fromScale(grow, 0.667 * grow) }, par)
                round(c1, 0.5)
                local c2 = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = HALF, BackgroundColor3 = color, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = z, Rotation = 45,
                                         Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.36 + dy), Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.7 * grow, 0.467 * grow) }, par)
                round(c2, 0.14)
            end

            for i, c in ipairs(CANDLES) do
                local wc = WAX[(i - 1) % #WAX + 1]
                local light = wc[1]:Lerp(WHITE, 0.4)
                c.h0, c.hh, c.lit, c.burnt, c.fv = c.h, c.h, true, false, 1
                c.b, c.bv, c.hit, c.smokeAt, c.dripT, c.ph = 0, 0, -99, -99, i * 3.7, i * 1.9

                c.glow = pill(root, C3(255, 200, 110), 4)
                c.glow.BackgroundTransparency = 0.86
                c.box = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = BOTC, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 6 }, root)
                c.body = mk("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = wc[1], BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 1 }, c.box)
                round(c.body, 0.3)
                c.bodyStroke = mk("UIStroke", { Color = outCol, Thickness = 2 }, c.body)

                -- wax lines: a highlight, a shaded side, two grooves and two rings around the candle
                local function strip(cx, w, h, cy, color, tr)
                    local f = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = HALF, BackgroundColor3 = color, BackgroundTransparency = tr, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 2,
                                            Position = UDim2.fromScale(cx, cy), Size = UDim2.fromScale(w, h) }, c.body)
                    round(f, 0.5)
                end
                strip(0.2, 0.1, 0.9, 0.5, WHITE, 0.35)
                strip(0.87, 0.2, 0.92, 0.5, wc[2], 0.5)
                strip(0.46, 0.035, 0.7, 0.55, wc[2], 0.35)
                strip(0.64, 0.035, 0.5, 0.42, wc[2], 0.5)
                c.rings = {}
                for r, off in ipairs({ 22, 44 }) do
                    c.rings[r] = { off = off, f = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = BOTC, BackgroundColor3 = wc[2], BackgroundTransparency = 0.35, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 3,
                                                                Size = UDim2.new(0.94, 0, 0, 2) }, c.body) }
                end

                -- melted pool on top; two wax streaks run down the sides and drain into a pile of wax at the base
                c.pool = pill(c.box, light, 4)
                mk("UIStroke", { Color = outCol, Thickness = 1.5 }, c.pool)
                c.drips = {}
                for d = 1, 2 do
                    local streak = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = TOPC, BackgroundColor3 = light, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 5 }, c.box)
                    round(streak, 0.5)
                    local bead = pill(streak, light, 1)
                    c.drips[d] = { f = streak, bead = bead, x = (d == 1) and 0.1 or 0.9, spd = (d == 1) and 0.13 or 0.095,
                                   ph = d * 0.37 + i * 0.13, pu = 0 }
                end
                c.pile, c.splash = 0, 0
                c.blobs = {}
                for bi = 1, 3 do   -- left, right, centre (centre is drawn last so it sits on top)
                    local bl = pill(root, wc[1], (bi == 3) and 8 or 7)
                    mk("UIStroke", { Color = outCol, Thickness = 2 }, bl)
                    bl.Visible = false
                    c.blobs[bi] = bl
                    if bi == 3 then
                        local hi = pill(bl, WHITE, 1)
                        hi.BackgroundTransparency = 0.4
                        hi.Position, hi.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.34, 0.2), UDim2.fromScale(0.28, 0.16)
                    end
                end

                -- teardrop: now and then a drop swells at the rim, lets go and falls onto the pile
                c.td = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = HALF, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 9, Visible = false }, root)
                flameLayer(c.td, outCol, 1, 1.28, 0.02)
                flameLayer(c.td, light, 2, 1, 0)
                local tg = pill(c.td, WHITE, 3)
                tg.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
                tg.Position, tg.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.36, 0.66), UDim2.fromScale(0.22, 0.15)
                c.tdS, c.tdT, c.tdY, c.tdV, c.tdSide = 0, 0, 0, 0, 1   -- 0 idle, 1 swelling, 2 falling, 3 splat
                c.tdNext = os.clock() + 2 + math.random() * 8

                -- cute face
                c.eyeL, c.eyeR = pill(c.body, INK, 4), pill(c.body, INK, 4)
                for _, e in ipairs({ c.eyeL, c.eyeR }) do
                    local gl = pill(e, WHITE, 1)
                    gl.Position, gl.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.35, 0.3), UDim2.fromScale(0.4, 0.3)
                end
                c.mouth = pill(c.body, INK, 4)
                c.blushL, c.blushR = pill(c.body, C3(255, 150, 188), 3), pill(c.body, C3(255, 150, 188), 3)
                c.blushL.BackgroundTransparency, c.blushR.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3, 0.3

                c.wick = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = BOTC, BackgroundColor3 = INK, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 5 }, c.box)

                -- cartoon flame: outline, fill and a pale core
                c.fc = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = BOTC, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 8 }, root)
                flameLayer(c.fc, C3(214, 92, 30), 1, 1.22, 0.02)
                flameLayer(c.fc, C3(255, 170, 60), 2, 1, 0)
                flameLayer(c.fc, C3(255, 242, 170), 3, 0.5, 0.12)

                c.smoke = {}
                for si = 1, 3 do
                    local sp = pill(root, C3(204, 198, 230), 9)
                    sp.Visible = false
                    c.smoke[si] = sp
                end

                c.btn = mk("TextButton", { AnchorPoint = BOTC, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", BorderSizePixel = 0, AutoButtonColor = false, ZIndex = 410 }, root)
                c.btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    local now = os.clock()
                    c.hit = now
                    c.bv = c.bv + 5
                    if c.burnt then            -- a fresh candle pops in
                        c.hh, c.burnt, c.lit = c.h0, false, true
                        c.bv = c.bv + 5
                    elseif c.lit then          -- blow it out
                        c.lit, c.smokeAt = false, now
                    else                       -- light it again
                        c.lit = true
                    end
                end)
            end

            -- the flying ghost: one copy behind the window, one faint copy above it clipped to the window rect
            local frontClip
            if D.front then
                frontClip = mk("Frame", { Name = "GhostClip", BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ClipsDescendants = true, ZIndex = 1, Active = false }, D.front)
            end
            local flyBack  = makeFlyer(root, 40)
            local flyFront = frontClip and makeFlyer(frontClip, 1)
            local fl = { idx = 0 }

            local gs, lastT = { pop = 0, popV = 0, wave = 0, wide = 0, at = -99 }, nil
            local btn = mk("TextButton", { AnchorPoint = HALF, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", BorderSizePixel = 0, AutoButtonColor = false, ZIndex = 400 }, root)
            btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                local now = os.clock()
                if now - gs.at < 0.4 then return end
                gs.at = now
                gs.popV = gs.popV + 340
                gs.wave, gs.wide = 1, 1
            end)

            return function(rect, sc, t, g)
                local dt = math.min(t - (lastT or t), 0.05)
                lastT = t
                gs.popV = gs.popV + (-130 * gs.pop - 7 * gs.popV) * dt   -- jump spring (design px)
                gs.pop = gs.pop + gs.popV * dt
                gs.wave = math.max(0, gs.wave - dt * 0.9)
                gs.wide = math.max(0, gs.wide - dt * 1.4)

                local k = sc * g
                local ow = math.max(2, 4 * k)
                local cx = rect.x + rect.w * 0.5 + math.sin(t * 0.9) * 5 * sc
                local bob = math.sin(t * 1.6) * 4 * sc * g
                local top = rect.y - (PEEK + gs.pop) * k + bob
                local bw, bh = 132 * k, 200 * k

                put(body, cx, top + bh * 0.5, bw, bh, 0, ow)

                -- stubby waving arms
                local sw = 8 + 30 * gs.wave
                put(armL, cx - bw * 0.5 - 8 * k, top + 64 * k, 50 * k, 26 * k,  30 + math.sin(t * 3.0) * sw, ow)
                put(armR, cx + bw * 0.5 + 8 * k, top + 64 * k, 50 * k, 26 * k, -30 - math.sin(t * 3.0 + 1.3) * sw, ow)

                -- eyes: blink every few seconds, go wide when scared
                local bt = t % 3.4
                local blink = (bt > 3.25) and math.sin((bt - 3.25) / 0.15 * math.pi) or 0
                local ew = (24 + 6 * gs.wide) * k
                local eh = (34 + 12 * gs.wide) * k * (1 - 0.92 * blink)
                local ey = top + 40 * k
                eyeL.Position, eyeL.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(cx - 27 * k, ey), UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1, ew), math.max(1, eh))
                eyeR.Position, eyeR.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(cx + 27 * k, ey), UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1, ew), math.max(1, eh))
                mouth.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(cx, top + 69 * k)
                mouth.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1, (15 + 11 * gs.wide) * k), math.max(1, (17 + 15 * gs.wide + math.sin(t * 2.4) * 1.2) * k))
                local bl = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1, 18 * k), math.max(1, 10 * k))
                blushL.Position, blushL.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(cx - 45 * k, top + 57 * k), bl
                blushR.Position, blushR.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(cx + 45 * k, top + 57 * k), bl
                shine.Position, shine.Rotation = UDim2.fromOffset(cx - 33 * k, top + 15 * k), -28
                shine.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1, 34 * k), math.max(1, 11 * k))
                shine2.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(cx - 52 * k, top + 29 * k)
                shine2.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1, 8 * k), math.max(1, 8 * k))

                btn.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(cx, top + 46 * k)
                btn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(8, 130 * k), math.max(8, 100 * k))

                -- "BOO!" pops up above the head and fades away
                local u = (t - gs.at) / 0.9
                if u >= 0 and u < 1 then
                    boo.Visible = true
                    boo.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(cx + 6 * k, top - 16 * k - u * 34 * k)
                    boo.TextSize = math.max(8, math.floor((30 + 10 * (1 - u)) * k))
                    boo.TextTransparency = u * u
                    booS.Transparency = u * u
                    booS.Thickness = math.max(1, 3 * k)
                else
                    boo.Visible = false
                end

                -- candles: melt, drip, flicker, squash when clicked
                for _, c in ipairs(CANDLES) do
                    if c.lit then
                        c.hh = math.max(MIN_H, c.hh - (c.h0 - MIN_H) / MELT * dt)
                        c.dripT = c.dripT + dt
                        if c.hh <= MIN_H + 0.01 then c.lit, c.burnt, c.smokeAt = false, true, t end   -- burnt down to a stub
                    end
                    c.fv = c.fv + ((c.lit and 1 or 0) - c.fv) * math.min(1, dt * (c.lit and 9 or 14))
                    c.bv = c.bv + (-90 * c.b - 9 * c.bv) * dt
                    c.b = math.clamp(c.b + c.bv * dt, -0.6, 1)

                    local cx = rect.x + rect.w * c.f
                    local baseY = rect.y + 6 * k                   -- base tucked behind the window
                    local W = 18 * k * (1 + 0.22 * c.b)
                    local CH = (c.hh + 6) * k * (1 - 0.18 * c.b)
                    local topY = baseY - CH
                    c.box.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(cx, baseY)
                    c.box.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(2, W), math.max(2, CH))
                    c.bodyStroke.Thickness = math.max(1, 2 * k)
                    c.pool.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 0)
                    c.pool.Size = UDim2.new(1.04, 0, 0, math.max(2, 7 * k))

                    for _, r in ipairs(c.rings) do
                        local o = r.off * k
                        r.f.Visible = o < CH - 12 * k
                        r.f.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -o)
                        r.f.Size = UDim2.new(0.94, 0, 0, math.max(1, 1.8 * k))
                    end

                    -- dripping wax: a bead runs down the side leaving a streak, hits the base (the pile splashes), then
                    -- the streak drains away into the pile
                    for _, d in ipairs(c.drips) do
                        local u = (c.dripT * d.spd + d.ph) % 1
                        local top0, bot = 3 * k, math.max(8 * k, CH - 3 * k)
                        local top, len, beadTr = top0, 0, 0
                        if u < 0.7 then
                            len = (bot - top0) * (u / 0.7) ^ 1.5
                        elseif u < 0.85 then
                            len = bot - top0
                            beadTr = (u - 0.7) / 0.15
                        else
                            local q = (u - 0.85) / 0.15
                            top = top0 + (bot - top0) * q ^ 1.2
                            len = bot - top
                            beadTr = 1
                        end
                        if d.pu < 0.7 and u >= 0.7 and c.lit then c.splash = 1 end
                        d.pu = u
                        d.f.Visible = len > 1
                        d.f.Position = UDim2.new(d.x, 0, 0, top)
                        d.f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(2, 4.4 * k), math.max(2, len))
                        d.bead.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0)
                        d.bead.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(2, 6.4 * k * (1 - 0.4 * beadTr)), math.max(2, 6.4 * k * (1 - 0.4 * beadTr)))
                        d.bead.BackgroundTransparency = beadTr
                    end

                    -- wax pile at the base: grows as the candle melts (shrinks away when a fresh candle is put in)
                    local want = math.clamp((c.h0 - c.hh) / (c.h0 - MIN_H), 0, 1)
                    c.pile = c.pile + (want - c.pile) * math.min(1, dt * 1.5)
                    c.splash = math.max(0, c.splash - dt * 2.5)
                    local ps = c.pile ^ 0.7
                    local vis = ps > 0.03
                    for _, bl in ipairs(c.blobs) do bl.Visible = vis end
                    if vis then
                        local Wp = (20 + 22 * ps) * k
                        local Hp = 11 * ps * k * (1 + 0.15 * c.splash)
                        local y0 = rect.y + 3 * k
                        local function blob(bl, dx, w, hv)
                            bl.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(cx + dx, y0)
                            bl.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(2, w), math.max(2, hv * 2))
                        end
                        blob(c.blobs[1], -0.42 * Wp, 0.62 * Wp, 0.55 * Hp)
                        blob(c.blobs[2], 0.4 * Wp, 0.7 * Wp, 0.62 * Hp)
                        blob(c.blobs[3], 0, Wp, Hp)
                    end

                    -- teardrop: swells at the rim, detaches, falls (with gravity) and splats onto the pile
                    local landY = rect.y + 2 * k - 11 * (c.pile ^ 0.7) * k * 0.7
                    if c.tdS == 0 and c.lit and t >= c.tdNext and CH > 30 * k then
                        c.tdS, c.tdT, c.tdSide = 1, t, (math.random() < 0.5) and -1 or 1
                    end
                    if c.tdS == 0 then
                        if c.td.Visible then c.td.Visible = false end
                    else
                        local tw, th, ty = 1, 1, topY
                        local tx = cx + c.tdSide * (W * 0.5 + 1 * k)
                        if c.tdS == 1 then
                            local ph = math.clamp((t - c.tdT) / 1.6, 0, 1)
                            tw = (1.5 + 5.5 * ph) * k
                            th = tw * (1.15 + 0.6 * ph * ph)
                            ty = topY + 3 * k + th * 0.5 + ph * ph * 2.5 * k      -- sags a little as it fills
                            tx = tx + math.sin(t * 14) * 0.25 * k * ph
                            if ph >= 1 then c.tdS, c.tdV, c.tdY = 2, 0, ty end
                        elseif c.tdS == 2 then
                            c.tdV = c.tdV + 640 * k * dt
                            c.tdY = c.tdY + c.tdV * dt
                            tw = 6.5 * k
                            th = tw * 1.75                                         -- stretches as it falls
                            ty = c.tdY
                            if ty + th * 0.5 >= landY then c.tdS, c.tdT, c.splash = 3, t, 1 end
                        end
                        if c.tdS == 3 then
                            local q = (t - c.tdT) / 0.14
                            if q >= 1 then
                                c.tdS, c.tdNext = 0, t + 4 + math.random() * 9
                                c.td.Visible = false
                            else
                                tw = 6.5 * k * (1 + 0.5 * q)
                                th = 6.5 * k * 1.75 * (1 - q)
                                ty = landY - th * 0.5
                            end
                        end
                        if c.tdS ~= 0 then
                            c.td.Visible = true
                            c.td.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(tx, ty)
                            c.td.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1, tw), math.max(1, th))
                        end
                    end

                    -- face: blinks, goes wide-eyed when clicked, sleepy when the flame is out
                    local showFace = CH > 36 * k
                    local fy = 17 * k
                    local surprised = (t - c.hit) < 0.5
                    local sleepy = not c.lit
                    local bt = (t + c.ph) % 3.7
                    local blink = (bt > 3.58) and math.sin((bt - 3.58) / 0.12 * math.pi) or 0
                    local ew = (3.4 + (surprised and 0.8 or 0)) * k
                    local eh = (4.6 + (surprised and 1.6 or 0)) * k * (sleepy and 0.22 or (1 - 0.9 * blink))
                    for _, e in ipairs({ c.eyeL, c.eyeR, c.mouth, c.blushL, c.blushR }) do e.Visible = showFace end
                    if showFace then
                        c.eyeL.Position = UDim2.new(0.3, 0, 0, fy)
                        c.eyeR.Position = UDim2.new(0.7, 0, 0, fy)
                        c.eyeL.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1, ew), math.max(1, eh))
                        c.eyeR.Size = c.eyeL.Size
                        c.mouth.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, fy + 5.8 * k)
