                        if surprised then c.mouth.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1, 3.2 * k), math.max(1, 3.8 * k))
                        elseif sleepy then c.mouth.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1, 3.6 * k), math.max(1, 1.4 * k))
                        else c.mouth.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1, 4.4 * k), math.max(1, 2.4 * k)) end
                        local bl = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1, 3.6 * k), math.max(1, 2.2 * k))
                        c.blushL.Position, c.blushL.Size = UDim2.new(0.12, 0, 0, fy + 3.6 * k), bl
                        c.blushR.Position, c.blushR.Size = UDim2.new(0.88, 0, 0, fy + 3.6 * k), bl
                    end

                    -- wick + flame
                    c.wick.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, math.max(1, 1 * k))
                    c.wick.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1, 2 * k), math.max(1, 6 * k))
                    local flick = math.sin(t * 9 + c.ph) * 0.5 + math.sin(t * 17 + c.ph * 2) * 0.3
                    local swayA = math.sin(t * 4 + c.ph)
                    local fv = c.fv
                    local fw = 11 * k * fv * (1 - flick * 0.08)
                    local fh = (19 + flick * 3.5) * k * fv
                    local wickTop = topY - 5 * k
                    c.fc.Visible = fv > 0.03
                    c.fc.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(cx + swayA * 1.2 * k, wickTop + 1 * k)
                    c.fc.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1, fw), math.max(1, fh))
                    c.fc.Rotation = swayA * 4
                    c.glow.Visible = fv > 0.03
                    c.glow.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(cx + swayA * 1.2 * k, wickTop - fh * 0.4)
                    local gsz = (46 + flick * 6) * k * fv
                    c.glow.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1, gsz), math.max(1, gsz))
                    c.glow.BackgroundTransparency = 0.84 + 0.05 * (0.5 - flick * 0.5)

                    -- smoke puffs after the flame is blown out
                    local su = (t - c.smokeAt) / 1.7
                    for si, sp in ipairs(c.smoke) do
                        local uu = su - (si - 1) * 0.14
                        if uu > 0 and uu < 1 then
                            sp.Visible = true
                            sp.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(cx + math.sin(uu * 7 + si) * 4 * k, wickTop - uu * 30 * k)
                            local sz = (5 + 8 * uu) * k
                            sp.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1, sz), math.max(1, sz))
                            sp.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35 + 0.65 * uu
                        elseif sp.Visible then
                            sp.Visible = false
                        end
                    end

                    c.btn.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(cx, baseY)
                    c.btn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(8, 30 * k), math.max(8, CH + 28 * k))
                end

                -- flying ghost: crosses the window left -> right, then right -> left, each time at a new height
                if frontClip then
                    frontClip.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(rect.x, rect.y)
                    frontClip.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(rect.w, rect.h)
                end
                if not fl.nextAt then fl.nextAt = t + 2 end
                if not fl.start and t >= fl.nextAt and g > 0.95 then
                    fl.start, fl.idx = t, fl.idx + 1
                end
                if fl.start and (t - fl.start) >= FLY.DUR then
                    fl.start, fl.nextAt = nil, t + 0.6 + (fl.idx % 3) * 0.5
                end
                if fl.start then
                    local u = (t - fl.start) / FLY.DUR
                    local lane = FLY.LANES[(fl.idx - 1) % #FLY.LANES + 1]
                    local m = 110 * sc
                    local ph = fl.idx * 1.7
                    local dir = (fl.idx % 2 == 1) and 1 or -1      -- odd crossings go left -> right, even ones right -> left
                    local fx = (dir > 0) and (rect.x - m + (rect.w + 2 * m) * u) or (rect.x + rect.w + m - (rect.w + 2 * m) * u)
                    local fy = rect.y + rect.h * lane + (math.sin(u * math.pi * 6 + ph) * 24 + math.sin(t * 2.1) * 4) * k
                    local rot = dir * (6 + math.cos(u * math.pi * 6 + ph) * 8)
                    local e = math.clamp(math.min(u, 1 - u) / 0.1, 0, 1)
                    e = e * e * (3 - 2 * e)                       -- fade in / out at the ends of the crossing
                    local S = FLY.SIZE * k
                    local bt2 = (t + fl.idx * 0.7) % 3.1
                    local blink = (bt2 > 2.97) and math.sin((bt2 - 2.97) / 0.13 * math.pi) or 0
                    flyBack.set(fx, fy, S, rot, FLY.OUT_TR + (1 - FLY.OUT_TR) * (1 - e), t, 0.014 * dir, blink)
                    if flyFront then
                        flyFront.set(fx - rect.x, fy - rect.y, S, rot, FLY.IN_TR + (1 - FLY.IN_TR) * (1 - e), t, 0.014 * dir, blink)
                    end
                else
                    flyBack.hide()
                    if flyFront then flyFront.hide() end
                end
            end
        end

        -- ---- HALO ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
        local function buildHalo(root, pal)
            local gold     = pal.base
            local goldLt   = pal.light
            local goldHi   = pal.light:Lerp(WHITE, 0.7)
            local goldDk   = pal.dark

            -- soft light pooled above the window (filled ellipse + a wide faint stroke)
            local aura = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = HALF, BackgroundColor3 = goldLt, BackgroundTransparency = 0.9, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 1 }, root)
            round(aura, 0.5)
            local glow = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = HALF, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 1 }, root)
            round(glow, 0.5)
            local gs = mk("UIStroke", { Color = gold, Thickness = 9, Transparency = 0.75 }, glow)

            -- dark gold rim around the outside of the ring
            local rim = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = HALF, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 2 }, root)
            round(rim, 0.5)
            local rimS = mk("UIStroke", { Color = goldDk, Thickness = 4, Transparency = 0 }, rim)

            -- the ring itself: gold gradient with a bright sweep that glides round it
            local ring = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = HALF, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 3 }, root)
            round(ring, 0.5)
            local rs = mk("UIStroke", { Color = WHITE, Thickness = 8, Transparency = 0 }, ring)
            local grad = mk("UIGradient", {
                Color = ColorSequence.new({
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,    gold),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.35, goldLt),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5,  goldHi),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.65, goldLt),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,    gold),
                }),
            }, rs)

            -- thin bright highlight along the top of the tube
            local hi = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = HALF, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 4 }, root)
            round(hi, 0.5)
            local hiS = mk("UIStroke", { Color = WHITE, Thickness = 2.5, Transparency = 0.35 }, hi)

            -- little jewels set around the ring; they orbit slowly (the far side is smaller and dimmer = depth)
            local NB = 10
            local beads = {}
            for i = 1, NB do
                local b = pill(root, (i % 2 == 0) and goldHi or goldLt, 5)
                mk("UIStroke", { Color = goldDk, Thickness = 2, Transparency = 0 }, b)
                local gl = pill(b, WHITE, 1)
                gl.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
                gl.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.36, 0.34)
                gl.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.3, 0.3)
                beads[i] = b
            end

            -- four-point sparkles that twinkle around the halo
            local sparks = {}
            for i = 1, 4 do
                local f = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = HALF, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 6 }, root)
                local vO = pill(f, goldDk, 1)   -- dark outline twins under the fills
                vO.Position, vO.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), UDim2.fromScale(0.34, 1.12)
                local hO = pill(f, goldDk, 1)
                hO.Position, hO.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), UDim2.fromScale(1.12, 0.34)
                local v = pill(f, goldHi, 2)
                v.Position, v.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), UDim2.fromScale(0.22, 1)
                local hz = pill(f, goldHi, 2)
                hz.Position, hz.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), UDim2.fromScale(1, 0.22)
                local core = pill(f, WHITE, 3)
                core.Position, core.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), UDim2.fromScale(0.36, 0.36)
                sparks[i] = { f = f, ph = i * 1.7, ang = (i - 1) * 1.57 + 0.5 }
            end

            -- tiny motes of light that drift up off the halo and fade away
            -- click the halo: it hops, wobbles, the jewels whirl round and every sparkle flashes
            local hs = { hop = 0, hopV = 0, wob = 0, wobV = 0, spin = 0, flash = 0, orb = 0, at = -99, dir = 1 }
            local lastHT
            local haloBtn = mk("TextButton", { AnchorPoint = HALF, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", BorderSizePixel = 0, AutoButtonColor = false, ZIndex = 400 }, root)
            haloBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                local now = os.clock()
                if now - hs.at < 0.3 then return end
                hs.at = now
                hs.dir = -hs.dir
                hs.hopV = hs.hopV - 190
                hs.wobV = hs.wobV + 260 * hs.dir
                hs.spin = 11 * hs.dir
                hs.flash = 1
            end)

            -- big white cartoon glints on the upper-left of the ring
            local shine1, shine2 = pill(root, WHITE, 6), pill(root, WHITE, 6)
            shine1.BackgroundTransparency, shine2.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1, 0.2
            local motes = {}
            for i = 1, 5 do
                motes[i] = { f = pill(root, goldHi, 5), ph = (i - 1) / 5, x = (i * 0.37) % 1 }
            end

            -- ---- WINGS: swept-back angel wings on both sides of the window --------------------------------
            -- Shape (right wing, mirrored for the left): the wing rises from the window edge in a curved "arm" (shoulder ->
            -- wrist), and rows of long feathers hang from that arm pointing down and outwards: round little coverts along the
            -- top, then short, medium and long flight feathers, the longest at the tip. All feathers lean the same way, so
            -- the outline is a smooth arch with a scalloped trailing edge, like a classic cartoon angel wing.
            local WING_K = 0.8                                            -- overall wing size
            local ARM = { { 0, 0 }, { 14, -96 }, { 112, -168 }, { 222, -128 } }   -- cubic curve points: shoulder -> wrist (steeper rise, rounder bend)
            local function armAt(u)
                local v = 1 - u
                local x = v * v * v * ARM[1][1] + 3 * v * v * u * ARM[2][1] + 3 * v * u * u * ARM[3][1] + u * u * u * ARM[4][1]
                local y = v * v * v * ARM[1][2] + 3 * v * v * u * ARM[2][2] + 3 * v * u * u * ARM[3][2] + u * u * u * ARM[4][2]
                return x, y
            end
            local WROWS = {   -- drawn back to front; a = pointing angle (deg, 0 = right, 90 = down), len/wd in px at scale 1
                { n = 14, u0 = 0.16, u1 = 1.00, a0 = 94, a1 = 66, l0 = 74, l1 = 176, wd0 = 15, wd1 = 13, tint = 0.00, lift = 0  },   -- long, slim flight feathers (almost parallel)
                { n = 12, u0 = 0.10, u1 = 0.94, a0 = 96, a1 = 70, l0 = 52, l1 = 118, wd0 = 15, wd1 = 13, tint = 0.16, lift = 0  },   -- secondary feathers
                { n = 11, u0 = 0.06, u1 = 0.88, a0 = 98, a1 = 76, l0 = 34, l1 = 70,  wd0 = 14, wd1 = 12, tint = 0.34, lift = -2 },   -- greater coverts
                { n = 12, u0 = 0.03, u1 = 0.84, a0 = 100, a1 = 80, l0 = 20, l1 = 38, wd0 = 14, wd1 = 12, tint = 0.52, lift = -5 },   -- small rounded coverts along the arm
            }
            local featherEdge = goldDk:Lerp(gold, 0.35)   -- softer outline than the halo's dark rim, so the wings read as light and airy
            local function makeWing(side)
                local W = { side = side, f = {} }
                W.holder = mk("Frame", { Name = "HaloWing", BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 0 }, root)
                local z = 1
                for r, row in ipairs(WROWS) do
                    for k = row.n, 1, -1 do   -- the tip is drawn first, so the feathers nearer the body overlap it
                        local t = (row.n == 1) and 0 or (k - 1) / (row.n - 1)
                        local fillC = WHITE:Lerp(pal.inner, row.tint * 0.6)
                        local out = pill(W.holder, featherEdge, z)
                        local fill = pill(W.holder, fillC, z + 1)
                        mk("UIGradient", {   -- cream at the root, pure white towards the tip
                            Color = ColorSequence.new({
                                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,   fillC:Lerp(pal.light, 0.5)),
                                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, fillC),
                                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,   WHITE),
                            }),
                        }, fill)
                        local sheen = mk("Frame", {   -- soft light along the upper edge of the feather
                            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.1, 0.14), Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.78, 0.2),
                            BackgroundColor3 = WHITE, BackgroundTransparency = 0.35, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = z + 2,
                        }, fill)
                        round(sheen, 0.5)
                        W.f[#W.f + 1] = {
                            out = out, fill = fill, row = r,
                            u = row.u0 + (row.u1 - row.u0) * t, a = row.a0 + (row.a1 - row.a0) * t,
                            len = row.l0 + (row.l1 - row.l0) * (t ^ 0.85), wd = row.wd0 + (row.wd1 - row.wd0) * t, lift = row.lift,
                        }
                        z = z + 3
                    end
                end
                return W
            end
            local wings = { makeWing(1), makeWing(-1) }

            return function(rect, sc, t, g)
                local dtH = math.min(t - (lastHT or t), 0.05)
                lastHT = t
                hs.hopV = hs.hopV + (-170 * hs.hop - 7 * hs.hopV) * dtH
                hs.hop = hs.hop + hs.hopV * dtH
                hs.wobV = hs.wobV + (-120 * hs.wob - 6 * hs.wobV) * dtH
                hs.wob = hs.wob + hs.wobV * dtH
                hs.spin = hs.spin * math.exp(-2.2 * dtH)
                hs.flash = math.max(0, hs.flash - dtH * 1.6)
                hs.orb = hs.orb + (0.35 + hs.spin) * dtH
                local bob = math.sin(t * 1.7) * 3 * sc
                local w, h = 118 * sc * g, 30 * sc * g
                local cx, cy = rect.x + rect.w * 0.5, rect.y - 24 * sc + bob + (1 - g) * 18 * sc + hs.hop * sc
                local pos = UDim2.fromOffset(cx, cy)
                local rot = math.sin(t * 0.9) * 4 + hs.wob
                haloBtn.Position, haloBtn.Rotation = pos, rot
                haloBtn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(8, w + 24 * sc), math.max(8, h * 2.4))
                local rr = math.rad(rot)
                local cr, sr = math.cos(rr), math.sin(rr)

                -- the light pulses gently
                local pulse = 0.5 + 0.5 * math.sin(t * 2.0)
                aura.Size, aura.Position, aura.Rotation = UDim2.fromOffset(w * 1.45, h * 2.5), pos, rot
                aura.BackgroundTransparency = 0.92 - 0.05 * pulse
                glow.Size, glow.Position, glow.Rotation = UDim2.fromOffset(w + 6 * sc, h + 4 * sc), pos, rot
                gs.Thickness = math.max(2, 14 * sc)
                gs.Transparency = 0.78 - 0.1 * pulse

                rim.Size, rim.Position, rim.Rotation = UDim2.fromOffset(w + 15 * sc, h + 13 * sc), pos, rot
                rimS.Thickness = math.max(2, 4 * sc)
                ring.Size, ring.Position, ring.Rotation = UDim2.fromOffset(w, h), pos, rot
                rs.Thickness = math.max(2, 8 * sc)
                grad.Offset = Vector2.new(((t * 0.45) % 2) - 1, 0)   -- the shine sweeps round and re-enters
                hi.Size, hi.Position, hi.Rotation = UDim2.fromOffset(w + 4 * sc, h + 3 * sc), pos, rot
                hiS.Thickness = math.max(1, 2.5 * sc)
                do   -- glints sit on the ring band, upper left
                    local ga = -2.45
                    local ex, ey = math.cos(ga) * (w * 0.5 + 4 * sc), math.sin(ga) * (h * 0.5 + 4 * sc)
                    shine1.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(cx + ex * cr - ey * sr, cy + ex * sr + ey * cr)
                    shine1.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1, 17 * sc * g), math.max(1, 4.5 * sc * g))
                    shine1.Rotation = rot - 28
                    local ga2 = -2.05
                    local fx, fy = math.cos(ga2) * (w * 0.5 + 4 * sc), math.sin(ga2) * (h * 0.5 + 4 * sc)
                    shine2.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(cx + fx * cr - fy * sr, cy + fx * sr + fy * cr)
                    shine2.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1, 5 * sc * g), math.max(1, 4.5 * sc * g))
                    shine2.Rotation = rot
                end

                -- wings: a slow, gentle flap rotating the whole wing about its shoulder (the tip lags a little, so it
                -- ripples); clicking the halo makes them beat fast for a moment
                do
                    local flap = math.sin(t * 1.5 + 0.4) * 5 + math.sin(t * 22) * hs.flash * 16 + hs.flash * 10
                    local ow = math.max(2, 3 * sc)
                    local K = WING_K * sc * g
                    for _, W in ipairs(wings) do
                        local sd = W.side
                        local sx = (sd > 0) and (rect.x + rect.w - 6 * sc) or (rect.x + 6 * sc)
                        local sy = rect.y + rect.h * 0.42
                        for _, F in ipairs(W.f) do
                            local bx, by = armAt(F.u)
                            bx, by = bx * K, (by + F.lift) * K
                            local ph = -math.rad(flap * (0.35 + 0.65 * F.u) + math.sin(t * 1.5 - F.u * 1.4 - F.row * 0.35) * 2.2)   -- minus = raised
                            local c, sn = math.cos(ph), math.sin(ph)
                            local rx, ry = bx * c - by * sn, bx * sn + by * c
                            local th = math.rad(F.a) + ph
                            if sd < 0 then rx = -rx; th = math.pi - th end
                            local L, Wd = math.max(2, F.len * K), math.max(2, F.wd * K)
                            local px, py = sx + rx + math.cos(th) * L * 0.5, sy + ry + math.sin(th) * L * 0.5
                            local pos2, rot2 = UDim2.fromOffset(px, py), math.deg(th)
                            F.fill.Position, F.fill.Rotation, F.fill.Size = pos2, rot2, UDim2.fromOffset(L, Wd)
                            F.out.Position,  F.out.Rotation,  F.out.Size  = pos2, rot2, UDim2.fromOffset(L + ow * 2, Wd + ow * 2)
                        end
                    end
                end

                local a0 = hs.orb
                for i = 1, NB do
                    local a = a0 + (i / NB) * 6.2832
                    local ex, ey = math.cos(a) * (w * 0.5 + 4 * sc), math.sin(a) * (h * 0.5 + 4 * sc)
                    local d = math.sin(a)   -- +1 = near side, -1 = far side
                    local b = beads[i]
                    b.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(cx + ex * cr - ey * sr, cy + ex * sr + ey * cr)
                    local S = math.max(1, (7.5 + 2.5 * d) * sc * g)
                    b.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(S, S)
                    b.BackgroundTransparency = 0.4 - 0.4 * (d * 0.5 + 0.5)
                end

                for _, sp in ipairs(sparks) do
                    local a = sp.ang + t * 0.2
                    local ex, ey = math.cos(a) * (w * 0.62), math.sin(a) * (h * 0.95)
                    local tw = math.max(math.max(0, math.sin(t * 2.6 + sp.ph)) ^ 2, hs.flash)   -- twinkle: mostly off, flashes briefly (all at once on click)
                    local S = math.max(0.5, (6 + 13 * tw) * sc * g)
                    sp.f.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(cx + ex * cr - ey * sr, cy + ex * sr + ey * cr - 3 * sc)
                    sp.f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(S, S)
                    sp.f.Rotation = t * 25 + sp.ph * 40
                end

                for _, m in ipairs(motes) do
                    local u = (t * 0.16 + m.ph) % 1
                    local S = math.max(0.5, (2 + 3 * math.sin(math.pi * u)) * sc * g)
                    m.f.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(
                        cx + (m.x - 0.5) * w * 0.9 + math.sin(t * 1.2 + m.ph * 9) * 3 * sc,
                        cy - h * 0.5 - u * 30 * sc)
                    m.f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(S, S)
                    m.f.BackgroundTransparency = u * 0.8
                end
            end
        end

        -- ---- BARNACLES ----------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- Acorn barnacles seen from above, the way they look crusted on a hull: a rough, slightly
        -- oval volcano of grey-ivory shell with ridged plates radiating from a raised crown and a
        -- small dark kite-shaped opening in the middle that slowly opens and shuts.
        --
        -- Layers per barnacle (back -> front):
        --   shadow   dark contact shadow on the surface
        --   base     the shell (gradient, lit top-left, dark outline)
        --   ring     faint growth ridge around the shell
        --   seams    3 thin dark lines = 6 plate seams radiating out from the crown
        --   cap      the raised crown (lighter, own outline)
        --   aper     dark opening        flesh   pink body that shows when it opens
        --   glint    small highlight on the crown
        --
        -- They are scattered (seeded, so the layout is always the same) along the bottom edge and
        -- the lower parts of the side edges, in a few dense patches with a mix of big, medium and
        -- tiny ones, the biggest drawn first so the small ones fill the gaps in front of them.

        -- { edge, patch centre (0..1 along the edge), patch spread, count }
        local BARN_PATCHES = {
            { "B", 0.10, 0.12, 7 },
            { "B", 0.34, 0.12, 6 },
            { "B", 0.58, 0.14, 7 },
            { "B", 0.84, 0.12, 7 },
            { "L", 0.12, 0.20, 5 },
            { "R", 0.10, 0.22, 5 },
            { "T",  0.03, 0.045, 5 },   -- top corners
            { "T",  0.97, 0.045, 5 },
            { "LT", 0.10, 0.20, 3 },    -- down the sides from the top corners
            { "RT", 0.10, 0.20, 3 },
        }

        local function buildBarnacles(root, pal)
            -- cartoon palette: warm cream shells, a rich tan shade, thick dark-navy outlines, a bright pink body inside
            local shellLight = Color3.fromRGB(255, 244, 216)
            local shellMid   = Color3.fromRGB(240, 214, 164)
            local shellDark  = Color3.fromRGB(196, 150, 96)
            local outline    = pal.dark
            local crownLight = Color3.fromRGB(255, 252, 235)
            local crownMid   = Color3.fromRGB(246, 224, 178)
            local seamCol    = Color3.fromRGB(176, 130, 84)
            local aperCol    = pal.dark:Lerp(BLACK, 0.2)
            local flesh      = Color3.fromRGB(255, 132, 168)

            local rng = Random.new(42)

            -- 1. generate the layout
            local list = {}
            for _, patch in ipairs(BARN_PATCHES) do
                for _ = 1, patch[4] do
                    local roll = rng:NextNumber()
                    local r0
                    if roll < 0.22 then r0 = 14 + rng:NextNumber() * 6          -- big
                    elseif roll < 0.62 then r0 = 8.5 + rng:NextNumber() * 4.5   -- medium
                    else r0 = 4.5 + rng:NextNumber() * 3 end                    -- tiny
                    list[#list + 1] = {
                        edge  = patch[1],
                        f     = math.clamp(patch[2] + (rng:NextNumber() - 0.5) * 2 * patch[3], 0.01, 0.99),
                        d     = -0.05 + rng:NextNumber() * 0.75,     -- how far out from the wall (in own radii)
                        r0    = r0,
                        ov    = 0.80 + rng:NextNumber() * 0.18,      -- oval-ness
                        rot   = rng:NextNumber() * 180,
                        aRot  = rng:NextNumber() * 180,
                        tint  = rng:NextNumber(),
                        ph    = rng:NextNumber() * 6.28,
                    }
                end
            end
            table.sort(list, function(a, b) return a.r0 > b.r0 end)   -- big ones first (behind)

            -- click a barnacle: it squashes, pops wide open showing its pink body, and the ones near it follow in a ripple
            local function poke(bn)
                local now = os.clock()
                if now - bn.pokeAt < 0.3 then return end
                bn.pokeAt = now
                if bn.cx then
                    for _, o in ipairs(list) do
                        if o ~= bn and o.cx then
                            local d = math.sqrt((o.cx - bn.cx) ^ 2 + (o.cy - bn.cy) ^ 2)
                            if d < 130 then o.pokeAt = now + 0.05 + d / 420 end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            -- 2. build the frames
            local zCur = 2
            for bi, bn in ipairs(list) do
                bn.pokeAt = -99
                bn.btn = mk("TextButton", { AnchorPoint = HALF, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", BorderSizePixel = 0,
                                            AutoButtonColor = false, ZIndex = 400 + bi }, root)   -- later (smaller) barnacles sit on top
                bn.btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() poke(bn) end)
                local z = zCur
                zCur = zCur + 12
                local h = mk("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
                                        Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = z }, root)
                local tintCol = shellMid:Lerp(shellDark, bn.tint * 0.3)

                bn.shadow = pill(h, shellDark, z)
                bn.shadow.BackgroundTransparency = 0.45

                bn.base = pill(h, tintCol, z + 1)
                mk("UIGradient", {
                    Color = ColorSequence.new({
                        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, shellLight),
                        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, tintCol),
                        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, shellDark),
                    }),
                    Rotation = 135,
                }, bn.base)
                bn.baseStroke = mk("UIStroke", { Color = outline, Thickness = 3, Transparency = 0 }, bn.base)

                bn.ring = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = HALF, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = z + 2 }, h)
                round(bn.ring, 0.5)
                bn.ringStroke = mk("UIStroke", { Color = seamCol, Thickness = 1.5, Transparency = 0.35 }, bn.ring)

                bn.seams = {}
                for i = 1, 3 do
                    local sm = pill(h, seamCol, z + 3)
                    sm.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
                    bn.seams[i] = { f = sm, ang = (i - 1) * 60 + (rng:NextNumber() - 0.5) * 14 }
                end

                bn.cap = pill(h, crownMid, z + 5)
                mk("UIGradient", {
                    Color = ColorSequence.new({
                        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, crownLight),
                        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, crownMid:Lerp(shellDark, 0.35)),
                    }),
                    Rotation = 135,
                }, bn.cap)
                bn.capStroke = mk("UIStroke", { Color = outline, Thickness = 2.5, Transparency = 0 }, bn.cap)

                bn.aper = pill(h, aperCol, z + 7)
                bn.flesh = pill(h, flesh, z + 8)
                bn.glint = pill(h, WHITE, z + 9)
                bn.glint.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
                bn.glint2 = pill(h, WHITE, z + 9)
                bn.glint2.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
            end

            -- 3. per-frame update
            return function(rect, sc, t, g)
                for _, bn in ipairs(list) do
                    local r = bn.r0 * 1.15 * sc * g   -- a bit bigger and chunkier
                    if r < 0.5 then
                        bn.shadow.Visible = false
                        bn.btn.Visible = false
                    else
                        bn.btn.Visible = true
                        bn.shadow.Visible = true
                        -- attachment point on the wall, pushed outward by d radii
                        local cx, cy
                        if bn.edge == "B" then
                            cx = rect.x + bn.f * rect.w
                            cy = rect.y + rect.h + bn.d * r
                        elseif bn.edge == "T" then
                            cx = rect.x + bn.f * rect.w
                            cy = rect.y - bn.d * r
                        elseif bn.edge == "LT" then
                            cx = rect.x - bn.d * r
                            cy = rect.y + rect.h * (bn.f * 0.45)
                        elseif bn.edge == "RT" then
                            cx = rect.x + rect.w + bn.d * r
                            cy = rect.y + rect.h * (bn.f * 0.45)
                        elseif bn.edge == "L" then
                            cx = rect.x - bn.d * r
                            cy = rect.y + rect.h * (1 - bn.f * 0.45)
                        else
                            cx = rect.x + rect.w + bn.d * r
                            cy = rect.y + rect.h * (1 - bn.f * 0.45)
                        end

                        bn.cx, bn.cy = cx, cy
                        local pk = t - bn.pokeAt   -- negative while a ripple is still on its way here
                        local sqz = 0
                        if pk > 0 and pk < 1.4 then sqz = 0.24 * math.exp(-pk * 5) * math.sin(pk * 22) end   -- squash first, then bounce
                        local w, hgt = r * 2 * (1 + sqz), r * 2 * bn.ov * (1 - sqz)
                        local pos = UDim2.fromOffset(cx, cy)
                        bn.btn.Position, bn.btn.Rotation = pos, bn.rot
                        bn.btn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(w, 10), math.max(hgt, 10))

                        bn.shadow.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(cx + r * 0.10, cy + r * 0.16)
                        bn.shadow.Size     = UDim2.fromOffset(w * 1.06, hgt * 1.06)
                        bn.shadow.Rotation = bn.rot

                        bn.base.Position = pos
                        bn.base.Size     = UDim2.fromOffset(w, hgt)
                        bn.base.Rotation = bn.rot
                        bn.baseStroke.Thickness = math.max(1.5, 3 * sc * g)

                        bn.ring.Position = pos
                        bn.ring.Size     = UDim2.fromOffset(w * 0.80, hgt * 0.80)
                        bn.ring.Rotation = bn.rot
                        bn.ringStroke.Thickness = math.max(0.8, 1.5 * sc * g)

                        local sw = math.max(1.2, r * 0.1)
                        for _, sm in ipairs(bn.seams) do
                            sm.f.Position = pos
                            sm.f.Size     = UDim2.fromOffset(sw, hgt * 0.92)
                            sm.f.Rotation = bn.rot + sm.ang
                        end

                        -- raised crown, slightly toward the light so it reads as a cone
                        local capPos = UDim2.fromOffset(cx - r * 0.06, cy - r * 0.07)
                        bn.cap.Position = capPos
                        bn.cap.Size     = UDim2.fromOffset(w * 0.52, hgt * 0.52)
                        bn.cap.Rotation = bn.rot
                        bn.capStroke.Thickness = math.max(1.2, 2.5 * sc * g)

                        -- opening: slow sine, squared, so it spends more time shut than open
                        local open = math.max(0, math.sin(t * 0.5 + bn.ph)) ^ 2
                        if pk > 0 and pk < 1.4 then   -- poked: snaps open fast, holds, then eases shut
                            open = math.max(open, math.min(1, pk * 8) * (1 - math.max(0, (pk - 0.8) / 0.6)))
                        end
                        local aw = w * (0.20 + 0.10 * open)
                        local ah = hgt * (0.09 + 0.07 * open)
                        bn.aper.Position = capPos
                        bn.aper.Size     = UDim2.fromOffset(aw, ah)
                        bn.aper.Rotation = bn.aRot

                        bn.flesh.Position = capPos
                        bn.flesh.Size     = UDim2.fromOffset(aw * 0.62 * open, ah * 0.55 * open)
                        bn.flesh.Rotation = bn.aRot
                        bn.flesh.Visible  = open > 0.05

                        bn.glint.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(cx - r * 0.34, cy - r * 0.38)
                        bn.glint.Size     = UDim2.fromOffset(w * 0.2, hgt * 0.12)
                        bn.glint.Rotation = bn.rot - 30
                        bn.glint2.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(cx - r * 0.12, cy - r * 0.52)
                        bn.glint2.Size     = UDim2.fromOffset(w * 0.07, hgt * 0.07)
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        -- ---- CRAB ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- A cartoon crab hiding BEHIND the window. Chunky, simple shapes with thick dark outlines (every part has an
        -- outline twin under all the fills, so a limb reads as one solid silhouette), a gloss stripe on top and a soft
        -- shade stripe underneath. Only what sticks out past the window edge is visible:
        --   * two GIANT claws on fat arms reaching out of the left / right sides (they wave and snap)
        --   * two big googly eyes on thick stalks over the top edge, with expressive eyebrows
        --   * wobbly antennae with ball tips
        --   * four stubby legs gripping the top edge
        -- Click any part: the claws snap angrily, the brows turn angry, the eyes pop up and the legs scrabble.
        local function buildCrab(root, pal)
            local cDark, cBase, cLight, cInner = pal.dark, pal.base, pal.light, pal.inner
            local bodyCol = cBase:Lerp(cLight, 0.08)   -- stays a rich red
            local tipCol  = cInner:Lerp(cLight, 0.45)
            local OUTZ, FILLZ, TIPZ, DETZ = 1, 10, 15, 20

            local function holder(z)
                return mk("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = z }, root)
            end
            local function put(f, cx, cy, w, h, rot)
                f.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(cx, cy)
                f.Size     = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(w, 0.5), math.max(h, 0.5))
                if rot then f.Rotation = rot end
            end
            -- a capsule / circle with an outline twin underneath and (optionally) gloss + shade stripes
            local function cap(parent, color, shaded)
                local c = {}
                c.out  = pill(parent, cDark, OUTZ)
                c.fill = pill(parent, color, FILLZ)
                if shaded then
                    c.sh = pill(parent, BLACK, DETZ - 1)
                    c.sh.BackgroundTransparency = 0.7
                    c.hl = pill(parent, WHITE, DETZ)
                    c.hl.BackgroundTransparency = 0.45
                end
                return c
            end
            local function setCap(c, cx, cy, w, h, rot, th)
                local pos = UDim2.fromOffset(cx, cy)
                c.out.Position,  c.out.Rotation,  c.out.Size  = pos, rot, UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(w, 0.5) + th * 2, math.max(h, 0.5) + th * 2)
                c.fill.Position, c.fill.Rotation, c.fill.Size = pos, rot, UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(w, 0.5), math.max(h, 0.5))
                if c.hl then
                    local r = math.rad(rot)
                    local nx, ny = math.sin(r), -math.cos(r)   -- perpendicular to the long axis, flipped to point up
                    if ny > 0.2 or (math.abs(ny) <= 0.2 and nx > 0) then nx, ny = -nx, -ny end
                    c.hl.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(cx + nx * h * 0.25, cy + ny * h * 0.25)
                    c.hl.Size     = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(w * 0.55, 0.5), math.max(h * 0.2, 0.5))
                    c.hl.Rotation = rot
                    c.sh.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(cx - nx * h * 0.28, cy - ny * h * 0.28)
                    c.sh.Size     = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(w * 0.7, 0.5), math.max(h * 0.2, 0.5))
                    c.sh.Rotation = rot
                end
            end
            -- capsule that starts at (x0, y0) and runs along the unit vector (dx, dy)
            local function setCapDir(c, x0, y0, dx, dy, len, wid, th)
                setCap(c, x0 + dx * len * 0.5, y0 + dy * len * 0.5, len, wid, math.deg(math.atan2(dy, dx)), th)
            end
            local function putDir(f, x0, y0, dx, dy, len, wid)
                put(f, x0 + dx * len * 0.5, y0 + dy * len * 0.5, len, wid, math.deg(math.atan2(dy, dx)))
            end
            local function turn(ux, uy, vx, vy, deg)   -- rotate unit vector u towards -v
                local r = math.rad(deg)
                local c, s = math.cos(r), math.sin(r)
                return ux * c - vx * s, uy * c - vy * s
            end
            local function dot(parent, color, z, tr)
                local d = pill(parent, color, z)
                d.BackgroundTransparency = tr or 0
                return d
            end

            local crab = { pokeAt = -99, exc = 0, pop = 0, popV = 0, squash = 0, squashV = 0, last = nil }

            -- ===== stubby legs (z 1..4) =====
            local PEEK = {
                { side = -1, off = 96,  k = 1.00, ph = 0.0 },
                { side = -1, off = 158, k = 0.86, ph = 1.7 },
                { side =  1, off = 96,  k = 1.00, ph = 3.1 },
                { side =  1, off = 158, k = 0.86, ph = 4.4 },
            }
            for i, L in ipairs(PEEK) do
                local h = holder(i)
                L.thigh = cap(h, bodyCol, true)
                L.shin  = cap(h, bodyCol, true)
                L.foot  = cap(h, bodyCol, false)
                L.spot  = dot(h, cDark, DETZ, 0.55)
            end

            -- ===== antennae (z 12) =====
            local antH = holder(12)
            local ANT = {
                { side = -1, spread = 24, curl = -1.2, n = 7, len = 17, w = 8.5, ph = 0.0, wob = 0.07, tip = 16 },
                { side =  1, spread = 24, curl =  1.2, n = 7, len = 17, w = 8.5, ph = 2.1, wob = 0.07, tip = 16 },
                { side = -1, spread = 6,  curl = -0.6, n = 5, len = 10, w = 7.0, ph = 1.0, wob = 0.11, tip = 12 },
                { side =  1, spread = 6,  curl =  0.6, n = 5, len = 10, w = 7.0, ph = 3.2, wob = 0.11, tip = 12 },
            }
            for _, a in ipairs(ANT) do
                local ah = mk("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 1 }, antH)
                a.c = newChain(ah, a.n, bodyCol, cBase, cDark, "ant")
                a.ball = pill(antH, tipCol, 50)
                mk("UIStroke", { Color = cDark, Thickness = 2.6, Transparency = 0.03 }, a.ball)
            end

            -- ===== googly eyes (stalks z 13, eyes z 14) =====
            local stalkH, eyeH = holder(13), holder(14)
            local eyes = {}
            for side = -1, 1, 2 do
                local E = { side = side, ph = side * 1.7 + 2 }
                E.stalk = cap(stalkH, bodyCol, true)
                E.ball  = cap(eyeH, cInner:Lerp(WHITE, 0.7), false)
                E.pupil = pill(eyeH, Color3.fromRGB(12, 10, 18), 3 + FILLZ)
                E.glint  = pill(eyeH, WHITE, 4 + FILLZ)
                E.glint2 = pill(eyeH, WHITE, 4 + FILLZ)
                E.glint2.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
                E.lid = cap(eyeH, bodyCol, false)
                E.lid.out.ZIndex, E.lid.fill.ZIndex = 5 + FILLZ, 6 + FILLZ
                E.lid.out.Visible, E.lid.fill.Visible = false, false
                E.brow = cap(eyeH, cDark, false)
                E.brow.out.ZIndex, E.brow.fill.ZIndex = 7 + FILLZ, 8 + FILLZ
                eyes[#eyes + 1] = E
            end

            -- ===== giant claws on fat arms (z 16 left / 17 right) =====
            local ARM = {
                { side =  1, fy = 0.34, k = 1.00, ph = 0.4 },
                { side = -1, fy = 0.52, k = 0.90, ph = 1.7 },
            }
            for _, Ar in ipairs(ARM) do
                local h = holder(Ar.side > 0 and 17 or 16)
                Ar.upper = cap(h, bodyCol, true)
                Ar.fore  = cap(h, bodyCol, true)
                Ar.elbow = cap(h, bodyCol, false)
                Ar.palm  = cap(h, bodyCol, true)
                Ar.fix   = cap(h, bodyCol, true)
                Ar.mov   = cap(h, bodyCol, true)
                Ar.fixTip = pill(h, tipCol, TIPZ)
                Ar.movTip = pill(h, tipCol, TIPZ)
                Ar.spots = {}
                for k = 1, 5 do Ar.spots[k] = dot(h, cDark, DETZ, 0.55) end
            end

            -- ===== click targets =====
            local function poke()
                local now = os.clock()
                if now - crab.pokeAt < 0.35 then return end
                crab.pokeAt = now
                crab.popV = crab.popV + 300      -- eyes spring up on their stalks (a damped spring, so they overshoot and settle)
                crab.squashV = crab.squashV + 9  -- the whole crab squashes and stretches like jelly
            end
            local function mkBtn()
                local b = mk("TextButton", { AnchorPoint = HALF, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", BorderSizePixel = 0,
                                             AutoButtonColor = false, ZIndex = 400 }, root)
                b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(poke)
                return b
            end
            local btnEyes, btnL, btnR = mkBtn(), mkBtn(), mkBtn()

            -- exc (0..1) is a smoothed "how worked up is the crab" value. Everything that reacts to a click is scaled by it,
            -- so the crab ramps up quickly and then calms down gradually instead of snapping back to idle.
            local function clawTheta(t, ph, pk, exc)
                local idle = 18 + math.sin(t * 0.9 + ph) * 8
                local cyc = (t / 4.2 + ph / 6.28) % 1
                if cyc < 0.2 then   -- a burst of three snaps every few seconds
                    idle = -10 + (1 + math.cos(cyc / 0.2 * 6.2832 * 3)) * 0.5 * 42
                end
                local snap = -10 + (1 + math.sin(pk * 32 + ph)) * 0.5 * 46   -- fast angry snapping
                local w = math.min(1, exc * 1.5)
                w = w * w * (3 - 2 * w)                                       -- smoothstep so the hand-over has no kink
                return idle + (snap - idle) * w
            end

            -- the claw: wrist (wx, wy), unit direction u towards the tips, side = which side of the window
            local function updClaw(Ar, wx, wy, ux, uy, side, s, theta, th)
                local vx, vy = side * (-uy), side * ux     -- +v points away from the window
                local rot = math.deg(math.atan2(uy, ux))
                local pr = 46 * s
                local px, py = wx + ux * 34 * s, wy + uy * 34 * s
                setCap(Ar.palm, px, py, pr * 2, pr * 1.94, rot, th)
                local L, FW = 80 * s, 46 * s
                -- fixed finger (outer side), curls slightly inwards
                local f0x, f0y = px + ux * 16 * s + vx * 24 * s, py + uy * 16 * s + vy * 24 * s
                local fdx, fdy = turn(ux, uy, vx, vy, 14)
                setCapDir(Ar.fix, f0x, f0y, fdx, fdy, L, FW, th)
                put(Ar.fixTip, f0x + fdx * (L - 20 * s), f0y + fdy * (L - 20 * s), 40 * s, FW * 0.76, math.deg(math.atan2(fdy, fdx)))
                -- hinged finger (inner side): theta = how wide it is open
                local m0x, m0y = px + ux * 16 * s - vx * 24 * s, py + uy * 16 * s - vy * 24 * s
                local mdx, mdy = turn(ux, uy, vx, vy, theta)
                setCapDir(Ar.mov, m0x, m0y, mdx, mdy, L * 0.96, FW * 0.96, th)
                put(Ar.movTip, m0x + mdx * (L * 0.96 - 20 * s), m0y + mdy * (L * 0.96 - 20 * s), 38 * s, FW * 0.72, math.deg(math.atan2(mdy, mdx)))
                -- chunky freckles on the palm
                local SP = { { 0.2, 0.30 }, { 0.45, -0.28 }, { 0.62, 0.22 } }
                for k = 1, 3 do
                    local sz = (8 + k * 1.5) * s
                    put(Ar.spots[k], wx + ux * 70 * s * SP[k][1] + vx * 46 * s * SP[k][2], wy + uy * 70 * s * SP[k][1] + vy * 46 * s * SP[k][2], sz, sz)
                end
            end

            -- ===== per-frame update =====
            return function(rect, sc, t, g)
                local now = os.clock()
                local pk = now - crab.pokeAt
                local dt = math.min(now - (crab.last or now), 0.05)
                crab.last = now
                -- target stays high for a moment, then eases away; exc chases it (fast up, slow down)
                local tgt = (pk < 0.7) and 1 or math.exp(-(pk - 0.7) * 1.35)
                crab.exc = crab.exc + (tgt - crab.exc) * math.min(1, dt * ((tgt > crab.exc) and 20 or 3))
                if crab.exc < 0.002 and tgt < 0.002 then crab.exc = 0 end
                local exc = crab.exc
                -- springs: eye pop + body squash
                crab.popV = crab.popV + (-150 * crab.pop - 9 * crab.popV) * dt
                crab.pop = crab.pop + crab.popV * dt
                crab.squashV = crab.squashV + (-110 * crab.squash - 7 * crab.squashV) * dt
                crab.squash = crab.squash + crab.squashV * dt
                local s = math.max(sc * g, 0.001)
                local th = 5.6 * s * (1 + 0.12 * exc)   -- outlines bulge a little when angry
                local bx = rect.x + rect.w * 0.5
                local top = rect.y

                -- eyes ------------------------------------------------------------------------
                local mouseX, mouseY, haveMouse = 0, 0, false
                pcall(function()
                    local m = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
                    local rp = root.AbsolutePosition
                    mouseX, mouseY, haveMouse = m.X - rp.X, m.Y - rp.Y, true
                end)
                local blinkF = (t / 3.6) % 1
                local blink = (blinkF > 0.955) and math.sin((blinkF - 0.955) / 0.045 * math.pi) or 0
                local pop = crab.pop * s
                local angry = exc
                for _, E in ipairs(eyes) do
                    local sd = E.side
                    local sx0, sy0 = bx + sd * 36 * s, top + 18 * s
                    local sway = math.sin(t * 1.3 + E.ph) * 6 + (math.sin(pk * 22 + E.ph) * 9 * exc)
                    local ang = math.rad(-90 + sd * 10 + sway)
                    local dx, dy = math.cos(ang), math.sin(ang)
                    local slen = (44 + math.sin(t * 0.8 + E.ph) * 5) * s * (1 + 0.12 * crab.squash) + pop
                    setCapDir(E.stalk, sx0, sy0, dx, dy, slen, 21 * s, th)
                    local ed = 48 * s
                    local ex, ey = sx0 + dx * (slen + 8 * s), sy0 + dy * (slen + 8 * s)
                    setCap(E.ball, ex, ey, ed, ed, 0, th)
                    local lx, ly
                    if haveMouse then lx, ly = mouseX - ex, mouseY - ey
                    else lx, ly = math.sin(t * 0.7 + E.ph), math.cos(t * 0.5) end
                    local ll = math.sqrt(lx * lx + ly * ly)
                    local kk = ll > 0.001 and math.min(1, ll / 140) or 0
                    local ox = (ll > 0.001 and lx / ll or 0) * kk * ed * 0.2
                    local oy = (ll > 0.001 and ly / ll or 0) * kk * ed * 0.2
                    put(E.pupil, ex + ox, ey + oy, ed * 0.52, ed * 0.52)
                    put(E.glint,  ex + ox - ed * 0.1,  ey + oy - ed * 0.12, ed * 0.17, ed * 0.17)
                    put(E.glint2, ex + ox + ed * 0.08, ey + oy + ed * 0.1,  ed * 0.08, ed * 0.08)
                    if blink > 0.02 then
                        local lh = ed * blink
                        setCap(E.lid, ex, ey - ed * 0.5 + lh * 0.5, ed * 1.02, lh, 0, th * 0.6)
                        E.lid.out.Visible, E.lid.fill.Visible = true, true
                    else
                        E.lid.out.Visible, E.lid.fill.Visible = false, false
                    end
                    -- eyebrows: happy-worried when calm, angry when poked
                    local tilt = -9 + 35 * angry
                    setCap(E.brow, ex, ey - ed * 0.68, ed * 1.0, 10 * s, -sd * tilt, th * 0.5)
                end
                put(btnEyes, bx, top - 36 * s, 150 * s, 110 * s)

                -- antennae --------------------------------------------------------------------
                for _, A in ipairs(ANT) do
                    local c = A.c
                    c.px[0], c.py[0] = bx + A.side * 8 * s, top + 12 * s
                    local a0 = math.rad(-90 + A.side * A.spread)
                    local step = A.len * s
                    local twitch = math.sin(pk * 34 + A.ph) * 0.16 * exc
                    for i = 1, A.n do
                        local an = a0 + math.rad(A.curl * 12) * (i / A.n) * 3 + math.sin(t * 2 + i * 0.55 + A.ph) * A.wob * i * 0.45 + twitch * (i / A.n)
                        c.px[i] = c.px[i - 1] + math.cos(an) * step
                        c.py[i] = c.py[i - 1] + math.sin(an) * step
                    end
                    placeChain(c, step, A.w * s, 3 * s, 1, math.max(2, 3.8 * s))
                    put(A.ball, c.px[A.n], c.py[A.n], A.tip * s, A.tip * s)
                end

                -- legs ------------------------------------------------------------------------
                for _, L in ipairs(PEEK) do
                    local sd, k = L.side, L.k * s
                    local sw = math.sin(t * 1.5 + L.ph)
                    local scr = math.abs(math.sin(pk * 18 + L.ph)) * 8 * s * exc
                    local hx, hy = bx + sd * L.off * s, top + 16 * s
                    local kx = hx + sd * 8 * k + sw * 2.5 * s
                    local ky = top - 42 * k - math.max(0, sw) * 3 * s - scr
                    local fx = kx + sd * 30 * k
                    local fy = top - 8 * s - scr * 0.4
                    local d1x, d1y = kx - hx, ky - hy
                    local l1 = math.sqrt(d1x * d1x + d1y * d1y)
                    if l1 < 0.001 then l1 = 1 end
                    setCapDir(L.thigh, hx, hy, d1x / l1, d1y / l1, l1, 28 * k, th)
                    local d2x, d2y = fx - kx, fy - ky
                    local l2 = math.sqrt(d2x * d2x + d2y * d2y)
                    if l2 < 0.001 then l2 = 1 end
                    setCapDir(L.shin, kx, ky, d2x / l2, d2y / l2, l2, 24 * k, th)
                    setCap(L.foot, fx, fy, 32 * k, 25 * k, 0, th)
                    put(L.spot, hx + (kx - hx) * 0.55, hy + (ky - hy) * 0.55, 9 * k, 9 * k)
                end

                -- arms + claws ---------------------------------------------------------------
                for _, Ar in ipairs(ARM) do
                    local sd, kk = Ar.side, Ar.k * s
                    -- the shoulder is tucked behind the window; only the part past the edge shows
                    local sx0 = ((sd > 0) and (rect.x + rect.w) or rect.x) - sd * 24 * s
                    local sy0 = rect.y + rect.h * Ar.fy
                    local raise = 14 * exc
                    local a1 = math.rad(14 + math.sin(t * 1.1 + Ar.ph) * 4 + raise * 0.5)
                    local a2 = math.rad(62 + math.sin(t * 1.5 + Ar.ph * 1.7) * 7 + raise)
                    local a3 = math.rad(100 + math.sin(t * 1.9 + Ar.ph) * 7 + (math.sin(pk * 24 + Ar.ph) * 6 * exc))
                    local l1, l2 = 76 * kk, 58 * kk
                    local ex = sx0 + sd * math.cos(a1) * l1
                    local ey = sy0 - math.sin(a1) * l1
                    local wx = ex + sd * math.cos(a2) * l2
                    local wy = ey - math.sin(a2) * l2
                    local thA = 5.6 * kk
                    setCapDir(Ar.upper, sx0, sy0, sd * math.cos(a1), -math.sin(a1), l1, 52 * kk, thA)
                    setCapDir(Ar.fore, ex, ey, sd * math.cos(a2), -math.sin(a2), l2, 46 * kk, thA)
                    setCap(Ar.elbow, ex, ey, 60 * kk, 60 * kk, 0, thA)
                    -- freckles on the arm
                    put(Ar.spots[4], sx0 + (ex - sx0) * 0.7, sy0 + (ey - sy0) * 0.7, 12 * kk, 12 * kk)
                    put(Ar.spots[5], ex + (wx - ex) * 0.55, ey + (wy - ey) * 0.55, 10 * kk, 10 * kk)
                    local ux, uy = sd * math.cos(a3), -math.sin(a3)
                    updClaw(Ar, wx, wy, ux, uy, sd, kk, clawTheta(t, Ar.ph, pk, exc), thA)
                    put((sd < 0) and btnL or btnR, wx + ux * 60 * kk, wy + uy * 60 * kk, 160 * kk, 160 * kk)
                end
            end
        end

        -- ---- FUNGI --------------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- A big cartoon toadstool that lives on a grass platform on top of the window.
        --   * TAP it: it squashes, boings and puffs spores.
        --   * DRAG it anywhere (it spins as you carry it and goes "ooh"). Let go over the window and it STAYS there, on top
        --     of the UI (let go near the top / bottom edge and it snaps onto that grass platform).
        --   * Let go anywhere outside the window and it FALLS, screaming. It drops off the bottom of the screen, comes back
        --     in from the top and lands on the top platform again. You can catch it mid-air.
        -- Flowers + small mushrooms grow out of the top and the sides, shelf fungi stick out of the side edges, and there is
        -- a grass platform on the top and bottom edge. Pure Frames, no images.
        local function buildFungi(root, pal)
            local SIN, COS, MAX, MIN, ABS, DEG, PI = math.sin, math.cos, math.max, math.min, math.abs, math.deg, math.pi
            local EXP, RND = math.exp, math.random
            local CAP, CAPLT, OUT, CREAM = pal.base, pal.light, pal.dark, pal.inner
            local LEAF, LEAFDK = C3(92, 196, 98), C3(24, 78, 52)
            local STEM, STEMDK = C3(255, 242, 214), C3(212, 180, 146)
            local EYE = C3(58, 30, 52)
            local FRONT = D.front or root
            local PLATFORMS = true   -- false = no grass platforms / bottom sprouts (the mushroom then stands on the bare window edge)
            local DIRT, DIRTDK = C3(128, 86, 58), C3(52, 30, 24)
            local GRASS, GRASSLT = LEAF, C3(150, 232, 120)
            local conns, strokes, lastSc = {}, {}, -1
            local GuiService, Debris = game:GetService("GuiService"), game:GetService("Debris")
            local sg = FRONT:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("ScreenGui")
            local SPARK = C3(255, 244, 150)
            -- Optional sound effects: paste Roblox audio ids here (like "rbxassetid://123456"). Empty = silent.
            local SOUND_IDS = { boing = "", scream = "", thud = "" }
            local function sfx(name, loop)
                local id = SOUND_IDS[name]
                if not id or id == "" then return nil end
                local snd = Instance.new("Sound")
                snd.SoundId, snd.Volume, snd.Looped = id, 0.6, loop or false
                snd.Parent = FRONT
                pcall(function() snd:Play() end)
                if not loop then Debris:AddItem(snd, 6) end
                return snd
            end
            local function guiInset()
                if sg and sg.IgnoreGuiInset then return Vector2.new(0, 0) end
                return GuiService:GetGuiInset()
            end

            local function ease(x)   -- ease-out-back
                x = MAX(0, MIN(1, x))
                return 1 + 2.70158 * (x - 1) ^ 3 + 1.70158 * (x - 1) ^ 2
            end
            local function OL(k) return MAX(1.4, 2.4 * k) end
            local function stroke(o, col, th, tr)
                local s = mk("UIStroke", { Color = col, Thickness = th, Transparency = tr or 0 }, o)
                strokes[#strokes + 1] = { s, th }
                return s
            end
            local function dot(parent, col, z, tr)   -- plain round / pill frame
                local f = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = HALF, BackgroundColor3 = col, BackgroundTransparency = tr or 0, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = z }, parent)
                round(f, 0.5)
                return f
            end
            local function place(f, x, y, w, h, rot)
                f.Position, f.Size, f.Rotation = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), UDim2.fromOffset(MAX(w, 0.5), MAX(h, 0.5)), rot or 0
            end
            -- a fill with a dark outline twin underneath it (so overlapping parts never show seams)
            local function blob(parent, col, outCol, z, r)
                local o = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = HALF, BackgroundColor3 = outCol, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = z }, parent)
                round(o, r or 0.5)
                local f = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = HALF, BackgroundColor3 = col, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = z + 1 }, parent)
                round(f, r or 0.5)
                return { o = o, f = f }
            end
            local function put(b, x, y, w, h, rot, ol)
                w, h = MAX(w, 0.5), MAX(h, 0.5)
                local p, s = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), UDim2.fromOffset(w, h)
                b.f.Position, b.f.Size, b.f.Rotation = p, s, rot or 0
                b.o.Position, b.o.Size, b.o.Rotation = p, UDim2.fromOffset(w + 2 * ol, h + 2 * ol), rot or 0
            end

            -- =====================================================================================
            --  THE BIG MUSHROOM  (lives in the FRONT layer so it can stand on top of the window)
            -- =====================================================================================
            local hh = mk("Frame", { Name = "FungiHero", BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 2 }, FRONT)
            local capW, capH, stemW, stemH, PADC = 190, 76, 58, 64, 6
            local streaks = {}
            for i = 1, 3 do streaks[i] = dot(hh, WHITE, 0, 0.6); streaks[i].Visible = false end
            local shadow = dot(hh, BLACK, 1, 0.78)
            local glow = dot(hh, CAPLT, 1, 0.88)
            glow.Visible = false   -- no aura around the mushroom
            local stem = blob(hh, STEM, OUT, 2, 0.4)
            mk("UIGradient", { Color = ColorSequence.new({
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, C3(255, 252, 240)), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, STEM), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, STEMDK) }) }, stem.f)
            local fibers = {}
            for i = 1, 4 do fibers[i] = dot(hh, STEMDK, 4, 0.45) end
            local ring = blob(hh, C3(240, 214, 176), OUT, 5, 0.5)

            local face = mk("Frame", { Name = "Face", BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 7 }, hh)
            local eyeL, eyeR = dot(face, EYE, 1), dot(face, EYE, 1)
            local glL, glR = dot(face, WHITE, 2), dot(face, WHITE, 2)
            local blL, blR = dot(face, C3(255, 132, 152), 1, 0.35), dot(face, C3(255, 132, 152), 1, 0.35)
            local mouth, tongue = dot(face, EYE, 1), dot(face, C3(255, 128, 140), 2)

            local gills = blob(hh, C3(255, 216, 200), OUT, 10, 0.5)
            local gl = {}
            for i = 1, 10 do gl[i] = dot(hh, C3(170, 100, 110), 12, 0.25) end

            -- the cap: only the top half of a big ellipse is shown (clipped), so the underside is flat
            local clip = mk("Frame", { Name = "Cap", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ClipsDescendants = true, ZIndex = 13 }, hh)
            local dome = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = HALF, BackgroundColor3 = WHITE, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 1 }, clip)
            round(dome, 0.5)
            mk("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Color = ColorSequence.new({
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, CAPLT), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.22, CAP),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, CAP:Lerp(OUT, 0.32)), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, OUT) }) }, dome)
            stroke(dome, OUT, 3)
            local shineA = dot(clip, WHITE, 3, 0.5)
            shineA.Rotation = -18
            local shineB = dot(clip, WHITE, 3, 0.3)
            local SPOT_DEF = { { 0.0, 0.5, 22 }, { 0.7, 0.95, 15 }, { 1.3, 0.4, 18 }, { 1.9, 1.1, 13 }, { 2.5, 0.6, 24 },
                               { 3.1, 0.9, 16 }, { 3.8, 0.45, 20 }, { 4.4, 1.05, 14 }, { 5.0, 0.65, 19 }, { 5.6, 0.35, 15 } }
            local spots = {}
            for i, d in ipairs(SPOT_DEF) do
                local sp = dot(clip, CREAM, 2)
                mk("UIStroke", { Color = OUT, Thickness = 1.5, Transparency = 0.55 }, sp)
                spots[i] = { f = sp, phi = d[1], th = d[2], size = d[3] }
            end
            local baseLine = dot(hh, OUT, 14)   -- closes the flat underside of the cap

            -- spore pool
            local pool = {}
            for i = 1, 16 do
                local f = dot(hh, C3(240, 255, 190), 20, 1)
                f.Visible = false
                pool[i] = { f = f, life = 0, max = 1, x = 0, y = 0, vx = 0, vy = 0, sz = 6 }
            end

            -- dust puffs (landings), dizzy eyes + orbiting stars, sleepy Zzz, rain cloud + drops, puddle
            local dustP = {}
            for i = 1, 12 do
                local f = dot(hh, C3(226, 212, 186), 19, 1)
                f.Visible = false
                dustP[i] = { f = f, life = 0, max = 1, x = 0, y = 0, vx = 0, sz = 8 }
            end
            local function dust(x, y, n, spread, sc)
                local c = 0
                for _, q in ipairs(dustP) do
                    if q.life <= 0 and c < n then
                        c = c + 1
                        local dir = (c % 2 == 0) and 1 or -1
                        q.x, q.y, q.vx = x + dir * RND() * 12 * sc, y, dir * (40 + RND() * 90 * spread) * sc
                        q.sz, q.max = 7 + RND() * 7, 0.5 + RND() * 0.4
                        q.life = q.max
                        q.f.Visible = true
                    end
                end
            end
            local function dizzyEye()
                return mk("TextLabel", { AnchorPoint = HALF, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "@", TextColor3 = EYE, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
                    TextSize = 14, ZIndex = 3, Visible = false, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, face)
            end
            local dzL, dzR = dizzyEye(), dizzyEye()
            local stars = {}
            for i = 1, 3 do stars[i] = dot(hh, C3(255, 226, 90), 25); stars[i].Visible = false end
            local zzz = {}
            for i = 1, 3 do
                zzz[i] = mk("TextLabel", { AnchorPoint = HALF, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "z", TextColor3 = C3(214, 228, 255), Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
                    TextSize = 12, TextStrokeColor3 = OUT, TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5, ZIndex = 26, Visible = false, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, hh)
            end
            local cloud = {}
            for i = 1, 4 do cloud[i] = dot(hh, (i == 4) and C3(190, 203, 224) or C3(238, 244, 252), (i == 4) and 23 or 24); cloud[i].Visible = false end
            local drops = {}
            for i = 1, 10 do drops[i] = dot(hh, C3(140, 200, 255), 6); drops[i].Visible = false end
            local puddle = dot(hh, C3(120, 180, 230), 0, 0.5)
            puddle.Visible = false
            local LEAK_X = { -0.44, -0.35, -0.27, 0.27, 0.35, 0.44 }   -- where water drips off the underside of the cap
            local leak = {}
            for i = 1, 6 do leak[i] = dot(hh, C3(150, 205, 255), 15); leak[i].Visible = false end

            -- STATE
            --   mode "anchor": standing on the UI (S.ax / S.ay = where, as a fraction of the window; ay 0 = top platform, 1 = bottom platform)
            --   mode "held":   being carried by the pointer
            --   mode "fall":   falling (screaming) after being dropped outside the window
            local S = { mode = "anchor", ax = 0.5, ay = 0, x = 0, y = 0, px = 0, py = 0, vx = 0, vy = 0,
                        yaw = 0, spin = 0, drag = false, moved = 0, downAt = 0, pendX = 0, pendY = 0, accDX = 0, accDY = 0, hvx = 0, hvy = 0,
                        sq = 0, sqV = 0, lean = 0, leanV = 0, scr = 0, ooh = 0,
                        idle = 1, blinkAt = 0, blinkT = -9, emitAt = 0, nextAmbient = 0, tx = 0, ty = 0, sc = 1 }
            S.lastAct, S.yawnT, S.yawnNext, S.sleepy, S.dizzy, S.fallT = os.clock(), -9, 0, 0, 0, 0
            S.shake, S.lx, S.bend, S.bendTop, S.wasFall, S.scrSnd = 0, 0, 0, true, false, nil
            S.cloud, S.puddle, S.rainOn, S.rainNext, S.rainEnd = 0, 0, false, os.clock() + 25, 0
            S.wet = 0   -- how soaked the cap is (drips for a while after the rain stops)
            S.mad, S.love, S.surp, S.happy = 0, 0, 0, 0          -- face moods (timers in seconds, happy is smoothed)
            S.sneezeT0, S.sneezeNext, S.sneezeFired = -9, 0, true
            S.winkT, S.winkNext, S.pokeN, S.pokeT = -9, os.clock() + 8, 0, 0
            local addBaby   -- assigned further down (needs the grass platform)

            local function spawn(x, y, vx, vy, sz, life)
                for _, q in ipairs(pool) do
                    if q.life <= 0 then
                        q.x, q.y, q.vx, q.vy, q.sz, q.max, q.life = x, y, vx, vy, sz, life, life
                        q.f.Visible = true
                        return
                    end
                end
            end
            local function burst(n)
                local sc = S.sc
                for _ = 1, n do
                    local a = -PI / 2 + (RND() - 0.5) * 2.6
                    local v = (60 + RND() * 110) * sc
                    spawn(S.tx + (RND() - 0.5) * capW * 0.5 * sc, S.ty + 4 * sc, COS(a) * v, SIN(a) * v, 5 + RND() * 5, 0.9 + RND() * 0.8)
                end
            end
            local function click()
                S.lastAct = os.clock()
                sfx("boing")
                if os.clock() - S.pokeT < 1.2 then S.pokeN = S.pokeN + 1 else S.pokeN = 1 end
                S.pokeT = os.clock()
                if S.pokeN >= 4 then S.mad, S.pokeN = 2.8, 0; S.leanV = S.leanV + 12 end   -- poked too much: grumpy
                S.sqV = S.sqV - 11
                S.leanV = S.leanV + ((RND() < 0.5) and -9 or 9)
                S.spin = S.spin + ((RND() < 0.5) and -7 or 7)
                burst(14)
            end
            local function land()   -- touches down on the top platform
                local rect, sc = S.rect, S.sc
                local hard = MIN(1, MAX(0, S.vy / (1700 * sc)))
                local fromFall = S.mode == "fall"
                S.ax = MAX(0.04, MIN(0.96, (S.x - rect.x) / MAX(rect.w, 1)))
                S.ay = 0
                S.mode = "anchor"
                S.sqV = S.sqV - 14 - 34 * hard   -- a hard landing splats it
                S.leanV = S.leanV + ((RND() < 0.5) and -7 or 7)
                burst(10 + math.floor(8 * hard))
                dust(S.x, S.y, 6 + math.floor(6 * hard), 0.6 + hard, sc)
                S.shake, S.lx, S.bend, S.bendTop = 0.6 + 0.4 * hard, S.x, 1, true   -- flowers sway, grass bends
                S.lastAct = os.clock()
                if S.fallT > 1.1 then S.dizzy = 2.6 end   -- long fall = dizzy spiral eyes
                S.fallT = 0
                if hard > 0.15 then sfx("thud") end
                if fromFall and addBaby then addBaby(S.x) end   -- a baby mushroom sprouts every time it is dropped
            end
            local function release()   -- the pointer was let go while carrying it
                local rect, sc = S.rect, S.sc
                if not rect then S.mode = "anchor" return end
                local inX = S.x >= rect.x and S.x <= rect.x + rect.w
                if inX and S.y >= rect.y - 2 * sc and S.y <= rect.y + rect.h + 40 * sc then
                    -- let go over the window: it stays right there, on top of the UI (near an edge it snaps onto that platform)
                    local ay = (S.y - rect.y) / MAX(rect.h, 1)
                    if S.y < rect.y + 26 * sc then ay = 0 elseif S.y > rect.y + rect.h - 26 * sc then ay = 1 end
                    S.ax = MAX(0.04, MIN(0.96, (S.x - rect.x) / MAX(rect.w, 1)))
                    S.ay = MAX(0, MIN(1, ay))
                    S.mode = "anchor"
                    S.sqV = S.sqV - 9
                    burst(6)
                    S.lastAct = os.clock()
                    dust(S.x, S.y, 4, 0.5, sc)
                    if S.ay <= 0 or S.ay >= 1 then S.lx, S.shake, S.bend, S.bendTop = S.x, 0.4, 0.6, S.ay <= 0 end
                else
                    -- let go outside: it falls (a little of the throw is kept)
                    S.mode = "fall"
                    S.vx = MAX(-800 * sc, MIN(800 * sc, S.hvx)) * 0.5
                    S.vy = MAX(-1400 * sc, MIN(500 * sc, S.hvy)) * 0.6
                    S.py = S.y
                end
            end

            -- hit areas: the cap and the stem
            local function newBtn() return mk("TextButton", { AnchorPoint = HALF, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", BorderSizePixel = 0, AutoButtonColor = false, ZIndex = 400 }, hh) end
            local btnCap, btnStem = newBtn(), newBtn()
            local function alive()
                if root.Parent then return true end
                for _, c in ipairs(conns) do c:Disconnect() end
                table.clear(conns)
                return false
            end
            local function isPress(ut) return ut == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or ut == Enum.UserInputType.Touch end
            local function onPress(input, stem)
                if isPress(input.UserInputType) then
                    S.drag, S.moved, S.downAt, S.pendX, S.pendY = true, 0, os.clock(), 0, 0
                    S.grabStem, S.spinDrag = stem, false
                    S.lastAct = os.clock()
                end
            end
            -- grab the CAP to carry it around, grab the STEM and swipe sideways to spin it in place
            btnCap.InputBegan:Connect(function(i) onPress(i, false) end)
            btnStem.InputBegan:Connect(function(i) onPress(i, true) end)
            conns[#conns + 1] = UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
                if not alive() or not S.drag then return end
                S.lastAct = os.clock()
                local ut = input.UserInputType
                if ut ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement and ut ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
                local dx, dy = input.Delta.X, input.Delta.Y
                S.moved = S.moved + ABS(dx) + ABS(dy)
                if S.mode ~= "held" then
                    S.pendX, S.pendY = S.pendX + dx, S.pendY + dy
                    if S.moved < 6 then return end   -- still just a tap
                    if S.grabStem and S.mode == "anchor" then   -- swiping the stem: spin it, don't move it
                        S.spinDrag = true
                        S.accDX = S.accDX + dx
                        S.yaw = S.yaw + dx * 0.035
                        return
                    end
                    S.mode, S.vx, S.vy = "held", 0, 0   -- (this also lets you catch it while it falls)
                    dx, dy = S.pendX, S.pendY
                end
                S.accDX, S.accDY = S.accDX + dx, S.accDY + dy
                local scr = FRONT.AbsoluteSize
                S.x = MAX(0, MIN(scr.X, S.x + dx))
                S.y = MAX(-200 * S.sc, MIN(scr.Y + 90 * S.sc, S.y + dy))
                S.yaw = S.yaw + dx * 0.022   -- it spins a little as you carry it
            end)
            conns[#conns + 1] = UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
                if not alive() then return end
                if S.drag and isPress(input.UserInputType) then
                    S.drag = false
                    if S.spinDrag then
                        S.spinDrag = false
                        S.spin = MAX(-14, MIN(14, S.hvx * 0.035))   -- keeps coasting with the swipe
                    elseif S.mode == "held" then release()
                    elseif S.moved < 6 and os.clock() - S.downAt < 0.45 then click() end
                end
            end)
            root.Destroying:Connect(function()
                for _, c in ipairs(conns) do c:Disconnect() end
                table.clear(conns)
            end)

            local lastT
            local function updHero(rect, sc, t, g)
                local dt = MIN(t - (lastT or t), 0.05)
                lastT = t
                S.sc, S.rect = sc, rect
                local scrSize = FRONT.AbsoluteSize
                local topSurf, botSurf = rect.y - 4 * sc, rect.y + rect.h - 6 * sc   -- tops of the two grass platforms
                local heroH = (stemH + capH) * sc

                -- ---- where is the base of the stem? ----
                if S.mode == "anchor" then
                    S.x = rect.x + rect.w * S.ax
                    if S.ay <= 0 then S.y = topSurf + sc
                    elseif S.ay >= 1 then S.y = botSurf + sc
                    else S.y = rect.y + rect.h * S.ay end
                    S.vx, S.vy = 0, 0
                elseif S.mode == "fall" then
                    S.fallT = S.fallT + dt
                    S.py = S.y
                    S.vy = MIN(S.vy + 2400 * sc * dt, 1700 * sc)
                    S.x = S.x + S.vx * dt
                    S.y = S.y + S.vy * dt
                    local surf = topSurf + sc
                    if S.py <= surf and S.y >= surf and S.x >= rect.x + rect.w * 0.04 and S.x <= rect.x + rect.w * 0.96 then
                        S.y = surf
                        land()
                    elseif S.y - heroH * 1.1 > scrSize.Y then
                        -- it fell off the bottom of the screen: it drops back in from the top, above the window
                        S.x = rect.x + rect.w * 0.5   -- always comes back down in the middle of the window
                        S.y, S.py, S.vx, S.vy = -20 * sc, -20 * sc, 0, 0
                        if surf <= S.y then land() end
                    end
                end
                local held, falling = S.mode == "held", S.mode == "fall"

                -- ---- little life: sound, sleepy / yawn, dizzy, shake + bend decay ----
                if falling and not S.wasFall then S.scrSnd = sfx("scream", true) end
                if S.wasFall and not falling and S.scrSnd then S.scrSnd:Destroy() S.scrSnd = nil end
                S.wasFall = falling
                if held then S.fallT = 0 end
                if held or falling or S.spinDrag then S.lastAct = t end
                S.shake = S.shake * EXP(-2.2 * dt)
                S.bend = S.bend * EXP(-2.6 * dt)
                if S.dizzy > 0 then S.dizzy = MAX(0, S.dizzy - dt) end
                local dizK = MIN(1, S.dizzy / 0.35)
                local idleFor = t - S.lastAct
                local sleepT = (S.mode == "anchor" and idleFor > 25 and S.dizzy <= 0) and 1 or 0
                S.sleepy = S.sleepy + (sleepT - S.sleepy) * MIN(1, dt * 1.5)
                if S.mode == "anchor" and idleFor > 8 and S.sleepy < 0.2 and t > S.yawnNext then S.yawnT = t; S.yawnNext = t + 9 + RND() * 6 end
                local yp = (t - S.yawnT) / 1.6
                local yawn = (yp >= 0 and yp <= 1) and SIN(yp * PI) or 0
                local spinV = S.spinDrag and MAX(-14, MIN(14, S.hvx * 0.035)) or S.spin
                local gk = MIN(1, ABS(spinV) / 9)   -- how fast it is spinning (spots glow)
                S.mad, S.love, S.surp = MAX(0, S.mad - dt), MAX(0, S.love - dt), MAX(0, S.surp - dt)
                local madK, loveK, surpK = MIN(1, S.mad / 0.3), MIN(1, S.love / 0.3), MIN(1, S.surp / 0.2)
                local happyT = ((S.rainOn and S.cloud > 0.6) or gk > 0.55 or S.love > 0) and 1 or 0   -- loves rain, spinning and snail pets
                S.happy = S.happy + (happyT - S.happy) * MIN(1, dt * 6)
                local happyK = S.happy * (1 - madK)
                -- shiver + sneeze while the cap is still wet after the rain
                local shiver = (S.mode == "anchor" and S.wet > 0.2 and not S.rainOn) and S.wet or 0
                if shiver > 0.25 and S.sleepy < 0.3 and t > S.sneezeNext then S.sneezeT0, S.sneezeNext, S.sneezeFired = t, t + 7 + RND() * 6, false end
                local snp = (t - S.sneezeT0) / 1.0
                local sneezeK, sneezeMouth = 0, 0
                if snp >= 0 and snp <= 1 then
                    sneezeK = (snp > 0.35) and 1 or 0   -- eyes screw shut
                    sneezeMouth = (snp < 0.7) and (snp / 0.7) or (1 - (snp - 0.7) / 0.3) * 1.3
                    if snp >= 0.7 and not S.sneezeFired then   -- AH-CHOO
                        S.sneezeFired = true
                        S.sqV = S.sqV - 13
                        S.leanV = S.leanV + ((RND() < 0.5) and -9 or 9)
                        burst(12)
                    end
                end
                -- an occasional wink while you are around
                if idleFor < 6 and S.mode == "anchor" and t > S.winkNext then S.winkT, S.winkNext = t, t + 10 + RND() * 10 end
                local wink = (t - S.winkT) < 0.45

                -- pointer speed (used for the swing while carried and for the throw)
                local ix, iy = S.accDX, S.accDY
                S.accDX, S.accDY = 0, 0
                local rr = MIN(1, dt * 10)
                S.hvx = S.hvx + (ix / MAX(dt, 0.001) - S.hvx) * rr
                S.hvy = S.hvy + (iy / MAX(dt, 0.001) - S.hvy) * rr

                -- face mood: "ooh" while carried, a scream while falling
                S.scr = S.scr + (((falling) and 1 or 0) - S.scr) * MIN(1, dt * 14)
                S.ooh = S.ooh + (((held) and 1 or 0) - S.ooh) * MIN(1, dt * 14)

                -- springs: squash / stretch and lean (it swings while carried, flaps while falling)
                local leanT = 0
                if held then leanT = -MAX(-1, MIN(1, S.hvx / 900)) elseif falling then leanT = SIN(t * 9) * 0.6 end
                if dizK > 0 then leanT = leanT + SIN(t * 6.5) * 0.55 * dizK end
                if shiver > 0 then leanT = leanT + SIN(t * 38) * 0.22 * shiver end
                S.sqV = S.sqV + (-150 * S.sq - 8 * S.sqV) * dt
                S.sq = S.sq + S.sqV * dt
                S.leanV = S.leanV + (-90 * (S.lean - leanT) - 6 * S.leanV) * dt
                S.lean = S.lean + S.leanV * dt

                -- spin: coasts after a click, settles with the face to the front (right away while falling so the scream shows)
                if held or S.spinDrag then
                    S.spin = S.spin * EXP(-4 * dt)
                else
                    S.yaw = S.yaw + S.spin * dt
                    S.spin = S.spin * EXP((falling and -6 or -1.7) * dt)
                    if falling or ABS(S.spin) < 0.7 then
                        local target = math.floor(S.yaw / (2 * PI) + 0.5) * 2 * PI
                        S.yaw = S.yaw + (target - S.yaw) * (1 - EXP((falling and -5 or -2.4) * dt))
                    end
                end
                local calm = (S.mode == "anchor" and ABS(S.spin) < 1.2 and not S.spinDrag) and 1 or 0
                S.idle = S.idle + (calm - S.idle) * MIN(1, dt * 3)
