                local yaw = S.yaw + SIN(t * 0.8) * 0.3 * S.idle * (1 - S.sleepy)   -- idle: it glances left and right

                -- ---- layout ----
                local k = sc * MAX(g, 0)
                local ol = OL(k)
                local bx, by = S.x + SIN(t * 47) * 1.6 * sc * S.scr + SIN(t * 60) * 1.4 * sc * madK + SIN(t * 45) * 0.8 * sc * shiver, S.y
                local sq = S.sq
                local sH, sW = stemH * k * (1 + 0.07 * sq), stemW * k * (1 - 0.05 * sq)
                local cW, cH = capW * k * (1 - 0.10 * sq), capH * k * (1 + 0.20 * sq)
                local lx = S.lean * 8 * k
                local stemCX = bx + lx * 0.25
                put(stem, stemCX, by - sH * 0.5, sW, sH, S.lean * 4, ol)

                -- soft ground shadow (only while it stands somewhere)
                shadow.Visible = S.mode == "anchor"
                place(shadow, bx, by + sc, sW * 1.7, 9 * k)

                local capBottom = by - sH * 0.875
                local ccx = bx + lx
                local p = PADC * k
                clip.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(ccx, capBottom)
                clip.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(cW + 2 * p, cH + p)
                dome.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(cW * 0.5 + p, cH + p)
                dome.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(cW, 2 * cH)
                place(shineA, p + cW * 0.26, p + cH * 0.27, cW * 0.26, cH * 0.15, -18)
                place(shineB, p + cW * 0.41, p + cH * 0.15, cW * 0.06, cH * 0.07)
                place(baseLine, ccx, capBottom, cW + 3 * k, 3.6 * k)

                local pulse = 0.5 + 0.5 * SIN(t * 1.8)
                place(glow, ccx, capBottom - cH * 0.55, cW * 1.4, cH * 1.9)
                glow.BackgroundTransparency = 0.91 - 0.05 * pulse - 0.1 * S.sleepy - 0.1 * gk

                -- speed lines above the cap while it plummets
                local sv = falling and MAX(0, MIN(1, (S.vy / sc - 300) / 900)) or 0
                for i = 1, 3 do
                    local f = streaks[i]
                    if sv > 0.02 then
                        f.Visible = true
                        local len = (30 + 70 * sv + i * 6) * k
                        place(f, ccx + (i - 2) * cW * 0.32, capBottom - cH - len * 0.5 - (8 + i * 5) * k, 2.5 * k, len)
                        f.BackgroundTransparency = 0.75 - 0.3 * sv
                    else
                        f.Visible = false
                    end
                end

                -- spots wrap round the dome (fake 3D)
                for _, sp in ipairs(spots) do
                    local a = sp.phi + yaw
                    local ca, sa = COS(a), SIN(a)
                    local cth, sth = COS(sp.th), SIN(sp.th)
                    if ca + 0.3 * sth > 0.08 then
                        sp.f.Visible = true
                        sp.f.BackgroundColor3 = CREAM:Lerp(SPARK, gk)
                        local x = p + cW * 0.5 + sa * cth * cW * 0.5
                        local y = (cH + p) - sth * cH * 0.97 + 0.1 * cH * ca * cth
                        place(sp.f, x, y, sp.size * k * MAX(0.2, ca), sp.size * k * (0.55 + 0.45 * cth))
                    else
                        sp.f.Visible = false
                    end
                end

                -- gills + their ridges wrap round the underside
                local gy = capBottom + 1 * k
                put(gills, ccx, gy, cW * 0.97, 17 * k, 0, ol)
                for i = 1, #gl do
                    local a = yaw + (i - 1) * (2 * PI / #gl)
                    local c = COS(a)
                    local f = gl[i]
                    if c > 0.12 then
                        f.Visible = true
                        place(f, ccx + SIN(a) * cW * 0.45, gy + 3.5 * k, 2.2 * k * c, 7 * k)
                    else
                        f.Visible = false
                    end
                end

                -- skirt ring + stem fibres + the face (all wrap round the stem)
                put(ring, stemCX, by - sH * 0.69, sW * 1.22, 11 * k, S.lean * 2, ol)
                for i = 1, #fibers do
                    local a = yaw + 0.7 + (i - 1) * 1.45
                    local c = COS(a)
                    local f = fibers[i]
                    if c > 0.1 then
                        f.Visible = true
                        place(f, stemCX + SIN(a) * sW * 0.44, by - sH * 0.22, 1.6 * k * c, 15 * k)
                    else
                        f.Visible = false
                    end
                end
                local fc = COS(yaw)
                if fc > 0.2 then
                    face.Visible = true
                    if t > S.blinkAt then S.blinkT = t; S.blinkAt = t + 2.2 + RND() * 2.8 end
                    local blink = (t - S.blinkT) < 0.13 and S.scr < 0.3 and S.ooh < 0.3
                    local scr, ooh = S.scr, S.ooh
                    local fx, fy = stemCX + SIN(yaw) * sW * 0.42 + SIN(t * 55) * 1.2 * k * scr, by - sH * 0.4
                    -- the eyes follow the pointer (or the snail while it is being petted)
                    local lx, ly = 0, 0
                    local look = 1 - MIN(1, scr * 2 + ooh * 0.6 + S.sleepy * 1.5 + yawn + dizK + sneezeK)
                    if look > 0.02 then
                        local tx, ty
                        if S.love > 0 and S.snailX then
                            tx, ty = S.snailX, S.snailY
                        else
                            local rel = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation() - guiInset() - FRONT.AbsolutePosition
                            tx, ty = rel.X, rel.Y
                        end
                        local ddx, ddy = tx - fx, ty - (fy - 4 * k)
                        local dd = math.sqrt(ddx * ddx + ddy * ddy) + 0.001
                        local reach = MIN(1, dd / (140 * k + 1))
                        lx, ly = ddx / dd * 2.8 * k * reach * look * fc, ddy / dd * 2.0 * k * reach * look
                    end
                    local dxE = (9.5 + 1.5 * scr) * k * fc
                    local ew = 7 * (1 + 0.4 * scr + 0.2 * ooh + 0.3 * happyK)
                    local ehB = 11 * (1 + 0.45 * scr + 0.25 * ooh + 0.4 * surpK) * (1 - 0.85 * yawn) * (1 - 0.88 * S.sleepy) * (1 - 0.8 * MAX(happyK * 0.8, sneezeK))
                    local ehL = blink and 1.6 or MAX(1.6, ehB * (1 - 0.3 * madK))
                    local ehR = (blink or wink) and 1.6 or ehL
                    local showEyes = dizK < 0.5
                    eyeL.Visible, eyeR.Visible, glL.Visible, glR.Visible = showEyes, showEyes, showEyes, showEyes
                    dzL.Visible, dzR.Visible = not showEyes, not showEyes
                    place(eyeL, fx - dxE + lx, fy - 4 * k + ly, ew * k * fc, ehL * k, 18 * madK)    -- grumpy = slanted eyes
                    place(eyeR, fx + dxE + lx, fy - 4 * k + ly, ew * k * fc, ehR * k, -18 * madK)
                    local gs = (blink or ehL < 4) and 0.5 or (3 + 1.2 * scr) * k
                    place(glL, fx - dxE + 1.2 * k * fc + lx * 1.3, fy - 4 * k - ehL * k * 0.3 + ly * 1.3, gs * fc, gs)
                    place(glR, fx + dxE + 1.2 * k * fc + lx * 1.3, fy - 4 * k - ehR * k * 0.3 + ly * 1.3, (ehR < 4 and 0.5 or gs) * fc, ehR < 4 and 0.5 or gs)
                    if not showEyes then   -- dizzy spirals
                        dzL.TextSize, dzR.TextSize = MAX(6, 17 * k), MAX(6, 17 * k)
                        place(dzL, fx - dxE, fy - 4 * k, 18 * k * fc, 18 * k, t * 420)
                        place(dzR, fx + dxE, fy - 4 * k, 18 * k * fc, 18 * k, -t * 420)
                    end
                    -- cheeks: bigger + pinker when happy / in love, red when grumpy
                    local bc = C3(255, 132, 152):Lerp(C3(255, 64, 64), madK)
                    blL.BackgroundColor3, blR.BackgroundColor3 = bc, bc
                    local bs = 1 + 0.5 * MAX(loveK, madK) + 0.3 * happyK
                    local bt = 0.35 - 0.2 * MAX(loveK, madK, happyK * 0.6)
                    blL.BackgroundTransparency, blR.BackgroundTransparency = bt, bt
                    place(blL, fx - 16.5 * k * fc, fy + 3 * k, 9 * k * fc * bs, 5 * k * bs)
                    place(blR, fx + 16.5 * k * fc, fy + 3 * k, 9 * k * fc * bs, 5 * k * bs)
                    -- mouth: smile / "ooh" / scream / yawn / big happy grin / surprised O / sneeze / flat grumpy line / chattering
                    local mh = (5 + 14 * scr + 5 * ooh + 3 * MAX(0, -sq) + 16 * yawn + 3 * S.sleepy * (0.5 + 0.5 * SIN(t * 1.6)) + 6 * happyK + 9 * surpK + 15 * sneezeMouth) * k
                    local mw = (9 + 4 * scr - 2 * ooh + 4 * yawn - 3 * S.sleepy + 6 * happyK - 3 * surpK + 3 * sneezeMouth) * k * fc
                    mh = mh + (2.4 * k - mh) * madK
                    mw = mw + (13 * k * fc - mw) * madK
                    if shiver > 0.2 then mh = mh * (0.7 + 0.25 * SIN(t * 40)) end
                    local my = fy + 4 * k + mh * 0.5
                    place(mouth, fx, my, mw, mh)
                    place(tongue, fx, my + mh * 0.22, mw * 0.6, MAX(3 * k, mh * 0.4))
                else
                    face.Visible = false
                end

                -- hit areas + spore source
                place(btnCap, ccx, capBottom - cH * 0.5, MAX(8, cW), MAX(8, cH))
                place(btnStem, stemCX, by - sH * 0.5, MAX(8, sW * 1.6), MAX(8, sH))
                S.tx, S.ty = ccx, capBottom - cH * 0.98

                -- spores: a lazy puff now and then (only while it stands)
                if S.mode == "anchor" and t > S.nextAmbient and g >= 0.95 then
                    S.nextAmbient = t + 1.3 + RND() * 1.6
                    local a = -PI / 2 + (RND() - 0.5) * 1.6
                    spawn(S.tx + (RND() - 0.5) * cW * 0.6, S.ty + 2 * sc, COS(a) * 22 * sc, SIN(a) * 30 * sc, 4 + RND() * 3, 1.8 + RND())
                end
                if ABS(spinV) > 5 and t > S.emitAt and g >= 0.95 then   -- a fanned-out spore trail while it spins fast
                    S.emitAt = t + 0.035
                    local dir = (spinV > 0) and 1 or -1
                    for _ = 1, 2 do
                        spawn(ccx + dir * cW * 0.5, capBottom - cH * (0.15 + 0.6 * RND()), dir * (70 + ABS(spinV) * 9) * sc, (RND() - 0.5) * 170 * sc, 4 + RND() * 4, 0.9)
                    end
                end
                for _, q in ipairs(pool) do
                    if q.life > 0 then
                        q.life = q.life - dt
                        if q.life <= 0 then
                            q.f.Visible = false
                        else
                            local damp = EXP(-1.2 * dt)
                            q.vx = q.vx * damp
                            q.vy = q.vy * damp - 10 * sc * dt
                            q.x = q.x + (q.vx + SIN(t * 3 + q.sz * 5) * 8 * sc) * dt
                            q.y = q.y + q.vy * dt
                            local u = q.life / q.max
                            local sz = MAX(1, q.sz * sc * (0.4 + 0.6 * u))
                            q.f.Position, q.f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(q.x, q.y), UDim2.fromOffset(sz, sz)
                            q.f.BackgroundTransparency = 1 - 0.85 * MIN(1, u * 2)
                        end
                    end
                end

                -- ---- rain cloud + puddle ----
                if S.mode ~= "anchor" then S.rainOn = false end
                if S.mode == "anchor" and not S.rainOn and t > S.rainNext and S.sleepy < 0.5 then S.rainOn, S.rainEnd, S.surp = true, t + 7 + RND() * 4, 0.8 end
                if S.rainOn and t > S.rainEnd then S.rainOn, S.rainNext = false, t + 40 + RND() * 50 end
                S.cloud = S.cloud + ((S.rainOn and 1 or 0) - S.cloud) * MIN(1, dt * 2.2)
                local onPlat = S.mode == "anchor" and (S.ay <= 0 or S.ay >= 1)
                local wet = S.rainOn and onPlat
                S.puddle = S.puddle + ((wet and 1 or 0) - S.puddle) * MIN(1, dt * (wet and 0.45 or 0.2))
                local soaking = S.rainOn and S.cloud > 0.6
                S.wet = S.wet + ((soaking and 1 or 0) - S.wet) * MIN(1, dt * (soaking and 0.7 or 0.07))
                local cv = S.cloud > 0.02
                for _, c in ipairs(cloud) do c.Visible = cv end
                local rv = S.rainOn and S.cloud > 0.6
                for _, d in ipairs(drops) do d.Visible = rv end
                if cv then
                    local cs = k * S.cloud
                    local cx, cy = ccx + SIN(t * 0.9) * 9 * k, capBottom - cH - (34 + 3 * SIN(t * 1.7)) * k
                    place(cloud[1], cx - 38 * cs, cy + 4 * cs, 52 * cs, 30 * cs)
                    place(cloud[2], cx, cy - 6 * cs, 70 * cs, 44 * cs)
                    place(cloud[3], cx + 38 * cs, cy + 4 * cs, 50 * cs, 28 * cs)
                    place(cloud[4], cx, cy + 11 * cs, 118 * cs, 18 * cs)
                    if rv then
                        local y0 = cy + 14 * k
                        for i, d in ipairs(drops) do
                            local u = (t * 1.5 + i * 0.37) % 1
                            local dx0 = (i - 5.5) * 24 * k
                            local r = dx0 / MAX(cW * 0.5, 1)
                            local ground = (ABS(r) < 1) and (capBottom - cH * math.sqrt(1 - r * r)) or by
                            ground = MAX(ground, y0 + 1)
                            place(d, ccx + dx0 + SIN(t * 0.9) * 9 * k, y0 + u * (ground - y0), 2.2 * k, 8 * k)
                            d.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25 + 0.5 * u ^ 3
                        end
                    end
                end
                local pv = S.puddle > 0.03 and onPlat
                puddle.Visible = pv
                if pv then
                    place(puddle, bx, by + sc, (34 + 96 * S.puddle) * k, (4 + 5 * S.puddle) * k)
                    puddle.BackgroundTransparency = 0.55 - 0.15 * S.puddle
                end

                -- ---- water dripping off the cap after the rain (fewer drips as it dries) ----
                for i, d in ipairs(leak) do
                    local on = S.mode == "anchor" and S.wet > 0.12 + (i - 1) * 0.14
                    d.Visible = on
                    if on then
                        local u = (t / (1.5 + (i % 3) * 0.35) + i * 0.31) % 1
                        local x, y0 = ccx + LEAK_X[i] * cW, capBottom + 10 * k
                        if u < 0.4 then   -- a bead swells on the gills...
                            local q = u / 0.4
                            local h = (1.6 + 3.2 * q) * k
                            place(d, x, y0 + h * 0.5, (1.2 + 2.2 * q) * k, h)
                            d.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
                        else               -- ...then lets go and falls
                            local q = (u - 0.4) / 0.6
                            place(d, x, y0 + 4 * k + q * q * MAX(0, by - y0 - 4 * k), 2.6 * k, 6.5 * k)
                            d.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2 + 0.6 * q ^ 4
                        end
                    end
                end

                -- ---- dust puffs from landings ----
                for _, q in ipairs(dustP) do
                    if q.life > 0 then
                        q.life = q.life - dt
                        if q.life <= 0 then
                            q.f.Visible = false
                        else
                            q.vx = q.vx * EXP(-3 * dt)
                            q.x = q.x + q.vx * dt
                            q.y = q.y - 16 * sc * dt
                            local u = q.life / q.max
                            local sz = q.sz * sc * (1.6 - 0.8 * u)
                            q.f.Position, q.f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(q.x, q.y), UDim2.fromOffset(sz, sz)
                            q.f.BackgroundTransparency = 1 - 0.65 * u
                        end
                    end
                end

                -- ---- dizzy stars + sleepy Zzz ----
                for i, st in ipairs(stars) do
                    st.Visible = S.dizzy > 0
                    if S.dizzy > 0 then
                        local a = t * 6 + i * 2.094
                        place(st, ccx + COS(a) * cW * 0.38, capBottom - cH - 9 * k + SIN(a) * 6 * k, 7 * k, 7 * k)
                    end
                end
                for i, z in ipairs(zzz) do
                    local show = S.sleepy > 0.4
                    z.Visible = show
                    if show then
                        local u = (t * 0.35 + i / 3) % 1
                        z.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(ccx + cW * 0.3 + u * 26 * k + SIN(u * 6) * 4 * k, capBottom - cH * 0.9 - u * 46 * k)
                        z.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20 * k, 20 * k)
                        z.TextSize = MAX(6, (9 + 9 * u) * k)
                        z.TextTransparency = 1 - MIN(1, (1 - u) * 1.6) * MIN(1, (S.sleepy - 0.4) * 3)
                        z.Rotation = -10 + 20 * u
                    end
                end
            end

            -- =====================================================================================
            --  GROWING PLANTS (flowers + little mushrooms), SHELF FUNGI, MOSS
            -- =====================================================================================
            local function rootOf(s, rect, sc)   -- where a plant is rooted + the direction it grows (radians, y down)
                local e = s.edge
                if e == "T" or e == "B" then
                    local x
                    if s.fromC then x = rect.x + rect.w * 0.5 + s.fromC * sc
                    elseif s.fromL then x = rect.x + s.fromL * sc
                    elseif s.fromR then x = rect.x + rect.w - s.fromR * sc
                    else x = rect.x + rect.w * (s.f or 0.5) end
                    if e == "T" then return x, rect.y + 3 * sc, -PI / 2 end
                    return x, rect.y + rect.h - 4 * sc, -PI / 2
                elseif e == "L" then
                    return rect.x + 3 * sc, rect.y + rect.h * s.f, PI
                end
                return rect.x + rect.w - 3 * sc, rect.y + rect.h * s.f, 0
            end

            local N = 3
            local function newPlant(parent, spec, idx)
                local hold = mk("Frame", { Name = "Plant", BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = idx }, parent)
                local isShroom = spec.kind == "shroom"
                local segs = {}
                for i = 1, N do
                    segs[i] = blob(hold, (isShroom and STEM or LEAF):Lerp(isShroom and STEMDK or LEAFDK, 0.08 * i), isShroom and OUT or LEAFDK, 1, 0.5)
                end
                local leaves = {}
                if not isShroom then
                    for i = 1, (spec.leaves or 1) do
                        leaves[i] = { b = blob(hold, LEAF:Lerp(WHITE, 0.12), LEAFDK, 1, 0.5), j = i + 1, sd = (i % 2 == 1) and 1 or -1 }
                    end
                end
                local head = {}
                if isShroom then
                    if spec.glow then head.glow = dot(hold, spec.cap:Lerp(WHITE, 0.35), 0, 0.82) end
                    head.gills = blob(hold, C3(255, 216, 200), OUT, 3, 0.5)
                    head.cap = blob(hold, spec.cap, OUT, 5, 0.5)
                    head.spots = {}
                    for i = 1, 3 do head.spots[i] = dot(hold, spec.spot or CREAM, 7) end
                    head.shine = dot(hold, WHITE, 8, 0.45)
                else
                    head.petals = {}
                    for i = 1, 6 do head.petals[i] = blob(hold, spec.col, spec.col:Lerp(OUT, 0.5), 3, 0.5) end
                    head.center = blob(hold, C3(255, 208, 64), C3(150, 90, 20), 9, 0.5)
                    head.glint = dot(hold, WHITE, 11, 0.2)
                end
                local px, py, hd = {}, {}, {}
                local SPOTS_ON_CAP = { { -0.22, 0.16, 0.2 }, { 0.2, 0.08, 0.15 }, { 0.0, 0.3, 0.12 } }

                return function(rect, sc, t, g, hx)
                    local rx, ry, a0 = rootOf(spec, rect, sc)
                    local hide = spec.avoid and ABS(rx - hx) < (96 + (spec.r or spec.capW or 14)) * sc
                    hold.Visible = not hide
                    if hide then return end
                    local gi = MIN(1, g) * ease((t - D.born - (spec.delay or 0)) / 0.8)
                    local k = sc * MAX(gi, 0)
                    local ol = OL(k)
                    local segL = spec.len * k / N
                    local w0 = (spec.w0 or (isShroom and 9 or 4.5)) * k
                    local w1 = (spec.w1 or (isShroom and 7 or 3)) * k
                    local sgn = spec.sgn or ((spec.edge == "L") and 1 or ((spec.edge == "R") and -1 or 1))
                    px[1], py[1] = rx, ry
                    local sk = (S.shake > 0.003) and S.shake * 0.5 * EXP(-((rx - S.lx) / (170 * sc)) ^ 2) or 0
                    for i = 1, N do
                        local u = i / N
                        local h = a0 + (spec.tilt or 0) + sgn * (spec.curl or 0.25) * u ^ 1.4 + SIN(t * (spec.sp or 1.3) + (spec.ph or 0) + i * 0.7) * 0.09 * u + sk * u * SIN(t * 14 - i * 0.8)
                        hd[i] = h
                        local nx, ny = px[i] + COS(h) * segL, py[i] + SIN(h) * segL
                        local wi = w0 + (w1 - w0) * (i - 0.5) / N
                        put(segs[i], (px[i] + nx) * 0.5, (py[i] + ny) * 0.5, segL + wi * 0.7, wi, DEG(h), ol)
                        px[i + 1], py[i + 1] = nx, ny
                    end
                    for _, lf in ipairs(leaves) do
                        local lh = hd[lf.j] + lf.sd * 0.95
                        local ls = (spec.leaf or 14) * k
                        put(lf.b, px[lf.j + 1] + COS(lh) * ls * 0.5, py[lf.j + 1] + SIN(lh) * ls * 0.5, ls, ls * 0.46, DEG(lh), ol)
                    end

                    local tx, ty, th = px[N + 1], py[N + 1], hd[N]
                    local dx, dy = COS(th), SIN(th)
                    if isShroom then
                        local cw = spec.capW * k
                        local ch = cw * 0.74
                        local ccx, ccy = tx + dx * ch * 0.3, ty + dy * ch * 0.3
                        local rot = DEG(th) + 90
                        put(head.cap, ccx, ccy, cw, ch, rot, ol)
                        put(head.gills, ccx - dx * ch * 0.46, ccy - dy * ch * 0.46, cw * 0.9, ch * 0.34, rot, ol)
                        local ex, ey = -dy, dx   -- cap's "width" axis
                        for i, sp in ipairs(SPOTS_ON_CAP) do
                            place(head.spots[i], ccx + ex * sp[1] * cw + dx * sp[2] * ch, ccy + ey * sp[1] * cw + dy * sp[2] * ch, sp[3] * cw, sp[3] * cw * 0.85, rot)
                        end
                        place(head.shine, ccx + ex * -0.24 * cw + dx * 0.24 * ch, ccy + ey * -0.24 * cw + dy * 0.24 * ch, cw * 0.2, cw * 0.1, rot - 20)
                        if head.glow then
                            local pz = 0.5 + 0.5 * SIN(t * 2 + (spec.ph or 0) * 3)
                            place(head.glow, ccx, ccy, cw * (1.9 + 0.2 * pz), cw * (1.9 + 0.2 * pz))
                            head.glow.BackgroundTransparency = 0.86 - 0.06 * pz
                        end
                    else
                        local rr = spec.r * k
                        for i = 1, 6 do
                            local a = th + (i - 1) * (PI / 3) + SIN(t * 0.9 + (spec.ph or 0)) * 0.12
                            put(head.petals[i], tx + COS(a) * rr * 0.58, ty + SIN(a) * rr * 0.58, rr, rr * 0.7, DEG(a), ol)
                        end
                        put(head.center, tx, ty, rr * 0.62, rr * 0.62, 0, ol * 0.8)
                        place(head.glint, tx - rr * 0.1, ty - rr * 0.12, rr * 0.18, rr * 0.18)
                    end
                end
            end

            local function newShelf(parent, spec, idx)   -- bracket fungus: a half disc stuck to the side edge
                local right = spec.edge == "R"
                local clip2 = mk("Frame", { Name = "Shelf", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(right and 0 or 1, 0.5), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ClipsDescendants = true, ZIndex = idx }, parent)
                local disc = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = HALF, BackgroundColor3 = WHITE, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, clip2)
                round(disc, 0.5)
                mk("UIGradient", { Rotation = right and 0 or 180, Color = ColorSequence.new({
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, C3(255, 232, 170)), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, C3(255, 232, 170)),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.75, spec.col), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, spec.col:Lerp(OUT, 0.4)) }) }, disc)
                stroke(disc, OUT, 2.6)
                local rings = {}
                for i = 1, 2 do
                    local r = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = HALF, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 2 }, clip2)
                    round(r, 0.5)
                    mk("UIStroke", { Color = spec.col:Lerp(OUT, 0.5), Thickness = 1.5, Transparency = 0.3 }, r)
                    rings[i] = r
                end
                return function(rect, sc, t, g)
                    local gi = MIN(1, g) * ease((t - D.born - (spec.delay or 0)) / 0.8)
                    local k = sc * MAX(gi, 0)
                    local W, Hh, pad = spec.w * k, spec.h * k, 5 * sc
                    local x0 = right and (rect.x + rect.w - 2 * sc) or (rect.x + 2 * sc)
                    clip2.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x0, rect.y + rect.h * spec.f)
                    clip2.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W * 0.5 + pad, Hh + 2 * pad)
                    local ex, ey = right and 0 or (W * 0.5 + pad), (Hh + 2 * pad) * 0.5
                    disc.Position, disc.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(ex, ey), UDim2.fromOffset(MAX(W, 1), MAX(Hh, 1))
                    rings[1].Position, rings[1].Size = UDim2.fromOffset(ex, ey), UDim2.fromOffset(MAX(W * 0.7, 1), MAX(Hh * 0.7, 1))
                    rings[2].Position, rings[2].Size = UDim2.fromOffset(ex, ey), UDim2.fromOffset(MAX(W * 0.4, 1), MAX(Hh * 0.4, 1))
                end
            end

            local FLW = { C3(255, 128, 168), C3(255, 214, 90), C3(186, 150, 255), C3(255, 250, 244), C3(255, 150, 90), C3(120, 200, 255) }
            local RED, ORG, PUR, BLU, PNK = C3(226, 64, 72), C3(255, 160, 60), C3(168, 110, 235), C3(90, 190, 230), C3(255, 120, 170)

            local PLANTS = {   -- top edge (behind the window, growing up)
                { kind = "flower", edge = "T", fromL = 30,  len = 46, r = 14, col = FLW[1], delay = 0.10, tilt = -0.15, curl = 0.35, ph = 0.2, leaves = 2, avoid = true },
                { kind = "shroom", edge = "T", fromL = 80,  len = 32, capW = 30, cap = PUR, glow = true, delay = 0.25, tilt = 0.10, curl = 0.2, ph = 1.1, avoid = true },
                { kind = "flower", edge = "T", fromL = 124, len = 28, r = 11, col = FLW[2], delay = 0.35, tilt = 0.12, curl = 0.3, ph = 2.0, leaves = 1, avoid = true },
                { kind = "flower", edge = "T", fromR = 30,  len = 44, r = 14, col = FLW[3], delay = 0.15, tilt = 0.15, curl = 0.35, sgn = -1, ph = 0.9, leaves = 2, avoid = true },
                { kind = "shroom", edge = "T", fromR = 78,  len = 36, capW = 32, cap = ORG, delay = 0.30, tilt = -0.10, curl = 0.2, sgn = -1, ph = 2.4, avoid = true },
                { kind = "flower", edge = "T", fromR = 124, len = 28, r = 11, col = FLW[4], delay = 0.40, tilt = -0.12, curl = 0.3, sgn = -1, ph = 3.1, leaves = 1, avoid = true },
                { kind = "shroom", edge = "T", fromC = -114, len = 24, capW = 24, cap = BLU, glow = true, delay = 0.45, tilt = -0.25, curl = 0.2, ph = 0.4 },
                { kind = "shroom", edge = "T", fromC = 116,  len = 22, capW = 22, cap = PNK, delay = 0.50, tilt = 0.25, curl = 0.2, sgn = -1, ph = 1.7 },
                -- left edge (growing out sideways, curling up)
                { kind = "flower", edge = "L", f = 0.12, len = 34, r = 12, col = FLW[5], delay = 0.30, curl = 1.0, ph = 0.5, leaves = 1 },
                { kind = "shroom", edge = "L", f = 0.37, len = 30, capW = 28, cap = RED, delay = 0.40, curl = 0.9, ph = 1.3 },
                { kind = "flower", edge = "L", f = 0.63, len = 32, r = 12, col = FLW[6], delay = 0.50, curl = 1.0, ph = 2.2, leaves = 1 },
                { kind = "shroom", edge = "L", f = 0.89, len = 28, capW = 26, cap = BLU, glow = true, delay = 0.60, curl = 0.9, ph = 3.0 },
                -- right edge
                { kind = "shroom", edge = "R", f = 0.12, len = 30, capW = 27, cap = PUR, glow = true, delay = 0.30, curl = 0.9, ph = 0.8 },
                { kind = "flower", edge = "R", f = 0.37, len = 34, r = 12, col = FLW[1], delay = 0.40, curl = 1.0, ph = 1.6, leaves = 1 },
                { kind = "shroom", edge = "R", f = 0.63, len = 32, capW = 28, cap = ORG, delay = 0.50, curl = 0.9, ph = 2.5 },
                { kind = "flower", edge = "R", f = 0.89, len = 30, r = 11, col = FLW[2], delay = 0.60, curl = 1.0, ph = 3.4, leaves = 1 },
            }
            local BOTTOM = {   -- bottom edge (in front, standing in the moss)
                { kind = "flower", edge = "B", f = 0.08, len = 20, r = 9,  col = FLW[3], delay = 0.55, tilt = -0.1, curl = 0.2, ph = 0.3, leaves = 1, w0 = 3, w1 = 2.4 },
                { kind = "shroom", edge = "B", f = 0.22, len = 15, capW = 19, cap = RED, delay = 0.62, tilt = 0.1, curl = 0.1, ph = 1.0, w0 = 6, w1 = 5 },
                { kind = "flower", edge = "B", f = 0.40, len = 18, r = 8,  col = FLW[2], delay = 0.70, tilt = 0.1, curl = 0.2, ph = 1.9, leaves = 1, w0 = 3, w1 = 2.4 },
                { kind = "shroom", edge = "B", f = 0.58, len = 16, capW = 20, cap = PUR, glow = true, delay = 0.78, tilt = -0.1, curl = 0.1, ph = 2.7, w0 = 6, w1 = 5 },
                { kind = "flower", edge = "B", f = 0.74, len = 19, r = 9,  col = FLW[1], delay = 0.84, tilt = 0.12, curl = 0.2, ph = 3.5, leaves = 1, w0 = 3, w1 = 2.4 },
            }
            local SHELVES = {
                { edge = "L", f = 0.24, w = 56, h = 26, col = C3(244, 160, 70), delay = 0.35 },
                { edge = "L", f = 0.50, w = 68, h = 30, col = C3(236, 120, 90), delay = 0.45 },
                { edge = "L", f = 0.76, w = 52, h = 24, col = C3(230, 170, 80), delay = 0.55 },
                { edge = "R", f = 0.24, w = 54, h = 24, col = C3(236, 120, 90), delay = 0.35 },
                { edge = "R", f = 0.50, w = 66, h = 30, col = C3(244, 160, 70), delay = 0.45 },
                { edge = "R", f = 0.76, w = 56, h = 26, col = C3(220, 140, 100), delay = 0.55 },
            }

            local plantUpd, staticUpd, critUpd = {}, {}, {}
            for i, spec in ipairs(PLANTS) do plantUpd[#plantUpd + 1] = newPlant(root, spec, 20 + i) end
            for i, spec in ipairs(SHELVES) do staticUpd[#staticUpd + 1] = newShelf(root, spec, 50 + i) end

            -- grass platforms along the top and bottom edge (the mushroom stands on them)
            local function newPlatform(parent, isTop, z)
                local dirt = blob(parent, DIRT, DIRTDK, z, 0.45)
                local grass = blob(parent, GRASS, LEAFDK, z + 2, 0.5)
                mk("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Color = ColorSequence.new(GRASSLT, GRASS) }, grass.f)
                local drips, blades = {}, {}
                for i = 1, 16 do
                    drips[i] = { f = dot(parent, GRASS, z + 3), u = (i - 0.5) / 16, h = 4 + 6 * (0.5 + 0.5 * SIN(i * 7.31)) }
                end
                for i = 1, 26 do
                    blades[i] = { f = dot(parent, GRASSLT:Lerp(GRASS, (i % 3) / 3), z + 1), u = (i - 0.5) / 26,
                                  h = 7 + 6 * (0.5 + 0.5 * SIN(i * 3.77)), rot = ((i % 5) - 2) * 9 }
                end
                return function(rect, sc, t, g)
                    local gi = MAX(0, MIN(1, g) * ease((t - D.born - (isTop and 0 or 0.1)) / 0.7))
                    local k = sc * gi
                    local W = (rect.w - 20 * sc) * MIN(1, gi)
                    local cx = rect.x + rect.w * 0.5
                    local x0 = cx - W * 0.5
                    local surf = isTop and (rect.y - 4 * sc) or (rect.y + rect.h - 6 * sc)
                    local ol = OL(k)
                    put(dirt, cx, surf + 6 * sc, W, 12 * k, 0, ol)
                    put(grass, cx, surf + 3.5 * sc, W + 4 * sc, 7 * k, 0, ol)
                    for _, d in ipairs(drips) do place(d.f, x0 + W * d.u, surf + 7 * sc + d.h * k * 0.4, 6 * k, d.h * k) end
                    local bendK = (S.bend > 0.003 and S.bendTop == isTop) and S.bend or 0
                    for _, b in ipairs(blades) do
                        local bxp = x0 + W * b.u
                        local extra = 0
                        if bendK > 0 then extra = bendK * EXP(-((bxp - S.lx) / (90 * sc)) ^ 2) * 38 * SIN(t * 17 - ABS(bxp - S.lx) * 0.05) end
                        place(b.f, bxp, surf - b.h * k * 0.5 + 2 * sc, 3 * k, b.h * k, b.rot + extra)
                    end
                end
            end

            if PLATFORMS then
                local fhold = mk("Frame", { Name = "FungiFront", BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 1 }, FRONT)
                staticUpd[#staticUpd + 1] = newPlatform(fhold, true, 30)
                staticUpd[#staticUpd + 1] = newPlatform(fhold, false, 40)
                for i, spec in ipairs(BOTTOM) do plantUpd[#plantUpd + 1] = newPlant(fhold, spec, 10 + i) end

                -- ---- baby mushrooms: one sprouts next to the big one every time it is dropped (max 5) ----
                local BABY_COLS = { RED, PUR, ORG, BLU, PNK }
                local babies, babyN = {}, 0
                local function babyVis(b, v)
                    b.stem.o.Visible, b.stem.f.Visible, b.cap.o.Visible, b.cap.f.Visible = v, v, v, v
                    b.s1.Visible, b.s2.Visible, b.e1.Visible, b.e2.Visible = v, v, v, v
                end
                for i = 1, 5 do
                    local z = 60 + i * 5
                    babies[i] = { stem = blob(fhold, STEM, OUT, z, 0.4), cap = blob(fhold, BABY_COLS[i], OUT, z + 2, 0.5),
                        s1 = dot(fhold, CREAM, z + 4), s2 = dot(fhold, CREAM, z + 4), e1 = dot(fhold, EYE, z + 1), e2 = dot(fhold, EYE, z + 1),
                        ax = 0.5, t0 = -1e9, on = false, ph = i * 1.7 }
                    babyVis(babies[i], false)
                end
                for i = 1, 2 do   -- two babies are already growing when the window opens
                    local b = babies[i]
                    b.ax, b.t0, b.on = (i == 1) and 0.2 or 0.8, os.clock() + 1.2 + i * 0.3, true
                    babyVis(b, true)
                end
                babyN = 2
                addBaby = function(x)
                    local rect = S.rect
                    if not rect then return end
                    babyN = babyN % 5 + 1
                    local b = babies[babyN]
                    local off = ((RND() < 0.5) and -1 or 1) * (115 + RND() * 70) * S.sc
                    if x + off < rect.x + rect.w * 0.06 or x + off > rect.x + rect.w * 0.94 then off = -off end
                    b.ax = MAX(0.05, MIN(0.95, (x + off - rect.x) / MAX(rect.w, 1)))
                    b.cap.f.BackgroundColor3 = BABY_COLS[RND(1, 5)]
                    b.t0, b.on = os.clock(), true
                    S.surp = 0.8   -- the big one is surprised by the new baby
                    babyVis(b, true)
                end
                critUpd[#critUpd + 1] = function(rect, sc, t, g)
                    for _, b in ipairs(babies) do
                        if b.on then
                            local k = sc * MAX(0, MIN(1, g) * ease((t - b.t0) / 0.7)) * 1.5
                            local ol = OL(k)
                            local x, y = rect.x + rect.w * b.ax, rect.y - 3 * sc
                            local w = SIN(t * 2.2 + b.ph) * 3
                            put(b.stem, x, y - 6.5 * k, 9 * k, 13 * k, w, ol)
                            put(b.cap, x + w * 0.2 * k, y - 15 * k, 24 * k, 15 * k, w, ol)
                            place(b.s1, x - 5 * k, y - 17 * k, 4 * k, 3.4 * k)
                            place(b.s2, x + 4.5 * k, y - 14 * k, 3.2 * k, 2.8 * k)
                            place(b.e1, x - 2.3 * k, y - 7 * k, 2 * k, 3 * k)
                            place(b.e2, x + 2.3 * k, y - 7 * k, 2 * k, 3 * k)
                        end
                    end
                end

                -- ---- a snail on the top platform (hides in its shell when the mushroom comes close) ----
                local SKIN = C3(224, 208, 160)
                local sn = { body = blob(fhold, SKIN, OUT, 70, 0.5), head = blob(fhold, SKIN, OUT, 71, 0.5), shell = blob(fhold, C3(232, 140, 70), OUT, 73, 0.5),
                    sp1 = dot(fhold, C3(184, 92, 46), 75), sp2 = dot(fhold, C3(246, 196, 120), 76), st1 = dot(fhold, SKIN, 69), st2 = dot(fhold, SKIN, 69),
                    tp1 = dot(fhold, EYE, 70), tp2 = dot(fhold, EYE, 70), p = 0.3, ret = 0 }
                -- ---- and a ladybug on the bottom one ----
                local bug = { body = blob(fhold, C3(226, 52, 52), OUT, 80, 0.5), head = dot(fhold, EYE, 81), line = dot(fhold, EYE, 83),
                    s1 = dot(fhold, EYE, 84), s2 = dot(fhold, EYE, 84), s3 = dot(fhold, EYE, 84) }
                -- tiny hearts for when the snail gets petted
                local hearts = {}
                for i = 1, 4 do
                    local c1, c2 = dot(fhold, C3(255, 105, 150), 90), dot(fhold, C3(255, 105, 150), 90)
                    local dm = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = HALF, BackgroundColor3 = C3(255, 105, 150), BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 90 }, fhold)
                    round(dm, 0.2)
                    c1.Visible, c2.Visible, dm.Visible = false, false, false
                    hearts[i] = { c1 = c1, c2 = c2, dm = dm, life = 0, max = 1.5, x = 0, y = 0 }
                end
                local function emitHeart(x, y)
                    for _, h in ipairs(hearts) do
                        if h.life <= 0 then h.life, h.x, h.y = h.max, x + (RND() - 0.5) * 16, y return end
                    end
                end
                -- the snail can be petted (tap: it ducks into its shell and the mushroom falls in love) and picked up (drag it anywhere, let go and it drops back onto the grass)
                sn.held, sn.drag, sn.moved, sn.downAt, sn.lift, sn.liftV, sn.hideT, sn.sq, sn.sqV, sn.dir, sn.hAt = false, false, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 1, 0
                local btnSn = mk("TextButton", { AnchorPoint = HALF, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", BorderSizePixel = 0, AutoButtonColor = false, ZIndex = 500 }, fhold)
                btnSn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
                    if isPress(input.UserInputType) then
                        sn.drag, sn.moved, sn.downAt = true, 0, os.clock()
                        S.lastAct = os.clock()
                    end
                end)
                conns[#conns + 1] = UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
                    if not alive() or not sn.drag then return end
                    local ut = input.UserInputType
                    if ut ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement and ut ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
                    local dx, dy = input.Delta.X, input.Delta.Y
                    sn.moved = sn.moved + ABS(dx) + ABS(dy)
                    if not sn.held then
                        if sn.moved < 6 then return end
                        sn.held = true
                        sn.x, sn.y = sn.drawX or 0, sn.drawY or 0
                    end
                    local scr = FRONT.AbsoluteSize
                    sn.x = MAX(0, MIN(scr.X, sn.x + dx))
                    sn.y = MAX(0, MIN(scr.Y, sn.y + dy))
                    S.lastAct = os.clock()
                end)
                conns[#conns + 1] = UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
                    if not alive() or not sn.drag or not isPress(input.UserInputType) then return end
                    sn.drag = false
                    if sn.held then
                        sn.held, sn.dropped = false, true   -- the update loop drops it (it knows where the grass is)
                    elseif sn.moved < 6 and os.clock() - sn.downAt < 0.5 then   -- petted
                        sn.hideT = os.clock() + 1.6
                        sn.sqV = 9
                        S.love = 2.6
                        S.lastAct = os.clock()
                        sfx("boing")
                    end
                end)
                local lastC
                critUpd[#critUpd + 1] = function(rect, sc, t, g)
                    local dt = MIN(t - (lastC or t), 0.05)
                    lastC = t
                    local k = sc * MAX(0, MIN(1, g) * ease((t - D.born - 0.9) / 0.6)) * 2.1   -- critters are drawn big enough to notice
                    local ol = OL(k)
                    -- snail
                    local base = rect.y - 3 * sc
                    if sn.dropped then   -- let go: remember where along the grass it is, then fall back down
                        sn.dropped = false
                        local uu = MAX(0, MIN(1, (sn.x - (rect.x + 20 * sc)) / MAX(rect.w - 40 * sc, 1)))
                        sn.p = (sn.dir >= 0) and uu or (2 - uu)
                        sn.lift, sn.liftV = MAX(0, base - sn.y), 0
                        if sn.lift <= 0 then sn.sqV = 8 end
                    end
                    local held = sn.held
                    local landedAt
                    if not held and sn.lift > 0 then
                        sn.liftV = sn.liftV - 2200 * sc * dt
                        sn.lift = sn.lift + sn.liftV * dt
                        if sn.lift <= 0 then sn.lift, sn.liftV, sn.sqV, landedAt = 0, 0, 10, true end
                    end
                    if not held and sn.lift <= 0 then sn.p = sn.p + dt * 0.016 * (1 - sn.ret) end
                    local u, dir = sn.p % 2, 1
                    if u > 1 then u, dir = 2 - u, -1 end
                    sn.dir = dir
                    local x = held and sn.x or (rect.x + 20 * sc + (rect.w - 40 * sc) * u)
                    local by0 = held and sn.y or (base - sn.lift)
                    if landedAt then
                        dust(x, by0, 5, 0.6, sc)
                        if S.mode == "anchor" and ABS(x - S.x) < 170 * sc then S.surp = 0.8 end   -- the mushroom jumps at the thud
                    end
                    local near = (not held) and ((S.mode == "anchor" and S.ay <= 0 and ABS(x - S.x) < 95 * sc) or os.clock() < sn.hideT)
                    sn.ret = sn.ret + ((near and 1 or 0) - sn.ret) * MIN(1, dt * 4)
                    local rr = 1 - sn.ret
                    sn.sqV = sn.sqV + (-130 * sn.sq - 9 * sn.sqV) * dt
                    sn.sq = sn.sq + sn.sqV * dt
                    local bw = 22 * k * (1 + 0.07 * SIN(t * 3.2)) * (1 - 0.25 * sn.ret) * (1 + 0.25 * sn.sq) * (held and 1.18 or 1)
                    put(sn.body, x, by0 - 3.2 * k, bw, 6.5 * k * (1 - 0.3 * sn.sq), 0, ol)
                    local hx = x + dir * (9 * rr + 2) * k
                    put(sn.head, hx, by0 - 4.5 * k, 7 * k * (0.4 + 0.6 * rr), 7 * k * (0.4 + 0.6 * rr), 0, ol)
                    local vis = rr > 0.12
                    sn.head.o.Visible, sn.head.f.Visible = vis, vis
                    for i, pair in ipairs({ { sn.st1, sn.tp1 }, { sn.st2, sn.tp2 } }) do
                        local sx, h = hx + dir * ((i - 1) * 3.4 - 1.2) * k, 7 * k * rr
                        pair[1].Visible, pair[2].Visible = vis, vis
                        place(pair[1], sx, by0 - 7.5 * k - h * 0.5, 1.6 * k, h, held and 25 * SIN(t * 14 + i * 2) or 0)   -- flails when picked up
                        place(pair[2], sx + (held and 3 * SIN(t * 14 + i * 2) * k or 0), by0 - 7.5 * k - h, 2.8 * k, 2.8 * k)
                    end
                    local scx, scy = x - dir * 2.5 * k, by0 - 9.5 * k
                    put(sn.shell, scx, scy, 13 * k * (1 - 0.1 * sn.sq), 13 * k * (1 - 0.1 * sn.sq), 0, ol)
                    place(sn.sp1, scx + dir * 0.4 * k, scy, 8.5 * k, 8.5 * k)
                    place(sn.sp2, scx + dir * 0.9 * k, scy, 3.6 * k, 3.6 * k)
                    sn.drawX, sn.drawY = x, by0
                    S.snailX, S.snailY = x, by0 - 8 * k
                    place(btnSn, x, by0 - 8 * k, MAX(34, 34 * k), MAX(30, 26 * k))
                    -- hearts while it is being petted
                    if S.love > 0 and not held and t > sn.hAt then sn.hAt = t + 0.35; emitHeart(x, by0 - 16 * k) end
                    for _, h in ipairs(hearts) do
                        if h.life > 0 then
                            h.life = h.life - dt
                            local on = h.life > 0
                            h.c1.Visible, h.c2.Visible, h.dm.Visible = on, on, on
                            if on then
                                local hu = 1 - h.life / h.max
                                local hy, hs = h.y - hu * 46 * sc, 11 * sc * (0.6 + 0.6 * MIN(1, hu * 4))
                                local hxp, ht = h.x + SIN(hu * 7) * 4 * sc, hu ^ 3
                                place(h.c1, hxp - 0.22 * hs, hy - 0.12 * hs, 0.52 * hs, 0.52 * hs)
                                place(h.c2, hxp + 0.22 * hs, hy - 0.12 * hs, 0.52 * hs, 0.52 * hs)
                                place(h.dm, hxp, hy + 0.12 * hs, 0.5 * hs, 0.5 * hs, 45)
                                h.c1.BackgroundTransparency, h.c2.BackgroundTransparency, h.dm.BackgroundTransparency = ht, ht, ht
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    -- ladybug
                    local ph = t * 0.13
                    local bx2 = rect.x + rect.w * (0.5 + 0.42 * SIN(ph))
                    local bd = (COS(ph) >= 0) and 1 or -1
                    local by2 = rect.y + rect.h - 5 * sc - ABS(SIN(t * 7)) * 0.6 * k
                    put(bug.body, bx2, by2 - 3 * k, 10 * k, 7.5 * k, 0, ol)
                    place(bug.head, bx2 + bd * 5.2 * k, by2 - 2.6 * k, 4 * k, 4 * k)
                    place(bug.line, bx2 - bd * 0.4 * k, by2 - 3 * k, 0.9 * k, 7 * k)
                    place(bug.s1, bx2 - bd * 2.6 * k, by2 - 4.4 * k, 1.8 * k, 1.8 * k)
                    place(bug.s2, bx2 + bd * 1.4 * k, by2 - 1.8 * k, 1.8 * k, 1.8 * k)
                    place(bug.s3, bx2 - bd * 2.4 * k, by2 - 1.6 * k, 1.6 * k, 1.6 * k)
                end
            end

            local frameN, lastKey, wasAnim = 0, nil, false
            return function(rect, sc, t, g)
                frameN = frameN + 1
                if ABS(sc - lastSc) > 0.004 then
                    lastSc = sc
                    for _, s in ipairs(strokes) do s[1].Thickness = MAX(1, s[2] * sc) end
                end
                updHero(rect, sc, t, g)
                local growing = (t - D.born) < 2.2 or g < 0.999
                if growing or frameN % 2 == 0 then   -- plants sway at ~30 fps, the hero runs at full rate
                    local hx = (S.mode == "anchor" and S.ay <= 0) and S.x or -1e9   -- top plants step aside for the mushroom
                    for _, u in ipairs(plantUpd) do u(rect, sc, t, g, hx) end
                end
                local key = rect.x + rect.y * 1.7 + rect.w * 3.1 + rect.h * 5.3 + sc * 97
                local anim = S.bend > 0.003   -- grass blades bending after a landing
                if growing or key ~= lastKey or anim or wasAnim then
                    lastKey = key
                    for _, u in ipairs(staticUpd) do u(rect, sc, t, g) end
                end
                wasAnim = anim
                for _, u in ipairs(critUpd) do
                    local okC, errC = pcall(u, rect, sc, t, g)   -- a critter problem never takes the whole decoration down
                    if not okC and not S.critWarned then S.critWarned = true end
                end
            end
        end

        -- ---- EXTRA DECORATIONS ----------------------------------------------------------------
        -- Small cartoon sprites scattered around the window that match the chosen decoration: starfish and bubbles for the
        -- crab, paw prints and hearts for the cat, carrots and daisies for the bunny, embers for the horns, sparkles for the
        -- halo, shells and bubbles for the barnacles. Every sprite is built from the same chunky shapes as the main
        -- decorations (a dark outline twin under all the fills). Some sit around the window and bob, others float up the sides.
        local function sprite(parent, parts, outCol)
            local h = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = HALF, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(30, 30) }, parent)
            local OL = 0.1   -- outline thickness as a fraction of the sprite's size
            for _, p in ipairs(parts) do
                local rad = (p.k == "r") and (p.r or 0.25) or 0.5
                if not p.noOut then
                    local o = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = HALF, Position = UDim2.fromScale(p.x, p.y), Size = UDim2.fromScale(p.w + OL, p.h + OL),
                        Rotation = p.rot or 0, BackgroundColor3 = outCol, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 1 }, h)
                    round(o, rad)
                end
                local f = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = HALF, Position = UDim2.fromScale(p.x, p.y), Size = UDim2.fromScale(p.w, p.h),
                    Rotation = p.rot or 0, BackgroundColor3 = p.col, BackgroundTransparency = p.tr or 0, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 2 + (p.z or 0) }, h)
                round(f, rad)
            end
            return h
        end

        local function gloss(x, y, w, h, rot) return { k = "c", x = x, y = y, w = w, h = h, rot = rot or -35, col = WHITE, tr = 0.3, z = 1, noOut = true } end

        local function flipParts(parts)   -- mirrors a sprite left <-> right (so fish can swim either way without turning upside down)
            local out = {}
            for i, p in ipairs(parts) do
                local q = {}
                for k, v in pairs(p) do q[k] = v end
                q.x = 1 - p.x
                q.rot = -(p.rot or 0)
                out[i] = q
            end
            return out
        end

        local function kindParts(kind)
            if kind == "bubble" then
                return { { k = "c", x = 0.5, y = 0.5, w = 0.84, h = 0.84, col = C3(170, 232, 248) }, gloss(0.3, 0.3, 0.26, 0.15, -40),
                         { k = "c", x = 0.67, y = 0.7, w = 0.1, h = 0.1, col = WHITE, tr = 0.4, z = 1, noOut = true } }, C3(10, 45, 90)
            elseif kind == "heart" then
                local H = C3(255, 105, 150)
                return { { k = "c", x = 0.3, y = 0.34, w = 0.5, h = 0.5, col = H }, { k = "c", x = 0.7, y = 0.34, w = 0.5, h = 0.5, col = H },
                         { k = "r", x = 0.5, y = 0.56, w = 0.5, h = 0.5, rot = 45, r = 0.2, col = H }, gloss(0.28, 0.27, 0.2, 0.12) }, C3(150, 30, 70)
            elseif kind == "paw" then
                local P = C3(255, 150, 190)
                return { { k = "c", x = 0.5, y = 0.68, w = 0.54, h = 0.44, col = P },
                         { k = "c", x = 0.17, y = 0.42, w = 0.22, h = 0.28, rot = -20, col = P }, { k = "c", x = 0.39, y = 0.22, w = 0.22, h = 0.28, rot = -6, col = P },
                         { k = "c", x = 0.61, y = 0.22, w = 0.22, h = 0.28, rot = 6, col = P },   { k = "c", x = 0.83, y = 0.42, w = 0.22, h = 0.28, rot = 20, col = P },
                         gloss(0.4, 0.6, 0.2, 0.1) }, C3(150, 50, 95)
            elseif kind == "carrot" then
                local O, G = C3(255, 150, 50), C3(90, 200, 90)
                local r = { { k = "c", x = 0.5, y = 0.64, w = 0.36, h = 0.7, col = O },
                            { k = "c", x = 0.5, y = 0.2, w = 0.14, h = 0.3, col = G }, { k = "c", x = 0.36, y = 0.24, w = 0.13, h = 0.28, rot = -28, col = G },
                            { k = "c", x = 0.64, y = 0.24, w = 0.13, h = 0.28, rot = 28, col = G }, gloss(0.4, 0.5, 0.08, 0.22, 10) }
                for _, y in ipairs({ 0.5, 0.64, 0.78 }) do r[#r + 1] = { k = "r", x = 0.5 + ((y == 0.64) and -0.05 or 0.05), y = y, w = 0.15, h = 0.035, r = 0.5, col = C3(200, 95, 20), z = 1, noOut = true } end
                return r, C3(130, 60, 10)
            elseif kind == "daisy" then
                local r = {}
                for i = 0, 5 do
                    local a = math.rad(i * 60)
                    r[#r + 1] = { k = "c", x = 0.5 + math.sin(a) * 0.28, y = 0.5 - math.cos(a) * 0.28, w = 0.34, h = 0.34, col = C3(255, 244, 250) }
                end
                r[#r + 1] = { k = "c", x = 0.5, y = 0.5, w = 0.34, h = 0.34, col = C3(255, 205, 70), z = 1 }
                return r, C3(150, 110, 140)
            elseif kind == "ember" then
                return { { k = "r", x = 0.5, y = 0.5, w = 0.56, h = 0.56, rot = 45, r = 0.2, col = C3(255, 110, 40) },
                         { k = "r", x = 0.5, y = 0.5, w = 0.28, h = 0.28, rot = 45, r = 0.2, col = C3(255, 220, 110), z = 1, noOut = true } }, C3(100, 20, 10)
            elseif kind == "spore" then   -- a soft glowing spore
                return { { k = "c", x = 0.5, y = 0.5, w = 0.84, h = 0.84, col = C3(226, 255, 176) }, { k = "c", x = 0.5, y = 0.5, w = 0.4, h = 0.4, col = C3(255, 255, 232), z = 1, noOut = true },
                         gloss(0.32, 0.3, 0.2, 0.12, -40) }, C3(60, 120, 70)
            elseif kind == "firefly" then
                return { { k = "c", x = 0.5, y = 0.5, w = 1, h = 1, col = C3(255, 240, 150), tr = 0.82, z = -1, noOut = true }, { k = "c", x = 0.5, y = 0.5, w = 0.6, h = 0.6, col = C3(255, 236, 130) }, { k = "c", x = 0.5, y = 0.5, w = 0.28, h = 0.28, col = WHITE, z = 1, noOut = true } }, C3(150, 120, 30)
            elseif kind == "sparkle" then
                local Gd = C3(255, 225, 120)
                return { { k = "c", x = 0.5, y = 0.5, w = 0.22, h = 1, col = Gd }, { k = "c", x = 0.5, y = 0.5, w = 1, h = 0.22, col = Gd },
                         { k = "c", x = 0.5, y = 0.5, w = 0.4, h = 0.4, col = C3(255, 250, 215), z = 1 } }, C3(140, 90, 10)
            elseif kind == "sucker" then   -- a sucker like the ones on the tentacles
                return { { k = "c", x = 0.5, y = 0.5, w = 0.86, h = 0.86, col = C3(222, 190, 255) }, { k = "c", x = 0.5, y = 0.5, w = 0.44, h = 0.44, col = C3(110, 58, 170), z = 1, noOut = true },
                         gloss(0.3, 0.28, 0.2, 0.12, -40) }, C3(50, 20, 95)
            elseif kind == "ink" then   -- ink drop
                local I = C3(150, 84, 225)
                return { { k = "c", x = 0.5, y = 0.64, w = 0.62, h = 0.62, col = I }, { k = "r", x = 0.5, y = 0.36, w = 0.38, h = 0.38, rot = 45, r = 0.12, col = I },
                         gloss(0.38, 0.6, 0.18, 0.1, -50) }, C3(50, 20, 95)
            elseif kind == "fish" or kind == "fish2" then   -- tiny fish (faces right; mirrored for the left-swimmers)
                local F = (kind == "fish") and C3(120, 232, 226) or C3(255, 150, 212)
                local Fd = (kind == "fish") and C3(70, 170, 190) or C3(215, 100, 170)
                return { { k = "r", x = 0.15, y = 0.5, w = 0.28, h = 0.28, rot = 45, r = 0.14, col = Fd },   -- tail
                         { k = "c", x = 0.52, y = 0.24, w = 0.22, h = 0.16, rot = -20, col = Fd },            -- top fin
                         { k = "c", x = 0.56, y = 0.5, w = 0.66, h = 0.44, col = F },                         -- body
                         { k = "c", x = 0.74, y = 0.45, w = 0.15, h = 0.15, col = WHITE, z = 1, noOut = true },   -- eye
                         { k = "c", x = 0.76, y = 0.46, w = 0.07, h = 0.07, col = C3(30, 14, 60), z = 2, noOut = true },
                         gloss(0.48, 0.4, 0.2, 0.08, -10) }, C3(50, 20, 95)
            elseif kind == "plankton" then   -- a little glowing speck cluster
                local Pk = C3(190, 255, 228)
                return { { k = "c", x = 0.4, y = 0.56, w = 0.46, h = 0.46, col = Pk },
                         { k = "c", x = 0.72, y = 0.34, w = 0.28, h = 0.28, col = C3(220, 200, 255) } }, C3(40, 60, 120)
            elseif kind == "ghostlet" then   -- a mini ghost
                local Gb = C3(244, 246, 255)
                return { { k = "c", x = 0.27, y = 0.84, w = 0.24, h = 0.24, col = Gb }, { k = "c", x = 0.5, y = 0.9, w = 0.24, h = 0.24, col = Gb },
                         { k = "c", x = 0.73, y = 0.84, w = 0.24, h = 0.24, col = Gb },
                         { k = "r", x = 0.5, y = 0.46, w = 0.68, h = 0.82, r = 0.5, col = Gb },
                         { k = "c", x = 0.38, y = 0.4, w = 0.1, h = 0.15, col = C3(40, 32, 80), z = 1, noOut = true },
                         { k = "c", x = 0.62, y = 0.4, w = 0.1, h = 0.15, col = C3(40, 32, 80), z = 1, noOut = true },
                         { k = "c", x = 0.5, y = 0.58, w = 0.09, h = 0.11, col = C3(40, 32, 80), z = 1, noOut = true },
                         { k = "c", x = 0.27, y = 0.55, w = 0.1, h = 0.06, col = C3(255, 160, 195), tr = 0.2, z = 1, noOut = true },
                         { k = "c", x = 0.73, y = 0.55, w = 0.1, h = 0.06, col = C3(255, 160, 195), tr = 0.2, z = 1, noOut = true },
                         gloss(0.32, 0.22, 0.2, 0.1, -30) }, C3(62, 54, 112)
            elseif kind == "wisp" then   -- a spooky blue flame
                local Wb = C3(150, 200, 255)
                return { { k = "c", x = 0.5, y = 0.66, w = 0.64, h = 0.64, col = Wb }, { k = "r", x = 0.5, y = 0.34, w = 0.36, h = 0.36, rot = 45, r = 0.12, col = Wb },
                         { k = "c", x = 0.5, y = 0.68, w = 0.3, h = 0.3, col = C3(228, 246, 255), z = 1, noOut = true },
                         gloss(0.36, 0.56, 0.16, 0.09, -40) }, C3(40, 50, 120)
            elseif kind == "star" then   -- starfish
                local R = C3(240, 84, 66)
                local r = {}
                for i = 0, 4 do
                    local a = math.rad(i * 72)
                    r[#r + 1] = { k = "c", x = 0.5 + math.sin(a) * 0.2, y = 0.5 - math.cos(a) * 0.2, w = 0.24, h = 0.58, rot = i * 72, col = R }
                end
                r[#r + 1] = { k = "c", x = 0.5, y = 0.5, w = 0.4, h = 0.4, col = R }
                for i = 0, 4 do
                    local a = math.rad(i * 72)
                    r[#r + 1] = { k = "c", x = 0.5 + math.sin(a) * 0.27, y = 0.5 - math.cos(a) * 0.27, w = 0.07, h = 0.07, col = C3(120, 20, 24), tr = 0.4, z = 1, noOut = true }
                end
                r[#r + 1] = gloss(0.42, 0.28, 0.1, 0.2, 5)
                return r, C3(84, 10, 16)
            else   -- shell
                local r = { { k = "c", x = 0.5, y = 0.54, w = 0.86, h = 0.68, col = C3(246, 228, 204) } }
                for _, a in ipairs({ -42, -21, 0, 21, 42 }) do
                    local ar = math.rad(a)
                    r[#r + 1] = { k = "r", x = 0.5 + math.sin(ar) * 0.19, y = 0.78 - math.cos(ar) * 0.19, w = 0.05, h = 0.3, rot = a, r = 0.5, col = C3(130, 100, 80), tr = 0.35, z = 1, noOut = true }
                end
                r[#r + 1] = gloss(0.34, 0.36, 0.22, 0.12)
                return r, C3(110, 85, 60)
            end
        end

        local EXTRA = {
            ["Tentacles"]   = { static = { "sucker", "ink" },   rise = "bubble", fish = { "fish", "fish2" }, plankton = "plankton" },
            ["Crab"]        = { static = { "star" },            rise = "bubble"  },
            ["Cat"]    = { static = { "paw" },             rise = "heart"   },
            ["Bunny"]  = { static = { "carrot", "daisy" }, rise = "daisy"   },
            ["Devil Horns"] = { static = { "ember" },           rise = "ember"   },
            ["Halo"]        = { static = { "sparkle" },         rise = "sparkle" },
            ["Barnacles"]   = { static = { "shell" },           rise = "bubble"  },
            ["Ghost"]       = { static = { "ghostlet", "wisp" }, rise = "wisp"   },
            ["Fungi"]       = { rise = "spore", plankton = "firefly" },
        }
        local SPOTS = { { "L", 0.30 }, { "L", 0.62 }, { "L", 0.88 }, { "R", 0.30 }, { "R", 0.62 }, { "R", 0.88 }, { "B", 0.25 }, { "B", 0.50 }, { "B", 0.75 } }

        local function buildExtras(parent, style)
            local spec = EXTRA[style]
            if not spec then return nil end
            local ex = mk("Frame", { Name = "Extras", BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 0 }, parent)   -- behind the main decoration
            local items, seed = {}, 0
            local function rnd() seed = seed + 1; return 0.5 + 0.5 * math.sin(seed * 12.9898) end
            for i, a in ipairs(spec.static and SPOTS or {}) do
                local kind = spec.static[(i - 1) % #spec.static + 1]
                local parts, oc = kindParts(kind)
                items[#items + 1] = { mode = "static", h = sprite(ex, parts, oc), side = a[1], f = a[2], kind = kind,
                                      ph = rnd() * 6.28, n = rnd(), tilt = ((i % 2 == 0) and 1 or -1) * (8 + 14 * rnd()) }
            end
            if spec.rise then
                for i = 1, 6 do
                    local parts, oc = kindParts(spec.rise)
                    items[#items + 1] = { mode = "rise", h = sprite(ex, parts, oc), dir = (i % 2 == 0) and 1 or -1, kind = spec.rise,
                                          ph = (i - 1) / 6 + rnd() * 0.08, n = rnd(), spd = 0.07 + 0.04 * rnd() }
                end
            end
            if spec.fish then   -- tiny fish cruising along the top and bottom edges of the window
                for i = 1, 5 do
                    local kind = spec.fish[(i - 1) % #spec.fish + 1]
                    local dir = (i % 2 == 0) and 1 or -1
                    local parts, oc = kindParts(kind)
                    if dir < 0 then parts = flipParts(parts) end
                    items[#items + 1] = { mode = "fish", h = sprite(ex, parts, oc), dir = dir, kind = kind, band = (i % 3 == 0) and "T" or "B",
                                          ph = (i - 1) / 5 + rnd() * 0.1, n = rnd(), spd = 0.035 + 0.03 * rnd(), lane = 14 + 22 * rnd() }
                end
            end
            if spec.plankton then   -- plankton drifting slowly around the window's outline
                for i = 1, 8 do
                    local parts, oc = kindParts(spec.plankton)
                    items[#items + 1] = { mode = "plank", h = sprite(ex, parts, oc), kind = spec.plankton,
                                          ph = (i - 1) / 8 + rnd() * 0.05, n = rnd(), spd = 0.012 + 0.012 * rnd(), off = 10 + 26 * rnd(), tw = rnd() * 6.28 }
                end
            end
            local lastT = -1
            return function(rect, sc, t, g)
                if t - lastT < 0.033 and g >= 1 then return end   -- the little sprites are slow: ~30 fps is plenty (full speed while growing out)
                lastT = t
                for _, it in ipairs(items) do
                    local h, x, y, S, rot = it.h
                    if it.mode == "fish" then
                        local u = (t * it.spd + it.ph) % 1   -- 0..1 across the window (and a bit beyond both ends)
                        local span = rect.w + 80 * sc
                        local ux = (it.dir > 0) and u or (1 - u)
                        x = rect.x - 40 * sc + span * ux
                        local lane = it.lane * sc
                        y = ((it.band == "T") and (rect.y - lane) or (rect.y + rect.h + lane)) + math.sin(t * 2.1 + it.ph * 20) * 4 * sc
                        S = (17 + 8 * it.n) * sc * g * math.sin(math.pi * u) ^ 0.4   -- fades in / out at the ends
                        rot = math.sin(t * 4 + it.ph * 20) * 7   -- tail-wag wiggle
                    elseif it.mode == "plank" then
                        local u = (t * it.spd + it.ph) % 1
                        local L = 2 * (rect.w + rect.h)
                        local d, o = u * L, it.off * sc
                        if d < rect.w then x, y = rect.x + d, rect.y - o
                        elseif d < rect.w + rect.h then x, y = rect.x + rect.w + o, rect.y + (d - rect.w)
                        elseif d < 2 * rect.w + rect.h then x, y = rect.x + rect.w - (d - rect.w - rect.h), rect.y + rect.h + o
                        else x, y = rect.x - o, rect.y + rect.h - (d - 2 * rect.w - rect.h) end
                        x = x + math.sin(t * 0.9 + it.tw) * 5 * sc
                        y = y + math.cos(t * 0.7 + it.tw) * 5 * sc
                        S = (7 + 6 * it.n) * sc * g * (0.75 + 0.3 * math.sin(t * 2.4 + it.tw))   -- twinkle
                        rot = t * 12 + it.tw * 20
                    elseif it.mode == "static" then
                        local m = 26 * sc
                        if it.side == "L" then x, y = rect.x - m, rect.y + rect.h * it.f
                        elseif it.side == "R" then x, y = rect.x + rect.w + m, rect.y + rect.h * it.f
                        else x, y = rect.x + rect.w * it.f, rect.y + rect.h + m end
                        x = x + math.cos(t * 0.9 + it.ph) * 2 * sc
                        y = y + math.sin(t * 1.2 + it.ph) * 3 * sc
                        S = (32 + 10 * it.n) * sc * g
                        rot = it.tilt + math.sin(t * 0.8 + it.ph) * 8
                        if it.kind == "sparkle" then S = S * (0.8 + 0.25 * math.sin(t * 3 + it.ph * 2)); rot = rot + t * 20 end
                    else
                        local u = (t * it.spd + it.ph) % 1
                        local off = (14 + 14 * it.n) * sc
                        x = ((it.dir > 0) and (rect.x + rect.w + off) or (rect.x - off)) + math.sin(t * 1.3 + it.ph * 9) * 7 * sc
                        y = rect.y + rect.h * (1.02 - 1.04 * u)
                        S = (14 + 12 * it.n) * sc * g * math.sin(math.pi * u) ^ 0.6   -- pops in at the bottom, shrinks away at the top
                        rot = math.sin(t * 1.5 + it.ph * 7) * 15 + ((it.kind == "sparkle") and t * 40 or 0)
                    end
                    S = math.max(S, 0.5)
                    h.Position, h.Size, h.Rotation = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), UDim2.fromOffset(S, S), rot
                end
            end
        end

        local BUILD = {
            ["Tentacles"]   = buildTentacles,
            ["Cat"]    = buildCatEars,
            ["Bunny"]  = buildBunnyEars,
            ["Devil Horns"] = buildHorns,
            ["Halo"]        = buildHalo,
            ["Barnacles"]   = buildBarnacles,
            ["Crab"]        = buildCrab,
            ["Ghost"]       = buildGhost,
            ["Fungi"]       = buildFungi,
        }

        -- ---- layer + frame loop -------------------------------------------------------------
        local layer = mk("Frame", {
            Name = "UIDecor", BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 0, Visible = false,
        }, screenGui)   -- ZIndex 0: drawn behind the window (the window is ZIndex 1)
        -- faint overlay ABOVE the window (ZIndex 2; popups, dialogs and tooltips are all 35+), used by the flying ghost.
        -- It never takes input.
        local front = mk("Frame", {
            Name = "UIDecorFront", BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 2, Visible = false, Active = false,
        }, screenGui)
        D.front = front

        local function rebuild(sel)
            layer:ClearAllChildren()
            front:ClearAllChildren()
            table.clear(D.active)
            D.whiskerMade = false
            local want = {}
            for _, n in ipairs(sel) do want[n] = true end
            for order, name in ipairs(D.STYLES) do
                if want[name] then
                    local pal = palette(name)
                    local root = mk("Frame", { Name = name, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = order }, layer)
                    local ok, upd = pcall(BUILD[name], root, pal)
                    if ok and upd then
                        local okE, upE = pcall(buildExtras, layer, name)   -- the matching sprites around the window
                        if okE and upE then D.active[#D.active + 1] = upE end
                        D.active[#D.active + 1] = upd
                    else
                        root:Destroy()
                    end
                end
            end
            D.born = os.clock()
        end

        local function poll()
            local sel = {}
            local v = Values.DecorStyles
            if type(v) == "table" then v = v[1] end   -- old configs saved a list
            if Cfg.DecorLocked then v = nil end        -- Premium feature while locked: nothing is drawn
            if v == "Cat Ears" or v == "Bunny Ears" then   -- renamed to just Cat / Bunny
                v = (v == "Cat Ears") and "Cat" or "Bunny"
                Values.DecorStyles = v
            end
            if type(v) == "string" and BUILD[v] then sel[1] = v end   -- only one decoration at a time
            table.sort(sel)
            local sig = table.concat(sel, ",")
            if sig ~= D.sig then
                D.sig = sig
                rebuild(sel)
            end
        end

        local conn
        conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
            if not screenGui.Parent then conn:Disconnect() return end
            local now = os.clock()
            if now - D.lastPoll > 0.2 then
                D.lastPoll = now
                poll()
            end

            -- Smooth show / hide: `D.p` (0..1) glides toward 1 while the window is up and toward 0 when it is hidden,
            -- minimized or still fading in. Growing out uses a soft ease-out-back (a little overshoot), shrinking away uses
            -- the mirrored ease-in-back (a tiny wind-up, then it tucks behind the window) instead of vanishing instantly.
            local want = #D.active > 0 and Main.Visible and Main.BackgroundTransparency < 0.75 and not Cfg.UIMinimized
            local dt = math.clamp(now - (D.lastFrame or now), 0, 0.1)
            D.lastFrame = now
            D.p = D.p or 0
            if want then
                if D.p <= 0 then D.born = now end   -- (re)start the staggered sprite grow-in
                D.p = math.min(1, D.p + dt / 0.55)
            else
                D.p = math.max(0, D.p - dt / 0.3)
            end
            if D.p <= 0 and not want then
                if layer.Visible then layer.Visible = false end
                if front.Visible then front.Visible = false end
                D.shown = false
                return
            end
            D.shown = true
            layer.Visible = true
            front.Visible = true

            local rect = D.rect
            if Main.Visible then   -- remember where the window was, so the shrink-away still lands on it after it hides
                local ap, as = Main.AbsolutePosition - layer.AbsolutePosition, Main.AbsoluteSize
                D.keepRect = { x = ap.X, y = ap.Y, w = as.X, h = as.Y }
            end
            local kr = D.keepRect
            if kr then rect.x, rect.y, rect.w, rect.h = kr.x, kr.y, kr.w, kr.h end
            local sc = math.max(MainScale.Scale, 0.05) * math.clamp(tonumber(Values.DecorSize) or 1, 0.4, 2)
            local t = now   -- decorations are always animated
            local g
            if want then
                local x = D.p
                g = 1 + 2.2 * (x - 1) ^ 3 + 1.2 * (x - 1) ^ 2   -- ease-out-back, gentler overshoot than before
            else
                local x = 1 - D.p                                  -- 0 -> 1 while leaving
                g = 1 - (2.2 * x ^ 3 - 1.2 * x ^ 2)                -- ease-in-back, mirrored
                g = math.max(0, g)
            end

            for i = #D.active, 1, -1 do
                local ok, err = pcall(D.active[i], rect, sc, t, g)
                if not ok then
                    table.remove(D.active, i)
                end
            end
        end)
        end)
    end

    -- builds the Settings tab (always last), then search / minimize / resize / confirm / hotkeys / intro
    local function finish(opts)
        createTab("Settings", "sliders-horizontal", "Menu scale, theme, sidebar and config")
        TAB_ORDER[#TAB_ORDER + 1] = "Settings"
        refreshTabsCanvas()

        -- hub defaults: Northern Lights theme, icon-only tabs, solid (non-transparent) window, two columns
        pcall(applyTheme, "NorthernLights")
        pcall(applySidebar, true, false)
        pcall(applySolid, true, false)
        pcall(switchTab, TAB_ORDER[1])

        do
            local page = pages["Settings"]
            local s1 = section(page, "UI SCALE", "monitor", false, "Resize the whole menu. You can also drag the curved grip in the bottom-right corner.")
            slider(s1, "Menu Scale", 0.5, 2.0, "Scale_Menu", function(v)
                if applyMenuScale then applyMenuScale(v, true, true) end
            end, "maximize", true)
            toggle(s1, "Auto Text Size", true, function(v)
                if Cfg.setAutoText then Cfg.setAutoText(v) end
            end, "AutoTextSize", "a-large-small", "Text gets larger on big screens so it stays easy to read.")

            local sT = section(page, "THEME", "palette", false, "Pick a color theme. Borders, cards and text all follow it.")
            do
                local grid = Instance.new("Frame")
                grid.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
                grid.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
                grid.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                grid.Parent = sT
                local gl = Instance.new("UIGridLayout")
                gl.CellSize = UDim2.new(0.333, -6, 0, 40)
                gl.CellPadding = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 8)
                gl.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
                gl.Parent = grid

                local chips = {}
                local chipLabels = {}
                local function relayoutChips()
                    local w = grid.AbsoluteSize.X / math.max(MainScale.Scale, 0.01)
                    if w <= 0 then return end
                    local n = (w >= 300) and 3 or 2
                    gl.CellSize = UDim2.new(1 / n, -math.ceil(8 * (n - 1) / n), 0, 40)
                    local narrow = n == 2 and w < 230
                    for _, c in ipairs(chipLabels) do
                        c.sw2.Visible = not narrow
                        c.l.Position = UDim2.new(0, narrow and 28 or 40, 0, 0)
                        c.l.Size = UDim2.new(1, -((narrow and 28 or 40) + 4), 1, 0)
                        c.l.TextSize = narrow and 10 or 11
                    end
                end
                grid:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(relayoutChips)
                local function refreshChips()
                    for n, c in pairs(chips) do
                        local sel = (n == CurrentTheme)
                        TweenService:Create(c.stroke, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
                            Color = sel and P.Accent or P.Line, Transparency = sel and 0 or 0.6,
                            Thickness = sel and 2 or 1
                        }):Play()
                    end
                end
                for i, t in ipairs(Themes) do
                    local chip = Instance.new("TextButton")
                    chip.LayoutOrder = i
                    chip.BackgroundColor3 = t.Grad and Color3.new(1, 1, 1) or t.Bg2
                    chip.Text = ""
                    chip.AutoButtonColor = false
                    chip.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    chip.ZIndex = 3
                    chip:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true)
                    chip.Parent = grid
                    corner(chip, 9)
                    if t.Grad then
                        local cg = Instance.new("UIGradient")
                        cg.Name = "ChipGrad"
                        cg.Color = ColorSequence.new(t.Grad[1], t.Grad[2])
                        cg.Rotation = t.GradRot or 135
                        cg:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true)
                        cg.Parent = chip
                    end
                    local cs = stroke(chip, P.Line, 1, 0.6)
                    local sw1 = Instance.new("Frame")
                    sw1.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14); sw1.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0.5, -7)
                    sw1.BackgroundColor3 = t.Accent; sw1.BorderSizePixel = 0; sw1.ZIndex = 4
                    sw1:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true); sw1.Parent = chip
                    corner(sw1, 7)
                    local sw2 = sw1:Clone(); sw2.Position = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0.5, -7)
                    sw2.BackgroundColor3 = t.Accent2; sw2.ZIndex = 3; sw2.Parent = chip
                    local l = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                    l.Size = UDim2.new(1, -44, 1, 0); l.Position = UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 0)
                    l.BackgroundTransparency = 1; l.Text = t.Name; l.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                    l.TextSize = 11; l.TextColor3 = t.Text; l.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                    l.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
                    l.ZIndex = 4; l:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true); l.Parent = chip
                    table.insert(chipLabels, { l = l, sw2 = sw2 })
                    chips[t.Name] = { stroke = cs }
                    chip.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                        applyTheme(t.Name)
                        refreshChips()
                    end)
                end
                Cfg.refreshChips = refreshChips
                task.defer(refreshChips)
                task.defer(relayoutChips)
            end

            local sSB = section(page, "CUSTOMIZER", "panel-left", false, "Change how the tab list, the sections and the elements inside them look.")
            toggle(sSB, "Show Tab Title", true, function(v)
                State.ShowTabTitle = v
                applyTabTitle(v, true)
            end, "ShowTabTitle", "heading")
            pcall(applyTabTitle, true, false)   -- on by default
            toggle(sSB, "Icons Only Tabs", true, function(v)
                State.TabsIconOnly = v
                applySidebar(v, true)
            end, "TabsIconOnly", "layout-grid")
            toggle(sSB, "Two Column Sections", true, function(v)
                State.TwoColumns = v
                animateColumns(v)
            end, "TwoColumns", "columns-2")
            toggle(sSB, "Collapsible Sections", true, function(v)
                State.CollapsibleSections = v
                for _, c in ipairs(SectionCtl) do
                    if not v then c.Collapsed = false end
                    c.Apply(true)
                end
            end, "CollapsibleSections", "chevrons-down-up")
            addTag(toggle(sSB, "Hide Section Borders", false, function(v)
                State.NoSectionBorders = v
                for _, c in ipairs(SectionCtl) do
                    local gb = c.Wrap:FindFirstChild("GlassBorder")
                    if gb then
                        TweenService:Create(gb, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), { Transparency = v and 1 or 0 }):Play()
                    end
                end
            end, "NoSectionBorders", "square-dashed"), "NEW", "new")
            toggle(sSB, "Compact Elements", false, function(v)
                State.CompactElements = v
                applyCompact(v, true)
            end, "CompactElements", "minimize-2")
            addTag(toggle(sSB, "Bold Section Titles", false, function(v)
                State.BoldSectionTitles = v
                applyBoldSections(v, true)
            end, "BoldSectionTitles", "bold"), "NEW", "new")
            pcall(applyBoldSections, false, false)   -- off by default

            local sDecor   -- the Decorations section (locked as Premium further down, together with Localization)
            if Cfg.Decor then
                local sDec = section(page, "UI DECORATIONS", "cat", false, "Adds decorations around the ui")
                sDecor = sDec
