                addTag(dropdown(sDec, "Decoration", Cfg.Decor.OPTIONS, "DecorStyles", function(v) Cfg.Decor.themeFor(v) end, "sparkles", false), "NEW", "new")
                slider(sDec, "Decoration Size", 0.5, 1.6, "DecorSize", nil, "scaling", false, 0.05)
            end

            -- ============================================================
            -- LOCALIZATION  (Settings > Localization)
            -- Uses the Modules.luau language system from https://github.com/72msreal-pixel/WindUI
            -- It tracks every text in the hub, translates it with your own tables or Google Translate (30 languages),
            -- caches the result in <workspace>/Nauru Hub/language/LanguageCache.json and remembers the chosen language.
            -- The module is built for WindUI, so a tiny stand-in object provides the 3 functions it hooks
            -- (CreateWindow / Localization / SetLanguage) and the hub's ScreenGui is attached as the root to translate.
            -- ============================================================
            do
                local LOC_URL    = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/72msreal-pixel/WindUI/refs/heads/main/Modules.luau"
                local LOC_FOLDER = "Nauru Hub"
                local LOC_CACHE  = LOC_FOLDER .. "/language/LanguageCache.json"
                local LOC_LANGS = {
                    { "en", "English" }, { "vi", "Tiếng Việt" }, { "zh-cn", "简体中文" }, { "zh-tw", "繁體中文" },
                    { "ja", "日本語" }, { "ko", "한국어" }, { "th", "ไทย" }, { "id", "Bahasa Indonesia" },
                    { "ms", "Bahasa Melayu" }, { "fil", "Filipino" }, { "fr", "Français" }, { "de", "Deutsch" },
                    { "es", "Español" }, { "pt", "Português" }, { "it", "Italiano" }, { "nl", "Nederlands" },
                    { "pl", "Polski" }, { "ru", "Русский" }, { "uk", "Українська" }, { "tr", "Türkçe" },
                    { "ar", "العربية" }, { "hi", "हिन्दी" }, { "bn", "বাংলা" }, { "ur", "اردو" },
                    { "fa", "فارسی" }, { "he", "עברית" }, { "el", "Ελληνικά" }, { "ro", "Română" },
                    { "sv", "Svenska" }, { "da", "Dansk" },
                }
                local LOC_NAMES, CODE_OF, NAME_OF = {}, {}, {}
                for _, l in ipairs(LOC_LANGS) do
                    LOC_NAMES[#LOC_NAMES + 1] = l[2]
                    CODE_OF[l[2]], NAME_OF[l[1]] = l[1], l[2]
                end
                if type(Values.Language) ~= "string" or not CODE_OF[Values.Language] then Values.Language = "English" end

                -- ---------- PREMIUM lock ----------
                -- Localization is a premium feature: the section is locked and clicking it opens a popup with the
                -- Discord server profile, the perks and the price. Set IS_PREMIUM = true to unlock it.
                local IS_PREMIUM   = false
                -- Premium = your Roblox username (or user id) is listed in the whitelist file on GitHub
                local WHITELIST_URL = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cvjhvc/premoum/refs/heads/main/whitelist"
                pcall(function()
                    local body = lucideFetch(WHITELIST_URL)
                    if type(body) ~= "string" or body == "" then return end
                    local me, myId = LocalPlayer.Name:lower(), tostring(LocalPlayer.UserId)
                    for line in body:gmatch("[^\r\n]+") do
                        line = (line:gsub("%-%-.*", ""))   -- allow  -- comments  and  # comments
                        line = (line:gsub("#.*", ""))
                        for token in line:gmatch("[%w_]+") do
                            if token:lower() == me or token == myId then IS_PREMIUM = true return end
                        end
                    end
                end)
                local PREM_INVITE  = "DXHHswuYPb"
                local PREM_URL     = "https://discord.gg/" .. PREM_INVITE
                local PREM_PRICE   = "450 Robux"
                local PREM_PERKS   = {   -- icon, title, one-line description, optional tag
                    { "layers",           "More Features",     "Extra tools that free users do not get" },
                    { "zap",              "Faster Updates",    "Fixes and new versions reach you first",   "POPULAR" },
                    { "rocket",           "Early Access",      "Try new features before everyone else" },
                    { "layout-dashboard", "More UI Features",  "Extra interface options and settings" },
                    { "sparkles",         "Decorations",       "Animated decorations around the window",   "NEW" },
                    { "languages",        "Localization",      "Use the whole UI in any language",         "NEW" },
                    { "badge-check",      "Custom Role",       "A special Premium role in our server" },
                }
                local GOLD         = Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 70)
                local GOLD_DK      = Color3.fromRGB(120, 78, 10)
                local premium      = { profile = nil, fetching = false }

                local function fetchAsset(url, file)   -- download a Discord CDN image so the UI can show it
                    local out
                    pcall(function()
                        local body = lucideFetch(url)
                        if body and writefile and getcustomasset then
                            if makefolder and isfolder and not isfolder("Nauru Hub") then makefolder("Nauru Hub") end
                            writefile("Nauru Hub/" .. file, body)
                            out = getcustomasset("Nauru Hub/" .. file)
                        end
                    end)
                    return out
                end
                local function commas(n)
                    n = tonumber(n)
                    if not n then return "?" end
                    local str = tostring(math.floor(n))
                    local rev = str:reverse():gsub("(%d%d%d)", "%1,"):reverse()
                    return (rev:gsub("^,", ""))
                end
                local function loadProfile(done)   -- live data from the Discord invite API, fetched once
                    if premium.profile ~= nil then done(premium.profile) return end
                    if premium.fetching then return end
                    premium.fetching = true
                    task.spawn(function()
                        local prof = false
                        pcall(function()
                            local body = lucideFetch("https://discord.com/api/v10/invites/" .. PREM_INVITE .. "?with_counts=true")
                            local data = body and HttpService:JSONDecode(body)
                            local g = data and data.guild
                            if g then
                                prof = { name = g.name, members = data.approximate_member_count, online = data.approximate_presence_count }
                                if g.icon then
                                    prof.icon = fetchAsset("https://cdn.discordapp.com/icons/" .. g.id .. "/" .. g.icon .. ".png?size=256", "premium_icon.png")
                                end
                                if g.banner then
                                    prof.banner = fetchAsset("https://cdn.discordapp.com/banners/" .. g.id .. "/" .. g.banner .. ".png?size=1024", "premium_banner.png")
                                elseif g.splash then
                                    prof.banner = fetchAsset("https://cdn.discordapp.com/splashes/" .. g.id .. "/" .. g.splash .. ".png?size=1024", "premium_banner.png")
                                end
                            end
                        end)
                        premium.profile = prof
                        premium.fetching = false
                        done(prof)
                    end)
                end

                -- The popup uses the same pieces as the hub's own dialogs: a CanvasGroup card (P.Bg2, 16px corners, glass border),
                -- section style captions, row-card style panels, accent / default buttons. It fades + slides in with a soft
                -- Quint ease and its blocks appear one after another.
                local function openPremium()
                    if screenGui:FindFirstChild("PremiumPopup") then return end
                    local vp = screenGui.AbsoluteSize
                    local W = math.clamp(vp.X - 24, 260, 360)
                    local MAXH = math.clamp(vp.Y - 36, 300, 620)
                    local HEADH, FOOTH = 54, 66

                    local overlay = Instance.new("TextButton")
                    overlay.Name = "PremiumPopup"
                    overlay.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
                    overlay.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
                    overlay.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    overlay.Text = ""
                    overlay.AutoButtonColor = false
                    overlay.Active = true
                    overlay.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    overlay.ZIndex = 60
                    overlay.Parent = screenGui
                    -- the popup is a fixed design: keep Auto Text Size / auto-fit away from its text, otherwise the sizes jump around
                    local function noFit(d)
                        if d:IsA("TextLabel") or d:IsA("TextButton") or d:IsA("TextBox") then d:SetAttribute("NoFit", true) end
                    end
                    overlay.DescendantAdded:Connect(noFit)

                    -- the window: same recipe as the hub (theme gradient, glass rim, sheen, inner rim). The gradient lives on a
                    -- child frame so the CanvasGroup can fade everything together without tinting its contents.
                    local card = Instance.new("CanvasGroup")
                    card.Name = "Card"
                    card.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
                    card.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 16)
                    card.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W, 420)
                    card.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    card.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    card.GroupTransparency = 1
                    card.Active = true
                    card.ZIndex = 61
                    card.Parent = overlay
                    corner(card, 18)
                    local cScale = Instance.new("UIScale", card)
                    cScale.Scale = 1   -- never animated: scaling text re-rasterizes it and it visibly changes size. The popup slides + fades only.
                    -- The glass outline used to sit on the CanvasGroup, where it was clipped / out of step with the fade, so it
                    -- popped in late. It is now its own frame on top of the card that moves, scales and fades with it.
                    local outline = Instance.new("Frame")
                    outline.Name = "CardOutline"
                    outline.AnchorPoint = card.AnchorPoint
                    outline.Position = card.Position
                    outline.Size = card.Size
                    outline.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    outline.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    outline.Active = false
                    outline.ZIndex = 67
                    outline.Parent = overlay
                    corner(outline, 18)
                    local cStroke = glassBorder(outline, 1.6, 0.12)
                    cStroke.Transparency = 1
                    local oScale = Instance.new("UIScale", outline)
                    oScale.Scale = 1
                    card:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Size"):Connect(function() outline.Size = card.Size end)

                    local bg = Instance.new("Frame")
                    bg.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
                    bg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
                    bg.BackgroundTransparency = 0.04
                    bg.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    bg.ZIndex = 61
                    bg.Parent = card
                    local bgGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient", bg)
                    bgGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new(Cfg.gradCur[1], Cfg.gradCur[2])
                    bgGrad.Rotation = Cfg.gradCur[3]
                    local shn = Instance.new("Frame")
                    shn.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
                    shn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
                    shn.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    shn.ZIndex = 61
                    shn.Parent = card
                    local shg = Instance.new("UIGradient", shn)
                    shg.Rotation = 55
                    shg.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
                        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.9), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0.985), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
                    })
                    local rim = Instance.new("Frame")
                    rim.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 1, -6)
                    rim.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 3)
                    rim.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    rim.ZIndex = 61
                    rim.Parent = card
                    corner(rim, 15)
                    stroke(rim, P.Text, 1, 0.92)

                    local fitK, opened = 1, false
                    local closing = false
                    local escConn
                    local function close()
                        if closing then return end
                        closing = true
                        if escConn then escConn:Disconnect() end
                        local ti = TweenInfo.new(0.24, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
                        TweenService:Create(overlay, ti, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()
                        TweenService:Create(card, ti, { GroupTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 14) }):Play()
                        TweenService:Create(cScale, ti, { Scale = fitK }):Play()
                        TweenService:Create(outline, ti, { Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 14) }):Play()
                        TweenService:Create(oScale, ti, { Scale = fitK }):Play()
                        TweenService:Create(cStroke, ti, { Transparency = 1 }):Play()
                        task.delay(0.28, function() overlay:Destroy() end)
                    end
                    overlay.MouseButton1Click:Connect(close)
                    escConn = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp, gp)
                        if inp.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then close() end
                    end)

                    local function label(parent, order, text, font, size, color, align)
                        local l = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                        l.LayoutOrder = order
                        l.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
                        l.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
                        l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                        l.Text = text
                        l.Font = font
                        l.TextSize = size
                        l.TextColor3 = color
                        l.TextWrapped = true
                        l.RichText = true
                        l.TextXAlignment = align or Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                        l.ZIndex = 64
                        l.Parent = parent
                        return l
                    end
                    local function mkIcon(parent, name, color, px)
                        local ic = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
                        ic.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(px, px)
                        ic.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                        ic.ZIndex = 65
                        ic.Parent = parent
                        if not (LucideReady and setIcon(ic, name, color)) then
                            ic.Visible = false
                            task.spawn(function()
                                local t0 = tick()
                                while not LucideReady and tick() - t0 < 30 do task.wait(0.2) end
                                if ic.Parent and LucideReady and setIcon(ic, name, color) then ic.Visible = true end
                            end)
                        end
                        return ic
                    end

                    local header = Instance.new("Frame")
                    header.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, HEADH)

                    header.ZIndex = 62
                    header.Parent = card

                    tile.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 11)
                    tile.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(32, 32)
                    tile.BackgroundColor3 = GOLD

                    tile.BorderSizePixel = 0

                    crown.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)

                    ttl.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
                    ttl.Position, ttl.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(58, 9), UDim2.new(1, -110, 0, 18)

                    xbStroke.Transparency = 0.4

                    hdiv.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1)
                    hdiv.BackgroundColor3 = P.Line2
                    hdiv.BackgroundTransparency = 0.45
                    hdiv.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    hdiv.ZIndex = 62

                    -- ---- scrolling body ----
                    local sc = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
                    sc.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, HEADH)
                    sc.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -(HEADH + FOOTH))
                    sc.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    sc.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    sc.ScrollBarThickness = 3   -- scrollable: on small screens the popup keeps its size and the body scrolls
                    sc.ScrollBarImageColor3 = P.Muted
                    sc.ScrollBarImageTransparency = 0.45
                    sc.ScrollingEnabled = true
                    sc.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y
                    sc.ElasticBehavior = Enum.ElasticBehavior.Never
                    sc.CanvasSize = UDim2.new()
                    sc.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
                    sc.ZIndex = 62
                    sc.Parent = card

                    list.Padding = UDim.new(0, 10)
                    list.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
                    list.Parent = sc
                    local sp = Instance.new("UIPadding", sc)
                    sp.PaddingTop, sp.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 14), UDim.new(0, 14)
                    sp.PaddingLeft, sp.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 14), UDim.new(0, 14)
                    local function fit()   -- the window is exactly as tall as its content (up to the screen height)
                        local H = list.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 28 + HEADH + FOOTH
                        local capH = math.max(220, vp.Y - 24)   -- never taller than the screen: the body scrolls instead of shrinking
                        card.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W, math.min(H, capH))
                        fitK = 1
                        if not closing and opened then cScale.Scale = fitK; oScale.Scale = fitK end
                    end
                    list:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(fit)

                    local blocks = {}
                    local function block(order)   -- each block fades in on its own
                        local f = Instance.new("CanvasGroup")
                        f.LayoutOrder = order
                        f.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
                        f.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
                        f.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                        f.BorderSizePixel = 0
                        f.GroupTransparency = 1
                        f.ZIndex = 63
                        f.Parent = sc
                        -- a CanvasGroup clips its children: without a margin the outside half of the glass rims on the cards was cut off
                        local bpad = Instance.new("UIPadding", f)
                        bpad.PaddingLeft, bpad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 3), UDim.new(0, 3)
                        bpad.PaddingTop, bpad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 3), UDim.new(0, 3)
                        local l = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
                        l.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
                        l.Parent = f
                        blocks[#blocks + 1] = f
                        return f
                    end
                    -- a section card, identical in look to the hub's sections: icon + small caps caption, divider, rows
                    local function panel(parent, title, iconName)
                        local f = Instance.new("Frame")
                        f.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
                        f.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
                        f.BackgroundColor3 = P.Bg2
                        f.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
                        f.BorderSizePixel = 0
                        f.ZIndex = 64
                        f.Parent = parent
                        corner(f, 8)
                        glassBorder(f, 1.4, 0.1)   -- visible glass rim (bright corner fading around the card)
                        local pd = Instance.new("UIPadding", f)
                        pd.PaddingLeft, pd.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 12), UDim.new(0, 12)
                        pd.PaddingTop, pd.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 10), UDim.new(0, 10)
                        local l = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
                        l.Padding = UDim.new(0, 7)
                        l.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
                        l.Parent = f
                        local head = Instance.new("Frame")
                        head.LayoutOrder = -3
                        head.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16)
                        head.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                        head.ZIndex = 65
                        head.Parent = f
                        local hi = mkIcon(head, iconName, P.Accent, 14)
                        hi.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 1)
                        local cap = label(head, 0, title, Enum.Font.GothamBold, 11, P.Muted)
                        cap.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
                        cap.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 0)
                        cap.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, 0)
                        cap.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center

                        dv.LayoutOrder = -2
                        dv.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1)
                        dv.BackgroundColor3 = P.Line2
                        dv.BackgroundTransparency = 0.45
                        dv.BorderSizePixel = 0
                        dv.ZIndex = 65
                        dv.Parent = f
                        return f
                    end
                    local function rowCard(parent, order, h)   -- same look as the hub's row cards
                        local r = Instance.new("Frame")

                        r.BorderSizePixel = 0
                        r.ZIndex = 65

                        stroke(r, P.Line, 1, 0.75)
                        local pd = Instance.new("UIPadding", r)
                        pd.PaddingLeft, pd.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 10), UDim.new(0, 10)
                        return r
                    end

                        c.TextSize = 9
                        c.TextColor3 = color
                        c.ZIndex = 67
                        c.Parent = parent

                        stroke(c, color, 1, 0.55)
                        local cpd = Instance.new("UIPadding", c)
                        cpd.PaddingLeft, cpd.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 6), UDim.new(0, 6)
                        return c
                    end

                    hdr.Parent = cmp
                    local hp = Instance.new("UIPadding", hdr)
                    hp.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 10)
                    for _, h in ipairs({ { "FREE", FREE_X, P.Muted }, { "PREMIUM", PREM_X, GOLD } }) do
                        local hl = label(hdr, 0, h[1], Enum.Font.GothamBold, 9, h[3], Enum.TextXAlignment.Center)
                        hl.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
                        hl.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
                        hl.Position = UDim2.new(1, h[2], 0.5, 0)
                        hl.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(56, 12)
                        hl.ZIndex = 66
                    end
                    local CMP = {
                        { "Core features",  true,  true },
                        { "More features",  false, true },
                        { "Decorations",    false, true },
                        { "Localization",   false, true },

                    }
                    for i, row in ipairs(CMP) do
                        local r = rowCard(cmp, i, 28)
                        local t = label(r, 0, row[1], Enum.Font.Gotham, 12, P.Text)
                        t.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
                        t.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
                        t.Size = UDim2.new(1, -110, 1, 0)
                        t.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
                        t.ZIndex = 66
                        local fi = row[2] and mkIcon(r, "check", P.Good, 14) or mkIcon(r, "x", P.Muted, 12)
                        fi.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
                        fi.Position = UDim2.new(1, FREE_X, 0.5, 0)
                        fi.ZIndex = 66
                        local pi2 = mkIcon(r, "check", GOLD, 14)
                        pi2.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
                        pi2.Position = UDim2.new(1, PREM_X, 0.5, 0)
                        pi2.ZIndex = 66
                    end

                    -- 6) closing note
                    local b6 = block(5)
                    local note = label(b6, 1, "Questions? Open a ticket in our Discord server and we will help you out.",
                        Enum.Font.Gotham, 11, P.Muted, Enum.TextXAlignment.Center)
                    note.ZIndex = 64
                    end

                    -- ---- footer: price on the left, accent "Join Server" button on the right ----
                    local foot = Instance.new("Frame")
                    foot.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1)
                    foot.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1)
                    foot.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, FOOTH)
                    foot.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    foot.ZIndex = 62
                    foot.Parent = card
                    local fdiv = Instance.new("Frame")
                    fdiv.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1)
                    fdiv.BackgroundColor3 = P.Line2
                    fdiv.BackgroundTransparency = 0.45
                    fdiv.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    fdiv.ZIndex = 62
                    fdiv.Parent = foot
                    local pc = label(foot, 0, "ONE-TIME PRICE", Enum.Font.GothamBold, 10, P.Muted)
                    pc.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
                    pc.Position, pc.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 14), UDim2.new(0.4, 0, 0, 12)
                    local pv = label(foot, 0, PREM_PRICE, Enum.Font.GothamBold, 16, GOLD)
                    pv.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
                    pv.Position, pv.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 28), UDim2.new(0.4, 0, 0, 22)
                    pv.TextWrapped = false
                    -- Robux icon right after the price (built-in Roblox texture; a drawn "R$" badge if it fails to load)
                    pcall(function()
                        local TS = game:GetService("TextService")
                        local tw = TS:GetTextSize(PREM_PRICE, 16, Enum.Font.GothamBold, Vector2.new(1000, 40)).X
                        local rx = 18 + tw + 8
                        -- the button starts at 52% of the width from the right edge: use the free space up to it
                        local free = W - 16 - W * 0.52 - rx - 8
                        local rs = math.clamp(math.floor(free), 20, 34)
                        local ry = 39 - rs / 2   -- vertically centred on the price
                        local rbx = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
                        rbx.Name = "RobuxIcon"
                        rbx.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(rx, ry)
                        rbx.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(rs, rs)
                        rbx.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                        rbx.Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/common/robux.png"
                        rbx.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
                        rbx.ZIndex = 64
                        rbx.Parent = foot
                        local badge = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                        badge.Name = "RobuxBadge"
                        badge.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(rx, ry)
                        badge.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(rs, rs)
                        badge.BackgroundColor3 = GOLD
                        badge.Text = "R$"
                        badge.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                        badge.TextSize = math.max(9, math.floor(rs * 0.5))
                        badge.TextColor3 = GOLD_DK
                        badge.Visible = false
                        badge.ZIndex = 64
                        badge.Parent = foot
                        corner(badge, rs / 2)
                        task.spawn(function()
                            local t0 = os.clock()
                            while rbx.Parent and not rbx.IsLoaded and os.clock() - t0 < 3 do task.wait(0.1) end
                            if rbx.Parent and not rbx.IsLoaded then rbx.Visible = false; badge.Visible = true end
                        end)
                    end)
                    local jb = Instance.new("TextButton")
                    jb.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
                    jb.Position = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0.5, 1)
                    jb.Size = UDim2.new(0.52, 0, 0, 38)
                    jb.BackgroundColor3 = P.Accent
                    jb.Text = "Join Server"
                    jb.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                    jb.TextSize = 13
                    jb.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
                    jb.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    jb.AutoButtonColor = false
                    jb.ZIndex = 64
                    jb.Parent = foot
                    corner(jb, 10)
                    softHover(jb, P.Accent, stroke(jb, P.Accent2, 1, 0.5))
                    -- Discord logo (asset image) + label in a centred row. A layout
                    -- places them side by side, so the logo can never overlap the text.
                    pcall(function()
                        jb.Text = ""
                        local row = Instance.new("Frame")
                        row.Name = "Content"
                        row.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
                        row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                        row.ZIndex = 65
                        row.Parent = jb
                        local rl = Instance.new("UIListLayout", row)
                        rl.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
                        rl.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
                        rl.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
                        rl.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
                        rl.Padding = UDim.new(0, 8)
                        local logo = Instance.new("ImageLabel")   -- the hub's Discord icon asset, drawn bigger
                        logo.Name = "DiscordLogo"
                        logo.LayoutOrder = 1
                        logo.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(34, 34)
                        logo.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                        logo.Image = "rbxassetid://132930589363646"
                        logo.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
                        logo.ZIndex = 65
                        logo.Parent = row
                        local jt = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                        jt.LayoutOrder = 2
                        jt.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X
                        jt.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
                        jt.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                        jt.Text = "Join Server"
                        jt.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                        jt.TextSize = 13
                        jt.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
                        jt.ZIndex = 65
                        jt.Parent = row
                    end)
                    jb.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                        pcall(function() setclipboard(PREM_URL) end)

                            local req = (syn and syn.request) or http_request or request
                            if req then

                                    Headers = { ["Content-Type"] = "application/json", ["Origin"] = "https://discord.com" },

                    TweenService:Create(card, tiO, { GroupTransparency = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0) }):Play()
                    TweenService:Create(cScale, TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Scale = fitK }):Play()
                    TweenService:Create(outline, tiO, { Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0) }):Play()
                    TweenService:Create(oScale, TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Scale = fitK }):Play()

                    for i, blk in ipairs(blocks) do
                        TweenService:Create(blk, TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out, 0, false, 0.15 + (i - 1) * 0.08),
