                local Loc = { ctl = nil, loading = false, pending = nil, failed = false }
                Cfg.Loc = Loc

                local sLoc = section(page, "LOCALIZATION", "languages", false,
                    "Change the language of the ui to whatever you want")
                local langDrop, statusRow
                local function setStatus(text)
                    if not statusRow then return end
                    local v = statusRow:FindFirstChild("Value")
                    if v and v.Text ~= text then v.Text = text end
                end

                local function locLoad()
                    if Loc.ctl or Loc.loading then return end
                    Loc.loading, Loc.failed = true, false
                    setStatus("Loading...")
                    task.spawn(function()
                        local ok, err = pcall(function()
                            local body = lucideFetch(LOC_URL)
                            assert(body and body ~= "", "could not download the language module (HTTP blocked?)")
                            local Modules = assert(loadstring(body))()
                            -- stand-in for WindUI: the module wraps these three functions when it installs
                            local stub = {}
                            function stub:CreateWindow(c) return { Folder = c and c.Folder } end
                            function stub:Localization(c) return c end
                            function stub:SetLanguage() return true end
                            function stub:GetLanguage() return "en" end
                            function stub:Notify(o)   -- the module's "Translating... / Done / Failed" messages
                                o = o or {}
                                local txt = tostring(o.Content or o.Desc or "")
                                local kind = (tostring(txt):lower():find("fail") or tostring(txt):lower():find("error")) and "warning" or "info"
                                pcall(notify, tostring(o.Title or "Language"), txt, kind, tonumber(o.Duration) or 3)
                            end
                            local ctl = Modules:Install(stub)

                            -- hub names, theme names and the language names themselves must never be translated
                            local keep = { "Nauru Hub", "Nauru", "Dead Rails", "Roblox", "Discord", "Lucide" }
                            for _, n in ipairs(LOC_NAMES) do keep[#keep + 1] = n end
                            pcall(function() for _, t in ipairs(Themes) do keep[#keep + 1] = t.Name end end)
                            stub:Localization({
                                Enabled = true, DefaultLanguage = "en", SourceLanguage = "en",
                                AutoTranslate = true, Notifications = true, SyncSwap = true,
                                Icons = { Start = "languages", Done = "badge-check", Fail = "triangle-alert" },
                                NoTranslations = keep,
                                Translations = { en = {} },
                            })
                            stub:CreateWindow({ Folder = LOC_FOLDER })
                            ctl:AttachRoot(screenGui)
                            Loc.ctl = ctl

                            pcall(function()   -- keep the dropdown + status in sync with the active language
                                ctl:OnChanged(function(new)
                                    local nm = NAME_OF[tostring(new)]
                                    if nm then
                                        Values.Language = nm
                                        if langDrop then langDrop.Set(nm, true) end
                                    end
                                    setStatus(nm or tostring(new))
                                end)
                            end)
                            local cur = ctl:GetLanguage()
                            local nm = NAME_OF[tostring(cur)]
                            if nm then
                                Values.Language = nm
                                if langDrop then langDrop.Set(nm, true) end
                            end
                            setStatus(nm or "English")
                        end)
                        Loc.loading = false
                        if not ok then
                            Loc.failed = true
                            setStatus("Failed")
                            pcall(notify, "Localization", "Could not load the language module: " .. tostring(err):sub(1, 90), "error", 5)
                            return
                        end
                        if Loc.pending then
                            local code = Loc.pending
                            Loc.pending = nil
                            pcall(function() Loc.ctl:SetLanguage(code) end)
                        end
                    end)
                end

                local function locSet(name)
                    if not IS_PREMIUM then return end   -- locked: nothing is loaded or translated without Premium
                    local code = CODE_OF[name]
                    if not code then return end
                    if not Loc.ctl then
                        if code == "en" and not Loc.loading then setStatus("English"); return end   -- nothing to undo yet
                        Loc.pending = code
                        locLoad()
                        return
                    end
                    local ok, res, why = pcall(function() return Loc.ctl:SetLanguage(code) end)
                    if not ok or res == false then
                        pcall(notify, "Language", "Could not switch language: " .. tostring(ok and why or res):sub(1, 90), "error", 4)
                    end
                end

                langDrop = dropdown(sLoc, "Language", LOC_NAMES, "Language", function(v) locSet(v) end, "languages", false)
                statusRow = infoRow(sLoc, "Status", "English")
                button(sLoc, "Reload Translations", function()
                    if not Loc.ctl then notify("Localization", "Pick a language first.", "info", 2.5) return end
                    pcall(function() Loc.ctl:Refresh(true) end)   -- re-applies all text and retries strings that failed before
                    notify("Localization", "Translations reloaded.", "success", 2.5)
                end, "refresh-cw")
                button(sLoc, "Clear Translation Cache", function()
                    if not Loc.ctl then
                        pcall(function() if type(delfile) == "function" and isfile(LOC_CACHE) then delfile(LOC_CACHE) end end)
                        notify("Localization", "Translation cache cleared.", "success", 2.5)
                        return
                    end
                    pcall(function() Loc.ctl.Manager:ClearCache() end)
                    pcall(function() Loc.ctl:Refresh(true) end)
                    notify("Localization", "Translation cache cleared.", "success", 2.5)
                end, "trash-2")

                pcall(function() Cfg.SetPremiumTag(IS_PREMIUM, openPremium) end)   -- FREE / PREMIUM tag by the search bar

                -- lock the whole section (every row gets a PREMIUM cover; clicking it opens the popup)
                if not IS_PREMIUM then
                    pcall(function() lockSectionOnce(sLoc, "Localization is a Premium feature.", "PREMIUM", openPremium) end)
                    -- Decorations are a Premium perk too: the same single lock, and no decoration is drawn while it is locked
                    Cfg.DecorLocked = true
                    if sDecor then
                        pcall(function() lockSectionOnce(sDecor, "Decorations are a Premium feature.", "PREMIUM", openPremium) end)
                    end
                end

                -- a language was used before (its cache file exists): bring the module up now so it restores that language
                task.defer(function()
                    if not IS_PREMIUM then return end
                    local used = false
                    pcall(function() used = type(isfile) == "function" and isfile(LOC_CACHE) end)
                    if used then locLoad() end
                end)
            end

            local sCfg = section(page, "CONFIG", "save", false, "Save and load named configs. A config marked Auto Load is applied on startup.")
            local cfgNameInput = UIKit.input(sCfg, "Config Name", "", "my config", nil, "ConfigName", "text-cursor-input", { MaxLength = 32 })
            local cfgDrop
            cfgDrop = dropdown(sCfg, "Saved Configs", {}, "ConfigSelected", function(v)
                cfgNameInput.Set(v, true)   -- picking a config fills the name box
            end, "folder-open")
            local autoRow = infoRow(sCfg, "Auto Load", "None")

            local lastList = nil
            local function refreshCfgList(force)
                local list = Cfg.list()
                local key = table.concat(list, "\n")
                if force or key ~= lastList then
                    lastList = key
                    cfgDrop.SetOptions(list)
                end
                local v = autoRow:FindFirstChild("Value")
                local want = Cfg.getAuto() or "None"
                if v and v.Text ~= want then v.Text = want end
            end

            local function targetName()
                local n = Cfg.clean(cfgNameInput.Get())
                if n == "" then n = Cfg.clean(Values.ConfigSelected) end
                return n
            end
            local function doSave(name, auto)
                local ok, res = Cfg.save(name)
                if not ok then notify("Save failed", res, "error", 4) return end
                if auto then
                    if Cfg.setAuto(res) then
                        notify("Saved as Auto Load", res .. " will load every time the hub starts.", "success", 3.5)
                    else
                        notify("Saved", res .. " was saved, but auto load could not be set.", "warning", 4)
                    end
                else
                    notify("Config saved", res, "success", 2.5)
                end
                cfgNameInput.Set(res, true)
                refreshCfgList(true)
                cfgDrop.Set(res, true)
            end
            local function saveFlow(auto)
                local name = targetName()
                if name == "" then
                    notify("Config name needed", "Type a name for your config first.", "warning", 3)
                    return
                end
                if Cfg.exists(name) then
                    openDialog({
                        Title = "Overwrite config?", Icon = "save",
                        Text = name .. " already exists. Replace it with your current settings?",
                        Buttons = {
                            { Text = "Cancel" },
                            { Text = "Overwrite", Style = "primary", Callback = function() doSave(name, auto) end },
                        },
                    })
                else
                    doSave(name, auto)
                end
            end

            button(sCfg, "Save Config", function() saveFlow(false) end, "save")
            button(sCfg, "Save as Auto Load", function() saveFlow(true) end, "rocket")
            button(sCfg, "Load Config", function()
                local name = Cfg.clean(Values.ConfigSelected)
                if name == "" then notify("No config selected", "Pick a config from the list first.", "warning", 3) return end
                local data = Cfg.read(name)
                if not data then
                    notify("Load failed", name .. " could not be read.", "error", 4)
                    refreshCfgList(true)
                    return
                end
                local ok, err = pcall(Cfg.apply, data)
                if ok then notify("Config loaded", name, "success", 3) else notify("Load failed", tostring(err), "error", 4) end
            end, "download")
            button(sCfg, "Delete Config", function()
                local name = Cfg.clean(Values.ConfigSelected)
                if name == "" then notify("No config selected", "Pick a config from the list first.", "warning", 3) return end
                openDialog({
                    Title = "Delete config?", Icon = "trash-2", IconColor = P.Bad,
                    Text = "This permanently deletes " .. name .. ".",
                    Buttons = {
                        { Text = "Cancel" },
                        { Text = "Delete", Style = "danger", Callback = function()
                            if Cfg.delete(name) then
                                if Cfg.getAuto() == name then Cfg.setAuto(nil) end
                                notify("Config deleted", name, "success", 2.5)
                            else
                                notify("Delete failed", "Could not delete " .. name .. ".", "error", 4)
                            end
                            refreshCfgList(true)
                        end },
                    },
                })
            end, "trash-2")
            button(sCfg, "Clear Auto Load", function()
                if Cfg.getAuto() then
                    Cfg.setAuto(nil)
                    notify("Auto load cleared", "No config will load on startup.", "info", 3)
                    refreshCfgList(false)
                else
                    notify("Auto load", "No auto load config is set.", "info", 2.5)
                end
            end, "rotate-ccw")

            -- keeps the list up to date (new / deleted files show up by themselves)
            task.spawn(function()
                pcall(refreshCfgList, true)
                while screenGui.Parent do
                    task.wait(2)
                    if Main.Visible and page.Visible then pcall(refreshCfgList, false) end
                end
            end)

            if opts and opts.extras then
                local okE, errE = pcall(opts.extras, UIKit.Hub.makeTab("Settings", "sliders-horizontal"))
            end

            local s3 = section(page, "MENU", "layout-dashboard", false, "Menu keybind and unloading.")
            keybindRow(s3, "Menu Keybind", "MENU", "keyboard")
            button(s3, "Unload UI", function()
                if requestUnload then requestUnload() end
            end, "power")
        end

        -- hide every scrollbar (wheel / drag scrolling still works)
        do
            local function hideBar(o)
                if o:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
                    o.ScrollBarThickness = 0
                    o.ScrollBarImageTransparency = 1
                    o.TopImage, o.MidImage, o.BottomImage = "", "", ""
                    o:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ScrollBarThickness"):Connect(function()
                        if o.ScrollBarThickness ~= 0 then o.ScrollBarThickness = 0 end
                    end)
                    o:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ScrollBarImageTransparency"):Connect(function()
                        if o.ScrollBarImageTransparency ~= 1 then o.ScrollBarImageTransparency = 1 end
                    end)
                end
            end
            for _, o in ipairs(screenGui:GetDescendants()) do hideBar(o) end
            screenGui.DescendantAdded:Connect(hideBar)
        end

        -- ============================================================
        -- SEARCH (filters rows + sections on every tab)
        -- ============================================================

        local function rowText(row)
            if row:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("UIGridLayout") then
                local parts = {}
                for _, d in ipairs(row:GetDescendants()) do
                    if d:IsA("TextLabel") then table.insert(parts, stripRich(d.Text)) end
                end
                return table.concat(parts, " "):lower()
            end
            local l = row:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel", true)
            return l and stripRich(l.Text):lower() or ""
        end

        local function applySearch(query)
            query = (query or ""):lower():gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
            local firstMatchTab, currentHasMatch = nil, false

            for _, tabName in ipairs(TAB_ORDER) do
                local page = pages[tabName]
                local tabHasMatch = false
                local subMatch, firstSub = {}, nil
                if page then
                    for _, wrap in ipairs(page:GetDescendants()) do
                        if wrap:IsA("Frame") and wrap:FindFirstChild("SectionDivider") then
                            local header = wrap:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Frame")
                            local titleLbl = header and header:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel")
                            local descLbl = wrap:FindFirstChild("SectionDesc") and wrap.SectionDesc:FindFirstChild("Text")
                            local titleMatch = query == "" or (titleLbl and stripRich(titleLbl.Text):lower():find(query, 1, true) ~= nil)
                                or (descLbl and stripRich(descLbl.Text):lower():find(query, 1, true) ~= nil)
                            local anyRow = false
                            for _, row in ipairs(wrap:GetChildren()) do
                                if row.Name == "SectionDesc" then
                                    row.Visible = true   -- the description never hides
                                elseif (row:IsA("Frame") or row:IsA("TextButton"))
                                and row ~= header and row.Name ~= "SectionDivider" then
                                    local show = titleMatch or rowText(row):find(query, 1, true) ~= nil
                                    row.Visible = show and (query ~= "" or not wrap:GetAttribute("Collapsed"))
                                    if show then anyRow = true end
                                end
                            end
                            local dv = wrap:FindFirstChild("SectionDivider")
                            if dv then dv.Visible = query ~= "" or not wrap:GetAttribute("Collapsed") end
                            wrap.Visible = (query == "") or titleMatch or anyRow
                            if wrap.Visible then
                                tabHasMatch = true
                                if query ~= "" then
                                    local sp = wrap.Parent   -- walk up past the column scroll frames to the sub page itself
                                    while sp and not (sp:IsA("ScrollingFrame") and sp:GetAttribute("SubName")) do sp = sp.Parent end
                                    if sp then
                                        subMatch[sp] = true
                                        firstSub = firstSub or sp
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                if firstSub then   -- the visible sub-tab has no match but another one does: switch to it
                    local act = Cfg.ActiveSub[tabName]
                    if act and not subMatch[act] and Cfg.SubSelect[firstSub] then pcall(Cfg.SubSelect[firstSub]) end
                end
                local btn = tabButtons[tabName]
                if btn then
                    TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
                        BackgroundTransparency = (query == "" or tabHasMatch) and 0.5 or 0.85
                    }):Play()
                end
                if tabHasMatch and not firstMatchTab then firstMatchTab = tabName end
                if tabHasMatch and page and page.Visible then currentHasMatch = true end
            end

            -- jump to the first tab that has results if the current one has none
            if query ~= "" and firstMatchTab and not currentHasMatch and not tabLocks[firstMatchTab] then
                switchTab(firstMatchTab)
            end
        end

        SearchBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
            applySearch(SearchBox.Text)
        end)

        -- ============================================================
        -- MINIMIZE / CLOSE
        -- ============================================================
        local isMinimized = false
        local minBusy = false
        local ResizeGrip
        do
        local SEARCH_T, WINDOW_T = 0.4, 0.65   -- minimize / restore speed (seconds)
        local EASE = Enum.EasingStyle.Sine
        local normalW, minShift = 690, 0
        local normalScale = 1   -- menu scale to restore when the pill expands again
        local boxOrigSize

        local function tw(o, ti, goal) local t = TweenService:Create(o, ti, goal); t:Play(); return t end
        local function searchFullW(w) return Cfg.SearchW end

        -- search bar: fully hidden state (zero width, clipped, everything transparent).
        -- It stays "Visible" the whole time and is only ever resized/faded, so it can never pop in for a frame.
        local function hideSearchFinal()
            if boxOrigSize then SearchBox.Size = boxOrigSize end
            SearchWrap.ClipsDescendants = true
            SearchWrap.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, Cfg.SearchH)
            SearchWrap.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            searchStroke.Transparency = 1
            SearchBox.Visible = false
        end
        local function freezeSearch(w)
            boxOrigSize = SearchBox.Size
            SearchBox.Size = UDim2.new(0, searchFullW(w) + boxOrigSize.X.Offset, boxOrigSize.Y.Scale, boxOrigSize.Y.Offset)
            SearchWrap.ClipsDescendants = true
        end
        local function unfreezeSearch()
            if boxOrigSize then SearchBox.Size = boxOrigSize end
            SearchWrap.Size = UDim2.new(0, Cfg.SearchW, 0, Cfg.SearchH)
            SearchWrap.ClipsDescendants = false
        end

        -- transparency (solid) button: shrinks + fades away / pops back
        local function solidFx() return SolidBtn:FindFirstChild("FxScale") end
        local SOLID_SHOWN  = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, -15)
        local SOLID_HIDDEN = UDim2.new(0, 42, 0.5, -15)   -- fully outside the clipped slot
        local function hideSolid(dur)
            SolidBtn:SetAttribute("Busy", true)
            tw(SolidBtn, TweenInfo.new(dur, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), { Position = SOLID_HIDDEN })
            task.delay(dur, function() SolidBtn.Visible = false end)
        end
        local function showSolid(dur)
            SolidBtn.Position = SOLID_HIDDEN
            SolidBtn.Visible = true
            tw(SolidBtn, TweenInfo.new(dur, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Position = SOLID_SHOWN })
            task.delay(dur, function() SolidBtn:SetAttribute("Busy", false) end)
        end

        -- body (sidebar tabs, divider, tab title, page content): remember original transparencies, fade them
        local bodyFade = {}
        local function collectBody()
            bodyFade = {}
            local function add(o)
                local rec
                if o:IsA("GuiObject") then rec = { BackgroundTransparency = o.BackgroundTransparency } end
                if o:IsA("TextLabel") or o:IsA("TextButton") then rec.TextTransparency = o.TextTransparency end
                if o:IsA("ImageLabel") or o:IsA("ImageButton") then rec.ImageTransparency = o.ImageTransparency end
                if o:IsA("UIStroke") then rec = { Transparency = o.Transparency } end
                if rec then bodyFade[o] = rec end
            end
            for _, root in ipairs({ TabsBar, Cfg.Profile, TabDivider, Cfg.IconDivider, tabTitleFrame }) do   -- the window icon + title stay visible when minimized
                add(root)
                for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do add(d) end
            end
        end
        local function setBodyHidden()
            for o, rec in pairs(bodyFade) do
                if o.Parent then
                    for prop in pairs(rec) do o[prop] = 1 end
                end
            end
            Content.GroupTransparency = 1
        end
        local function fadeBody(show, dur, delay)
            local ti = TweenInfo.new(dur, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad,
                show and Enum.EasingDirection.Out or Enum.EasingDirection.In, 0, false, delay or 0)
            for o, rec in pairs(bodyFade) do
                if o.Parent then
                    local goal = {}
                    for prop, orig in pairs(rec) do goal[prop] = show and orig or 1 end
                    TweenService:Create(o, ti, goal):Play()
                end
            end
            TweenService:Create(Content, ti, { GroupTransparency = show and 0 or 1 }):Play()
        end

        local function anchorRight()
            local p, w, sc = Main.Position, Main.Size.X.Offset, MainScale.Scale
            Main.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
            Main.Position = UDim2.new(p.X.Scale, p.X.Offset + w * sc, p.Y.Scale, p.Y.Offset)
        end
        local function anchorLeft()
            local p, w, sc = Main.Position, Main.Size.X.Offset, MainScale.Scale
            Main.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
            Main.Position = UDim2.new(p.X.Scale, math.floor(p.X.Offset - w * sc + 0.5), p.Y.Scale, p.Y.Offset)
        end

        MinBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if minBusy then return end
            minBusy = true
            isMinimized = not isMinimized
            if isMinimized then Cfg.popScaleLocked = true; Cfg.UIMinimized = true end   -- decorations hide right away
            if isMinimized and Cfg.updateTabHints then pcall(Cfg.updateTabHints) end   -- tab arrow pills fade out right away
            anchorRight()   -- pin the right edge for the whole animation (one conversion, before anything moves)
            RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()   -- let the anchor change render once before any tween starts
            local fullH = 580
            local sc = MainScale.Scale
            local sBg, sStrokeT = 0.3, 0.5   -- search bar's resting look

            local function freezeBody(w)
                local bw = w - (sidebarW + 44)
                Content.Size = UDim2.new(0, bw, 0, fullH - 68 - headerH)
                tabTitleFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, bw, 0, 24)
                TabsBar.Size = UDim2.new(0, sidebarW, 0, fullH - 216)
                TabDivider.Size = UDim2.new(0, 1, 0, fullH)
            end

            if isMinimized then
                TopIcons.min(true)
                normalW = Main.Size.X.Offset
                local w = normalW
                if ResizeGrip then ResizeGrip.Visible = false end
                pcall(function() SearchBox:ReleaseFocus() end)

                -- phase 1: everything inside the UI fades out while the search bar wipes away
                collectBody()
                fadeBody(false, 0.32, 0)
                hideSolid(0.3)
                freezeSearch(w)
                local ti1 = TweenInfo.new(SEARCH_T, EASE, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut)
                local t1 = tw(SearchWrap, ti1, { Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, Cfg.SearchH), BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
                tw(searchStroke, ti1, { Transparency = 1 })
                t1.Completed:Wait()
                hideSearchFinal()

                -- phase 2: shrink the window, slide the icon to the centre
                freezeBody(w)
                TabsBar.Visible = false
                Cfg.Profile.Visible = false
                TabDivider.Visible = false
                Cfg.IconDivider.Visible = false
                Content.Visible = false
                tabTitleFrame.Visible = false
                local minW = 152   -- window icon on the left, the two buttons (minimize + close) on the right
                local ti2 = TweenInfo.new(WINDOW_T, EASE, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut)
                tw(Cfg.Brand, ti2, { Size = UDim2.new(0, 52, 0, 52) })   -- icon button shrinks to just the icon
                tw(Cfg.BrandIcon, ti2, { Size = UDim2.new(0, 46, 0, 46) })
                tw(TitleLine, ti2, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 55), Size = UDim2.new(1, -32, 0, 1) })   -- line stays and spreads under the pill
                local t2 = tw(Main, ti2, { Size = UDim2.new(0, minW, 0, 56) })
                -- shrink the pill on small screens (phones): cap its on-screen width/height to a share of the viewport.
                -- Big screens are unaffected (the cap is never below the current scale there).
                normalScale = sc
                local vp = screenGui.AbsoluteSize
                local fit = math.min((vp.X * 0.42) / minW, (vp.Y * 0.11) / 56)
                local pillScale = math.min(sc, math.max(0.45, fit))
                if pillScale < sc - 0.005 then tw(MainScale, ti2, { Scale = pillScale }) end
                t2.Completed:Wait()
            else
                TopIcons.min(false)
                local w = normalW

                -- phase 1: window grows, the icon slides back; the inside fades in near the end of the growth
                freezeBody(w)
                setBodyHidden()                     -- hidden BEFORE becoming visible -> no flash
                TabsBar.Visible = true
                Cfg.Profile.Visible = true
                TabDivider.Visible = true
                Cfg.IconDivider.Visible = true
                Content.Visible = true
                tabTitleFrame.Visible = headerH > 0
                fadeBody(true, 0.35, WINDOW_T * 0.5)
                local ti2 = TweenInfo.new(WINDOW_T, EASE, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut)
                tw(Cfg.Brand, ti2, { Size = UDim2.new(0, sidebarW - 8, 0, 100) })   -- icon button follows the sidebar width again
                tw(Cfg.BrandIcon, ti2, { Size = UDim2.new(0, 104, 0, 104) })
                tw(TitleLine, ti2, { Position = UDim2.new(0, sidebarW + 32, 0, 55), Size = UDim2.new(1, -(sidebarW + 48), 0, 1) })
                local t2 = tw(Main, ti2, { Size = UDim2.new(0, w, 0, fullH) })
                if math.abs(MainScale.Scale - normalScale) > 0.005 then tw(MainScale, ti2, { Scale = normalScale }) end
                t2.Completed:Wait()
                MainScale.Scale = normalScale
                Cfg.popScaleLocked = false
                Cfg.UIMinimized = false   -- the window is full size again: decorations grow back out
                if Cfg.updateTabHints then pcall(Cfg.updateTabHints) end   -- tab arrow pills may fade back in
                if Cfg.syncPopScale then Cfg.syncPopScale() end
                Content.Size = UDim2.new(1, -(sidebarW + 44), 1, -(68 + headerH))
                tabTitleFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -(sidebarW + 44), 0, 24)
                TabsBar.Size = UDim2.new(0, sidebarW, 1, -216)
                TabDivider.Size = UDim2.new(0, 1, 1, 0)
                if ResizeGrip then ResizeGrip.Visible = true end

                -- phase 2: search bar wipes back in (it was never toggled visible, only resized) + solid button pops back
                freezeSearch(w)
                hideSearchFinal()
                freezeSearch(w)
                SearchBox.Visible = true
                showSolid(0.3)
                local ti1 = TweenInfo.new(SEARCH_T, EASE, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut)
                local t1 = tw(SearchWrap, ti1, { Size = UDim2.new(0, searchFullW(w), 0, Cfg.SearchH), BackgroundTransparency = sBg })
                tw(searchStroke, ti1, { Transparency = sStrokeT })
                t1.Completed:Wait()
                unfreezeSearch()
            end
            anchorLeft()
            minBusy = false
        end)
        end -- minimize scope

        -- ============================================================
        -- CORNER GRIP (curved line, drag to scale the whole UI)
        -- ============================================================
        do
            local MIN_S, MAX_S = 0.5, 2.0

            applyMenuScale = function(v, keepCenter, fromSlider)
                v = math.clamp(math.floor(v * 100 + 0.5) / 100, MIN_S, MAX_S)
                local old = MainScale.Scale
                if keepCenter then
                    -- UIScale grows from the top-left, so shift the position by half the
                    -- size change (uses the unscaled Size, so no stale AbsoluteSize reads)
                    local w, h = Main.Size.X.Offset, Main.Size.Y.Offset
                    local p = Main.Position
                    Main.Position = UDim2.new(
                        p.X.Scale, p.X.Offset + w * (old - v) / 2,
                        p.Y.Scale, p.Y.Offset + h * (old - v) / 2
                    )
                end
                MainScale.Scale = v
                Values.Scale_Menu = v
                if not fromSlider and SliderSetters.Scale_Menu then SliderSetters.Scale_Menu(v) end
            end

            ResizeGrip = Instance.new("TextButton")
            ResizeGrip.Name = "ResizeGrip"
            ResizeGrip.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1)
            ResizeGrip.Size = UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 30)
            ResizeGrip.Position = UDim2.new(1, -3, 1, -3)
            ResizeGrip.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            ResizeGrip.Text = ""
            ResizeGrip.AutoButtonColor = false
            ResizeGrip.ZIndex = 20
            ResizeGrip.Parent = Main
            ResizeGrip.Visible = not isMinimized

            -- circle outline whose gradient only reveals the bottom-right arc,
            -- so it hugs the rounded corner of the window
            local arc = Instance.new("Frame")
            arc.Name = "Arc"
            arc.Size = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 22)
            arc.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 4)
            arc.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            arc.BorderSizePixel = 0
            arc.ZIndex = 20
            arc.Parent = ResizeGrip
            corner(arc, 11)
            local arcStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
            arcStroke.Color = P.Muted
            arcStroke.Thickness = 2
            arcStroke.Transparency = 0.35
            arcStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
            arcStroke.LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Round
            arcStroke.Parent = arc
            local arcGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient")
            arcGrad.Rotation = 45
            arcGrad.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,    1),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.70, 1),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.80, 0),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1,    0),
            })
            arcGrad.Parent = arcStroke

            local dragging, dragInput, startPos, startScale, moved = false, nil, nil, 1, false
            local function tintArc(col, tr)
                TweenService:Create(arcStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { Color = col, Transparency = tr }):Play()
            end
            local function stopDrag(save)
                if not dragging then return end
                dragging, dragInput, moved = false, nil, false
                tintArc(P.Muted, 0.35)
                -- (settings are only written by the Save buttons in Settings > Config)
            end

            ResizeGrip.MouseEnter:Connect(function() if not dragging then tintArc(P.Text, 0.1) end end)
            ResizeGrip.MouseLeave:Connect(function() if not dragging then tintArc(P.Muted, 0.35) end end)

            ResizeGrip.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
                if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
                or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    dragging = true
                    dragInput = input
                    startPos = input.Position
                    startScale = MainScale.Scale
                    moved = false
                    tintArc(P.Accent, 0)
                end
            end)

            UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
                if not dragging then return end
                if dragInput.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    if input ~= dragInput then return end
                else
                    if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then return end
                    -- safety: if the button was released somewhere we never saw, stop now
                    if not UserInputService:IsMouseButtonPressed(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1) then
                        stopDrag(true)
                        return
                    end
                end
                if not Main.Visible or not ResizeGrip.Visible then stopDrag(false) return end

                local d = input.Position - startPos
                if not moved then
                    if Vector2.new(d.X, d.Y).Magnitude < 4 then return end   -- ignore tiny jitter / plain clicks
                    moved = true
                end
                -- 1 unit of scale == the window's base width/height
                applyMenuScale(startScale + (d.X / 690 + d.Y / 580) / 2, false, false)
            end)

            UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
                if not dragging then return end
                if dragInput.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    if input == dragInput then stopDrag(true) end
                elseif input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                    stopDrag(true)
                end
            end)
            UserInputService.WindowFocusReleased:Connect(function() stopDrag(true) end)
        end

        -- ============================================================
        -- AUTO FIT: shrink the menu on phones / small screens so the whole window is on screen
        --   (only ever scales DOWN to fit; the Menu Scale slider and the corner grip still work as before)
        -- ============================================================
        do
            local WIN_W, WIN_H, MARGIN = 690, 580, 16
            local function fitScale()
                local vp = screenGui.AbsoluteSize
                if vp.X <= 0 or vp.Y <= 0 then
                    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
                    vp = cam and cam.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1280, 720)
                end
                local s = math.min((vp.X - MARGIN * 2) / WIN_W, (vp.Y - MARGIN * 2) / WIN_H, 1)
                return math.clamp(s, 0.5, 1)
            end

            -- at startup: use the fitting scale if the window wouldn't fit at 100%
            local s = fitScale()
            if s < MainScale.Scale then applyMenuScale(s, true, false) end

            -- rotating the phone / resizing the window: shrink again if the menu no longer fits
            screenGui:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(function()
                if isMinimized then return end
                local fit = fitScale()
                if MainScale.Scale > fit + 0.005 then applyMenuScale(fit, true, false) end
            end)
        end

        -- ============================================================
        -- AUTO TEXT SIZE: text grows on big screens / phones so it stays readable.
        -- This only decides the WANTED size (Cfg.textBoost); the AUTO TEXT FIT system below is the one and only
        -- thing that touches TextSize: it starts from the wanted size and steps down until the text fits its box.
        -- phones: factor = 1 / menu scale (the menu is shrunk to fit the screen), kept between 1.2x and 1.5x
        -- others: factor = (screen size relative to 1280x720) / menu scale, kept between 1x and 1.4x
        -- ============================================================
        do
            local enabled, factor = true, 1

            local function computeFactor()
                if not enabled then return 1 end
                if IS_MOBILE then
                    return math.clamp(1 / math.max(MainScale.Scale, 0.01), 1.2, 1.5)
                end
                local vp = screenGui.AbsoluteSize
                if vp.X <= 0 or vp.Y <= 0 then
                    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
                    vp = cam and cam.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1280, 720)
                end
                local screenFactor = math.min(vp.X / 1280, vp.Y / 720)
                return math.clamp(screenFactor / math.max(MainScale.Scale, 0.01), 1, 1.4)
            end

            -- design size -> wanted size. Body text (12-15) all gets the same size, small captions (<=11) another one,
            -- headings keep their own ratio, so every label of a kind starts from exactly the same size
            Cfg.textBoost = function(design)
                if factor <= 1.0001 then return design end
                local ref = design
                if design <= 11 then ref = 11 elseif design <= 15 then ref = 13 end
                return math.clamp(math.max(design, math.floor(ref * factor + 0.5)), 1, 100)
            end

            local pending = false
            local function scheduleRefresh()
                if pending then return end
                pending = true
                task.delay(0.1, function()
                    pending = false
                    local nf = computeFactor()
                    if math.abs(nf - factor) >= 0.02 then
                        factor = nf
                        if Cfg.refitText then Cfg.refitText() end
                    end
                end)
            end

            factor = computeFactor()
            screenGui:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(scheduleRefresh)
            MainScale:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Scale"):Connect(scheduleRefresh)

            Cfg.setAutoText = function(on)
                enabled = on ~= false
                factor = computeFactor()
                if Cfg.refitText then Cfg.refitText() end
            end
        end

        -- ============================================================
        -- AUTO TEXT FIT: titles / labels / buttons shrink to fit their box
        --   Re-checked whenever the window, the menu scale, the screen size or the text itself changes.
        --   Text never grows past the size it was designed with, so big screens look exactly as before;
        --   on phones / small windows long titles shrink (down to ~60%) instead of being cut off with "...".
        --   Add  label:SetAttribute("NoFit", true)  to opt a label out.
        -- ============================================================
        do
            local TextSvc = game:GetService("TextService")
            local MIN_RATIO, ABS_MIN, PAD, BUDGET = 0.6, 8, 4, 160
            local recs = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
            local dirty = {}

            local function queue(o) dirty[o] = true end

            -- product of every UIScale from the label up to the ScreenGui (menu scale, pop-up scale, ...)
            local function totalScale(o)
                local s, p = 1, o
                while p and p ~= screenGui do
                    local u = p:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale")
                    if u then s = s * u.Scale end
                    p = p.Parent
                end
                return math.max(s, 0.01)
            end

            local function fitOne(o)
                local rec = recs[o]
                if not rec or not o.Parent then return end
                if o.TextScaled or o.Font == Enum.Font.Unknown or o:GetAttribute("NoFit") then return end

                -- wrapped text that grows downward by itself (section / element descriptions): it can never
                -- run out of width, so it simply takes the wanted (boosted) size
                if (o.AutomaticSize == Enum.AutomaticSize.Y and o.TextWrapped)
                    or (o.AutomaticSize == Enum.AutomaticSize.X and o:GetAttribute("AutoGrow")) then
                    local want = Cfg.textBoost and Cfg.textBoost(rec.base) or rec.base
                    if o.TextSize ~= want then
                        rec.applied = want
                        o.TextSize = want
                    end
                    return
                end
                if o.AutomaticSize ~= Enum.AutomaticSize.None then return end

                local txt = o.Text
                -- inputs with nothing typed show their placeholder (the subtle text), which uses the same size
                if txt == "" and o:IsA("TextBox") then txt = o.PlaceholderText end
                if txt == "" then
                    local want = Cfg.textBoost and Cfg.textBoost(rec.base) or rec.base
                    if o.TextSize ~= want then
                        rec.applied = want
                        o.TextSize = want
                    end
                    return
                end

                local s = totalScale(o)
                local w = o.AbsoluteSize.X / s - PAD
                local h = o.AbsoluteSize.Y / s
                if w < 6 or h < 6 then return end   -- not laid out yet / hidden (re-queued when its size changes)

                local base, wrapped = rec.base, o.TextWrapped
                if Cfg.textBoost then base = Cfg.textBoost(rec.base) end   -- Auto Text Size: start from the wanted (bigger) size
                if rec.w == w and rec.h == h and rec.txt == txt and rec.fnt == o.Font
                    and rec.wrap == wrapped and rec.fitBase == base then
                    return
                end
                rec.w, rec.h, rec.txt, rec.fnt, rec.wrap, rec.fitBase = w, h, txt, o.Font, wrapped, base

                local measure = o.RichText and stripRich(txt) or txt
                local function fits(size)
                    local ok, sz = pcall(TextSvc.GetTextSize, TextSvc, measure, size, o.Font,
                        Vector2.new(wrapped and w or 10000, 10000))
                    if not ok then return true end
                    if wrapped then return sz.Y <= h + 1 and sz.X <= w + 1 end
                    return sz.X <= w + 1
                end

                local minSize = base <= ABS_MIN and base or math.max(ABS_MIN, math.floor(base * MIN_RATIO))
                local size = base
                while size > minSize and not fits(size) do size = size - 1 end
                if o.TextSize ~= size then
                    rec.applied = size
                    o.TextSize = size
                end
            end

            local function register(o)
                if recs[o] or not (o:IsA("TextLabel") or o:IsA("TextButton") or o:IsA("TextBox")) then return end
                local rec = { base = o.TextSize }
                recs[o] = rec
                o:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(function() queue(o) end)
                o:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function() queue(o) end)
                o:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Font"):Connect(function() queue(o) end)
                o:GetPropertyChangedSignal("TextWrapped"):Connect(function() queue(o) end)
                if o:IsA("TextBox") then
                    o:GetPropertyChangedSignal("PlaceholderText"):Connect(function() queue(o) end)
                end
                o:GetPropertyChangedSignal("TextSize"):Connect(function()
                    if o.TextSize == rec.applied then return end   -- our own change
                    rec.base = o.TextSize                           -- the UI set a new design size
                    rec.applied = nil
                    queue(o)
                end)
                queue(o)
            end

            local function refitAll()
                for o in pairs(recs) do dirty[o] = true end
                for _, f in ipairs(Cfg.SubRecenters or {}) do task.defer(f) end   -- sub-tab pills re-fit their names
            end
            Cfg.fitText = register
            Cfg.refitText = refitAll

            -- process a limited batch per frame so dragging the scale grip / rotating the phone never stutters
            local conn
            conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if not screenGui.Parent then
                    conn:Disconnect()
                    return
                end
                local batch, n = {}, 0
                for o in pairs(dirty) do
                    n = n + 1
                    batch[n] = o
                    if n >= BUDGET then break end
                end
                for i = 1, n do
                    dirty[batch[i]] = nil
                    pcall(fitOne, batch[i])
                end
            end)

            for _, d in ipairs(screenGui:GetDescendants()) do register(d) end
            screenGui.DescendantAdded:Connect(register)
            screenGui:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(refitAll)   -- screen resize / rotation
        end

        -- ============================================================
        -- CLOSE CONFIRMATION
        -- ============================================================
        local ConfirmOverlay = Instance.new("TextButton")
        ConfirmOverlay.Name = "ConfirmOverlay"
        ConfirmOverlay.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        ConfirmOverlay.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
        ConfirmOverlay.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        ConfirmOverlay.Text = ""
        ConfirmOverlay.AutoButtonColor = false
        ConfirmOverlay.Active = true
        ConfirmOverlay.Visible = false
        ConfirmOverlay.ZIndex = 50
        ConfirmOverlay.Parent = screenGui

        local Dialog = Instance.new("CanvasGroup")
        Dialog.Name = "Dialog"
        Dialog.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        Dialog.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
        Dialog.Size = UDim2.new(0, 310, 0, 160)
        Dialog.BackgroundColor3 = P.Bg2
        Dialog.BackgroundTransparency = 0
        Dialog.BorderSizePixel = 0
        Dialog.ZIndex = 51
        Dialog.Parent = ConfirmOverlay
        corner(Dialog, 16)
        glassBorder(Dialog, 1.5, 0.1)
        local CONFIRM_SLIDE = 14   -- slide instead of scale, so the text never re-rasterizes

        local dIcon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
        dIcon.Size = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 22)
        dIcon.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -11, 0, 16)
        dIcon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        dIcon.ImageColor3 = P.Bad
        dIcon.Visible = false
        dIcon.ZIndex = 52
        dIcon.Parent = Dialog

        local dTitle = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        dTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 22)
        dTitle.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 44)
        dTitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        dTitle.Text = "Destroy the UI?"
        dTitle.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        dTitle.TextSize = 16
        dTitle.TextColor3 = P.Text
        dTitle.ZIndex = 52
        dTitle.Parent = Dialog

        local dSub = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        dSub.Size = UDim2.new(1, -32, 0, 30)
        dSub.Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 68)
        dSub.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        dSub.Text = "This closes the hub and turns off all features."
        dSub.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        dSub.TextSize = 12
        dSub.TextWrapped = true
        dSub.TextColor3 = P.Muted
        dSub.ZIndex = 52
        dSub.Parent = Dialog

        task.spawn(function()
            local t0 = tick()
            while not LucideReady and tick() - t0 < 30 do task.wait(0.2) end
            if LucideReady then setIcon(dIcon, "trash-2", P.Bad) end
        end)

        local function dialogButton(text, xScale, xOff, bg, txtCol)
            local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
            b.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -22, 0, 36)
            b.Position = UDim2.new(xScale, xOff, 1, -52)
            b.BackgroundColor3 = bg
            b.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
            b.Text = text
            b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            b.TextSize = 13
            b.TextColor3 = txtCol
            b.BorderSizePixel = 0
            b.AutoButtonColor = false
            b.ZIndex = 52
            b.Parent = Dialog
            corner(b, 10)
            softHover(b, bg, stroke(b, P.Line2, 1, 0.5))
            return b
        end

        local CancelBtn = dialogButton("Cancel", 0, 16, P.Bg3, P.Text)
        local YesBtn    = dialogButton("Yes",    0.5, 6, Color3.fromRGB(196, 48, 58), Color3.new(1, 1, 1))   -- deeper red than the theme's soft red
        YesBtn:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true)

        local confirmBusy = false
        local function showConfirm()
            if confirmBusy or ConfirmOverlay.Visible then return end
            ConfirmOverlay.Visible = true
            Dialog.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, CONFIRM_SLIDE)
            Dialog.GroupTransparency = 1
            local dStroke = Dialog:FindFirstChild("GlassBorder")
            if dStroke then dStroke.Transparency = 1 end
            local fadeIn = TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
            TweenService:Create(ConfirmOverlay, fadeIn, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.45 }):Play()
            TweenService:Create(Dialog, fadeIn, { GroupTransparency = 0 }):Play()
            if dStroke then TweenService:Create(dStroke, fadeIn, { Transparency = 0 }):Play() end
            TweenService:Create(Dialog, fadeIn, { Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0) }):Play()
        end

        local function hideConfirm()
            if confirmBusy then return end
            confirmBusy = true
            -- overlay, dialog and its outline all fade with the same tween so nothing lingers
            local fadeOut = TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
            TweenService:Create(ConfirmOverlay, fadeOut, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()
            TweenService:Create(Dialog, fadeOut, { GroupTransparency = 1 }):Play()
            local dStroke = Dialog:FindFirstChild("GlassBorder")
            if dStroke then TweenService:Create(dStroke, fadeOut, { Transparency = 1 }):Play() end
            local t = TweenService:Create(Dialog, fadeOut, { Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, CONFIRM_SLIDE) })
            t:Play()
            t.Completed:Wait()
            ConfirmOverlay.Visible = false
            confirmBusy = false
        end

        -- fades the whole hub out (and shrinks the window slightly) before it is destroyed
        local function fadeOutHub()
            local ti = TweenInfo.new(0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In)      -- shrink
            local fadeTi   = TweenInfo.new(0.4,  Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out) -- everything else, all at once
            local strokeTi = TweenInfo.new(0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out) -- outlines go first, never last
            local w, h = Main.Size.X.Offset, Main.Size.Y.Offset
            local old = MainScale.Scale
            local nv = old * 0.94
            local p = Main.Position
            TweenService:Create(MainScale, ti, { Scale = nv }):Play()
            TweenService:Create(Main, ti, { Position = UDim2.new(
                p.X.Scale, p.X.Offset + w * (old - nv) / 2,
                p.Y.Scale, p.Y.Offset + h * (old - nv) / 2) }):Play()
            for _, o in ipairs(screenGui:GetDescendants()) do
                local props
                if o:IsA("CanvasGroup") then
                    props = { GroupTransparency = 1, BackgroundTransparency = 1 }
                elseif o:IsA("GuiObject") then
                    props = { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }
                    if o:IsA("TextLabel") or o:IsA("TextButton") or o:IsA("TextBox") then
                        props.TextTransparency = 1
                        props.TextStrokeTransparency = 1
                    end
                    if o:IsA("ImageLabel") or o:IsA("ImageButton") then props.ImageTransparency = 1 end
                    if o:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then props.ScrollBarImageTransparency = 1 end
                elseif o:IsA("UIStroke") then
                    props = { Transparency = 1 }
                end
                if props then pcall(function()
                    TweenService:Create(o, o:IsA("UIStroke") and strokeTi or fadeTi, props):Play()
                end) end
            end
            task.wait(0.42)
        end

        local function destroyHub()
            pcall(fadeOutHub)
            screenGui:Destroy()
        end

        requestUnload = showConfirm
        CloseBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(showConfirm)
        CancelBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(hideConfirm)
        YesBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(destroyHub)

        -- ============================================================
        -- HOTKEYS
        -- ============================================================
        UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gp)
            if gp or IsBinding then return end
            if input.KeyCode == KEYBINDS.MENU then
                Main.Visible = not Main.Visible
                return
            end
        end)

        -- ============================================================
        -- INTRO ANIMATION  (smooth fade + rise; the window is never scaled, so text can't resize or jitter)
        -- ============================================================
        Main.Visible = false
        task.spawn(function()
            -- 1) wait for the first tab, then give async content + the column layout time to finish building
            local t0 = os.clock()
            while not currentTabName and os.clock() - t0 < 2.5 do task.wait() end
            task.wait(0.7)
            if State.TwoColumns then pcall(applyColumns, true) end
            for _ = 1, 6 do RunService.RenderStepped:Wait() end   -- let auto-sizing settle before anything is shown

            -- 2) remember every resting transparency
            local rec = {}
            local function add(o)
                local r
                -- everything inside Content (pages, class cards, toggles...) is faded as one group through Content,
                -- so stale per-object values can never overwrite what the features set for themselves
                if o ~= Content and o:IsDescendantOf(Content) then return end
                if o:IsA("GuiObject") then
                    r = { BackgroundTransparency = o.BackgroundTransparency }
                    if o:IsA("TextLabel") or o:IsA("TextButton") or o:IsA("TextBox") then r.TextTransparency = o.TextTransparency end
                    if o:IsA("ImageLabel") or o:IsA("ImageButton") or o:IsA("ViewportFrame") then r.ImageTransparency = o.ImageTransparency end
                    if o:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then r.ScrollBarImageTransparency = o.ScrollBarImageTransparency end
                    if o:IsA("CanvasGroup") then r.GroupTransparency = (o == Content) and 0 or o.GroupTransparency end
                elseif o:IsA("UIStroke") then
                    r = { Transparency = o.Transparency }
                end
                if r then rec[o] = r end
            end
            add(Main)
            for _, d in ipairs(Main:GetDescendants()) do add(d) end

            local base = Main.Position
            local RISE = 20
            local ok = pcall(function()
                for o, r in pairs(rec) do
                    for prop in pairs(r) do o[prop] = 1 end
                end
                Main.Position = UDim2.new(base.X.Scale, base.X.Offset, base.Y.Scale, base.Y.Offset + RISE)
                Main.Visible = true
                task.wait()

                -- 3) window frame first, then the contents a beat later
                local frameTi   = TweenInfo.new(0.55, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
                local contentTi = TweenInfo.new(0.5,  Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out, 0, false, 0.14)
                for o, r in pairs(rec) do
                    if o.Parent then
                        TweenService:Create(o, (o == Content) and contentTi or frameTi, r):Play()
                    end
                end
                TweenService:Create(Main, TweenInfo.new(0.7, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Position = base }):Play()

                -- the open page glides up slightly after the window
                local pg = currentTabName and pages[currentTabName]
                if pg then
                    pg.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 14)
                    TweenService:Create(pg, TweenInfo.new(0.65, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out, 0, false, 0.12),
                        { Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0) }):Play()
                end
            end)
            if not ok then   -- never leave the window invisible
                for o, r in pairs(rec) do
                    if o.Parent then for prop, v in pairs(r) do pcall(function() o[prop] = v end) end end
                end
                Main.Position = base
                Main.Visible = true
            end
        end)

        -- ============================================================
        -- STARTUP
        -- ============================================================
        task.spawn(function()
            task.wait(1)
            -- features are built after the window opens: wait for them (max 45s) so the config reaches every switch
            local waited = os.clock()
            while not Cfg.FeaturesLoaded and os.clock() - waited < 45 do task.wait(0.25) end
            -- only a config saved with "Save as Auto Load" is applied on startup; otherwise everything starts at defaults
            local auto = Cfg.getAuto()
            local loaded = false
            if auto then
                local data = Cfg.read(auto)
                if data then loaded = pcall(Cfg.apply, data) end
            end
            notify("Nauru Hub loaded", "Press " .. KEYBINDS.MENU.Name .. " to toggle the menu.", "success", 4)
            if loaded then notify("Auto load", "Loaded config: " .. auto, "info", 3.5) end
        end)

        pcall(applyColumns, true)
        pcall(switchTab, TAB_ORDER[1])
    end

    return {
        UI = { lockSection = lockSection, makeRowCard = makeRowCard, button = button, corner = corner, stroke = stroke, P = P, Pages = pages,
            icon = function(img, name, color)
                task.spawn(function()
                    local t0 = os.clock()
                    while not LucideReady and os.clock() - t0 < 30 do task.wait(0.2) end
                    if LucideReady then pcall(setIcon, img, name, color or P.Accent) end
                end)
            end },
        WindUI = UIKit.Hub.WindUI,
        Window = UIKit.Hub.Window,
        finish = finish,
        resetAll = UIKit.Hub.resetAll,
        screenGui = screenGui,
        Cfg = Cfg,
        MainScale = MainScale,
        SearchBox = SearchBox,
    }
end)()
